Chapter 551: Abandoned Person's Shirt

Teng Dong Lang and Bing Xiao come from the same background. They both served in the royal army."

“Even so, could he bring military equipment out with him?”

"It must have been a special situation. His Thin Ice Wall Shield is gold level. He must have exchanged it for his military merits. It's his personal property. What's so strange about him bringing it out? Even if it's not a personal item, haven't we seen plenty of military equipment on the black market?"

People were discussing it heatedly.

At the same time, many people sighed in regret for the dragonman youngster.

He had clearly done his best, but in the end, all his efforts were blocked by a shield.

However, the dragonman youngster was not disappointed.

He had expected gold level defensive equipment.

He was about to change direction and continue close combat when the whip returned to defend.

At the same time, Teng Dong Lang's eyes suddenly lit up.

·

Battle skill—Invisible Vine - Entangling Heart!

This was a very rare battle skill.

Even at the gold level, it could only be used at close range to hit the enemy in front of him.

This was one of Teng Dong Lang's trump cards for self-defense.

He was good at mid-range combat, but once a strong enemy closed the distance and engaged him in close combat, putting him in danger, this battle skill was an excellent way to escape from a difficult situation!

The most powerful aspect of this battle skill was that it was difficult to defend against.

It was an attack on the mind!

The dragonman youngster was hit by this battle skill, and his originally high fighting spirit quickly diminished, and his determination began to waver.

Negative emotions such as loss and despair entangled his heart like vines, quickly spreading and covering it.

At the same time, his battle qi was suppressed, breaking his state of full-power, high-speed consumption and slowing down significantly.

Externally, the dragonman youngster suddenly became listless, exposing a huge opening.

Teng Dong Lang took advantage of the opportunity to strike with the gold level whip.

Ice vines sprouted from the ground, binding the dragonman youngster.

Teng Dong Lang immediately used his whip-type battle skills to attack with all his might.

The whip danced wildly, causing the spectators to gasp in surprise.

The dragonman youngster did not have time to dodge and was covered by countless whip shadows, enduring the fierce attack.

Smack, smack, smack.

The light golden halo around the dragonman youngster's body quickly dimmed under the lashes of the whip, then shattered with a bang.

His earrings, nose ring, arm rings, and ankle rings shook violently and buzzed loudly.

The whip struck his dragon scales and the Golden Silk Chainmail.

The Golden Silk Chainmail was not intact.

It had already sustained significant damage in the battle against Xiong Ju and now, under the relentless lashes of the whip, it could no longer bear the strain.

The dragon scales couldn't withstand the blows in areas not protected by the chain mail and shattered one after another.

At this critical moment, a sudden chill entered the dragonman youngster's heart, and his chaotic will was suddenly stimulated, as if he had suddenly swallowed a mouthful of ice, and he suddenly sobered up!

“It's the Cold Heart Ice Bag and the Ice Heart Headband!” The dragonman youngster instantly realized what had saved him.

His negative emotions were severely suppressed, his battle qi was replenished, and his movements became agile.

His listless state was completely gone.

He swung his short spear, struggling to clear the space and escape from the enemy's attack.

His skin was torn and covered in blood.

He was in pretty bad shape.

His Golden Silk Chainmail was completely destroyed and damaged beyond repair.

The components of the Diamond Protection Set were dull and lifeless. It would take some time to reform the Diamond Body.

The dragonman youngster immediately retreated.

But Teng Dong Lang would not let him escape so easily.

Ice vines sprouted one after another, blocking the dragonman youngster with all their might.

Battle skill—Extended Whip.

The gold level whip suddenly grew in length, doubling its attack range and keeping the dragonman youngster within striking distance.

Teng Dong Lang stood motionless, while the dragonman youngster suffered his pursuit.

Then, he suddenly opened his mouth and spat out cold mist, causing frost flowers to fill the sky.

Battle skill—Mouth Mist - Calm Breath.

Battle skill—Mouth Mist - Frostbite.

Teng Dong Lang used two battle skills at the same time.

In the fog, all the aura coming from Teng Dong Lang merged into one, and it was impossible to distinguish between them.

Previously, the dragonman youngster could sense the aura of the whip and the ice vine and could tell which one was gold level and which one was an illusion.

But now, these auras were completely consistent, and the dragonman youngster's vision was also obscured by the fog.

At the same time, countless frost flowers brought icy cold damage, sucking and eroding the dragonman youngster's torn body, transforming into gold level icy cold battle qi that invaded his body and eroded it.

youngster's torn body, transforming into gold level icy cold battle qi that invaded his body and eroded it.

In the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' audience area, Men Shi, Tripleblade, and others couldn't help but stand up.

“It's dangerous!”

"Teng Dong Lang wants to press his advantage. Even though he has a firm lead, he has no intention of fighting a war of attrition! He wants to defeat the commander now!"

It wasn't just them; many spectators saw Teng Dong Lang's intention at this moment.

Bu Quan clasped her hands tightly together, her heart cold: “Could it be that Commander Long Fu's best attack this time has become the trigger for his defeat?”

“We're going to win.” In the viewing room, Bing Xiao stared at the magic image, the corners of his mouth slightly upturned.

But in the next moment, he saw Teng Dong Lang's face suddenly turn slightly startled in the magic image!

The fog obscured his vision, but Teng Dong Lang could clearly sense the situation inside.

Because this was within his Base.

In his perception, the dragonman youngster accurately avoided the powerful gold level whips.

Even in the thick fog, he could see clearly.

“Is it those goggles?” Teng Dong Lang guessed the truth.

It was the ski goggles that allowed the dragonman youngster to clearly see Teng Dong Lang's various ice vines and the gold level whip among the whip shadows.

The dragonman youngster suddenly rushed out of the cold fog!

The spectators couldn't help but cry out in unison.

Because they saw that Commander Long Fu not only still maintained his combat effectiveness, but also did not retreat, instead continuing to rush towards Teng Dong Lang!

Teng Dong Lang's heart skipped a beat.

He had used his Extended Whip battle skill and made the attack distance too long.

Now it was not easy to cancel the battle skill and retrieve the long whip, which would take at least three seconds.

Those three seconds were enough to become his huge weakness!

“Teng Dong Lang, come and taste the power of my reverse scale,” the dragonman youngster roared.

When he was still more than ten meters away from Teng Dong Lang, he ruthlessly activated his Sword Return Dragon Scale with full force, without holding back in the slightest.

A large amount of sword light shot out, forming a dazzling sword light storm!

The storm swept fiercely past, instantly engulfing Teng Dong Lang's entire figure.

Teng Dong Lang's field of vision was filled with white light.

The Thin Ice Wall Shield in front of him still stood stubbornly in front of him, blocking the sword qi coming from the front.

But there was too much sword qi.

It overflowed the shield and hit Teng Dong Lang's body.

The Flowing Wave Armor activated.

The water-like armor blocked the impact of the sword qi.

This was a gold level defensive item.

Its design was ingenious.

Driven by Teng Dong Lang's gold level battle qi, it was able to fully unleash its power, which was much stronger than the one in Xiong Ju's hands.

However, after a few seconds, the Flowing Wave Armor also showed signs of being unable to withstand the attack.

The reason was simple.

This sword qi attack was more comprehensive than the Machine Gun Fist, covering almost Teng Dong Lang's entire body.

Therefore, the Flowing Wave Armor could not be repaired in time.

Soon, the armor collapsed completely!

The sword qi began to wash away Teng Dong Lang's leather armor.

His leather armor was only silver level, so of course it couldn't withstand this level of attack, and it shattered in less than a second.

At this critical moment, Teng Dong Lang remained motionless.

He didn't even use any defensive battle skills!

Because in the next second, his inner shirt took effect.

A large number of intricate magic lines appeared on the surface of the snow-white shirt. The magic runes moved quickly, like chains.

A mysterious power was generated.

The shirt emitted a grayish-white light.

The light enveloped Teng Dong Lang's entire body, and despite the sword qi, it easily expanded and turned into a sphere in the blink of an eye.

All the sword qi washed over the gray sphere and then mysteriously disappeared.

Gold level defensive equipment—the Abandoned Person's Shirt.

The gray light sphere was not a traditional defensive measure, but rather a teleportation spell.

It channeled the massive sword qi surging toward it into spatial fissures or alternate dimensions.

The sword qi dissipates completely.

Crack, crack.

Cracks spread across every corner of the Thin Ice Wall Shield, and it finally couldn't bear the burden, completely shattering into pieces of thin ice.

Teng Dong Lang glanced nostalgically at the ice fragments scattered all over the ground.

This shield, which had accompanied him for many years and experienced many thrilling battles with him, had finally reached its end.

Teng Dong Lang raised his eyes, and in an instant, his gaze became sharp and cold, firmly locked on the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster was panting heavily.

When he poured out the accumulated power into his dragon scale, he was completely focused and exhausted his mind.

It was extremely mentally exhausting.

However, the sword qi did not achieve its goal. Although Teng Dong Lang lost a gold level shield and his silver level leather armor, his body remained unscathed!

The entire venue fell silent.

This clash of power had filled everyone with fear due to the overwhelming sword qi.

The result of the competition disappointed most people.

"How powerful is Teng Dong Lang! Until now, he hasn't been forced back by Long Fu, and he doesn't have a single scratch on him!" Some people exclaimed, even if they hated Teng Dong Lang, they couldn't help but admire his strength.

"No, don't look at the surface. In fact, he has been forced to use all his skills." Some people held a different view.

This view was agreed upon by many people.

“Look, when Teng Dong Lang was defending against the sword qi attack, he was completely unable to control the ice vines to deal with Commander Long Fu.”

“This is the best proof.”

“He can hold nothing back anymore!”

“What a pity.”

“I didn't expect Teng Dong Lang to have such special defensive equipment.”

“Long Fu has done well enough.”

"He is not just a normal silver level, but perhaps an ordinary gold level, and he would have won long ago. But unfortunately, Teng Dong Lang is not just an ordinary gold level, he is the leader of a gang!"

A button suddenly broke off and fell at Teng Dong Lang's feet.

Teng Dong Lang glanced at it and found that it was a button from the collar of the Abandoned Person's Shirt.

This meant that during the defense just now, this shirt had also been pushed to its limit. Although it had successfully repelled the sword qi, it was not without a cost.

Chapter 552: That’s the Magician Scroll I Gave!

The collar of his white shirt was half open, exposing half of Teng Dong Lang's chest.

The thin Teng Dong Lang stood still and sneered at the dragonman youngster, "Now, what hope do you have? Commander Long Fu."

"Dragon breath?"

"Perhaps you can try it."

The dragonman youngster had used this spell when fighting against Rou Zang. The Bayonet Gang had already obtained relevant information, and Teng Dong Lang had long prepared countermeasures.

The dragonman youngster's decision not to use the Scorching Dragon Breath was undoubtedly correct.

The dragonman youngster panted heavily and replied deliberately, “That's right, although the Sword Return Reverse Scale didn't defeat you, I've seen through your trump card.”

“Next, I'll use my dragon breath to finish you off!”

He first showed his determination to fight, but quickly changed his tone: “However...”

“You are indeed formidable; I've seen it for myself.”

“Perhaps...”

He was deliberately stalling for time.

When Teng Dong Lang spoke earlier, the ice vines did not attack.

Every time the dragonman youngster took a breath, his condition improved.

This was because the bloodline of the Flame Dragon King was rapidly healing his injuries. At the same time, the Automatic Blood Bottle he had swallowed earlier was also having a healing effect.

Don't forget, there was also the Grand Arena Medal!

This comprehensive silver level item was also enhancing the dragonman youngster's own recovery ability.

“Cunning dragonman!” Teng Dong Lang snorted coldly, immediately seeing through the dragonman youngster's delaying tactic.

Even if the dragonman youngster wanted to compromise with the Bayonet Gang, revise the previous agreement, and no longer make exorbitant demands, he wouldn't say it out loud in the dueling arena.

A large number of ice vines and whips attacked again.

The dragonman youngster clenched one fist and held a short spear in the other, defending himself with all his might while retreating.

The retreat was not easy.

With many new wounds and a large amount of battle qi expended, he finally pulled away, escaped the coverage of the Base, and was safe again.

“What's the point?” Ban Lan Gen sneered, "If it were me, I would admit defeat now. He won't have any chance next."

“Even if there's no chance, they have to fight to the end!” Xiong Ju glanced at Ban Lan Gen, his disapproval evident.

Although he had lost the previous fierce battle with the dragonman youngster, he had gained a lot of respect for him.

The dragonman youngster's determination at this moment, just like before, resonated deeply with Xiong Ju.

“Something's wrong,” Bing Xiao suddenly narrowed his eyes and stared at the magic image, “His recovery speed is a little too fast.”

“I clearly didn't see him take any medicine.”

“His recovery is far beyond that of the previous duel.”

Bing Xiao keenly noticed something unusual about the dragonman youngster.

It was mainly due to the effect of the Automatic Blood Bottle.

But Ban Lan Gen said, "It's useless. Even if he can recover, even if he can recover to his pre-battle state, so what?"

“Lord Teng Dong Lang's Base has expanded greatly, and his advantage is unbreakable.”

"Of course, this battle skill consumes a lot of battle qi. Lord Teng Dong Lang's battle qi reserves should be close to the warning line now."

“But it doesn't matter!”

"He still has my Battle Qi Syringe."

“Haha, look, the gang leader is starting to use my equipment.”

In the magic image, Teng Dong Lang took out the Battle Qi Syringe and jabbed himself in the chest.

His aura quickly rose, and his battle qi reserves quickly returned to their peak state.

“Now, his only weakness no longer exists, hahaha.” Ban Lan Gen laughed loudly.

“Quickly adjust it and enlarge the magic image. I want to see the expression on Long Fu's face right now.”

However, when Bing Xiao adjusted the magic image to reveal the details of the dragonman youngster's face, everyone saw a bright light flashing in his dragon eyes.

Then, they heard the dragonman youngster say, “I've been waiting for you to use this equipment, Teng Dong Lang.”

“Next, try this!”

The dragonman youngster took an item out of his bosom.

It was a gold level spell scroll.

“What is it?”

The entire audience was attracted by this scroll.

Only Bu Quan understood the details of this scroll. She was so excited that her whole body trembled slightly, and she shouted in her heart, "That's right, use it. Use it quickly, Commander Long Fu!"

The dragonman youngster opened the scroll, infused it with battle qi and spirit power, and without a moment's hesitation, triggered the scroll.

Gold level spell—Lava Hell!

The spell was released very quickly.

A huge magic circle spread out almost instantly, centered on the spot where the dragonman youngster was standing, covering almost the entire dueling arena.

Looking down from above, one could see that Teng Dong Lang's Base only occupied one-third of the magic formation.

The extremely complex magic formation suddenly appeared and then disappeared just as quickly.

But with just a glance, the owner of the Snow Bird Dueling Arena stood up in shock and shouted, “Quick, activate all defenses!”

The Snow Bird Dueling Arena immediately activated its full defenses, and a massive hemispherical light barrier suddenly appeared in the arena.

The light barrier, pale blue in color, covered the entire dueling arena, instantly separating the duelists from the audience.

Within the light barrier, the magic was taking effect.

The air suddenly became scorching hot.

Endless heat rose up, breaking through the ground to form lava fountains.

The fountains started small but quickly expanded, rising upward to form miniature volcanoes.

The volcanoes erupted, showering the sky with fiery rain.

Within a dozen seconds, the once-flat terrain of the arena became rugged and uneven. Some areas were covered in miniature volcanoes, while others were filled with lava pools.

Under the shocked gaze of the spectators, a hellish scene took over the original arena.

“Lava Hell spell, it's the Lava Hell spell,” someone recognized.

“What an extravagant move, I didn't expect Commander Long Fu to have such a trick up his sleeve,” many people shouted excitedly.

The audience outside the arena broke into cheers.

What they wanted to see most was this kind of dramatic duel, which was becoming more and more exciting.

“It's so exciting. It's been ten years since I've seen such an exciting heroic duel.”

“Commander Long Fu really wants to win, so much so that he's willing to use up a scroll like this.”

“A gold level spell scroll, and a battlefield spell like Lava Hell at that. This scroll has an astonishing market price, and it's priceless!”

The audience was buzzing with excitement, talking animatedly, many of them with faces flushed with excitement, even brighter than the flames of lava in the arena.

Listening to everyone's discussions and conversations, Bu Quan's eyes, hidden behind her glasses, revealed intense joy and pride.

She proudly shouted in her heart, “You never would have guessed, but this scroll was a gift from me to Commander Long Fu.”

“Don't ask about the price.”

“It's a free gift.”

“It's free!”

To be honest, after giving away the treasure, Bu Quan left the dock with some regret and doubt.

After all, giving away three precious items was equivalent to giving away half of her savings.

At the same time, Bu Quan was also skeptical about whether Commander Long Fu could live up to expectations and embarrass Teng Dong Lang in battle.

Now, Bu Quan had witnessed the entire process of how the dragonman youngster fought resolutely, how he fought fiercely, and how he used this spell scroll.

“Commander Long Fu, how lucky I am to have met you!”

“Thank you, thank you for forcing Teng Dong Lang to this point.”

"Come on, come on! If you can defeat Teng Dong Lang in public and destroy the reputation of the Bayonet Gang, I will give you all my savings!"

"I, Bu Quan, will repay your kindness!"

Inside the arena.

For the first time, Teng Dong Lang's face was as gloomy as water.

The moment the Lava Hell spell was released, he took the initiative to withdraw his Base.

The Base battle skill was to transform the battlefield and create a field advantage.

The Lava Hell also transformed the terrain, but one was fire and the other was ice, one was magic, and the other was a battle skill, and the two were completely incompatible.

If Teng Dong Lang still wanted to keep his Base, he would have to counteract the full power of the Lava Hell.

This was obviously quite unwise!

The power of the Lava Hell was enormous, and it would be very foolish to resist it head-on.

Magicians used spells with the help of external support. Although their own magic power was also involved, in fact, they had always been using spirit power to pry open a part of the power of heaven and earth.

Battle qi cultivators were skilled at using their own strength. When faced with magic, they always dodged and focused on breaking through the enemy's guard.

Exhausting all their battle qi against magic?

Unless forced by special circumstances, battle qi cultivators would never do such a thing.

A smart person like Teng Dong Lang would naturally not do such a thing!

He scanned the drastically changed battlefield and finally looked far away at the dragonman youngster: “I underestimated you, Commander Long Fu.”

"This is a carefully weakened battlefield-class spell scroll, “This is not the true power of Lava Hell."

Once this spell was used, the tactics he had previously adopted became a joke. The advantages he had worked so hard to accumulate were all gone.

In fact, it was even worse than that.

In order to maintain and accumulate the advantages of his Base, Teng Dong Lang had invested a lot of battle qi into it.

He remained motionless, defending himself for a long time, and even wasted a gold level shield.

Just as he was about to turn his advantage into victory, a spell scroll completely negated it.

Who would have thought that the dragonman youngster would have such a scroll?

To be fair, Teng Dong Lang's tactics were not wrong. He took advantage of the situation and misled the dragonman youngster for a period of time.

After that, Teng Dong Lang was willing to persevere and invest himself, which allowed him to maintain the initiative. Even though he had to remain motionless for a long time.

Every time the dragonman youngster charged, the spectators realized that the former was one step closer to defeat.

It was all thanks to the Lava Hell spell scroll that turned the tide!

It was no exaggeration to say that the dragonman youngster made 500% use of this scroll in actual combat.

This was because Teng Dong Lang's tactics happened to be completely countered by this scroll!

Who made Teng Dong Lang choose such a tactic?

The dragonman youngster did not consciously influence or control him. This was completely beyond the dragonman youngster's capabilities.

Therefore, Teng Dong Lang made this choice himself.

Chapter 553: Hope of Defeating a Gold Level

Even Bu Quan, who had given him the spell scroll, had not realized that it would bring such a reward.

At the time, she said to the dragonman youngster, “I hope this scroll will be of some help to you.”

It wasn't until now that Bu Quan realized what a great gift she had given the dragonman youngster!

A powerful weapon!

A trump card!

“If you lose this duel, then you won't have lost to just me,” the dragonman youngster said loudly.

Struck by the sudden gratitude, Bu Quan's body trembled, and her eyes reddened slightly.

“You cunning dragonman, but in this situation, it's not your turn to declare victory!”

Teng Dong Lang swung his whip and took the initiative to pounce on the dragonman youngster, shouting coldly, “This battle has only just begun.”

"Go! Charge!" Countless spectators cheered for the dragonman youngster.

The members of the Bayonet Gang cheered for their leader.

Amidst the excited atmosphere, the dragonman youngster did not engage in a passionate battle but instead retreated intensely.

He picked up the golden longbow, crossbow, and quiver with arrows left in the corner, and began to shoot from a distance while moving.

His tactics had changed again!

This scene greatly surprised the excited spectators.

But after they reacted, they could only accept this fact with strange and bitter expressions on their faces.

A calm analysis showed that the dragonman youngster's tactical choice was undoubtedly the most correct.

The dragonman youngster now had the advantage of the terrain, while Teng Dong Lang was suffering in the Lava Hell, constantly being weakened.

Teng Dong Lang had just used a Battle Qi Syringe, restoring his battle qi reserves to their peak, and his combat effectiveness was still intact.

The dragonman youngster, on the other hand, was injured and needed time to recover.

The members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps knew even more key information.

The Grand Arena Medal!

With this equipment, the longer the dragonman youngster stalled, the greater the increase he would get.

Before, there was no way.

If the dragonman youngster didn't stop him in advance, Teng Dong Lang's Base would quickly spread throughout the dueling arena, giving him such a great advantage that the dragonman youngster would be quickly defeated.

Now, the dragonman youngster had the advantage in time.

The dragonman youngster's tactical choice made Teng Dong Lang's face even more ugly.

This kind of clever enemy was the most troublesome.

Seeing Teng Dong Lang's expression, Bu Quan smiled like a flower.

It had been many years since she had seen her enemy suffer so much, being toyed with by a silver level battle qi cultivator in front of everyone.

This made her feel the joy of revenge.

“Even if you lose, Commander Long Fu, you have already met my expectations by coming this far.”

“After this duel, I will send you three more pieces of equipment!”

“Gold level.”

“Don't ask about the price.”

“It's a gift.”

Teng Dong Lang saw through the dragonman youngster's tactics and naturally tried his best to catch up and bring him within his attack range.

However, based on the information he had obtained earlier, the dragonman youngster knew that he was best at mid-range attacks, so he did not let his guard down in the slightest and did his best to maintain a long distance.

Teng Dong Lang naturally had a set of tricks for fighting distant enemies, which allowed him to close the distance between them.

However, when the dragonman youngster was hit by his battle skill, his speed dropped dramatically, and he was forced to clash with Teng Dong Lang, but by then, he had already shot all the arrows in his quiver!

The dragonman youngster held a short spear in one hand and clenched his fist in the other and clashed with Teng Dong Lang again.

He had thrown the short spear before and tried to retrieve it.

Teng Dong Lang had anticipated this and used it as bait to lure the dragonman youngster to pick up the spear, thereby bringing him within his attack range.

After a few attempts, the dragonman youngster saw through Teng Dong Lang's trap and had no intention of picking up the spear again for a short time.

With a spear in one hand and a fist in the other, he could use both spear and fist techniques, which gave him much more combat options than if he had two spears or two fists.

“What I need to do most now is to stall for time.”

“Let the effect of the Grand Arena Medal increase.”

“At the same time, I can still take hits in battle and recharge the energy of the Sword Return Dragon Scale!”

The dragonman youngster did not seek to achieve anything, but only to avoid mistakes, fighting while retreating.

Teng Dong Lang chased after him, constantly sneering and taunting him with his words, but to little effect.

After all, the dragonman youngster was weak against the strong, so he had no burden in using such tactics.

On the other hand, Teng Dong Lang, as a gold level battle qi cultivator, was unable to defeat the dragonman youngster for a long time, which greatly damaged his reputation.

Teng Dong Lang became increasingly angry and frequently used his battle skills.

The dragonman youngster's Diamond Body had recovered, and he relied mainly on this defense to struggle against Teng Dong Lang's attacks.

Teng Dong Lang specialized in ice battle skills, which he was skilled at using, and when used in combination, they were even more unpredictable.

Despite his best efforts to dodge, the dragonman youngster was still frequently hit.

There was no way around it, his movements were too monotonous, rushing straight ahead and sideways, making it easy to predict his movements.

After being hit, the cold air corroded the dragonman youngster's body, slowing down both his movement speed and attack speed.

Fortunately, the Cold Heart Ice Bag allowed the dragonman youngster to counteract the negative effects of the cold air corroding his body.

There was another key factor.

Before the battle, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had given the dragonman youngster a batch of dragon beast materials.

After the dragonman youngster swallowed them with his Bloodcore, his white dragon bloodline increased.

The Bloodcore had long since undergone a mutation, giving the dragonman youngster some basic frost resistance.

Although it was only basic, it triggered a qualitative change, allowing him to narrowly resist the negative state.

Teng Dong Lang's prolonged attack and frequent use of battle skills led to a sharp increase in battle qi consumption.

The dragonman youngster was also in a difficult situation.

There were several hair-raising moments when the defensive aura of the Diamond Body was broken by Teng Dong Lang.

His Golden Silk Chainmail was extremely tattered.

His dragon scales were broken, and the youngster's skin was covered with whip marks.

However, with the Automatic Blood Bottle, the Grand Arena Medal, and the recovery abilities of his legendary bloodline, the dragonman youngster was able to endure the pain.

He had brought medicine with him this time as well.

But since Teng Dong Lang took the initiative to attack, his offensive was continuous and endless, and they worked together, not giving the dragonman youngster any time to take medicine.

Not to mention using Life Scorching Dragon Breath.

Facing such a strong enemy as Teng Dong Lang, using such a big flaw in his technique was tantamount to begging for defeat and death.

"We can win this battle! The commander will definitely win." Lan Zao's face was determined, his eyes shining brightly.

“Although the situation is difficult, we do have a chance of winning.” Men Shi nodded in agreement.

He now admired the dragonman youngster greatly.

After all, Teng Dong Lang was at the gold level.

Not only had the dragonman youngster not been defeated, but he had also persisted in fighting until now, and there was even hope of victory!

Although he had turned the tables with a spell scroll, Men Shi felt from the bottom of his heart that the dragonman youngster deserved it.

It was all because of the dragonman youngster's hard work and fighting skills that had supported him and brought him to this point.

He deserved it!

Tripleblade's expression was complex as he exclaimed, "If he wins this duel, the commander will gain unimaginable fame! And the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will also benefit greatly."

“It's not that no one at the silver level has ever defeated someone at the gold level, but those are basically special circumstances.”

“Today, both sides are fighting at full strength, and in front of so many spectators; a rare occurrence in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom for the last decade.”

"As long as he defeats Teng Dong Lang in this battle, the commander will be famous throughout the kingdom. Our mercenary corps will rise to become the most sought-after one!"

The gap between the silver level and the gold level was obvious.

Precisely because the vast majority of people couldn't do it, if the silver level won, it would be all the more precious!

Tripleblade once led a mercenary group and was quite knowledgeable about their business and future prospects.

Upon hearing this, Men Shi became even more excited.

But at the same time, he also became worried and immediately asked, "Doesn't our identity matter? After all, we are outsiders, and we have a background as arms dealers..."

Men Shi was not one of the survivors, so he was not yet qualified to know the core identity of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Tripleblade smiled and shrugged indifferently, "What's there to fear? We're a mercenary group registered through official channels. Even if they suspect us, they'd have to find the Mercenary Guild first."

Tripleblade added in his mind, "Besides, we have no connections to arms dealers. Even if the Ice Sculpture Kingdom wanted to investigate us or gather evidence of our criminal records, it's impossible."

“If they can't investigate us, they can't cause us trouble.”

“They might even overestimate us because of this, and out of fear, they won't dare to take action easily.”

Men Shi was reassured and couldn't help but have higher expectations for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and his own future.

He stared at the dragonman youngster in the fierce battle and muttered, "How is the energy storage of the Sword Return Dragon Scale? It should be almost full, right?"

Men Shi was even more eager to see the dragonman youngster turn the tables and win with the Sword Return Dragon Scale.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' audience area was full of anticipation, but in contrast, the atmosphere in the Bayonet Gang's VIP viewing room was solemn, and the leaders were worried.

Xiong Ju frowned deeply and Ban Lan Gen stopped shouting as he revealed a bitter expression.

Spring Boxer also looked nervous. Although he admired the dragonman youngster, he was more inclined to see his own gang leader win.

Bing Xiao was, after all, the former gang leader, and seeing this situation, he spoke up to boost morale: “There's no need to worry too much.”

"Even if the Sword Return Dragon Scale successfully accumulates energy, it still needs the right moment to release it. Before, it was because the gang leader stood still that he was easily hit."

“If Commander Long Fu rashly unleashes his sword qi, it is very likely that the gang leader will be able to dodge it.”

“His best tactic should be to fight the gang leader at close range.”

“Only at the closest distance can he maximize the chance of his sword qi hitting the target.”

“But it's not that easy to achieve this.”

“You have to believe in the gang leader, he won't let Long Fu succeed easily!”

Without a doubt, the Sword Return Dragon Scale was the dragonman youngster's trump card.

Not only the two combatants, but also the forces behind them and the countless spectators were thinking about this key factor.

Suddenly, a dazzling white-hot sword qi erupted from the Sword Return Dragon Scale!

Chapter 554: The Final Spear Throw

The sword qi shot out and was shattered by the gold level whip.

At the same time, it also startled many people.

Ban Lan Gen let out a low cry, causing Xiong Ju to glare at him fiercely.

Bing Xiao also frowned slightly in dissatisfaction, but to be honest, his heart had just skipped a beat.

On the surface, Bing Xiao remained calm. He had to stabilize the morale of his troops.

He snorted coldly, with a hint of disdain, and analyzed the situation, “It seems that Commander Long Fu wants to take action.”

“He has been on the defensive the whole time. If he doesn't act now, he will really be defeated.”

“That sword qi blast just now was both a threat and a test.”

“But our gang leader had already anticipated it and directly shattered it with a whip.”

As soon as Bing Xiao finished speaking, the dragonman youngster fired several more sword qi blasts in the magic image.

Teng Dong Lang immediately switched from offense to defense and directly extinguished the sword qi blasts.

“What's going on?”

“What kind of tactic is this?”

For a moment, the audience looked puzzled.

Bing Xiao's brow furrowed, and he also felt confused: if Long Fu abused his sword qi like this, how could he store up more energy?

In the next moment, the dragonman youngster used the sword qi blasts even more frequently.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh.

For a moment, sword qi flew everywhere.

Teng Dong Lang was forced to retreat.

The dragonman youngster seized this opportunity and quickly retreated, taking medicine while retreating.

Unexpectedly, Teng Dong Lang chuckled softly and immediately used his battle skill, Extended Whip.

The whip lashed out, interrupting the dragonman youngster's action of taking medicine, and he almost spilled the medicine.

Seeing this scene, Bing Xiao suddenly frowned and laughed loudly, “Hahaha, I know.”

“It's battle qi!” The human ringleader on the side followed suit and spoke up, as he also understood.

Bing Xiao nodded, his face brightening as if the clouds had cleared: "That's right, that's it! Long Fu's battle qi is insufficient."

“He's not a gold level battle qi cultivator. As a silver level battle qi cultivator, he can't compare to a gold level battle qi cultivator in terms of the quality and quantity of his battle qi.”

Bing Xiao looked at Xiong Ju again: "Do you remember the last duel? Long Fu and you were both in the final stages, constantly taking medicine."

“Judging from that situation, Long Fu's battle qi reserves and recovery speed are only slightly above normal.”

"Don't forget, there is another key point. He practices the Bursting Explosion battle qi, which is powerful but easily depleted."

Xiong Ju nodded, a flash of recollection passing through his eyes: "That's right. His battle skills also consume battle qi very quickly. Once the battle time is extended, he needs to replenish a large amount of battle qi."

"Come to think of it, after Long Fu discovered the gang leader's Base, he entered a state of full power. He has been fighting in this state until now, so it's normal for him to be low on battle qi."

The others also realized this.

“Yes, he's only at the silver level.” Ban Lan Gen's expression was somewhat complicated.

He realized that, at some point, he had begun to regard the dragonman youngster as being at the gold level.

It was only at this moment, when he was reminded of this, that he understood that the person who was currently fighting back and forth with the gang leader, and still had a chance of victory, was actually at the same silver level as him!

The others felt the same way as Ban Lan Gen.

Spring Boxer sighed, “Commander Long Fu is truly remarkable to have made it this far.”

“Although he has luxurious equipment, his own strength is also sufficient, which is why he has been able to fight this far.”

In the past, when others heard Spring Boxer's praise, they would roll their eyes and feel annoyed.

But now, they had no objections.

Even though the dragonman youngster was their enemy and had caused them great trouble and even humiliation, they had to admit that the dragonman youngster was no ordinary silver level!

Even Bing Xiao sighed, “I have to say, Long Fu is no ordinary person.”

“Even if someone else had the same equipment as him, it would be unlikely that they could have fought until now.”

“His fighting spirit, technique, and mental strength are all top-notch. He has come this far, so even though he lost, he still has honor.”

Although the duel was still ongoing, from his words, Bing Xiao had already considered the dragonman youngster to be the loser.

More and more spectators also saw through the dragonman youngster's predicament.

Insufficient battle qi!

This discovery made the people who supported the dragonman youngster and expected him to defeat the stronger opponent look gloomy, sighing with regret for the dragonman youngster.

"Without battle qi, he has lost his foundation. How can he fight at an extraordinary level?"

"Alas! Commander Long Fu ultimately failed."

“It's already quite remarkable that he made it this far.”

"Teng Dong Lang gave it his all. His battle skills, equipment, and mental strength were all excellent. If it had been an ordinary gold level battle qi cultivator, Long Fu might have won."

“Commander Long Fu chose a strong opponent, and because of that, he proved his own extraordinary abilities.”

“If he could take a potion to replenish his battle qi, Long Fu might still have hope.”

"Come on, you saw what just happened. Teng Dong Lang is a veteran of the battlefield, how could he give Long Fu such an opportunity?"

But Bu Quan never gave up hope.

"Hang in there, hang in there! Commander Long Fu."

She secretly cheered on the dragonman youngster and prayed for him.

“As long as you find an opportunity to take the potion, you will be able to replenish your battle qi!”

She was good at alchemy, but not at all good at fighting. Her insight was poor, and she didn't realize that it was actually very difficult for the dragonman youngster to find such an opportunity.

The dragonman youngster rolled around awkwardly and dodged the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

When Teng Dong Lang began his pursuit, he immediately used this annoying weapon.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer pursued relentlessly.

The dragonman youngster had no choice but to draw on his sword qi again to temporarily repel it.

“Is this as far as I can go?” The dragonman youngster's heart was filled with regret.

His battle qi was severely depleted, and under duress, he had no choice but to draw on the sword qi reserves in his Sword Return Dragon Scale ahead of time.

But this was undoubtedly drinking poison to quench his thirst.

“With such a severe depletion of sword qi, there's no way I can turn the tide.”

“If I could take a potion, I could delay it a little longer.”

In fact, he had already taken a potion.

It was not a healing potion stored in an Automatic Blood Bottle, but one specifically used to restore battle qi.

Earlier, when the dragonman youngster repeatedly attacked Teng Dong Lang's Base, and after retreating to a safe distance, he swallowed it with a flourish.

Otherwise, with only his own battle qi reserves, he would not have been able to hold out to this extent.

However, once Teng Dong Lang was no longer bound by his Base and took the initiative to pursue, the dragonman youngster no longer had the opportunity to take the medicine.

“Taking medicine in front of him would be a mistake!”

The dragonman youngster tried once, and after failing, he completely understood that he would not have such an opportunity.

Teng Dong Lang's strength surpassed his own, and he was also very rigorous, controlling the attack distance with exquisite precision.

This medium range was enough for him to get close in time to prevent the dragonman youngster from taking medicine. At the same time, if the dragonman youngster charged at him, he could quickly retreat.

Up to this point in the battle, he had not shown any flying abilities.

The dragonman youngster was basically certain that Teng Dong Lang did not have any flying battle skills.

If he did, he would not need to run around in this Lava Hell.

However, Teng Dong Lang's mobility was not weaker than that of the dragonman youngster. More accurately, it was slightly superior to that of the dragonman youngster.

Even moving at full speed, the dragonman youngster couldn't shake him off or pull away.

The youngster racked his brains, searching for a way to break the deadlock.

“Spear technique can't solve my predicament.”

“Until now, I still haven't picked up a short spear.”

Teng Dong Lang was really good at controlling the battlefield.

Of course, this was based on his solid advantage.

“Fist techniques won't work either.”

“Not to mention long-range attacks, I've almost used up all my arrows.”

The dragonman youngster thought about his equipment.

Only the Sword Return Dragon Scale gave him hope!

He couldn't rely on defensive equipment in this situation.

Thinking this, the youngster suddenly felt his legs go weak, and he staggered, almost falling down while running at full speed!

The dragonman youngster was startled and instantly understood: his body had also reached its limit.

Even though the Automatic Blood Bottle was constantly healing his wounds, the blood he had lost could not be quickly replenished.

Only the flesh wounds could heal quickly.

This scene fell into the eyes of those who cared, once again triggering a wave of sighs.

Teng Dong Lang saw the dragonman youngster's weakness and attacked again, whipping the dragonman youngster's back with a whip.

Smack!

The dragonman youngster was knocked down on the spot.

He used the momentum to roll on the ground, dodging the hammer blow from the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

He kicked the ground with his back foot, causing the ring mound to flash with yellow light, and counterattacked Teng Dong Lang.

Teng Dong Lang was prepared and quickly retreated.

The whip was light, but when it landed on the dragonman youngster, it was as sharp as a sword.

The dragonman youngster was whipped until blood splattered, his momentum dissipated, and he could only retreat.

Teng Dong Lang followed again.

He followed the tactical principle of advancing when the enemy retreated and retreating when the enemy advanced, firmly controlling the distance between the two sides.

The dragonman youngster was only at the silver level and could not condense his battle qi into a corporeal form, so he was completely overwhelmed.

He kept trying to get closer to Teng Dong Lang.

But times were different. Teng Dong Lang was no longer bound by his Base and was very agile. The distance was clearly not long, but after actually trying, the dragonman youngster couldn't help but feel a sense of despair.

“The gang leader's control of distance is simply exquisite,” Xiong Ju exclaimed.

Ban Lan Gen laughed loudly again, "Hahaha, this is so funny. Look at the dragonman, every time he tries to get close for a melee battle, he gets beaten to a pulp by the gang leader and has to run away again. It's really embarrassing."

Bing Xiao stroked his beard and smiled.

As the battle progressed, the dragonman youngster used more and more sword qi, and they all gradually calmed down.

There was no chance at all!

“It's difficult this time,” Tripleblade sighed, "If the commander could get into close combat, there would still be a little hope. It depends on whether Teng Dong Lang will make a mistake next."

Obviously, Tripleblade also saw that, based on Teng Dong Lang's current abilities, under normal circumstances, it was impossible for the dragonman youngster to get close to him.

If it was just the beginning of the battle, there might still be a chance for close combat.

But the battle had been going on for so long that both Teng Dong Lang and the dragonman youngster had a clear understanding of each other's abilities.

The boost brought by the Grand Arena Medal, which the dragonman youngster had secretly hoped for, could not offset his current huge disadvantage.

“If the Grand Arena Medal was a gold level equipment, perhaps the current situation would be different.”

“But it's only silver level.” Tripleblade sighed deeply in his heart.

In this gold level battle, silver level equipment seemed a little inadequate.

But that's not to say that the Grand Arena Medal didn't help the dragonman youngster.

Its help was still substantial.

Without this help, the dragonman youngster might not have been able to hold out until now.

But this level of help was far from enough to allow the dragonman youngster to turn the tables again.

Outside the arena.

The owner of the Snow Bird Dueling Arena was also shaking his head.

“What a pity.”

“It's such a shame!”

He expressed pity for the dragonman youngster.

If the dragonman youngster defeated the stronger opponent, then the Snow Bird Dueling Arena would also benefit greatly.

The dragonman youngster's fame would spread throughout the kingdom, and the Snow Bird Dueling Arena would also become well-known.

Not only that, but the video of a silver level defeating the gold level expert would definitely become the hottest bestseller of the year throughout the country.

This would be a very considerable fortune!

The dragonman youngster's vision began to darken.

He stumbled and lost his strength more and more often.

His injuries were also becoming more serious.

There was still a lot of healing potion left in the Automatic Blood Bottle, but even with the recovery speed of the dragon bloodline, it couldn't keep up with the damage caused by Teng Dong Lang.

The dragonman youngster was sliding into the abyss of defeat!

Teng Dong Lang played extremely steadily, not giving the dragonman youngster any chance.

Expecting him to make a mistake?

It was simply impossible!

“No, it's not over yet.”

“Commander Long Fu, you still have a chance. Don't give up.”

“Even if there's only a sliver of a chance, you have to keep going, right?”

Bu Quan stared at the magic image.

She never gave up hope, stubbornly waiting for a miracle to happen.

“You have no hope!” Teng Dong Lang sneered, chasing after him and striking the dragonman youngster's back again.

“Long Fu, the outcome of this battle had been decided from the very beginning.”

“Even if you used that special spell scroll, what difference did it make?”

“At most, it can only delay your defeat...”

Teng Dong Lang paused, not finishing his sentence, as the dragonman youngster counterattacked again.

He immediately retreated, successfully pulling away.

But this time, the dragonman youngster did not retreat.

He had truly reached his limit.

“I can only bet on this one chance!” With this determination, the dragonman youngster resolutely used the Sword Return Dragon Scale.

In the next moment, a torrent of sword qi gushed forth.

But this time, the scale of the sword qi was much smaller than last time.

Teng Dong Lang was already on guard and immediately used his battle skill to increase his speed and dodge it.

But in the next moment, his legs went weak and his expression changed suddenly.

At that moment, he failed to unleash his battle qi!

His battle skill immediately drew a backlash.

The next moment, the sword qi rushed towards him.

At the most critical moment, he made a mistake!

Seeing this scene, a large number of spectators gasped and stood up.

In the VIP viewing room.

Bing Xiao and the others couldn't sit still either and stood up to watch.

“What's going on? The leader didn't dodge it?” Xiong Ju couldn't believe it.

The next moment, a gray light formed a hemispherical defensive shield, appearing within the sword qi surge.

The Abandoned Person's Shirt!

No matter how the sword qi surge surged, this layer of light remained unmoved.

It was a very strange defense method.

It didn't harden against the attack but instead transmitted the sword qi away.

In just a few seconds, the sword qi storm came to an abrupt halt.

The accumulation this time was not as sufficient as last time. And before it poured out, the dragonman youngster repeatedly drew on and consumed it.

The most powerful counterattack had been used up.

But the dragonman youngster still had one last strike left.

Battle skill—Spear Throw!

He clenched the gold level short spear tightly in his hand and threw it with all his remaining battle qi and strength.

The short spear collided with the gray-white light ball but was not teleported away.

The dragonman youngster seemed to hear an explosion.

The white-hot sword qi slowly dissipated, and the gray light ball disappeared, revealing the protected user.

Teng Dong Lang's lower body was in a lunge position, his upper body was straight, and his face was fierce.

He stood motionless, as if frozen in place.

The dragonman youngster fell to his knees with a thud.

He was about to admit defeat when suddenly his pupils contracted, and he froze on the spot.

Then, his mouth slowly opened wide.

“Is this an illusion?”

In his field of vision, there was a large hole in Teng Dong Lang's heart, visible from front to back.

The short spear had fallen behind him, covered in blood.

Along the path it had flown, there were large amounts of flesh and fragments of heart.

This was...

A fatal blow.

Plop.

As if a gentle breeze had blown, Teng Dong Lang's body fell to the ground like a broken sack.

Immediately afterwards, bright red blood quickly spread out, like a red carpet, covering the body of a gold level battle qi cultivator.

He...

Was dead.

Chapter 555: The Bayonet Gang is in Danger

The entire arena fell silent.

Teng Dong Lang's death came as a shock to everyone.

Even the dragonman youngster who killed Teng Dong Lang was no exception.

Looking at the corpse not far away, the dragonman youngster's heart was filled with a sense of unreality.

In the audience area at the edge of the arena.

Lan Zao, Tripleblade, and the others were all stunned.

They had imagined that the dragonman youngster would use his Sword Return Dragon Scale, seize the last chance, and win with difficulty. They never thought that the dragonman youngster would kill Teng Dong Lang!

“Ah, ah, ah!” Men Shi was so excited that he couldn't speak in complete sentences. He could only point at Teng Dong Lang's corpse and stare wide-eyed.

There was someone even more overjoyed than him, and that was Bu Quan.

Bu Quan had jumped out of her seat and was so close to the magic image that she was almost about to dive into it.

“Dead?”

“Teng Dong Lang is dead!”

"Commander Long Fu killed him?

“Oh my God, oh my God, I must be dreaming.”

She couldn't help but pinch herself, and the pain immediately came.

The next moment, she was in tears.

Of course, it wasn't from the pain, but from the joy in her heart!

What could be more satisfying than revenge?

Especially seeing the leader of the Bayonet Gang die right before her eyes!

In the VIP viewing room.

“Impossible.” Bing Xiao also left his seat, the calmness and composure on his face completely disappearing.

He stared intently at the magic image and growled, “This is absolutely impossible!”

“This is fake, it must be fake.”

He had such a big advantage before, but at the last moment, everything took a sudden turn for the worse.

Not only did Teng Dong Lang lose, but he also lost his life.

This was far too different from the previous battle situation, far beyond the comprehension of the higher-ups of the Bayonet Gang.

Xiong Ju, Ban Lan Gen, and the others were all stunned.

Suddenly, Spring Boxer shouted loudly, burst through the door, and rushed out.

The others were in a daze and didn't hear what the Spring Boxer had shouted.

But that shout snapped them out of their stupor.

“Oh God, great Oak Grandfather, what have I seen?” Ban Lan Gen covered his face in panic.

"Am I hallucinating? Is that what's happening?" The human ringleader looked at the others with a hopeful gaze, hoping for an answer.

But Xiong Ju shook his head.

In those few seconds, this fierce fighter of the Bayonet Gang had already turned red-eyed, filled with bloodshot veins.

He growled softly, “He’s dead! Our gang leader has fallen in battle!”

The human ringleader’s body swayed as if struck by lightning.

He collapsed onto his seat, staring at the pool of blood and corpses in the magic image, muttering, “Dead? The gang leader... died just like that?"

“Something's fishy, something's wrong!” the demon hybrid ringleader screamed. "Why did the gang leader suddenly stop at the last moment instead of dodging? He clearly had the ability to do so. And why didn't the Abandoned Person's Shirt block that short spear, even though he had activated it?"

“Someone desecrated this heroic duel. Yes, there must have been outsiders meddling!”

“They saw that the Long Fu had no hope of winning, so they tampered with it.”

“Right, it must be that way!”

“What are we waiting for?” Upon hearing this, Xiong Ju was so angry that his hair stood on end, "Grab your weapons, let's go find Long Fu and ask him about it, and search the scene for evidence!”

In the Bayonet Gang, Xiong Ju respected Teng Dong Lang the most.

Teng Dong Lang's death had a strong psychological impact on Xiong Ju.

Originally, since his own gang leader had died in a heroic duel, Xiong Ju couldn’t say much, only feeling filled with sorrow and pain.

But now, upon hearing that someone had tampered with the sacred heroic duel in his heart, he was filled with rage and wanted to uncover the truth.

Seeing Xiong Ju approach the wall and grab the Gold Flower Hacksaw, the human ringleader hurriedly stopped him: “Wait!”

“What's most important now isn't to uncover the truth.”

“The gang leader is dead, and our Bayonet Gang is in danger!”

These words had no effect on Xiong Ju, but they startled Bing Xiao, who had been staring blankly, as if he had been doused with icy seawater in the dead of winter.

A strong sense of crisis rose within him, and Bing Xiao snapped back to reality.

He shouted, “Everyone, stay calm!”

“Quickly collect the leader's body and return to the Bayonet Gang.”

“I will immediately summon my slave to defend the headquarters!”

“Until the next gang leader is elected, I will take charge of the entire situation.”

"Everyone, we must unite. The Bayonet Gang is now in its most dangerous moment. To overcome this crisis, we must work together!"

At this critical moment, the former gang leader stepped forward and took charge of the situation.

The other ringleaders turned their attention to him.

Whether it was hatred, anger, confusion, or panic, seeing Bing Xiao take such responsibility, they gradually calmed down.

Seeing the ringleaders show their obedience, Bing Xiao breathed a sigh of relief.

The first step was complete.

He had just been worried that his prestige was not enough to lead these people.

After all, they were not his confidants, but Teng Dong Lang's.

The position of gang leader was also quite tempting to these ringleaders

Now it seemed that these ringleaders were still reasonable.

However, the real headache was still there, and it had to be faced with caution and resolved with all efforts.

The Bayonet Gang was already in a bad situation, and they wanted to use this heroic duel to solve their problems.

This was the solution agreed upon by Teng Dong Lang and Bing Xiao.

The Bayonet Gang wanted to fully demonstrate their strength in this duel to stabilize the hearts of the people and revive their prestige.

It was because of this consideration that Teng Dong Lang took advantage of the situation and adopted the Base tactic at the beginning of the battle. The goal was to remain motionless and push Long Fu aside with ease.

But unexpectedly, the final outcome of this duel was his death in battle!

Who could have predicted this ending?

Even the most daring guess would have been that Long Fu would win and defeat Teng Dong Lang.

Due to Teng Dong Lang's death in battle, the Bayonet Gang's situation immediately fell into an irreparably dire state.

This was the world of the strong.

The Bayonet Gang was able to become the local tyrant and occupy a large amount of resources in Snow Bird Harbor for one main reason: their gang leader was at the gold level.

Otherwise, Bing Xiao would not have voluntarily ceded his position to Teng Dong Lang.

The four gold level experts were the highest-ranking figures in Snow Bird Harbor.

Teng Dong Lang was one of them.

Now that Teng Dong Lang had died in battle, the four highest levels of power were missing a piece, and the balanced political landscape had been disrupted.

“Fortunately, the situation is not the worst.”

“I still have my gold level giant slave, so I can still barely control the situation.”

“However, the Bayonet Gang's territory must be reduced.”

“Only Teng Dong Lang could handle something of this scale when he was alive.”

Thinking of this, Bing Xiao couldn't help but cast a hateful and murderous gaze at the dragonman youngster in the magic image.

It was him.

It was this dragonman who caused the Bayonet Gang to fall into its current situation.

Damn him.

He deserved to die!

Bing Xiao was filled with regret.

If he had ignored the consequences and surrounded and killed the dragonman youngster during the conflict, would he have avoided the current trouble?

“Teng Dong Lang is dead?!”

"My god, the leader of a gang was killed by Long Fu!

“Why didn't he dodge in the end?”

"His breathing was disordered, so he failed to use his battle skills? Well deserved! Such a sinner truly deserves such an end."

"Snow Bird Harbor is about to erupt into chaos. The Bayonet Gang has lost its strongest combat power; there will undoubtedly be turmoil. I must leave this place of trouble immediately."

The crowd gradually grew noisy.

People finally accepted the reality before them.

Some were excitedly chatting, some were discussing their doubts, and others were thinking more long-term, already planning to leave tonight.

It was foreseeable that Snow Bird Harbor would be plunged into turmoil for some time to come.

Teng Dong Lang was dead!

With the fall of a gold level powerhouse, his sudden disappearance and the power vacuum he left behind would trigger fierce competition among all levels and factions.

“Let me in, let me in!” Spring Boxer rushed forward, attracting countless curses, and was eventually stopped at the edge of the arena.

He shouted; his eyes fixed on Teng Dong Lang's body.

The owner of the Snow Bird Dueling Arena glanced at Teng Dong Lang, then scanned the entire arena, and announced loudly: "The winner of this duel is Commander Long Fu."

Countless people cheered in unison.

Many people put aside their discussions and plans to leave, and at this moment, they offered their applause and encouragement to the dragonman youngster.

The members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps immediately rushed into the arena and helped their leader up.

But this time was different from last time. The dragonman youngster frowned, his eyes full of doubt.

He did not enjoy this moment of glory that belonged to him, but said to Lan Zao, “Let's go, let's leave here and go back to the ship first.”

As soon as he finished speaking, the closed door of the arena was forced open from the outside.

A group of people barged in.

The arena owner was about to get angry, but when he saw that the person leading the group was the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, he immediately shut his mouth.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord looked at the arena, first confirming Teng Dong Lang's death, then looking at the dragonman youngster.

He congratulated him, "Commander Long Fu, you are truly a fighter! You are a true believer in justice!"

“You actually killed a gold level opponent as a silver level.”

“You have made a name for yourself in one battle!”

“City Lord...” The dragonman youngster was about to respond but saw the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord raise his hand to stop him from continuing.

Then, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's voice clearly spread throughout the arena: “I once again declare that the Bayonet Gang is guilty of heinous crimes and is a cancer on Snow Bird Harbor. Only by eradicating this cancer can we bring a truly bright future to this city.”

“Bystanders, do not move rashly. I am here in person only to arrest the relevant gang members!”

"All members of the Bayonet Gang, listen up. Surrendering your weapons is your only way out. If you resist, you will end up like this person."

With that, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took a human head out of his spatial storage ring and threw it on the ground.

The skull was huge, with a ferocious expression frozen on its face before death.

It was the head of a giant, exuding a gold level aura.

Seeing such a head, Bing Xiao's face turned pale in an instant. He immediately recognized it as the head of his gold level giant slave!

“The Bayonet Gang is in danger!”

“Run away, break out of the dueling arena first, then scatter and escape.”

“Every man for himself!”

A flash of cruelty flashed in Bing Xiao's eyes as he gave the order in a hoarse voice.

Chapter 556: Choosing a Store

The date of the duel between the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang was destined to be remembered by the whole of Snow Bird Harbor.

Because on this day, two major events occurred simultaneously.

These two events were closely related to each other.

First, the leader of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, as a silver level battle qi cultivator, launched a heroic challenge and not only successfully defeated the gold level Teng Dong Lang, but also killed him on the spot.

Second, the Bayonet Gang was destroyed!

The leader of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's army was known for his decisive actions.

He directly mobilized the magic teams, all the city guards, and even the military camps outside the city.

He struck like thunder, and nothing could stand in his way!

The Bayonet Gang's defenses were extremely weak.

At that time, the heroic duel between Teng Dong Lang, the leader of the Bayonet Gang, attracted almost all of the gang's high-ranking members. The only one left to preside over daily affairs was the dwarf ringleader.

The latter actually wanted to come and watch the battle, but unfortunately, he was the only loser in the lottery.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took the lead and personally intervened.

The dwarf ringleader was only at the silver level, unlike the powerful dragonman youngster who could fight above his level, so he was unable to resist. He tried to escape, but his speed was not fast enough, and after suffering heavy losses, he was captured alive.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spared his life because he recognized his talent for forging.

He wiped out most of the Bayonet Gang's base and rushed to the Snow Bird Dueling Arena.

When he burst in, the Bayonet Gang's senior members were completely unaware and did not have time to gather any military intelligence.

The current leader of the Bayonet Gang, Teng Dong Lang, was killed in the duel, and the former leader, Bing Xiao, had just stabilized the senior members when the army arrived at the city gates.

Bing Xiao still had fighting spirit, but the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took the initiative and took out the head of the gold level giant slave.

It turned out that long before his large-scale operation, he had joined forces with the Sea Bird Bishop to launch a successful surprise attack and kill the gold level giant slave.

When Bing Xiao saw this, he felt as if he had fallen into an ice cave. He immediately realized that the Bayonet Gang was doomed and ordered a breakout and escape on the spot.

However, since the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had already led his troops over, he was naturally fully prepared.

The magic teams had already formed their formation, waiting for the members of the Bayonet Gang to break through.

The city guards and the army outside the city acted as auxiliaries, forming a tight encirclement, like a giant net, trapping any small fry who wanted to escape.

Without the gold level at the helm, no matter how many silver level members the Bayonet Gang had, they were all small fry in the eyes of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord!

After a period of chaos in the Snow Bird Dueling Arena, the scene became clear.

Inside the arena was the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and outside the arena was the Sea Bird Bishop.

All the ringleaders of the Bayonet Gang were caught, and Bing Xiao was no exception.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had succeeded in his surprise attack just one day later, wiping out the entire Bayonet Gang!

The people heard this bombshell news amid the chaos and were stunned for a long time.

For a moment, they found it difficult to accept that the Bayonet Gang, once such a huge organization, had been destroyed in a single day!

After this battle, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's reputation suddenly rose to its peak in a single day, and he became the object of praise and admiration from the people.

His popularity was unmatched, comparable to that of the dragonman youngster.

Under the protection of Men Shi, Tripleblade, and Lan Zao, the dragonman youngster successfully left the dueling arena and returned to the flagship at the dock to rest.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps did not participate in the encirclement and suppression of the Bayonet Gang.

This was an agreement made when the two sides formed a contract earlier.

Late at night, one day later.

The city government palace held a large banquet, inviting almost all the celebrities and powerful figures in Snow Bird Harbor.

In the study on the second floor, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was alone with the dragonman youngster.

The city Lord took the initiative to pour wine for the dragonman youngster and introduced, “This is Cold Emotion Wine.”

“Even in the capital of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, this is a very rare wine.”

“I only have one bottle of it in my wine cellar!”

“I'll offer a toast to you, Commander Long Fu. You are the biggest contributor to our defeat of the Bayonet Gang today.”

The wine was poured into a glass, and immediately a layer of frost formed on the surface of the glass.

The dragonman youngster took the wine glass handed to him by the city Lord. He knew nothing about wine, but he had no doubt about the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's praise for this wine.

This was because the bright red wine exuded a gold level aura.

This was a gold level potion!

The dragonman youngster was curious about the effects of the cold wine, but he also knew what to say at this moment.

“City Lord, your praise makes me feel uneasy!”

“Everyone knows that you made the greatest contribution to the destruction of the Bayonet Gang, and no one can compare to you.”

“Just one day has passed, and the people of Snow Bird Harbor all believe that you are the most accomplished city Lord in the history of Snow Bird Harbor!”

Hearing these words, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord laughed loudly, his eyes narrowing with laughter, making no attempt to hide his pride.

He handed over his wine cup.

The dragonman youngster also handed over his wine cup at the right moment.

Clink.

The two wine cups gently collided in the middle.

Immediately, the fine wine flowed into the mouths of the two.

Although the dragonman youngster's ability to taste wine was almost zero, good things were good things. The moment the Cold Emotion Wine entered his mouth, his eyes changed.

When the Cold Emotion Wine first entered his mouth, it seemed bland and cold, but then it exploded on his taste buds and in his throat, becoming warm and fragrant.

In an instant, the strong aroma of the wine spread throughout the dragonman youngster's entire body and mind, affecting his mood as if he were ascending to heaven.

What wonderful enjoyment!

For a moment, both of them in the room forgot to speak.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was the first to open his eyes and break the silence: "Did you know? Every year, the entire Drunkard Harbor holds a Wine God Festival, and Cold Emotion Wine is one of the offerings. In other words, this is the enjoyment of the gods."

The dragonman youngster knew that Drunkard Harbor was one of the eight major harbors of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, with a trade status equivalent to that of Snow Bird Harbor.

However, he was unaware that the Wine God cult was so prevalent in Drunkard Harbor.

He couldn't help but feel curious: "As far as I know, there is no Wine God among your country's deities. I wonder which deity is being worshipped at the Wine God Festival in Drunkard Harbor?"

There were many Wine Gods in this world.

As far as the dragonman youngster knew, there were three among the dwarves, one among the humans, two among the elves, and one among the demons.

From this, it could be seen that competition for the identity of the Wine God was fierce.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not hesitate and replied immediately, “Of course, it is the Wine God of the Sheng Ming Empire.”

The dragonman youngster's expression became even more strange, "This is a human deity. Has your country's openness reached such a level?"

Faith was a resource of the deities.

Every parish was a contested area among the churches.

The faith of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom was a resource of the snow elf's deities. Yet one of the eight great harbors, Drunkard Harbor, was actually holding a large-scale festival for the human Wine God.

A large-scale festival was one that affected the entire city.

To give an example, it would be like the scale of the heroic duel between the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang.

If there weren't many believers, it would be impossible to hold a festival of this scale.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head and said helplessly, "The Sheng Ming Empire is the world's number one power, with unparalleled strength. A few years ago, the human race used an astonishing trade shock to transform Drunkard Harbor. The original church had been completely defeated, and the faith in that place had been completely lost. Of course, the royal family would not admit this publicly. But you can completely regard Drunkard Harbor as the parish of the human race's Wine God!"

“I see.” The dragonman youngster fell silent.

The Empire's influence was everywhere and was constantly growing.

Even the independent overseas kingdom of Ice Sculpture Kingdom was not spared.

The dragonman youngster couldn't help but worry secretly: if they failed to atone for their sins, would the Ice Sculpture Kingdom become their place of refuge?

The two chatted for a while, then turned to the main topic.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took out an activity map and spread it out on the desk between them.

After a few changes, the map showed several streets.

Most of the shops on these streets were marked with different colors.

“Choose one of these,” said the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

This was one of the terms of the deal they had agreed upon earlier—the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would provide the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with a shop.

Now was the time to fulfill that agreement.

The dragonman youngster looked closely and quickly discovered that these shops were not originally the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's assets, but all came from the Bayonet Gang.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had destroyed the entire Bayonet Gang this time, and he had made a fortune!

Not only did he gain explosive growth in terms of reputation and prestige, but the actual profits were also unimaginably rich. After all, almost all of the Bayonet Gang's assets had fallen into his hands.

Now, the dragonman youngster recalled the agreement he had made with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord on the ship.

The dragonman youngster only intended to fulfill his heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang and did not want the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to participate in the upcoming battle.

At that time, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not hesitate and agreed to this request.

"I was worried that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would become his henchmen and sacrifice too much. In fact, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was also unwilling to have our corps participate."

“Because this was a battle that was bound to be won.”

“The more people participated, the more the profits would be divided.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord planned carefully before acting, and the entire operation went according to his plan from start to finish, without any unexpected incidents.

He reaped all the rewards.

Of course, he would definitely distribute part of the profits to the Sea Bird Church.

Before this operation, he must have reached an agreement with the bishop of the Sea Bird Church. The two sides must have discussed the distribution of profits in advance.

Whether it was the operation to surround and kill the mysterious assassin or the operation to destroy the Bayonet Gang, the Sea Bird Church was the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's most steadfast ally.

With the destruction of the Bayonet Gang, the political landscape of Snow Bird Harbor underwent a huge change.

Previously, there were four powerful factions, but now there were two strong ones and one weak one.

The weakest faction was, of course, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance.

The dragonman youngster and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord reached an agreement, and on the day they returned to the harbor, the branch leader gave the order: gather all the men, retreat into a defensive position, and protect important places such as warehouses and shops.

Chapter 557: Harmonious Discussion

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Sea Bird Church Bishop clearly had no intention of bringing the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance Branch Leader along, throwing all their plans behind him.

The former two acted together, moving too quickly and with too much effect. By the time the Clam Cloth Branch Leader wanted to intervene, he found that the situation had already been completely decided.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps did not participate in the battle to destroy the Bayonet Gang. Although they protected themselves, they also lost the opportunity to obtain more spoils.

But the dragonman youngster did not regret this.

“In the current situation, there is no need to act as an eyesore.”

“If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were to participate in the battle, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would see it as us coming to take advantage.”

“He is too well prepared and does not need the help of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to easily swallow up the Bayonet Gang.”

“Even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps participates in the battle and obtains some resources, it will provoke the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's aversion, which is completely not worth it.”

"The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will establish a stronghold here. As the local Lord, it would be too easy for him to deal with the fixed assets left behind by outsiders. There are too many ways to do so."

“For now, maintaining a good cooperative relationship with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is the top priority.”

The dragonman youngster would not have had this realization before, but after this experience in Snow Bird Harbor, he learned a lot from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The young leader became more mature.

He had become more thoughtful in his considerations, no longer limited to using force to deal with problems, and his political sensitivity had also improved somewhat.

Seeing that the dragonman youngster was looking at the activity map but was slow to make a choice, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord couldn't help but point out three places in succession.

He said, "I suggest you choose from these three places, as they are all in very good locations. Of course, there are differences between them, so you need to compare them carefully."

The dragonman youngster shook his head slightly and smiled, “I need to think about this matter.”

He had not thought about making his own decision on this matter because he trusted Zi Di's judgment more.

There was no need to ignore a talented businessperson and make a rash decision himself. That was not something a wise leader should do.

“Of course,” said the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord as he put away the activity map.

He said in an expectant tone, "I believe that if your group's crossbows are put on sale, they will definitely sell very well."

“I have no problem with granting the qualifications for selling weapons.”

"As you know, our country only has a ban on armor, not a ban on crossbows. As long as you don't sell gold level armor, there will be no problem."

The dragonman youngster nodded and said emotionally, “So, the gold level defensive items that can be used are not full body armor but rather shields and accessories.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed and said, “That's right.”
