Whether it was the Diamond Protection Set, the Flowing Wave Armor, or the Thin Ice Wall Shield, it was all the same.

At most, there was Teng Dong Lang's Abandoned Person's Shirt, but this garment did not actually have orthodox defensive properties but rather took an unconventional approach.

The dragonman youngster pondered, “In the short term, we only plan to sell one type of potion.”

This potion was naturally the dragon rider potion that he had discussed with Zi Di before.

“As for those alchemy crossbows.” At this point, the dragonman youngster paused and showed a hint of helplessness, “That's not something I can decide.”

The implication was that he still had to listen to the specific orders of the arms dealer behind him.

The implication was that he still had to listen to the specific orders of the arms dealer behind him.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was fooled, and although his expression did not change, his eyes clearly flashed.

It had to be said that the excuse and identity of an arms dealer and smuggler were really useful.

Such bluffing made the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps more respected and feared.

Unknowingly, it had already gained considerable benefits for the dragonman youngster.

The two continued to talk.

For the dragonman youngster, good news came one after another.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had collected more dragon beast corpses, which would be transferred to the dragonman youngster during the next handover.

He also found two pieces of gold level equipment, both of which would be sold to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Speaking of gold equipment, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had lent out some, which had already been returned by the dragonman youngster yesterday.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was not as generous as Bu Quan, who would give things away for free.

Of course, what was borrowed had to be returned.

The news that surprised the dragonman youngster the most was about the alchemists.

Currently, a silver level alchemist was on his way to Snow Bird Harbor.

According to his itinerary, he would arrive at the dock in a day. At that time, once both sides signed the contract, he would immediately join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

With the destruction of the Bayonet Gang, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' biggest obstacle suddenly disappeared, and their future suddenly became bright.

The same was true for the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

In fact, he gained far more benefits than the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was just an outsider.

During his conversation with the dragonman youngster, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord also admitted that after this battle, his influence within his faction had skyrocketed several times over. When he was raising those resources through his connections before, it was nowhere near as easy as it was now.

Many people saw his achievements and were more willing to actively cooperate with him.

That was why this silver level alchemist was able to arrive at Snow Bird Harbor so quickly.

The other two alchemists were also handing over their original work and tasks.

“So, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will welcome three silver level alchemists?” This good news made it difficult for the dragonman youngster to hide the joy on his face.

But he did not get carried away.

Throughout the entire process, he remained quite cautious.

When talking to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, every word he said was carefully thought out and weighed before he spoke.

To be honest, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's decisive destruction of the entire Bayonet Gang really surprised the dragonman youngster.

Such tactics were truly formidable.

The most outstanding aspect of this operation was that the destruction of the Bayonet Gang did not have a significant negative impact on Snow Bird Harbor or the general public.

Normally, the destruction of such a large organization would inevitably result in a considerable number of innocent people being affected.

But not this time!

This showed that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was in complete control of the situation.

He had once told the dragonman youngster that he had been preparing for many years and was quite well prepared.

When the dragonman youngster heard this, he thought that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was trying to boost his confidence. He was encouraging the dragonman youngster to cooperate with him.

However, it turned out that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's words were not exaggerated in the slightest.

He was indeed very well prepared.

Throughout the entire process, the Bayonet Gang did not put up any resistance.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had clearly guaranteed the dragonman youngster that he would definitely win this time.

Now, looking back, the dragonman youngster realized that with such thorough preparation, it was only natural to have confidence in victory.

Similarly, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not dare to underestimate the dragonman youngster in the slightest.

He had almost suffered a loss before.

That night, on the Love Triangle, when he heard the dragonman youngster say that he could accurately locate the mysterious assassin, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord subconsciously stood up from his seat, his heart filled with surprise and doubt.

That left a deep impression on him.

On the other hand, the outcome of the duel between the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang clearly told everyone that the dragonman youngster could not simply be regarded as a silver level battle qi cultivator.

The dragonman youngster was extraordinary.

Thirdly, he also had interests to pursue.

The alchemy crossbow that the dragonman youngster used in his heroic duel was indeed very sophisticated.

From this, it could be inferred that his other weapons would also be of good quality.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hoped to have such weapons supplied to himself.

Even though he was about to step down from his position as city Lord, it did not prevent him from maintaining contact with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In many cases, the exchange of resources could generate invisible benefits beyond the transaction itself. For example, political influence.

For all of the above reasons, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord took a more equal attitude toward the dragonman youngster.

The two talked very smoothly.

The atmosphere in the study was harmonious.

Neither of them felt the passage of time as they talked. Both sides were very sincere and respectful of each other, willing to make concessions, and quickly reached a lot of consensuses.

But just then, the sound of fighting suddenly came from downstairs.

Chapter 558: Bu Quan Shares a Dance

The dragonman youngster was slightly taken aback.

With the Bayonet Gang destroyed, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's faction was at its peak. Who would dare to attack the city government palace?

Could it be the remnants of the Bayonet Gang?

But immediately afterwards, the dragonman youngster heard his own voice.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord paused for a moment, then realized what was happening and explained with a smile, "Time flies."

“The dance is about to begin.”

He reached out and gently stroked the air above the desk, and a miniature magic image immediately appeared.

The magic image showed the scene in the first-floor hall below them.

"Before the dance begins, we have carefully prepared a magic image that vividly depicts your heroic battle stance, Commander Long Fu. I believe this video will definitely have an excellent warm-up effect."

“Of course, please forgive us if we have offended you.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke very politely, but upon closer inspection, his strong personality was still evident.

The dragonman youngster immediately said that it was his honor.

At this moment, in the first-floor hall of the city government palace.

The lights were bright.

Long tables were arranged on both sides of the hall.

The tables, covered with white cloth, were adorned with exquisite candles, emitting a fragrant aroma. Exquisite silver platters held a variety of delicious dishes.

Gentlemen were dressed in formal attire, and noblewomen and ladies competed in elegance.

Some were tasting fine wine, stopping here and there to chat in small groups. Others gathered together, discussing the same topic, laughing in unison from time to time.

Some were tasting fine wine, stopping here and there to chat in small groups. Others gathered together, discussing the same topic, laughing in unison from time to time.

Everyone's eyes were immediately drawn to the magic images.

Soon, people were praising the dragonman youngster in the images.

The dragonman youngster in the study on the second floor saw his previous battle through the images.

He soon discovered that this time, the magic images showed much more than just the battle between him and Teng Dong Lang.

From the very beginning, his duel with Ban Lan Gen had been recorded.

Ban Lan Gen was careless and underestimated his opponent, and the dragonman youngster knocked him unconscious with one punch.

Next was the battle between the dragonman youngster and Spring Boxer.

He completely suppressed his opponent, standing still in place, becoming a classic image. Spring Boxer gave it his all, but still couldn't shake him, allowing the dragonman youngster to show his strength.

Next was the battle between the dragonman youngster and Xiong Ju.

It was a tough battle.

Finally, there was a heroic duel between him and Teng Dong Lang.

The dragonman youngster was suppressed for most of the fight, but he held on to the hope of turning the tide and ultimately defeated the stronger opponent!

It was a classic battle.

Of course, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would not combine all the battle scenes together, as that would be too long.

Therefore, this magic image was carefully edited.

The focus was on the dragonman youngster's brave and heroic appearance in battle.

Many of the scenes were deliberately slowed down or even paused, either to show the dragonman youngster's brilliant performance or the miserable state of his opponents, highlighting the dragonman youngster's strength and intelligence in battle, as well as his courage, persistence, and other rare qualities.

In particular, the image of him covered in blood and wounds during his battle with Teng Dong Lang was repeated many times.

Finally, when Teng Dong Lang fell in a pool of blood, the dragonman youngster's previous desperate struggle was rewarded.

Seeing the final scene, everyone in the hall applauded spontaneously.

However, the magic image did not stop after being played once.

Instead, it began to repeat.

Even so, almost no one around him looked away, and they continued to watch with great interest.

This time, the band in the hall played a rousing battle song at just the right moment, allowing the dragonman youngster's battle to resonate with everyone and stir their blood.

This was a competitive world.

This was the world of the strong.

This was especially true in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, where heroic duels were prevalent.

The image presented by the dragonman youngster undoubtedly deeply resonated with the culture and values of the kingdom.

In addition, he had defeated the notorious leader of the Bayonet Gang, so many people considered him an ally of justice and a hero.

Not only did the famous ladies in the audience stare intently, but even the men were excited and subconsciously put themselves in his shoes. In the study on the second floor, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord focused on the expressions of these guests.

After finding that it was as he expected, he smiled at the dragonman youngster and said, "Do you know? You are already famous."

“The name of Long Fu and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are spreading rapidly throughout the country because of this battle!”

“You are completely famous!”

After a pause, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord also told the dragonman youngster some good news.

"More than 30,000 copies of the magic images of your duel with Teng Dong Lang have been sold. This is a terrifying number, considering that it has only been one day since the duel ended."

“And I believe that the video of this duel will definitely be widely sold across the country, and many people will buy it for their collections.”

“The owner of the Snow Bird Dueling Arena has already contacted you, right?”

The dragonman youngster nodded: “Yes, he took the initiative to talk to me and even voluntarily reduced his own share of the profits to give me more.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's smile grew even wider as he said bluntly, “I told him to do that.”

“You are a hero, and you must not be treated unfairly!”

"In the process of destroying the Bayonet Gang, you personally killed the leader of this criminal organization. You are the one who deserves the most credit! You are a fighter who practices the way of justice."

“The previously agreed-upon profit share was too unfair!”

“How can we treat a fighter and hero unfairly?”

“If we did that, how would the outside world view me and Snow Bird Harbor?”

The dragonman youngster immediately understood that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was actively expressing his goodwill and trying to get closer to him.

He was selling himself very well.

He had nothing to lose, so he was generous and said very nice things.

The dragonman youngster understood this and immediately thanked the city Lord.

When the Snow Bird Dueling Arena owner came to him to revise the contract, he was a little surprised, but he didn't expect that the biggest driving force behind it was the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

According to this profit-sharing ratio and previous sales cases, if his magic images of this standard were widely sold nationwide, they would bring the dragonman youngster at least 500,000 gold coins in revenue!

Of course, the revenue would not be obtained in a lump sum but would continue to be increased with continued sales.

The dragonman youngster sighed from the bottom of his heart, “After this battle, I feel like the world has changed.”

“On the night I returned to the dock after the battle, I received a large number of invitation letters, so many that they were like snowflakes falling from the sky.”

“The president of the Mercenary Guild Branch also came to my door in person to tell me that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been promoted to a higher level.”

“Although this did not comply with the procedures of the Mercenary Guild, special treatment for special cases is also one of the guild's rules.”

“He also told me that many merchant alliances, families, and so on had specifically requested for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to carry out some tasks.”

“These tasks all had the same characteristics: they were very simple, and the rewards were generous.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nodded and smiled, explaining, “This is their way of showing goodwill.”

"For a mercenary group, this kind of goodwill is even more beneficial. Not only can you receive many gifts, but you can also improve your resume and level by completing these tasks."

“However, this situation is not something that a silver level mercenary group can enjoy.”

"Your battle with Teng Dong Lang was a real victory. Countless people witnessed this duel with their own eyes."

"Being able to kill a gold level person, you are already considered half a gold level. That's why you are being treated so specially."

The dragonman youngster then changed the subject: “But now, there is still a hidden danger.”

"We previously accused the Bayonet Gang of desecrating the sacred heroic duel. Until now, we haven't produced any evidence. What should we do?"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord laughed loudly, “Commander Long Fu, you don't need to worry.”

“There will always be evidence of guilt.”

“Now, the upper echelons of the Bayonet Gang are either dead or have been arrested.”

"I have already given orders to severely torture these ringleaders. No matter how tough they are, there will always be a day when they can't hold out any longer."

“We can wait slowly, there's no rush.”

That's right.

Now, the Bayonet Gang has been destroyed.

Unless someone deliberately targeted the dragonman youngster or the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, they would not deliberately dig up this mistake and attack them, accusing them of false accusation.

Seeing that there was still worry on the dragonman youngster's face, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said again, “Don't worry, I guarantee that we will definitely be able to collect evidence of this matter.”

With the assurance of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the dragonman youngster finally relaxed his brow and said, “I believe you, City Lord!”

“Only in this way can the Bayonet Gang's foundation be completely eradicated.”

Just then, the magic image showed Teng Dong Lang lying in a pool of blood again.

The dragonman youngster's brow furrowed slightly again, and he said deliberately, “When I think back on this battle, I actually feel a little ashamed.”

“If Teng Dong Lang hadn't made a fatal mistake at the last moment, I wouldn't have been able to defeat him.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord looked deeply at the dragonman youngster, took out the wine bottle again, and poured wine for the two of them.

Taking advantage of this action, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said reassuringly, “Commander Long Fu, you don't have to think that way.”

“Teng Dong Lang's mistake shows that he lacks training and combat experience.”

“He was too obsessed with power and spent most of his time dealing with the affairs of the Bayonet Gang.”

“Therefore, he paid the price for his choices.”

"As for you, you fought against a stronger opponent and never gave up. Your fighting spirit never wavered. You endured countless blows and persevered."

“Finally, the miracle has arrived.”

“There was indeed an element of luck involved, but what mattered most was your dedication and effort.”

“The people will not see you as someone who got lucky; they will admire and respect you, even worship you.”

“Enough of these trivial thoughts.”

“Come with me downstairs. Our great hero, today you are the star of the show.”

The dragonman youngster had no choice but to follow.

He walked down the stairs with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and appeared in the main hall on the first floor.

The crowd immediately cheered.

The dragonman youngster suppressed his doubts and put on a smile.

After a series of events in Snow Bird Harbor, he had a better understanding of human relationships.

He knew that in many cases, he could rely on others to deal with his enemies.

The cooperation with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a complete win-win situation. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' situation had improved greatly as a result, and they had reaped huge rewards.

Everyone present was a high-ranking figure in Snow Bird Harbor, and getting to know them, even if only by face, was more beneficial than harmful.

The dragonman youngster was now a popular figure.

Such fame and influence were definitely advantageous when it came to socializing.

People were happy to interact with him and were even flattered by the dragonman youngster's enthusiasm and kindness.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord motioned for the dragonman youngster to stand beside him.

He stood on the platform in the middle of the stairs, slightly higher than everyone else in the room, and said a few words focusing on congratulating the dragonman youngster on his victory in the duel. Then, he announced the official start of the ball.

The magic images occupying the dance floor disappeared, and the bands began to play melodious dance music.

Originally, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was supposed to lead the first dance, but just now, he had announced that this time, the dragonman youngster would lead the dance.

The dragonman youngster walked down the stairs and entered the hall.

Almost all the famous ladies and noblewomen cast expectant and seductive glances at him.

But the dragonman youngster had already found his target.

He walked straight to a relatively secluded dining table where five or six people were gathered.

At the center of the crowd was Bu Quan.

This silver level Alchemy Great Master was the focus of attention wherever she went.

However, people were not interested in her appearance, but rather her ability to make alchemy tools and her foundation of owning many alchemy workshops.

At this moment, they were discussing business or future cooperation projects with Bu Quan.

The dragonman youngster's every move attracted everyone's attention.

When they saw him walking towards them step by step, Bu Quan and the others looked surprised.

Among them, there were mostly men, and Bu Quan was the only woman.

In fact, Bu Quan, who always wore black-rimmed glasses, was far from being the most beautiful one there.

When Bu Quan saw the dragonman youngster walking towards her, her heart began to beat faster.

Thump, thump, thump!

Her heartbeat quickened, and at the same time, a blush of panic appeared on her face.

The dragonman youngster walked up to Bu Quan, bowed slightly, and extended his dragon claw as elegantly as possible: “Miss Bu Quan, would you do me the honor of being my first dance partner tonight?”

Bu Quan's mouth opened slightly, and her head was buzzing with confusion.

She couldn't speak, but she subconsciously reached out her hand.

So, the dragonman youngster took her hand and led her onto the dance floor.

The dragonman youngster was not good at dancing, but as a battle qi cultivator, he had excellent control over his body.

In contrast, Bu Quan was obviously unfamiliar with the dance steps and looked nervous.

Bu Quan came from a lower-middle-class family and suffered a tragedy when her nanny, who was like a mother to her, was killed by the Bayonet Gang. She fled to the Alchemy Guild and was given asylum.

In the days that followed, she studied diligently, determined to seek revenge.

After achieving success, she gave up the opportunity for further education, pretended to compromise with the Bayonet Gang, returned to Snow Bird Harbor, built an alchemy workshop to increase her influence, and secretly gathered evidence to prepare for bringing down the Bayonet Gang.

Dancing?

Such activities had long since faded from her life!

She had no interest in dancing whatsoever.

So, when the dragonman youngster invited her to dance, she inevitably felt nervous.

She couldn't dance, but she didn't refuse.

Because it was the dragonman youngster who invited her.

He was the benefactor who helped her take revenge, the great hero in her heart!!

Chapter 559: Agreeing to a Marriage Proposal

Bu Quan seemed to have fallen into a state of dizziness.

Everything before her eyes had become distorted. The crystal chandelier had turned into a hazy white starlight, and the gilded wall decorations had become blurry columns of light.

The stage had turned pink, and the bubbles that rose were shaped like hearts.

Her heart was filled with immense surprise and happiness.

“Is this all a dream?” She stared blankly, incredulously, as if she were in a dream.

The dragonman youngster also sensed Bu Quan's unusual state.

Not only had her eyes lost their focus, but her limbs were limp and weak, and she was practically lying on top of him.

A magic image of her nanny appeared before her.

In the past, whenever she felt that she couldn't go on, she would activate this alchemy device to draw hope for survival and the motivation for revenge from maternal love.

“Nanny, please rest in peace.”

“I have avenged you with the alchemy device I gave out.”

How many times had she awakened from similar nightmares like this one?

But this time, recalling the scene that had unfolded in the dueling arena, the sorrow in Bu Quan's heart was no longer as intense, and the pain had diminished significantly.

She closed the alchemy device and put it back in the drawer.

There was another alchemy device in the drawer, which also stored a magic image. This magic image recorded the duel between the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang.

Bu Quan placed the two alchemy devices side by side.

In another corner of the drawer was a graduation certificate.

It had been signed by her mentor.

During her days studying at the Alchemy Guild, her female mentor had provided her with immense support.

The scene of their parting remained deeply etched in her memory.

“Teacher, I'm leaving,” Bu Quan said reluctantly at the entrance of the magician tower.

The middle-aged female elf sighed, “Bu Quan, don't waste your talent. Don't disappoint my expectations of you.”

The female mentor knew what Bu Quan intended to do upon returning to Snow Bird Harbor.

She wanted to say something comforting but knew it was best not to reveal this secret.

She paused for a moment before finally saying, “I won't say anything else.”

“Here is your graduation certificate. Use it to report to the local Alchemy Guild, and you will receive the necessary support.”

“Return as soon as possible.”

“No matter when you return, the doors here will always be open for you.”

The other apprentices behind the female magician heard these words and were immediately stunned, then began to show envy and jealousy toward Bu Quan.

“I'll remember it!” Bu Quan choked up, her eyes reddening, and finally left.

As the memory faded, Bu Quan's gaze once again focused on the graduation certificate.

She gently traced her fingertips over the certificate's cover, feeling the texture of the matte surface.

The nanny taught Bu Quan to doubt.

Her mentor, however, taught her to always maintain curiosity and a thirst for knowledge.

These two principles combined forged Bu Quan’s heart as a magician.

Her original plan was after successfully avenging herself against the Bayonet Gang, she would return to the magician tower, back to her mentor’s side, and continue exploring the vast, mysterious realm of alchemy.

Bu Quan never imagined that the Bayonet Gang’s downfall would come so suddenly, so swiftly.

This surprised and delighted her, but it also caught her off guard.

At the ball, the dragonman youngster's invitation made her heart flutter.

At this point in her life, she had reached a crossroad.

One path led to the magician tower and the world of alchemy that she was curious about and longed for.

The other path led to a dragonman youngster who was smiling and inviting her with outstretched arms.

However, behind the dragonman youngster was a layer of fog.

After thinking about it carefully, Bu Quan realized that she actually knew very little about the dragonman youngster.

"What kind of person is he really? All I've seen is his performance in battle, but what is his true personality? What is his past experience?"

"Could he be just like the Bayonet Gang in reality? He invited me so warmly, so my alchemy talent must be the most important thing."

“After all, my perception of him and his perception of me are completely different.”

“Behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is a mysterious arms dealer. I am an Alchemy Great Master with several alchemy workshops under my command. Isn't that exactly what an arms dealer needs?"

The more Bu Quan thought about it, the more her brow furrowed.

In her heart, she felt curiosity, confusion, and growing suspicion towards the dragonman youngster.

Just as Zi Di had analyzed, when Bu Quan really thought about it, she would drift further and further away from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After all, the connection between the two was weak, and they had only met a handful of times.

The wheels of the carriage rolled swiftly over the cobblestone street, emitting a series of soft clinks.

Inside the carriage sat an alchemist, sharing Bu Quan's curiosity and concerns.

"Commander Long Fu, what kind of person is he? How could he defeat and kill a gold level person with only a silver level power?

"What kind of organization is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps? Is the mercenary corps just their cover identity?"

This alchemist was named Zhong Yong. He was a silver level magician and had reached the level of Alchemy Master.

His body was covered in blue fish scales, his head was that of a fish, and his fish eyes were yellow with a hint of green.

He was not tall but there were fish fins growing on his back that shimmered with a golden glow.

These were the distinctive traits of the fishman race—the Bluefin bloodline.

The magic aptitude of a fishman was quite poor, but the Bluefin bloodline was a relatively rare magic bloodline among the fishman race.

Zhong Yong was also quite fortunate.

The concentration of the Bluefin bloodline within his body was not low, sustaining him through his cultivation journey all the way to the silver level, where he finally reached his limit.

The dragonman youngster's battle record astonished Zhong Yong.

Although he was a magician, as another transcendent, Zhong Yong understood how rare such a record was.

Even before meeting Commander Long Fu, Zhong Yong already felt a sense of pressure.

“Anyone capable of achieving such feats must be extraordinary. This explains why the family transferred me to him.”

Zhong Yong was willing to submit to such a powerful figure.

What he truly worried about was whether the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could provide him with a good working environment. Could they provide him with sufficient alchemy materials?

One had to know that mercenary groups and families were different.

Mercenary groups are often on the move, but Zhong Yong had already grown accustomed to—and enjoyed—the peaceful life of preparing potions at the family base.

Zhong Yong had never imagined that his once unchanging life would undergo such a drastic change just two days ago.

His personal will was easily overlooked in the face of the family's grand plans.

Zhong Yong, who had been raised by the family since childhood, though unwilling, did not even utter a word to advocate for himself. Because he always understood his place.

He was a fishman, an Alchemy Master cultivated by the family, and now a crucial bargaining chip for the family’s diplomatic relations.

“The most important thing is my salary.”

“My annual salary within the family is 260 gold.”

“The normal market rate is 300 gold per year.”

“When negotiating with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps later, I'll start by asking for 500 gold, leaving room for bargaining.”

“Both of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' magicians were assassinated. They are so desperate for an alchemist, and with a mysterious arms dealer backing them, they might agree to the 500 gold condition!”

The carriage arrived at the dock before dawn.

In the distance, where the sea met the sky, a faint white glow had already appeared.

It was dawn.

The fish market had already opened, and the sounds of buying and selling were growing louder.

Zhong Yong stepped down from the carriage, paused at the dock, and spotted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ships.

“Why are there only three ships?” The fishman magician was disappointed, "Even the flagship is only iron level?”

Such assets were quite pitiful.

What Zhong Yong didn't know was that before he arrived at the dock, Zong Ge had already led a group of people out to sea for military training.

During this period, the vast majority of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lived on the ships and hadn't even set foot on the dock.

After the Bayonet Gang was wiped out, Zong Ge finally got his chance.

He waited patiently for another day until the dragonman youngster returned from the banquet and he was finally sure it was safe.

The next morning, he couldn't wait to lead his men out to sea.

As he put it, these men needed training—a lot of training—to make up for the time wasted earlier!

The fishman magician stated his purpose, and the guarding mercenaries immediately went to report.

Soon, the dragonman youngster personally came down to greet him: “Hello, Magician Zhong Yong, I am Long Fu. Welcome to my mercenary group.”

Zhong Yong hurriedly bowed, showing gratitude.

The dragonman youngster stood alone before him, and his imposing stature and extraordinary demeanor made him feel the presence of a true powerhouse.

“He's so young.”

“The man before me is the one who defeated a gold level as a silver level!”

“Yet he has come personally to greet me.”

Zhong Yong felt a sense of being valued, and his resolve from the carriage only grew stronger.

“It seems the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps desperately needs an alchemist. A yearly salary of 500 gold is very likely. As long as I insist...”

Zhong Yong pondered, but the next moment, the dragonman youngster did not board the ship with him.

A human female magician approached Zhong Yong: "Greetings, Magician Zhong Yong. I am Yao Ma, a silver level magician. Please board the ship first.”

Yao Ma was Zi Di’s new identity.

Zhong Yong was puzzled: “Isn’t the commander coming with us?”

“No, he’s waiting for an important guest!” Zi Di explained with a smile, “We just received the news ourselves.”

At this moment, Zhong Yong finally understood that the dragonman youngster hadn’t come to greet him after all!

Chapter 563: Bu Quan Will Never Agree to Join

“An important guest?”

“What kind of person could be more important than me?”

The fishman magician Zhong Yong heard Zi Di’s explanation and couldn’t help but feel a bit suspicious.

He stared intently at Zi Di and muttered to himself, “Also, didn’t they say that the only two magicians in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had already been assassinated? How could there be another human female magician, and one of the silver level at that?”

“Since the commander is going to greet the distinguished guest, I’ll wait here too.” Zhong Yong didn’t rush to the captain’s cabin but lingered in place out of curiosity.

Soon, the distinguished guest he had been expecting arrived.

There was no fanfare, just an ordinary carriage.

Very ordinary.

“No, that’s not right!” After carefully examining it, Zhong Yong’s eyes widened in shock.

As an alchemist, he gradually realized that the carriage, though seemingly ordinary, contained a great deal of alchemy technology and was a high-spec alchemy creation.

Its ordinary appearance was deliberately crafted.

In reality, it had high defensive capabilities.

From the moment the door opened, Zhong Yong caught a glimpse of the interior of the carriage and spotted one of the alchemy components.

“A teleportation formation! The carriage is carrying a miniature teleportation formation!”

“This carriage must have cost at least 2,000 gold pieces!”

At that moment, Bu Quan stepped down from the carriage.

“A young snow elf girl?” Zhong Yong was puzzled, "She's too young, and she's only a silver level magician."

“Is she the only one in the carriage?”

"Is she the distinguished guest Commander Long Fu has been waiting for? Perhaps she's his lover, or maybe not..."

“Wait a moment.” Zhong Yong's expression changed, "She... she seems to be Bu Quan?”

Zhong Yong was deeply shaken.

He knew Bu Quan.

In fact, many alchemists knew Bu Quan, though Bu Quan did not know them.

Bu Quan was quite famous in the alchemy circles of the Ice Sculpture Capital.

She was a genius, one of the most brilliant new stars in alchemy in recent decades, and the winner of the Warm Snow Cup Alchemy Competition.

Her bloodline and talent were exceptional. The royal grand magician had once praised her, saying she possessed an extraordinary ability to make cognitive advances, and that if she continued to develop, she would achieve many innovations in alchemy and create results beyond the imagination of ordinary people.

Although the royal grand magician was not an alchemist, he was widely recognized as the strongest magician in the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom. His evaluation of Bu Quan was undoubtedly very high.

“Miss Bu Quan, welcome to your arrival.” The dragonman youngster smiled as he walked to the carriage door to greet her.

Bu Quan also smiled at the dragonman youngster: “Commander Long Fu, I apologize for visiting you so early.”

“Regarding last night's matter, I have already considered my answer.”

“May I board the ship to discuss this further?”

“Of course, please come this way.”

The dragonman youngster escorted Bu Quan and Zhong Yong into the captain's cabin.

Zi Di followed them inside.

After everyone had seated themselves, the dragonman youngster introduced Zi Di to Bu Quan and Zhong Yong first.

“This is the Magician Yao Ma who has recently joined our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“She is trustworthy.”

Bu Quan glanced at Zi Di, a thoughtful expression fleeting across her eyes behind her glasses.

Zi Di laughed and said, “Newly joining is just a cover story.”

“Actually, I was ordered by the head of my family to come and support the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“Thanks to the brave fighting of Commander Long Fu, we have broken through the deadlock at Snow Bird Harbor. Therefore, in the future, the head of the family will also increase investment in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

The words of the dragonman youngster were thought-provoking, and it was easy to connect them to the arms dealer background of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zi Di spoke the “truth” directly, immediately making Bu Quan and Zhong Yong feel her sincerity.

Furthermore, Zi Di also used the background of a powerful family.

Bu Quan thought to herself, “I knew it.”

Zhong Yong also thought to himself, “No wonder.”

The fishman magician adjusted his posture in his seat.

He silently warned himself: Be cautious when dealing with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. This is no ordinary force. Their backers could easily dispatch silver level reinforcements.

As for why they didn't dispatch gold level reinforcements?

The fishman magician already had the answer.

Because this was an unspoken agreement, or rather, an unwritten rule.

Silver level cultivators were considered low tier transcendents, suitable for reconnaissance.

Gold level was already mid-tier, with individual strength not to be underestimated; often, the ruler of a city was of this level.

When a gold level individual arrived, it always attracted attention and instilled fear.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps came to explore the market, not to invade.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord originally had four gold level individuals, occupying the highest echelons of power. Among them, only one was an outsider, serving as the Clam Cloth Branch Leader.

This was because the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance had extensive business operations within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, with a scale of operations that was extremely vast.

With royal permission, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance was permitted to station a gold level individual at the main transportation harbor.

Local factions rarely allowed outsider gold level individuals to reside permanently.

A single gold level individual could rival an entire army, easily undermining local rule.

“This fishman magician...” The dragonman youngster was about to introduce him.

Zhong Yong hurriedly stood up.

He dared not be presumptuous and introduced himself proactively: “My name is Zhong Yong. I possess the bloodline of the Bluefin fishman and specialize in alchemy. The most common potions I create are the teary water and anti-movement powder.”

Zhong Yong was savvy enough to adopt this attitude, which immediately earned him smiles from the dragonman youngster and Zi Di.

In fact, the survivors were all aware of Zhong Yong’s background.

They knew the details of the few alchemists who had come.

When Zhong Yong was sold into slavery, he was still in his mother’s womb.

His mother died shortly after he was born.

It was a stroke of luck for Zhong Yong.

The noble family that purchased him had a granddaughter who needed a toy.

Zhong Yong became a prop for her make-believe games, primarily playing the role of an infant.

As the granddaughter grew older, he became a pet akin to a cat or dog.

Thus, during his childhood, Zhong Yong received treatment that was unimaginable for most fishman slaves.

As he grew older, his bloodline traits became more pronounced, and he was even detected to have magician potential!

Magician bloodlines among fishmen were far rarer than among humans, and even more so compared to snow elves.

The eldest granddaughter also grew up gradually. Due to the affection cultivated since childhood and the promising future benefits, she bestowed the name Zhong Yong upon him and invested resources to nurture him.

Once he had completed his education, he worked in the noble alchemist's workshop, creating value.

Zhong Yong's personal background was straightforward.

What pleased the dragonman youngster and Zi Di was that Zhong Yong had been raised by snow elves from a young age, receiving a servant's education, and had remained consistent, showing remarkable maturity.

He was good at following orders and was a very competent subordinate.

This could be seen from his recent performance.

“Magician Zhong Yong, you have traveled a long way to get here. Please have a cup of tea first.” The dragonman youngster greeted Zhong Yong first, gesturing for him to sit down again.

Then, the dragonman youngster explained to him in a gentle tone: “Although you arrived at the dock first this morning, the matter involving me and Bu Quan Miss actually occurred earlier.”

“Please wait a moment, okay?”

“Of course, of course,” Zhong Yong hurriedly replied. Deep down, he was extremely curious about why Bu Quan, such a genius, was involved with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Could it be that they are discussing a major deal?”

“This is good, I can see some of the strength of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as an observer.”

Zhong Yong was filled with curiosity and looked forward to the negotiations between the dragonman youngster and Bu Quan.

But then, the dragonman youngster got straight to the point: "Last night's invitation to Miss Bu Quan made me reflect on myself. It was my uncontrollable emotions that caused such a sudden move. I've been eagerly awaiting your answer. Have you made up your mind? Will you join us?"

Bu Quan opened her mouth and was about to answer.

Plop.

Zhong Yong, who had just taken a sip of tea, spat it out immediately.

Fortunately, he lowered his head in time, spitting it onto the floor.

"Cough cough cough. I-I apologize for my rudeness." Zhong Yong coughed while apologizing, looking utterly embarrassed.

At the same time, he was filled with shock.

“Goodness gracious, what did I just hear?”

"Long Fu is actually recruiting Bu Quan? What gives him the right?"

Zhong Yong admitted that the dragonman youngster was indeed extraordinary, capable of slaying a gold level opponent as a silver level. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had powerful backers; anyone capable of being an arms dealer must have formidable connections.

But none of this gave grounds for recruiting Bu Quan.

An outsider might not be qualified, but Zhong Yong was an insider; he was well aware of Bu Quan's status.

She was a prodigy who had been the focus of attention since she first emerged. Her mentor adored her and was willing to invest heavily in her cultivation.

The investment was substantial, almost treating Bu Quan as the next heir to the magician tower.

The president of the Royal Alchemy Guild also held high hopes for Bu Quan. During a banquet, he publicly evaluated the alchemy prodigies that had emerged in the kingdom over the past few decades.

Bu Quan was ranked fifth.

This position meant that as long as Bu Quan's bloodline potential was high enough, she would grow up to become a pillar of the alchemy field in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

How could such a figure be recruited by a mercenary group?

Even if the arms dealer behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were to intervene, it still wouldn't feel right.

In Zhong Yong's heart, Bu Quan's bright future should be like this.

She would be nurtured and continue to grow.

She would achieve success step by step, and her title would rise at a speed that ordinary people could not match.

When she became a gold level alchemist, she would be entrusted with important responsibilities.

She would be cherished and protected by the kingdom, free from any mundane concerns, allowing her to devote all her energy to the study of alchemy.

Perhaps, in the distant future, one of the alchemy items she created would greatly enhance the military strength of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Or perhaps an alchemy technique she improved would significantly improve the quality of life for the people. Or perhaps an alchemy principle she discovered and verified would become a tool for alchemists worldwide to explore, and her name would be remembered by all, immortalized for generations to come.

“This is the destiny a genius like Bu Quan should have.”

“This is the path a person like Bu Quan should take, the life that best showcases her value.”

“But now, she's been recruited by an arms dealer?”

"What's so hard to guess about an arms dealer's motives? He just wants to use Bu Quan's alchemy skills to manufacture weapons!”

“But is that something Bu Quan should be doing?”

“Of course not.”

“That’s something alchemists like me should be doing. A genius like Bu Quan should be allowed to create and explore the frontiers of alchemy for the kingdom!”

Zhong Yong harbored the pride of an alchemist.

He had no choice but to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and he valued the background of the dragonman youngster and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But compared to Bu Quan, Zhong Yong looked down on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps from the bottom of his heart! He deeply felt that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and the arms dealers behind it, were unworthy of a genius like Bu Quan.

“Bu Quan will never agree to this!”

Yes, magicians were short on funds and pursued various resources. But it was only to facilitate their pursuit of knowledge and exploration of mysteries.

The stench of money could not buy a magician's dignity!

As a magician, Zhong Yong naturally had his own dignity.

Especially since he was an alchemist and resided in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, where alchemists were treated with such high regard.

He believed he was merely forced into this situation, a fate he could not resist.

But this was not what he desired.

The life he sought was to own his own alchemy workshop, craft alchemy items with his own skills, sell them to sustain himself, and simultaneously fund his own alchemy experiments, dedicating his entire life to exploring the mysteries of alchemy.

This was the heart of a magician.

Rising above mortals, pursuing knowledge and truth!

Zhong Yong believed himself to possess the heart of a magician, but such was simply his fate.

He was even more convinced that Bu Quan also possessed the heart of a magician!

How could someone without the heart of a magician exhibit such genius and receive such high praise from so many people?

“Bu Quan would never agree to such a recruitment!” Zhong Yong growled inwardly.

He was incomparably certain.

Chapter 564: Goddess, You Cannot Be Simping!

Love Triangle, captain's cabin.

Zi Di gave the dragonman youngster a subtle glare.

How could they negotiate like this?

Who started off by demanding answers?

According to Zi Di's understanding, negotiation could be likened to a war. Verbal language and body language were the main weapons in this war, ultimately achieving the goal of influencing others and obtaining personal gain.

The most important part of the early stages of war was preparation and intelligence gathering.

Therefore, before negotiations, both parties usually started with lighter topics. Like the weather, for example.

During this process, Zi Di would probe and confirm the other party's physical condition, emotions, interests, and even personal values.

These things could all become tools Zi Di could use, or even fatal weaknesses in her opponent.

However, after the dragonman youngster invited Bu Quan to the captain's cabin, he immediately asked for the answer.

That was too reckless!

It was like two armies clashing, with one side launching a full-scale charge from the start, completely bypassing the preliminary intelligence gathering, mid-game tactical shifts, and the back-and-forth exchanges of moves and countermeasures.

"Moreover, directly demanding answers reveals a sense of superiority that can be off-putting. Additionally, the urgency you display will only become your own weakness." Zi Di looked at the dragonman youngster and sighed helplessly.

The dragonman youngster sensed Zi Di's gaze, blinked, and realized he was holding her back.

Zi Di didn't bother with the dragonman youngster and immediately turned to Bu Quan, trying to salvage the situation: "Miss Bu Quan, there's no need to answer immediately. Regarding the invitation to join the mercenary group, it's not just us—even the clan leader is very sincere. Any conditions can be discussed!"

Zhong Yong, standing nearby, heard this and immediately felt envious.

He also wanted such treatment—who wouldn't?

“Unfortunately, I'm not a genius like Miss Bu Quan.”

“And your Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps isn’t qualified to recruit her either.”

“Even if the conditions are good, what use are they?”

“Can you give her endless alchemy knowledge? Can you nurture her so that her talent and abilities can be fully realized?”

“No. Only in the right soil can rare and precious flowers bloom.”

Bu Quan looked at Zi Di, nodded slightly, then shook her head: “Let's not talk about this for now, okay?”

“Let's discuss the reward first.”

"Last night at the ball, I wanted to discuss this important matter with Commander Long Fu. But unexpectedly, I had the honor of receiving the commander's invitation, which disrupted my original plans."

“However, in fact, this matter is very important to me.”

“Before this matter is resolved, can we not discuss anything else?”

“This...” Zi Di's smile grew forced.

Her heart sank.

This situation was not good!

Bu Quan adopted a calculating expression, a gesture that proved she was setting aside the influence of her feelings toward Long Fu and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster shook his head in refusal: “Bu Quan, you don’t owe me any favors. In fact, I’m the one who owes you.”

“As for the specific reasons, I already mentioned them at last night’s banquet, right?”

“Of course,” Bu Quan smiled, “but that’s your reason, not enough to serve as a standard for action.”

"The Bayonet Gang was annihilated, and Teng Dong Lang was killed by you, the commander, in full view of everyone. Most importantly, you used the spell scroll I gave you to turn the tide in this battle."

“This is the best revenge for me.”

“There is no doubt that you are my benefactor.”

“I must repay you!”

“Repaying you is the prerequisite for everything.”

Bu Quan had already anticipated the dragonman youngster's attitude and displayed an unusual assertiveness.

“This...” The dragonman youngster was about to refuse.

Zi Di thought to herself, “Commander, please speak less.”

She once again stopped the dragonman youngster with her gaze, then said seriously to Bu Quan, “Let's talk about it properly.”

Bu Quan took out a list and placed it on the desk.

“These are my main assets in Snow Bird Harbor, and also my personal repayment to you, Commander Long Fu.”

Everyone's attention was immediately drawn to the list.

Bu Quan's main assets consisted of four alchemy workshops. Specifically, one large alchemy workshop, two medium-sized ones, and one small one.

Bu Quan then gave a brief introduction.

"The large alchemy workshop is located on the southeast coast of Snow Bird Harbor, equipped with a ventilation system and a cubic furnace, capable of processing silver level materials on a large scale. Its primary function is to supply processed materials to the other three alchemy workshops."

"The two medium-sized alchemy workshops are located in Mercury Cave and Rose Beach. The former utilizes local resources and has a complete production line for crafting alchemy puppets. The latter draws on the geothermal energy beneath the beach, primarily for the production of potions."

"The small workshop is located within the city and is managed by me year-round. It houses alchemy components such as a living fossil workbench, a hollow coral forging anvil, a flame engineering hammer, and a blue crystal future projector."

Zhong Yong listened beside her, his mouth agape, nearly drooling with envy.

His life's dream was to own his own alchemy workshop, to support himself while continuing to explore the field of alchemy.

He never imagined that Bu Quan would give out four of his dream workshops at once!

Zhong Yong, being an expert, quickly assessed the value of these four alchemy workshops in his mind.

"In large alchemy workshops, the ventilation wheel is the core energy source. Such alchemy devices are already renowned for their affordability and quality. The cubic furnace, however, is no ordinary device. It can be folded up for portability, with a volume less than one percent of its operational state. Such portable alchemy tools are typically used for setting up temporary camps. Using them in a fixed location is actually a waste of functionality."

"The two medium-sized alchemy workshops are carefully selected for their locations, clearly designed with specific structures, making them custom-made alchemy workshops. They produce alchemy puppets and potions, with significant production volumes."

"The value of the small workshops is the greatest, and it's quite exaggerated! Every tool mentioned by Bu Quan is made of gold level components!"

"The living fossil workbench effectively maintains the activity of materials. It also has a built-in spell that activates everything, allowing for automated material processing. For example, it can make wood actively jump into flames to burn itself. It can make potions actively crowd together, spin wildly, and complete the mixing process on their own."

"The main material for making coral anvils is rare golden coral. The manufacturing process is extremely labor-intensive, with each hollowed-out section being a miniature compressed space. With such a structure, the coral anvil can store a large amount of materials. It can instantly absorb waste materials during the material forging process. It can also store and retrieve suitable materials, saving a significant amount of materials and preventing waste. The spell it carries significantly increases the success rate of material processing and the quality of the final product."

"The flame engineering hammer is a fully flame-formed alchemy tool that can be scaled to any size. Besides forging, it also functions as a furnace, capable of enveloping and melting various materials for refinement, making it highly convenient and practical."

"The blue crystal future projector is the most precious treasure among them all. It is extremely rare, and alchemists often have no way to purchase it."

"Its function is highly powerful, enabling the prediction of alchemy outcomes and their expression through magic images. In other words, with it, alchemists can foresee the success or failure of an important alchemy endeavor in advance. Furthermore, they can carefully analyze the magic images to identify the specific cause of failure and avoid it beforehand."

"Bu Quan is indeed a talent that the Alchemist Guild values. These precious items must be the rewards she exchanged for completing her tasks using her contribution points."

The more Zhong Yong analyzed, the more he felt a sense of joy.

The reason was simple.

Bu Quan was giving these to the dragonman youngster as a token of gratitude.

So, once Zhong Yong joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, wouldn’t he have the opportunity to access and use these alchemy devices?

“I never expected such a good thing!”

“Thank you, Miss Bu Quan. On my first day joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, you’ve already sent such a generous gift to improve our working conditions!”

Although Zhong Yong didn't understand the full story, after eavesdropping, he vaguely understood Bu Quan's attitude toward the dragonman youngster and the motive behind this action.

“These assets are of great value, yet Bu Quan gave them away so easily.”

“I understand!”

“Bu Quan did this to distance herself from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and reject Long Fu's invitation.”

Zhong Yong felt he had guessed Bu Quan’s intention.

On one hand, he marveled at Bu Quan’s generosity, giving such a heavy gift of gratitude on a whim—truly the personality of a rising star.

On the other hand, he also approved of Bu Quan’s actions.

Why should the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps try to recruit Bu Quan?

How dare they make such an excessive demand?

How thick-skinned can they be?

It could be said that Bu Quan was like a pearl, a rising star in the alchemy circle.

The dragonman youngster recruiting Bu Quan felt like an outsider coming to the kingdom to court the princess.

The key was that this outsider wasn't even a prince; at best, he was just a peddler.

They weren't a match.

The princess's future could not end up like this.

The dragonman youngster couldn't bring the princess happiness!

Zhong Yong was just about to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps but hadn't officially joined yet, so his approval of the dragonman youngster and others was very low.

Additionally, he grew up in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, and though he was a fishman, he had been treated kindly due to his bloodline.

Therefore, he always believed himself to be a resident of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, a member of its alchemists.

Bu Quan was his idol!

Young, snow elf, genius, talent, and brilliance—Zhong Yong aspired to become such an alchemist.

Unfortunately, in reality, he was far from Bu Quan's level.

At the same time, he saw many similarities between himself and Bu Quan.

For instance, the hardships of her early years, her relentless personal efforts, her thirst for knowledge, and her dedication to the study of alchemy.

Zhong Yong had been secretly observing Bu Quan for a long time.

Many alchemists paid attention to Bu Quan, and it was quite normal for a genius like her to attract attention.

To put it dramatically, Bu Quan was like Zhong Yong's goddess.

The former had lived out the life the latter most desired.

Therefore, when Zhong Yong discovered that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had recruited Bu Quan, he felt Bu Quan was undervalued, and his heart was filled with resentment and aversion.

To some extent, this was him protecting his personal ideals, not wanting them to be tarnished.

The dragonman youngster sighed, “This gift is too valuable.”

Bu Quan shook her head and insisted, “Commander Long Fu, please accept it.”

Seeing this, Zi Di's heart sank to the bottom.

Her thoughts were the same as Zhong Yong's.

Bu Quan was using material wealth to repay the gratitude in her heart.

This was precisely what Zi Di did not want to see.

This sense of gratitude was the greatest bargaining chip in the negotiations between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Bu Quan.

Yet Bu Quan was undermining it.

“What should we do?” Zi Di thought urgently.

"We cannot accept this gift hastily; we need to delay and negotiate."

"We need to show our bargaining chips. The database from the War Merchant is a huge temptation for any alchemist!"

“Additionally, perhaps her gratitude toward the commander can also be utilized.”

As Zi Di pondered the negotiation strategy, she heard the dragonman youngster speak again.

“Alright, I accept this token of gratitude.”

Zi Di’s eyes widened in shock, as if struck by lightning.

Then she heard the dragonman youngster ask, “And what is your answer?”

Zi Di was furious, so much so that she wanted to punch the dragonman youngster in the head.

But then she saw Bu Quan flash a dazzling smile and nod, saying, "I accept your invitation, Commander Long Fu!"

Zi Di: ????

The dragonman youngster burst into laughter, unable to resist clapping his hands forcefully.

Thud.

The next moment, the utterly shocked fishman Zhong Yong fell from his chair onto the ground.

He opened his mouth agape as if his world view had collapsed.

He seemed to be screaming—

Goddess, what's happened to you?!

Goddess, you can't be simping!!!

Chapter 565: I Don’t Want to Regret

There was no wind on the sea.

The sea surface was calm.

Tall fish fins, like battle flags, suddenly appeared above the water.

A large group of sailfish flicked their powerful tails and swam swiftly through the shallow waters with agile movements.

In front of them, a silver level whale was frantically trying to escape.

The whale was massive and moved slowly, quickly caught up by the sailfish.

The sailfish darted like arrows, piercing the whale’s defenses with their long, sharp beaks

The whale bled profusely, emitting agonized cries.

Soon, the massive whale lost its life, its heavy body sinking slowly to the ocean floor.

The sailfish remained in place, gathering into a group, quietly awaiting the arrival of their leader.

The aura of a gold level magic beast swiftly rose from beneath the sea surface.

The leader of the fish school appeared.

It was a gold level sailfish, but it had only a fish head remaining.

The sailfish head emitted cries and magic fluctuations, continuing to command the sailfish school.

The sailfish dived to the bottom, using their razor-sharp mouths to slice through the whale's corpse.

Soon, blood spread rapidly, staining more and more seawater red.

Not long after, an eerie, gloomy mist covered the battlefield, and the ghost ship sailed into the area.

On the ship's rail stood Cang Xu, Xiao Wang Zi, and Ku Feng.

Their leader, Jian Gui, had merged with the ghost ship in his ghostly form. This allowed him to expand the ghost ship's reconnaissance range. Whenever they entered unfamiliar waters, Jian Gui would do this to prevent unforeseen circumstances.

As for Qing Xin, the only living person on board and a wind magician, during the ghost ship's voyage, she frequently used the Wind Booster spell to increase the ship's speed, contributing her value.

The sailfish school immediately became agitated and chaotic upon the ghost ship's arrival.

Almost all living beings instinctively felt fear, disgust, and other negative emotions toward undead lifeforms.

Positive energy and negative energy inherently opposed each other.

“Another silver level whale,” Ku Feng exclaimed with admiration, "Today's luck is good; this is already the third one this morning.”

Cang Xu smiled: “After all, this is the Whale Song Sea; it's normal for there to be more whales here.”

Xiao Wang Zi looked enviously at Cang Xu: "You can actually command the existing school of fish through the death head. Not only for hunting, but also to have the fish school process the corpses for us—that's truly convenient!"

Cang Xu smiled; he hadn't anticipated such benefits from practicing the Flying Head Spell.

The group of necromancers departed from Jian Gui's safe house and headed directly for the White Hair Trench.

During this time, Cang Xu practiced and gained a basic mastery of the necromancy spells involved in the Flying Head Spell.

As they were about to enter the Whale Song Sea, they were attacked by a school of sailfish.

Jian Gui personally intervened, dispersing the school of fish, after which Cang Xu voluntarily requested and successfully obtained the head of the sailfish leader.

He utilized the Flying Head Spell, narrowly succeeding in gaining control of a gold level death head.

Through this sailfish head, he unexpectedly discovered that he could indirectly control the sailfish school.

During subsequent voyages, he focused on gathering sailfish, gradually forming the current-sized school.

The Flying Head Spell had already begun to demonstrate its advantages in Cang Xu's hands.

It should be noted that while Cang Xu appeared to be a silver level necromancer, his true rank was only iron level.

Yet he had managed to transcend two levels to control a gold level death head.

This degree of crossing levels was absurd.

Among the three necromancer aspects, the bone aspect controlled skeletons, the corpse aspect commanded zombies, and the soul aspect wielded ghosts—all capable of forming armies.

They could also cross realms to control servants stronger than their own bodies.

But Cang Xu easily compared and found that their realm-crossing capabilities paled in comparison to the Flying Head Spell.

“The Flying Head Spell must have drawn upon the essence of the corpse and soul aspects, making transcending levels much easier.”

"Through the death head, I can indirectly control the beast herd. But two conditions must be met: first, the beast herd must have very limited intelligence. Second, the death head must restrain all its undead aura."

The whale was quickly sliced to pieces by the sailfish swarm.

"As per our previous agreement, the flesh and bones are yours. The souls belong to Lord Jian Gui; I'll only take the heads." Cang Xu distributed the spoils.

After tasting the benefits of the Flying Head Spell, Cang Xu now viewed every head with a different perspective.

Xiao Wang Zi saw another whale head fall into Cang Xu's hands and couldn't help but exclaim, “This is already the fifth one, right?”

“Unfortunately, whales exist in family units.”

“However, summoning five whale death heads at once is still quite impressive.”

Xiao Wang Zi imagined the scene and became excited.

Cang Xu gave a wry smile and replied, “You're underestimating the difficulty of the Head Creation Spell.”

“In fact, the higher the level of the skull material, the harder it is to succeed.”

“I was very lucky to have mastered the art of making sailfish death heads; such success is almost impossible to replicate.”

Cang Xu gradually began to grasp some of the mysteries of the Head Creation Spell.

Although the Head Creation Spell is a necromancy spell, necromancy was merely the foundation. What was truly utilized was a vast formation of alchemy techniques.

One could even say that alchemy techniques were the more critical element, significantly determining the success rate of death head creation.

The more appropriate and advanced the alchemy techniques employed, the higher the quality of the resulting death head, endowed with extraordinary power.

This posed a significant challenge for Cang Xu.

Cang Xu's expertise in alchemy was quite ordinary, and he had no natural talent for it.

Even attempting to replicate alchemy formations would likely result in failure.

In recent days, Cang Xu had showcased his skills through the inheritance of the Flying Head Spell.

But he was well aware that his alchemy abilities would severely limit the potential of the Flying Head Spell in the future.

“To avoid this weakness, the most direct solution is to rely on external assistance.”

“But where can I find an alchemist who is suitable for me and whom I can trust to collaborate with?”

The people around Cang Xu were not suitable.

Among them, Xiao Wang Zi had a notable talent for alchemy.

But Cang Xu had no intention of asking him for help.

Because Xiao Wang Zi was a necromancer himself.

If he participated in the creation of the death head, he would easily uncover the secrets of the Flying Head Spell during the process.

This would be very easy for him.

Cang Xu was also thinking of using the teachings of the Flying Head Spell as a bargaining chip to trade with these necromancers.

Especially Jian Gui.

If possible, Cang Xu wanted to obtain more advanced necromantic inheritance from Jian Gui. Whether it was soul crystals or memory crystals, such knowledge would bring immense assistance to both Cang Xu himself and the survivors.

“But if one isn't a necromancer, which normal alchemist would collaborate with a necromancer?”

“This is something that can't be hidden or avoided.”

“The foundation for creating a death head is undead magic power.”

While Cang Xu was worrying about the future, he didn't know that two alchemists had already joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

And one of them was a prodigious new talent among the few in the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom!

When the fishman magician Zhong Yong heard that Bu Quan had agreed to recruit him, he was so shocked that he fell off his seat.

“Magician Zhong Yong, are you okay?” the dragonman youngster expressed his concern.

Zhong Yong hurriedly said, “I just didn't sit properly. I'm fine.”

But his stammering tone and shocked expression revealed the extreme turmoil in his heart at that moment.

Bu Quan maintained the posture she had when she agreed to recruit him. She stared at the dragonman youngster with intense eyes, her face flushed.

Zi Di did not hide her surprise, but seeing Bu Quan's gaze, she couldn't help but be taken aback.

She instinctively looked at the dragonman youngster.

The latter looked at Bu Quan and laughed foolishly.

The ghost girl's expression darkened, filled with confusion, unease, and irritation.

She forced herself to suppress her emotions and pulled out a blank spell scroll: “Let's discuss the specifics.”

“The most important item—remuneration.”

“What is your ideal salary, Bu Quan?”

Bu Quan paused, unsure of how to respond: “I’m sorry, Magician Yao Ma, I haven’t thought about that yet.”

“You can decide.”

Zi Di froze, wondering: Was that her honest answer, or was it a negotiation tactic?

Zhong Yong was stunned!

“What do you mean, ‘you can decide’?”

“Hey, you're Bu Quan!”

“How can you be so casual about something as important as compensation?”

“Please show some dignity!”

“You're an Alchemy Great Master, a genius of the kingdom!”

Zhong Yong roared in his heart, frustrated by her lack of ambition.

Bu Quan's 'you can decide' didn't faze Zi Di.

The ghost girl had prepared extensively and immediately began speaking confidently: “As far as I know, there are currently three main methods for hiring alchemists...”

She detailed the differences between the three methods and kept observing Bu Quan's reactions throughout.

Bu Quan no longer stared at the dragonman youngster.

She had already exhausted her courage by staring at him earlier.

Her gaze shifted around, and after a moment of thought, she replied, "I don't have high demands for an annual salary. If I must say, I prefer a profit-sharing arrangement. For each alchemy item sold, a proportion of the profits should be allocated to me. The specific proportion will be determined based on the specific alchemy item. What do you think?"

“No problem.”

Bu Quan added, “After joining, I hope the working environment will be better, at least as good as the conditions I had in Snow Bird Harbor.”

“That's also no problem,” the dragonman youngster replied, "The gratuity you receive will be entirely yours to use. In the future, we will definitely provide you with better alchemy tools."

The negotiations proceeded smoothly.

Bu Quan was easy to talk to, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was indeed sincere.

But just as they were about to sign the contract, the fishman Zhong Yong couldn't help but ask, “Miss Bu Quan, what exactly made you decide to join a mercenary group?”

Bu Quan's expression faltered slightly, and images of her mother and mentor flashed before her eyes.

She smiled faintly: “Indeed, I have a clearer path in life, and I can be certain that this path will be full of promise.”

"But last night, I tossed and turned, unable to sleep. I asked myself if I will regret this."

“In the future, when I have achieved more and grown old, will I regret choosing a safe option in life when I look back on today?”

“I am still young.”

“I don't want to regret it.”

Her tone was calm, yet it revealed her resolve.

After speaking, she signed the magic contract.

Zhong Yong sighed deeply.

Zi Di's expression changed. She turned her head to look at the dragonman youngster, her gaze growing somber.

This time, it was the dragonman youngster who had a bad feeling.

Chapter 566: Building the Bloodline Purification Formation

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The alchemy cabin.

Hiss.

A soft click sounded as the hatch slid open.

Zi Di, expressionless, stepped into the alchemy cabin first.

Following closely behind her were two alchemy puppets.

The puppets were equipped with swords and shields, clearly the swordsman guards.

The two swordsman guards carried a fishman, one in front and one behind, into the alchemy cabin.

The hatch closed immediately.

The swordsman guards gently placed the fishman on the ground, then stepped back, firmly guarding the hatch.

The fishman emanated the aura of a silver level magician—it was Zhong Yong.

Zi Di took a vial from her bosom and removed the stopper.

A wisp of yellow-green smoke immediately drifted out of the bottle. It seemed drawn to the fishman magician, approaching him on its own.

The fishman magician was unconscious, but upon sensing the yellow-green smoke, his brows furrowed tightly, and his limbs began to twitch.

Soon, he suddenly opened his eyes amid the twitching.

His eyes were filled with bloodshot veins.

“Where... where am I?”

“What was I doing?”

The fishman magician's vision was blurry, and his mind was foggy, leaving him disoriented.

He heard Zi Di's voice: “Zhong Yong, get up. You've arrived.”

Zhong Yong recognized the familiar voice. He lifted his head and saw Zi Di.

His vision cleared, and his mental faculties quickly returned. The memories of what had just happened flooded back.

“I remember now!”

“I received orders from my family to go to Snow Bird Harbor and join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and from then on, serve them.”

“What I never expected was that I would see Bu Quan on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ship.”

“Bu Quan had also joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, giving them all four of her alchemy workshops, and even agreeing to recruit others to join as well.”

“Most astonishingly, she had no demands regarding compensation!”

As his memories gradually returned, Zhong Yong’s heart was filled with a mix of emotions.

Shock, doubt, pity for Bu Quan, envy for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and hatred for the dragonman youngster.

“Bu Quan's gaze toward Long Fu was off!”

“If I'm not mistaken, she must have fallen for Long Fu.”

“Oh my god, how could this happen...”

“The princess has really been brought away by an outsider!”

Zi Di grew impatient and urged again, “Zhong Yong, the magic potion I gave you to drink doesn't have the side effect of causing limb weakness.”

Her voice startled Zhong Yong, who quickly suppressed his surging emotions.

He immediately stood up.

As a fishman, even when standing straight, his head didn't reach Zi Di's chin.

“Magician Yao Ma,” Zhong Yong bowed slightly to Zi Di, then surveyed his surroundings.

At that moment, he was utterly shocked.

The gold level aura filled the entire ship's cabin.

Even without activating it, Zhong Yong could tell that the alchemy formation in the cabin was of the highest specifications.

Various alchemy tools were neatly arranged along the walls, fully equipped.

A bizarre-looking alchemy puppet stood in an auxiliary position within the alchemy formation. It resembled a giant crab, and as Zhong Yong observed it, it seemed to be observing him in return.

“This... where is this?” Zhong Yong asked.

“Of course, it's our group's secret workshop,” Zi Di said with a smile, her expression calm.

Zhong Yong realized what it was, and his expression grew tense, "Then, what exactly is the content of my assessment?”

No wonder he was tense.

According to the previous agreement, his annual salary would depend on his alchemy abilities.

This required a preliminary alchemy test.

Although Zhong Yong did not want to see Bu Quan join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he could not stop it. He could only watch as Bu Quan signed his name on the contract and recorded his magic power, life force, and other vital signs.

Afterward, the group discussed compensation, and Zi Di proposed the alchemy test.

Both Bu Quan and Zhong Yong had no objections to this process.

Zi Di separated Bu Quan and Zhong Yong, giving Zhong Yong a potion to drink.

After drinking it, Zhong Yong immediately fell into a deep sleep, unconscious.

During this time, Zi Di secretly brought him back to the Deep Sea Monster Fish and directly to the alchemy cabin.

“This is what the test is about.” Zi Di activated the alchemy formation.

After a spatial fluctuation, a piece of formation plate and an incomplete formation diagram suddenly appeared.

They floated slowly through the air and eventually landed in front of Zhong Yong.

He quickly picked them up and examined them carefully.

He had encountered many formation diagram panels before, but gold level ones were rare.

After reviewing them once, he focused his attention on the formation diagram.

It was a fragmented alchemy formation diagram.

Zhong Yong studied it carefully and couldn't help but be amazed.

He couldn't understand it!

“Is the test content this difficult?” Zhong Yong's heart sank, “Or are they intentionally testing such a difficult question just to suppress the salary?”

“Never mind.”

“What can I do?”

“I must join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, even if the remuneration is poor, I have to do it.”

“Plus, Bu Quan has already joined this organization. She not only has no demands regarding salary but also contributed four alchemy workshops!”

“Compared to her, what am I?”

Zhong Yong gradually lost all hope.

Seeing his pale face and dejected expression, Zi Di’s heart couldn’t help but tighten.

After a moment, Zi Di asked, “Tell me, what did you notice about this formation diagram?”

Zhong Yong shook his head, "I'm just too incompetent

“This formation diagram is very incomplete, and I can't deduce the function of the complete formation diagram.”

“I think this should be a human alchemy formation, which seems to have a healing effect on the human body?”

"Pardon my frankness, Magician Yao Ma. The more incomplete the formation diagram, the harder it is to deduce and reconstruct. This incomplete diagram is likely only one-eighth of the original. Deducing the original diagram from this is extremely difficult. Perhaps Miss Bu Quan has the ability, but this task far exceeds my capabilities!"

Zi Di nodded slightly: “I didn't ask you to deduce the original diagram. I am also a magician, so I understand the difficulty involved.”

“The content of this assessment is for you to create this part of the formation diagram and engrave it on the formation diagram panel.”

“Just that?” Zhong Yong suddenly looked up, his face full of surprise.

"Of course. Do you find it difficult?" Zi Di asked with concern.

“This is easy!” Zhong Yong exclaimed with delight, "Although I specialize in potion-making, laying out such a formation is merely basic training.”

“Basic training...” Zi Di recalled the times she and Cang Xu had collaborated, repeatedly attempting and failing, and couldn't help but sigh.

"Then do your best and let me see your basic skills. The alchemy formation here can be lent to you for use. The alchemy puppet within the formation will monitor the entire process and record your work as a magic image. The commander and others will review it.” Zi Di said.

“Got it.”

“Do a good job!” Zi Di reminded him as she stepped out of the cabin.

“Sure thing!” Zhong Yong looked excited.

Meanwhile, on the Love Triangle.

Bu Quan was also studying an incomplete formation diagram.

This damaged formation and the one that tested Zhong Yong both came from the same place—the Bloodline Purification formation.

Zi Di had knocked Zhong Yong unconscious and secretly brought him aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, but she couldn’t treat Bu Quan the same way.
