Bu Quan’s status and contract were vastly different from Zhong Yong’s.

Although Zhong Yong had just joined, the strict contract allowed him to gain trust instantly.

Bu Quan, however, was not the same; her contract was very lenient. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could not sign a contract with her that was as restrictive as Zhong Yong's, which was nearly akin to a slave contract.

Bu Quan studied the formation diagram, becoming increasingly engrossed in it.

It wasn't until the dragonman youngster coughed a few times, interrupting her thoughts, that she looked up.

Bu Quan said sheepishly, " Sorry, when I study these alchemy-related things, I get so engrossed that I often lose track of time."

The dragonman youngster smiled, “It's okay.”

Bu Quan also smiled, "Is this the content of this test? It's quite interesting."

“Although the formation's structure is simple, upon closer examination, one can see that despite its simplicity, it is incredibly efficient and harmoniously structured. It must have been created by a master.”

“Hmm... so far, I've only deduced three possibilities.”

“I'll draw it out right away.”

Bu Quan spoke as she extended her index finger, sketching in the air before her.

She manipulated her magic power to linger in the air and take on different colors.

The magic lines intertwined, adorned with magic runes.

The dragonman youngster was dazzled and hurriedly said, “Miss Bu Quan, I think you might have misunderstood our intentions.”

“This test only requires you to construct this incomplete diagram and engrave it onto the formation diagram panel.”

"Ah? Is it that simple?" Bu Quan was taken aback.

Having left Zhong Yong aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Zi Di, who was still uneasy, had just returned when she heard Bu Quan's words.

“Simple...”

Zi Di's eyelid twitched slightly.

However, when she pushed open the captain's cabin door and saw the three possibilities Bu Quan had sketched out in midair, she was still deeply shaken.

80%

Because she keenly noticed that one of the possibilities bore an 80% similarity to the complete formation diagram!

In an instant, she realized the huge gap between herself and Bu Quan.

She also truly understood Bu Quan's innate talent in the field of alchemy for the first time.

“That's amazing!”

“How did you manage to do that in such a short time, Miss Bu Quan?”

“If you had a few days, you could even deduce the complete formation diagram, right?” Zi Di marveled.

Bu Quan's face flushed slightly as she shook her head and replied earnestly, “It's not that simple.”

“The work of deducing the original diagram becomes increasingly difficult as you progress.”

"The initial stages are the easiest. Because we can identify the incomplete diagram and follow the clues."

“But afterward, we have no markers and no direction, so we can only speculate on multiple possibilities, continue to test one by one, and keep undergoing trial and error.”

"The workload here is enormous. The more incomplete the formation diagram, the more so."

"Moreover, not all formation diagrams can be restored. After all, some of the designer's ingenious ideas cannot be deduced conventionally."

Upon hearing this, the dragonman youngster sighed in relief.

He realized that Zi Di’s flattery was actually a probe.

She was worried that Bu Quan might deduce the formation diagram and uncover the secrets of the Bloodline Purification formation.

Bu Quan sighed regretfully, “If I were given a second fragment of the diagram, and it were not closely connected to this one, perhaps I could deduce it within half a year.”

“This formation diagram is not overly complex or intricate.”

“Of course, during this period, I would be unable to attend to other matters.”

Upon hearing this, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di exchanged a subtle glance, both thinking to themselves, “There will be no second fragment of the diagram for you!”

Bu Quan's talent was too outstanding, so much so that it made both the dragonman youngster and Zi Di feel threatened.

Chapter 567: The Big Guy’s Appetite

Two days later.

In the iceberg sea.

Zong Ge led most of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in training at sea.

At this point, they were already far away from Ice Sculpture Island.

In order to maximize the effectiveness of the military training, Zong Ge had not returned to harbor for the past two days.

“There’s good news from Snow Bird Harbor,” Tripleblade approached Zong Ge and reported, “Our commander has successfully recruited Bu Quan. We now have an Alchemy Great Master.”

Upon hearing this, Zong Ge raised his eyebrows.

He took the intelligence report, examined it carefully, and also showed a pleased expression.

“That’s excellent.”

"A silver level Alchemy Great Master who hasn't reached the bloodline limit. Such a fortunate event has occurred."

"Oh... it's because the Bayonet Gang and Bu Quan have a deep-seated grudge against each other. This is an unexpected gain."

Tripleblade nodded but also expressed concern: “This is a genius talent, extremely gifted.”

“Although we're both willing, the Alchemy Guild probably won't let her go easily, and there will be more trouble later.”

Tripleblade had run a mercenary group before and knew that someone like Bu Quan was hard for the current Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to keep.

Zong Ge nodded slightly: "An Alchemy Great Master is like a never-ending gold mine. Especially such a special alchemist."

“Bu Quan is still young; her achievements may one day surpass those of a master.”

“Having such a companion would be a blessing for all of us.”

“We’ve seized a lot of alchemy documents, which are the best resources for cultivating alchemists.”

Thinking of this, Zong Ge stroked his chin: “Did the communication mention anything about recruitment?”

“Not yet,” Tripleblade replied.

Zong Ge smiled: “Bu Quan has joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Once this news spreads, it will surely stir up excitement.”

“I bet the recruitment results will be fantastic.”

“Our commander has really made a name for himself this time.”

“Yes, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has also gained fame throughout the kingdom.” Tripleblade sighed with emotion.

Once upon a time, he had also dreamed of such a thing, dreaming that his mercenary group could become famous nationwide.

Who would have thought that after being stranded on the Mystifying Monster Island, surviving countless dangers, and eventually arriving at the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, his former dream would be realized in this way.

Tripleblade looked at Zong Ge and said again, "The commander was able to achieve such military exploits, and it’s all thanks to you, sir, for training him. Otherwise, would he have mastered such solid gun skills?"

Zong Ge waved his hand, showing no intention of taking credit: “I only played a supporting role; he was the main one.”

“Bu Quan is a genius in alchemy, but Long Fu... In my opinion, his fighter talent far surpasses that of Bu Quan.”

Tripleblade wore a complex expression.

Zong Ge had once advised the dragonman youngster to conceal his innate fighter talents, lest they be revealed and dampen the training enthusiasm of most mercenaries.

However, this information was not withheld from the survivors.

Tripleblade and the others were all aware of this truth.

At that moment, a mercenary stepped forward and reported loudly, “Sir, there is a small island ahead.”

"Oh? Go take a look." Zong Ge's eyes lit up.

Although they had a map of this sea area, it was not detailed enough to record every single island.

This was related to the special nature of the iceberg sea area.

Soon, the ships of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps arrived around the island.

It was an iceberg floating on the sea surface, forming a slowly drifting ice island.

The mercenaries soon discovered something new: “Lord Shi Qi, we've spotted a group of sea lions at the southeast corner of the ice island.”

Zong Ge reviewed the detailed intelligence and personally observed the scene through a telescope.

There were at least two hundred sea lions, with two silver level magic beasts—one male and one female—leading the herd. There were approximately twenty iron level magic beasts among the sea lions.

“Excellent, perfect for training,” Zong Ge praised lightly, then issued the order to prepare for attack.

Upon receiving the order, the mercenaries sprang into action—some waving flags, others blowing horns.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ships gradually closed in. The sea lions grew restless, and the two leaders let out a loud roar. Soon, the entire herd joined in, emitting a deafening roar as a threat and warning.

Zong Ge remained calm, adjusting the ship formation.

The iron level magic ship, the Iron Lump, led the charge.

The sea lion herd leaped into the sea from the edge of the iceberg and surrounded the Iron Lump.

The Iron Lump, with its thick hull, withstood the sea lions' attacks from all directions, as well as magic spells.

Zong Ge remained on the White Bandage, leading the other ships to quickly surround the Iron Lump.

The mercenaries stood at the ship's rail, holding alchemy crossbows and muskets, firing at the sea lions.

Soon, the sea lions suffered heavy losses.

The two leaders were enraged. One leaped onto the Iron Lump, while the other charged toward the White Bandage.

Zong Ge did not intervene personally.

Men Shi and Chi Lai were also ordered to observe the battle.

The Quick Knife Squad led by Kuai Tui, along with the Backwater Battle Squad, boarded the battlefield and each took on one of the two silver level magic beasts.

Magic beasts have different physiques from humans and snow elves, with thick, rugged skin, yet they lack intelligence, making them ideal targets for practical combat training.

After about sixteen minutes of combat, the two sea lion leaders were enraged to the extreme, disregarding their injuries, and rampaged wildly across the deck.

Zong Ge finally ordered Men Shi and Chi Lai to act.

Both combat teams were extremely exhausted and withdrew smoothly.

Men Shi and Chi Lai found it difficult to block them head-on. Despite their battle qi enhancements, they could not match the sea lion beasts' strength.

This was a difference in race and bloodline.

Fortunately, the Iron Lump and White Bandage were magic ships capable of withstanding such intense combat.

Men Shi, being a dwarf, was stronger than Chi Lai, the human, with a lower center of gravity and a focus on defense, allowing him to handle the situation more calmly.

Chi Lai's skills were primarily in artillery fire, but he was somewhat lacking in close combat, so he could only maneuver around the sea lion as much as possible.

“Ah, I'll handle this!” The big guy suddenly intervened in the battle.

Bam!

He collided with the silver sea lion leader using his shoulder.

Both sides were subjected to a powerful recoil force, losing all momentum.

Zong Ge's eyes lit up at the sight.

Chi Lai muttered, “Thanks!”

He then rushed forward, firing his musket directly into the sea lion's eyes.

Deprived of vision, the sea lion was enraged and charged wildly once more.

The big guy gritted his teeth and fought the sea lion, transforming into a beast himself. The scene of the struggle attracted the attention of many people.

Tripleblade's eyes lit up: "I didn't expect that after the big guy ascended to the transcendent realm, his physical abilities would increase so terrifyingly. He can actually go head-to-head with a silver level beast!"

“His bloodline seems to be extraordinary.”

“Who would have thought that he could become a transcendent.”

Zong Ge sighed regretfully, “Unfortunately, although he has become bronze level, he hasn't mastered any battle skills, and his hand-to-hand combat techniques are even more pitiful.”

“If he could make up for these shortcomings, he would be a formidable weapon on the battlefield.”

Zong Ge watched the big guy's intense battle, his eyes gradually brightening.

He developed a strong interest in training the big guy.

Tripleblade expressed his concerns: “Sir, please be cautious.”

“The big guy has a mental illness.”

“If he were to master solid hand-to-hand combat skills, or even battle skills, it would actually be a major risk for us.”

Zong Ge shook his head, having already made up his mind: “With my strength, I can keep him in check.”

Soon, the battle ended.

After the two leaders were killed, the sea lion herd dived into the sea in panic, fleeing for their lives.

Zong Ge ordered his men to shoot and fire cannons, killing most of them.

After cleaning up the battlefield, Tripleblade reported the results: “Sir, with this batch of sea lion meat, we can sail for another half day.”

Zong Ge sighed: “Let's go back tonight.”

“There's no need to wait until we reach the food reserve warning line before returning to harbor.”

"This time, I miscalculated. I didn't expect that after becoming a bronze level cultivator, the big guy would eat so much!"

The big guy's appetite had always been far greater than that of an ordinary person, but after reaching the bronze level, he ate even more than before.

His appetite had increased dramatically, with his food intake reaching nearly 80 times that of before, becoming the main consumer of provisions.

The reason for this was that after becoming a transcendent, the battle qi cultivator's body required a much larger intake of energy.

The big guy needed at least 80 times the amount of ordinary food to sustain himself.

However, if the main ingredient was magic beast meat, his appetite would return to that of an ordinary person.

When the news of Zong Ge returning to harbor came back, the dragonman youngster was hosting the recruitment event.

The recruitment location was their own shop.

The shop's location was personally selected by Zi Di. With her business acumen, she naturally chose the best possible spot.

Those who came to sign up to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had already formed a long line extending from the shop. The line was over 200 meters long, stretching all the way to the corner of the street.

“Commander Long Fu, I am a magic apprentice who can currently craft deodorizing lozenges and fever detection reagents.” A snow elf entered the room and, upon seeing the dragonman youngster, couldn't help but reveal an excited expression.

The dragonman youngster had seen such expressions many times before.

He had gained national fame and had slain the leader of the Bayonet Gang, earning the admiration—even worship—of many in Snow Bird Harbor.

The dragonman youngster put down the communication device in his hand, smiled, and asked the magic apprentice being interviewed, “Can you make magic cuisine?”

The magic apprentice looked startled, then replied, “Oh, well, I can make most ordinary magic cuisine.”

“Bronze level magic cuisine is also possible, but the taste isn't great.”

“Iron level cuisine is very difficult for me.”

The magic apprentice was only bronze level and spoke honestly to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster nodded, “If I hire you to make magic cuisine, would you be willing?”

The snow elf immediately looked troubled, “Are you asking me to be a magic chef?”

“I'd rather work in the alchemy workshop, making potions...”

“In fact, I came here to apply for that kind of job.”

The dragonman youngster grinned, "I understand. I'll double your salary. Would you be willing to become the magic chef for our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?"

“Double the salary?” The snow elf's eyes lit up, and quickly agreed, “Of course, I'm willing!”

The dragonman youngster reminded, “If you join, you'll need to accompany the group on missions in the future.”

"No problem. Even going out to sea is fine!" The snow elf apprentice nodded eagerly.

As long as the money was right, everything else was negotiable.

Next, it was time to sign the magic contract.

“This is the fourth magic chef.”

“Xiao Guai, you won't have to worry about food and drink when you come back.” The dragonman youngster was satisfied with the results of the recruitment drive and thought to himself.

Bam!

Just then, the door to the interview room was pushed open with a bang.

A snow elf strode in with his head held high and a cold expression on his face.

“Commander Long Fu, I am Zhong Bei, a magician from the Alchemy Guild. We need to have a serious talk about Bu Quan joining your group!”

“Even if you defeated the stronger opponent and killed Teng Dong Lang, you still don’t have the qualifications to recruit our nation’s alchemy genius.”

Chapter 568: Zi Di – Originally I was Determined to Recruit Her

“A magician from the Alchemy Guild?” The dragonman youngster saw the magician burst in and was not surprised, but thought to himself: Finally, they're here.

Earlier, the dragonman youngster had learned that Bu Quan was a silver level alchemist and a genius.

Combined with subsequent intelligence from acquisitions and information obtained from Zhong Yong, the dragonman youngster’s understanding gradually deepened, and he finally realized: even among silver level alchemists, Bu Quan was extraordinary.

Bu Quan’s talent had been acknowledged by many prominent alchemists.

It could be said that she was one of the Alchemy Guild’s most prized assets.

Although it could be said that Bu Quan voluntarily joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, as an external mercenary organization, recruiting such talent would inevitably lead to complications.

The dragonman youngster put himself in their shoes: if the big guy were in such a situation, kidnapped by those with ulterior motives and taken to another organization, how would he feel? What would he do?

He would naturally be unhappy and unwilling.

If the organization harbored malicious intent, the dragonman youngster would likely resort to violent action and destroy it by force!

Facing a colossus like the Alchemy Guild, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ situation would be dire if they recruited Bu Quan.

Before Bu Quan’s second visit to the dock, Zi Di had already discussed the matter privately with the dragonman youngster.

She made it clear: "If we recruit Bu Quan, it could bring us major trouble. If we provoke the Alchemy Guild's retaliation, the current Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps might not be able to withstand it."

The key word here was “current.”

Clearly, Zi Di was optimistic about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ future, especially since they are about to complete the Bloodline Purification formation.

“If Bu Quan has a strong desire to join us voluntarily, our strategy for recruiting her must be adjusted to better handle future complications,” Zi Di suggested.

The dragonman youngster furrowed his brows in concern: “Perhaps abandoning the recruitment of Bu Quan, becoming friends with her, and reaching some form of cooperation could also be a viable option.”

Zi Di shook her head firmly and advised the dragonman youngster: "Lord, do not focus solely on the present; consider the future. In the future, Bu Quan's role will be irreplaceable."

“According to our agreement with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, we will obtain three silver level alchemists.”

“Since these three alchemists were traded as bargaining chips, do not expect them to have much potential for growth.”

“Unless they are allowed to use the Bloodline Purification formation.”

“Bu Quan is different.”

"If she grows up to become a gold level alchemist or her alchemy skill rises another level, her value will far exceed the combined value of these three silver level alchemists!”

“Then we'll make a huge profit!”

Zi Di changed the subject slightly and continued.

"Of course, the issue with Bu Quan is her loyalty. Her loyalty hasn't been tested, unlike our other companions."

"But that's not her fault. She simply hasn't shared experiences with us, like the Mystifying Monster Island incident."

“Therefore, we need to have her join us and act together to increase our shared experiences.”

"Only through constant interaction can we cultivate her trust in us. We can also gradually understand her character and personality. If things go smoothly, she could become a core member of our group in the future."

“While our bloodline can be enhanced, wisdom is a different matter altogether.”

"I cannot deny that a significant portion of Bu Quan's alchemy talent stems from her bloodline. However, there are alchemists with the same bloodline as hers who lack her alchemy prowess."

“She is well worth recruiting.”

“We have the alchemy data from the War Merchant, but we cannot fully capitalize on it to maximize its value.”

“Even if we had three silver level alchemists, it would be the same.”

“My and Cang Xu’s alchemy talent... Commander, you should know this very well.”

“One must know their own limitations!”

“If Bu Quan joins us, it will have a tremendous positive impact on the present and future of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“In any case, we cannot afford to let this opportunity slip away.”

“Since fate has brought her to us, we should cherish this chance and quickly bring her into our fold.”

“If we merely befriend her and let her stay outside, we won’t have time to cultivate our feelings for one another.”

“Over time, her gratitude toward you, the captain, and her goodwill toward our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will gradually fade.”

“It’s like how nobles maintain their mistresses—they must spend time with them periodically to sustain the relationship.”

Zi Di’s peculiar analogy caused the dragonman youngster’s expression to grow somewhat peculiar.

She didn’t notice this and remained immersed in her own thoughts.

“So now is the best opportunity to recruit her.”

“Though there will be risks from the Alchemy Guild, if we handle it properly, those risks will be significantly reduced.”

“Even if the situation becomes critical in the future, we can proactively terminate the contract to avoid the risk.”

Zi Di’s point was to try and not give up easily. Though the risk was significant, from a long-term perspective, the benefits Bu Quan could bring to the survivors were immense.

She then proposed a series of solutions.

The dragonman youngster accepted her suggestions and implemented them accordingly.

Ultimately, the agreement between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Bu Quan was very lenient.

Bu Quan had once said that she had no specific requirements for compensation, leaving it up to the dragonman youngster to decide.

Thus, Zi Di proposed a compensation of one gold coin.

This was undoubtedly more symbolic than practical.

This compensation left Bu Quan herself momentarily speechless.

Zi Di quickly added: The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would formally hire Bu Quan as a senior alchemy advisor accompanying the group. The true compensation would be some valuable alchemy knowledge.

The specific form of this knowledge included data and materials from alchemy experiments, as well as blueprints and formulas for alchemy potions and tools.

Upon hearing this, Bu Quan's astonishment faded, and her eyes lit up.

She was very interested in this form of compensation and agreed without much hesitation.

When the dragonman youngster asked about Zhong Yong's ideal compensation, the fishman magician could only grimace and propose a low price of 100 gold.

Before this, Zhong Yong had intended to demand a high price of 500 gold, but he unexpectedly faced such fierce competition from Bu Quan.

Seeing the situation was unfavorable, Zhong Yong could only abandon his previous plan and voluntarily lower his intended salary.

This action undoubtedly made the dragonman youngster and Zi Di view him more favorably.

The two had, of course, conducted thorough research into the hiring costs for an alchemist.

Typically, an alchemist like Zhong Yong would command an annual salary of 200 gold pieces.

The hiring costs for an alchemist far exceed those of fighters at the same level and even surpass those of conventional magicians.

The reason was simple: alchemists could create value on their own.

They could create alchemy items, and by selling these items, the hiring party could receive a steady stream of returns.

Hiring an alchemist for 200 gold might seem expensive, but in reality, it was quite profitable for the hiring party. Additionally, the terms of such contracts did not impose strict constraints on alchemists.

Zhong Yong was different from most situations in the market.

Essentially, he was just a slave. The contract to be signed with him would undoubtedly be very strict.

Zi Di shook her head at the 100 gold offer.

She said, “a yearly salary of 100 gold is simply too low,” and proactively raised the salary to 300 gold.

That's not all.

Zi Di also stated that in the future, Zhong Yong's performance will be evaluated annually. Different performance levels would result in different tiers of rewards. Zhong Yong's loyalty to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would entitle him to a fixed dividend annually once he reached three years of service. Additionally, as the years increased, the proportion of the fixed dividend would also grow.

Zi Di was a wise merchant who pursued profit.

Therefore, she proactively increased Zhong Yong's wages to incentivize his productivity and ultimately expand profits.

Zhong Yong was overjoyed!

The terms of this contract offered him a glimpse of a new way of life.

Two days have passed since they signed the contract.

During this time, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps discreetly leaked some information.

This move yielded results—now, the alchemist from the Alchemy Guild had come knocking on their door.

The appearance of Zhong Bei was not so much something the dragonman youngster had anticipated as it was part of his plan!

When the alchemists came to hold him accountable, the dragonman youngster immediately stood up, smiled, and greeted them, “Magician Zhong Bei, hello, please sit down.”

The snow elf was arrogant and cold-faced, but as the saying goes, one would not slap a smiling face. He nodded faintly and sat down beside the chair.

The dragonman youngster moved over and sat beside him.

A mercenary brought tea and pastries.

“I don't need your hospitality,” the snow elf magician said without glancing at the tea and pastries.

He stared into the dragonman youngster's eyes with a firm expression.

“Commander Long Fu, you may not fully understand the severity of this matter.”

"You deceived Magician Bu Quan with your recruitment tactics! Even if you have already signed a magic contract with her, it is useless."

"You have harbored improper intentions! Magician Bu Quan is a member of the Alchemy Guild. Do you think the Alchemy Guild is comparable to the Bayonet Gang?”

The dragonman youngster hurriedly waved his hands.

“Magician Zhong Bei, I have never intended to oppose the Alchemy Guild.”

“Even with a million guts, I would never become your enemy.”

“On one hand, I’m well aware that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps isn’t a match for the Alchemy Guild.”

“On the other hand, why would I want to oppose my friends?”

“There are some things we can discuss properly.”

After hearing these words, the snow elf magician Zhong Bei couldn’t help but scrutinize the dragonman youngster before him once more.

Earlier, he had actually been quite nervous.

Who didn't know the dragonman youngster's reputation?

The origins of his conflict with the Bayonet Gang were a topic of lively discussion among the citizens of Snow Bird Harbor.

The most widely circulated version was Bing Xiao had a confrontation with the dragonman youngster and others in a shop over a gold level potion. He mocked the newly arrived Long Fu and others, which sparked the dragonman youngster’s spirit of resistance.

Later, Bing Xiao even influenced the Bayonet Gang to repeatedly harass the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Not only did he impose various obstacles, but he also sent assassins to kill two magicians from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster rose up in defiance, ultimately overcoming the stronger foe, and justice triumphed over evil!

In Zhong Bei's heart, the dragonman youngster was a very tough individual.

Who would challenge Teng Dong Lang if they weren't tough?

However, upon first contact, he was surprised to discover that the dragonman youngster was actually quite easy to talk to, and his attitude was very polite!

"Perhaps the Alchemy Guild's reputation is too great; the Bayonet Gang can't compare. Has Long Fu already backed down?"

Thinking this, Zhong Bei couldn't help but feel hopeful: “Perhaps this time, I can resolve this crisis easily.”

Chapter 569: Dragonman – Bu Quan Did Everything of Her Own Accord

Finding the dragonman youngster easy to talk to, the alchemist Zhong Bei was pleasantly surprised.

If it weren't for the problem with Bu Quan, he wouldn't have wanted to cause trouble for the dragonman youngster.

Zhong Bei sighed inwardly, knowing that this time, he had no choice but to come.

The dragonman youngster’s attitude caused Zhong Bei to lower his guard, and his tone softened.

He introduced himself, “I am Magician Bu Quan's guardian, primarily responsible for ensuring her safety in everyday life. In emergencies beyond my personal capabilities, I can request assistance from the Alchemist Guild. My authority to request aid is quite high, and in necessary situations, a gold level alchemist will be teleported here to provide reinforcements.”

Gold level transcendents typically held high social status, such as being the ruler of a city. They were usually busy with their own affairs.

However, Bu Quan could obtain gold level reinforcements from the Alchemy Guild during dangerous situations.

Bu Quan was well protected, which also confirms her immense talent.

Of course, in addition to protecting Bu Quan, Magician Zhong Bei also had routine monitoring duties.

Every so often, he compiled a report on Bu Quan's performance and behavior during that period and submitted it as intelligence.

This intelligence was compiled into a dossier, which would serve as an important reference for higher-ups when considering Bu Quan for future assignments.

If Bu Quan were to encounter other unforeseen circumstances, such as leaking state secrets of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom or being recruited by enemies, then Zhong Bei's role would extend beyond mere protection.

By the time Zhong Bei learned that Bu Quan had joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, it was already too late.

He knew his work had been compromised.

Although he had monitored Bu Quan and learned of her two visits to the dragonman youngster, he never anticipated that Bu Quan, who had only met him a few times, would agree to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

Such a talented individual as Bu Quan had been recruited by an external mercenary organization!

After all, she was a key talent being cultivated by the Alchemy Guild. Once trained, Bu Quan was supposed to serve the royal family and become one of the pillars of the Alchemy Guild.

Now, she had been lured away by others.

What was to be done?

Zhong Bei knew that if this matter were exposed, he would be severely dealt with for failing his duties.

Fortunately, at this time, only rumors had leaked out. He had to do everything in his power to resolve this matter before it became common knowledge.

“Commander Long Fu, as long as you voluntarily revoke this contract, I can pretend nothing ever happened,” Zhong Bei said.

The dragonman youngster smiled and shook his head: "Magician Zhong Bei, I admit I did actively recruit Bu Quan. Who wouldn't be tempted by such a talent?"

“But the specifics may differ from what you imagine.”

“The real process was like this...”

The dragonman youngster briefly recounted the events, emphasizing Bu Quan's initiative and willingness throughout his narrative.

Zhong Bei finally understood that Bu Quan had directly gifted her four alchemy workshops to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps out of gratitude.

One had to know that this was almost equivalent to her entire assets in Snow Bird Harbor!

Magician Zhong Bei furrowed his brows deeply.

There was no doubt that the real situation was far more complicated than he had imagined.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was being stubborn or deceiving Bu Quan, it would almost be like handing over the upper hand to Zhong Bei.

Zhong Bei, backed by the Alchemy Guild, could not only easily suppress the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps but also take the opportunity to extort a sum of money.

However, the issue now was that Bu Quan acted of her own accord, and her personal will was extremely strong.

The situation was extremely complicated!

Even the Alchemy Guild, or even Bu Quan's mentor personally intervening, would find it difficult to directly intervene in Bu Quan's personal actions.

On one hand, this was due to the broader context of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

In the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, alchemists held a very high status. The independent and free will of magicians was respected and protected.

If the Alchemy Guild were to forcibly request Bu Quan to revoke the contract, it would undoubtedly infringe upon the independent and free will of magicians. This would lead to widespread resentment, and even resistance, among most magicians.

On the other hand, the Alchemy Guild had to also consider Bu Quan's genuine feelings.

They cultivated Bu Quan with the aim of nurturing a talent with innovative capabilities. They hoped Bu Quan could achieve success in unexplored fields and drive iterative updates to alchemy technology across the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Such talent should never be stifled.

If Bu Quan developed feelings of aversion, it would severely undermine her motivation.

The Alchemy Guild's forced intervention would trigger Bu Quan's passive resistance. Even if they restricted her freedom, the Alchemy Guild would only end up with a skilled alchemist.

For example, suppose the big guy wasn't kidnapped by someone with ulterior motives, but voluntarily and actively joined another organization, and the big guy had strong feelings for this organization, the dragonman youngster would find this situation tricky to handle, as he had to consider the big guy's feelings.

If the methods were too harsh and coercive, it could easily lead to a feud, which would be completely counterproductive.

If such a situation were to arise, the dragonman youngster would thoroughly investigate to uncover the truth.

Once he confirmed that the big guy was determined to join this organization, and the organization was well-intentioned and would not conflict with their side, the dragonman youngster would let go.

He would also actively seek to befriend the organization, provide support, and advocate for the organization to improve the big guy's treatment.

The old boatwright entrusted the big guy to the dragonman youngster.

For this comrade who has been through life and death, the dragonman youngster cared for him and had never once considered controlling the latter's entire life.

In the case of Bu Quan, the situation was similar.

Bu Quan possessed exceptional alchemy talent, which had caught the attention of the Alchemy Guild, but this talent would only bear fruit in the future.

She needed time to grow.

If Bu Quan had already matured and gained access to some of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's secrets, the dragonman youngster would never have attempted to recruit her.

In fact, once Bu Quan had grown up, every person she came into contact with each day would be investigated, monitored, and even arrested and interrogated by the kingdom's spies.

While Bu Quan's talent was good, it was not so exceptional that a gold level individual would serve as her personal bodyguard, providing 24/7 protection.

Seeing the grim expression on the magician's face, the dragonman youngster felt a surge of amusement.

He had learned a great deal from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Now he had come to understand the excitement of strategic maneuvering beyond combat.

This feeling was truly wonderful.

Everything was under his control.

Though the dragonman youngster appeared to be in trouble, with Magician Zhong Bei pressing him relentlessly, in reality, the dragonman youngster had complete control of the situation.

He then took out a piece of paper from his pocket and handed it to Magician Zhong Bei.

“This is the detailed content of the contract we signed with Magician Bu Quan.”

“Magician Zhong Bei can examine it carefully.”

“You can verify it with Magician Bu Quan herself.”

Chapter 570: Zhong Bei - Bu Quan, You’re Foolish

Zhong Bei took the paper and looked at it carefully.

“Senior alchemy advisor accompanying the group?”

He saw the key words.

He secretly breathed a sigh of relief.

Compared to regular members, so-called “advisors” were undoubtedly non-staff members of an organization.

From this perspective, Bu Quan hadn’t fully joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps yet.

“But this ‘accompanying the group’…”

The more Zhong Bei pondered this phrase, the deeper his brows furrowed.

This phrase was also crucial.

“If Magician Bu Quan travels with the mercenary group, then what’s the difference between that and fully joining?”

Zhong Bei pondered this, feeling gloomy.

As he continued reading, he saw her written salary.

His face immediately betrayed astonishment, and he muttered in disbelief, "What does this mean? Magician Bu Quan's annual salary is only one gold coin?"

A surge of anger welled up in Zhong Bei's heart.

He looked up at the dragonman youngster, his gaze filled with accusation: "Magician Bu Quan is a prodigy favored by the Alchemy Guild! Yet the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only offers a yearly salary of one gold coin. Isn't this taking advantage of her? Is her gratitude toward the commander being exploited in this way? How can you exploit her value so ruthlessly? Where is your conscience?"

The dragonman youngster remained calm: "Magician Zhong Bei, you’ve misunderstood. We’ve never had any thoughts or actions of exploiting her gratitude."

"In fact, I greatly admire Magician Bu Quan. She is a true magician, possessing a pure magician’s heart, where her curiosity and pursuit of knowledge far surpass worldly wealth."

“Please look further.”

“This is the true compensation we are paying.”

“This remuneration differs from conventional salary.”

“In this contract, the annual salary of 1 gold coin is merely symbolic.”

Mage Zhong Bei shifted his gaze and continued reading.

The paper stated: The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will provide Bu Quan with valuable alchemy knowledge.

The knowledge includes alchemy experiment data, blueprints for alchemy tools, formulas for alchemy potions, and various alchemy techniques.

This alchemy knowledge must be precious and satisfactory to Bu Quan.

If Magician Bu Quan is dissatisfied with the alchemy knowledge at the time of payment, she may request a replacement.

At the end of each year when the annual salary is paid, both parties have the right to revise the contract and negotiate amicably. This includes, but is not limited to, termination of employment, etc.

In addition, Magician Bu Quan has the right to unilaterally terminate the contract at any time. However, when exercising this right, Bu Quan must pay half the value of the salary previously received from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Compensation will only be accepted in the form of alchemy knowledge.

The specific value of the alchemy knowledge will be determined and appraised by professionals.

Upon reading this, Magician Zhong Bei's brows finally relaxed.

Clearly, the contract between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Bu Quan was drafted with considerable flexibility.

This significantly reduced his aversion and suspicion toward the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Even he had to acknowledge that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps genuinely sought to recruit Bu Quan.

Bu Quan's pursuit of alchemy knowledge over money also aligned well with the values of magicians.

“Spreading this contract would actually enhance Bu Quan's reputation.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has handled this quite well, starting with the intention of parting ways amicably!”

“According to the contract, Bu Quan can leave the mercenary group at any time.”

"She only needs to pay half the value of the alchemy knowledge. This is something the Alchemy Guild can fully accommodate. If she doesn't wish to remain in the mercenary group, she can wait until the end of the year, when she receives her annual salary, to negotiate. She can also terminate the contract at any time. In such a case, she would need to compensate for certain losses. The Alchemy Guild can fully assist her in redeeming herself and even use this opportunity to owe her a favor."

Magician Zhong Bei hadn't yet realized the severity of the issue.

In his view, how much alchemy knowledge could the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps possibly provide?

Even if they had arms dealers backing them, how could they compare to the Alchemy Guild of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom?

“There's another option...”

"Since they have to pay annually for alchemy knowledge that satisfies Magician Bu Quan, we can teach Bu Quan many alchemy techniques in advance, making it harder for her to be satisfied."

"Over time, her gratitude toward the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will fade, and she will gradually come to terms with reality. How much can the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps offer to satisfy her?"

“But this situation doesn't help me at all.”

“I have to nullify this contract, or else I'll be found guilty of neglecting my duty!”

Upon seeing this, Magician Zhong Bei gained a deeper understanding of the true situation.

“So, to resolve this matter, the key lies with Magician Bu Quan.”

"Regarding her, I don't really have any good ideas, so I can only try to persuade her with kind words. If that doesn’t work, I’ll have to go all out and expend my resources to make her change her mind.”

Neglecting his duties would have a significant impact on Magician Zhong Bei's assessment.

Zhong Bei glanced at the dragonman youngster again and thought to himself, “I need to negotiate with Bu Quan, but I can’t give up on Long Fu either.”

He forced a smile onto his face.

Before barging in, he never would have imagined that he would smile at the dragonman youngster.

But there was no choice; at this moment, he had to beg them.

Zhong Bei pleaded with the dragonman youngster to lend a hand.

If the dragonman youngster helped him persuade Bu Quan to change her mind, Zhong Bei personally promised to reward him generously.

The dragonman youngster burst into laughter, shook his head immediately, and rejected the request.

“No matter how generous the reward, it couldn't compare to Magician Bu Quan herself.”

Magician Zhong Bei furrowed his brows and said threateningly, “Commander Long Fu, please consider this carefully.”

“You must understand one thing: no matter how complex your background may be, your group's strength pales in comparison to our nation's Alchemy Guild.”

“Magician Bu Quan is so grateful to you; are you really going to take advantage of her?”

"Why not think about it from her perspective? She may be a genius, but she hasn't truly grown yet."

“And the environment suitable for her growth is not your group, but the Alchemy Guild, which has vast knowledge, numerous mentors, and many alchemy predecessors.”

“Forcing her to stay here will only hinder her future.”

“Over time, Magician Bu Quan will come to realize this and may even request to leave, or worse, develop hostility toward you.”

"Rather than risk that, it would be better to let her go voluntarily. In the future, you might gain the friendship of a great alchemist!"

Magician Zhong Bei had a way with words, appealing to the dragonman youngster with reason and emotion.

The dragonman youngster still shook his head.

"Magician Zhong Bei, I must emphasize once more: we are not forcing her to stay, nor are we deceiving her. In fact, we couldn't force or deceive someone like her anyway."

“It's entirely her own strong desire to join us.”

“For such talent, we can't let go just because of external pressure.”

"Put yourself in my shoes. If you were the leader of a mercenary group, would you give in so easily? Would you back down?"

"If I did that, the reputation of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be ruined. If we have to abandon a talent who actively wants to join us just because of a small threat, how will others view us?"

“Any organization that wants to grow must absorb fresh blood from the outside.”

“Please understand our predicament.”

As soon as these words were spoken, Magician Zhong Bei's expression turned extremely dark.

The dragonman youngster softened his tone and continued:

“However, if Magician Zhong Bei can successfully persuade Bu Quan, we will immediately let go.”

“If this matter spreads, parting ways amicably after meeting would be more beneficial for our mercenary group’s reputation.”

“Magician Zhong Bei, you have already seen the true contents of the contract. When we signed the contract initially, we had already anticipated this kind of scenario."

The dragonman youngster spoke so reasonably that Magician Zhong Bei couldn't help but nod in agreement.

The contents of the contract were easy to verify.

Magician Zhong Bei was highly likely to believe it was true and clearly sensed the dragonman youngster's sincerity.

Realizing he couldn't get the dragonman youngster's help, Magician Zhong Bei also left without hesitation.

He walked quickly.

As a guardian, he naturally knew Bu Quan's exact location.

“The most important thing now is to find Bu Quan and explain the consequences to her.”

“What she's doing is essentially destroying her own future!”

“No matter how grateful she is, it's not worth it to go this far. Sigh, she's still too young."

The dragonman youngster stood at the window, watching the magician leave, and used the alchemy communication device to contact Zi Di: “Has the alchemy data been handed over to Bu Quan?”

Zi Di's voice came through: "This is half of her annual salary paid in advance, and she accepted this arrangement. She is now completely immersed in it, devoting herself entirely to her research."

“Then it's settled,” the dragonman youngster said with a smile.

Moments later, Zhong Bei met with Bu Quan.

After learning the purpose of his visit, Bu Quan remained unmoved: “Magician Zhong Bei, joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was indeed something I voluntarily agreed to after being recruited by Commander Long Fu.”

“Thank you for your analysis and advice, but I made this decision after careful consideration.”

“In the short term, I will not leave the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“You're being foolish, Magician Bu Quan,” Zhong Bei exclaimed in frustration, "Was my previous analysis incorrect? No matter how you weigh the pros and cons, joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a mistake."

“This is too detrimental to your future development!”

But Bu Quan shook her head: “I don't see it that way.”

“If life is just about weighing pros and cons, then it's too boring.”

“In my past life, revenge has been my primary life goal.”

“Now that I have successfully avenged myself, it's time to try a new life.”

Zhong Bei continued to plead with her, but Bu Quan grew impatient and took the initiative to send him off: “It's already very late. Magician Zhong Bei, I won't entertain you any longer.”

Zhong Bei walked out of the alchemy workshop's gate, looking up at the afternoon sun still high in the sky, his face pale with disappointment.

After sending off Zhong Bei, Bu Quan plunged into the alchemy laboratory.

She looked at the alchemy documents brought by Zi Di, her eyes sparkling: “I never expected the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to have such advanced alchemy documents!”

She looked at the alchemy documents brought by Zi Di, her eyes sparkling: “I never expected the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to have such advanced alchemy documents!”

Chapter 571: Zi Di vs Bu Quan

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been operating the shop they acquired through a deal with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord for two days.

The shop was bustling with people, and the counter was crowded with customers.

“Give me two doses of dragon rider potion.”

"I want ten doses. Here's the money! Hurry up and give me the potions."

Those purchasing the potions were visibly agitated. Because anyone could see that the shelves behind the counter were already empty.

Estimating roughly, there were less than 30 dragon rider potions remaining.

If you were a step too late, you wouldn't be able to buy any.

This was only in the morning, or more accurately, not even the entire morning had passed.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ inventory was almost completely sold out.

The sales of dragon rider potions, after being validated by the market, had become extremely popular.

At that moment, two figures were sneaking around near the back door of the shop.

“Xiao Fan, I'm here.”

“Uncle, hurry up, come this way, don't let others notice us.”

Under the shop clerk's guidance, they entered the warehouse behind the shop.

There were still 50 doses of dragon rider potions in the warehouse.

The transaction was quickly completed, and the uncle took the 50 doses of dragon rider potion.

He was very pleased and patted the shop clerk Xiao Fan on the shoulder before leaving: "Good job. If any new stock arrives, let me know promptly."

The shop clerk was nervous and urged the uncle to leave quickly, comforting himself in his heart: "This is just normal sales; Uncle didn't shortchange me a single coin!"

The two who took the back door thought no one had noticed, but in fact, the entire transaction had been observed by Zi Di and Bu Quan.

In another room of the shop, Zi Di said to Bu Quan, “You saw it, right? That's how it really is; I didn't lie to you.”

“We need more dragon rider potions.”

“You need to put your research aside and focus on pharmaceutical production.”

Bu Quan didn't respond directly, but her tone betrayed a hint of surprise: “I never expected such a potion to be so popular.”

“I've also crafted many alchemy tools and sold them, but I've never encountered such a situation.”

"I already know the formula for the dragon rider potion, and it is indeed ingenious. But some of the alchemy items I’ve made are even more ingenious and of higher quality. Why haven’t they ever been this popular?”

“If I hadn’t seen it with my own eyes, I’d find it hard to believe that reality is really like this.”

Zi Di smiled and replied, “The reason is simple. The dragon rider potion fills a gap in the potion market.”

“Magic potions can’t be taken casually.”

“When a transcendent uses one potion, using another potion afterward comes with many restrictions.”

“In such situations, administering potions to mounts can resolve many issues in urgent battles.”

Bu Quan, upon being reminded, understood: "Thinking about it carefully, there truly are no potions suitable for mounts. Most potions on the market are designed for transcendents themselves."

“If I had known about this situation earlier, perhaps I would have also created and sold such potions.”

Which magician would complain about having too much money?

However, Magician Bu Quan had never paid much attention to making money.

Her life's focus was taking revenge on the Bayonet Gang. Beyond that, she pursued knowledge and explored further in the field of alchemy.

Basically, Bu Quan hadn't studied the market.

The alchemy tools she created were primarily for alchemy research. Selling them was merely to recoup funds for the next experiment.

"I didn't expect corruption to arise within the shop so soon after it opened. Such members cannot be tolerated; they must be dealt with." Bu Quan suggested again.

Zi Di shook her head, revealing a subtle smile.

“Just give him a good scolding; there's no need for a major crackdown.”

“In fact, this is exactly what we want to see.”

“What do you mean?” Bu Quan was puzzled.

Zi Di stared into her eyes and said, "We, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, are outsiders; we're not locals of Snow Bird Harbor. Therefore, we have hired a large number of locals to serve as peripheral members of our mercenary group. Almost all the people working in this shop are such peripheral members. We hope they can form a collusive relationship with customers, which will be more advantageous for our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to establish a foothold here.”

Bu Quan didn't understand: "Commander Long Fu publicly executed the gold level Teng Dong Lang, and the mercenary group has already gained fame throughout the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Doesn't that count as establishing a foothold?"

Zi Di sighed: "The reputation has indeed spread, but only within the mercenary group. From now on, we won't lack mercenary missions."

"But in terms of commercial operations, we've only just begun. What we need is to stay grounded and integrate with local powers. Only then can our business thrive in the long term."

Bu Quan grasped something and nodded in realization: “So, you didn't intervene to stop this private transaction because a faction capable of purchasing 50 doses of dragon rider potion must be quite powerful.”

“Such transactions, if continued over the long term, will also cultivate your network of connections.”

Zi Di immediately corrected her: “Not ‘your’ network, but our network.”

Bu Quan smiled awkwardly.

She had only recently joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and some of her thinking habits had not yet shifted; she needed time to adjust.

Bu Quan thought for a moment and added, “Since the dragon rider potion is in such high demand and supply is insufficient, perhaps we should raise the selling price of the potion.”

Zi Di shook her head again.

“The current price is already the most appropriate; there’s no need to adjust it further.”

“Any potion is a consumable item; the price cannot be too high.”

"Of course, if we raise the price, we can indeed ensure profits and prevent some of the current private transactions. However, in the long run, doing so would actually result in losses."

“How so?” Bu Quan asked for clarification.

Zi Di replied, “The strong sales of the dragon rider potion will undoubtedly inspire many powerful individuals.”

“I believe that soon, similar potions to the dragon rider potion will appear on the market.”

“In fact, our biggest competitor is not someone else, but the Alchemy Guild of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“The Alchemy Guild has long dominated the majority of the kingdom's alchemy market. Once this colossus sees the business opportunities in the dragon rider potion, they will definitely enter the market.”

“Once competitors emerge, we will have to lower prices, even sell at a loss.”

“The primary purpose of this is to capture the market.”

"If we capture the market and successfully secure the majority share in the mount potion sector, we will have established our reputation, truly built our own brand, and left a deep impression on customers throughout the Ice Sculpture Kingdom."

"Once we reach this stage, we will have a voice that cannot be ignored in the kingdom's business circles. When collaborating with others in the future, we will have the qualifications for equal dialogue."

“Therefore, the current period is extremely critical.”

“If we fail to seize this opportunity and secure the market, we will inevitably face bitter consequences in the future and encounter fierce competition. Countless others will see the opportunity and rush into this market, becoming our competitors."

"Let alone the great nobles, merchants, or renowned alchemists, even the Alchemy Guild. In terms of productivity, connections, and other aspects, the guild is far superior to us."

“Our current advantages are only twofold.”

"The first is that Commander Long Fu slew Teng Dong Lang. Riding this wave, we can seize the market faster. Because many people will feel curious and have a slight preference for the dragon rider potion sold by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps before their first purchase, making them more willing to try buying it."

"The second advantage is time. Our competitors need time to develop premium products, and they also need time to produce sufficient quantities to compete in the market."

“I've made a preliminary estimate that we need to secure at least 30% of the market share to successfully establish a foothold in the commercial landscape.”

“With 48% of the market share, it's like establishing a forward base camp.”

“Securing 65% of the market share is what truly establishes a moat.”

"The dragon rider potion has been very well received in the market, but over time, its popularity will decline sharply. Because the potion itself doesn't have much competitive advantage."

“We are currently using it as our flagship product to capture the market and establish our influence.”

“In the future, we will develop unique alchemy tools and leverage our influence to expand our market share and establish our stronghold in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom market.”

Zi Di explained her business plan to Bu Quan.

She continued, "So, you should understand that pharmaceuticals are crucial to us! This is the core focus of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Of course, if there are any shortcomings in this business plan, please do not hesitate to offer your guidance, Magician Bu Quan."

Zi Di smiled at Bu Quan.

When it came to alchemy talent, Zi Di acknowledged Bu Quan’s superiority. However, in terms of business, she was the expert, while Bu Quan was as inexperienced as a novice.

Bu Quan fell silent, her gaze toward Zi Di now carrying a new sense of appraisal.

She couldn’t help but think of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ background as arms dealers.

She realized for the first time that the female magician before her, Yao Ma, possessed business acumen beyond that of ordinary people.

Bu Quan couldn't help but exclaim, “No wonder your family sent you here to assist Commander Long Fu.”

“It seems like your collaboration is very harmonious. How long have you known each other?”

Bu Quan forced herself to sound casual, as if she were merely asking out of curiosity.

From the day the contract was signed, Bu Quan had observed the exchange of glances between Zi Di and Long Fu, keeping it in her mind.

She wasn't actually concerned about the dragon rider potion or Zi Di's business plans.

She was concerned about other matters.

A sharp gleam flashed briefly in Zi Di's eyes.

As the president of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, she had been exposed to such matters from a young age. Such probing from Bu Quan was obvious to her.

Zi Di paused for a moment before deciding to speak honestly.

“The time I’ve known Commander Long Fu hasn’t been very long.”

“At most, it’s only been two or three months.”

“But how to put it… we hit it off immediately and work together seamlessly. We've solved many problems together, and our relationship is indeed very close!"

As she spoke these words, Zi Di's lips curled slightly, revealing a happy, sweet smile.

If possible, Zi Di would have liked to announce her romantic relationship with the dragonman youngster directly.

This relationship was known to all the survivors.

But what could she do? Her current identity was Yao Ma.

She had to conceal it and couldn't tell Bu Quan directly.

Chapter 572: Conditioning Bu Quan Successfully

Bu Quan's feelings for the dragonman youngster were not only felt by the dragonman youngster himself, but also very clearly by Zi Di.

When the two parties signed the contract, Zi Di saw the look in Bu Quan's eyes as he looked at the dragonman youngster.

That intense gaze was all too familiar to Zi Di!

The flush on Bu Quan’s face also betrayed the secret in her heart

“So that’s how it is…” After hearing Zi Di’s response, Bu Quan’s expression darkened slightly.

The smile on Zi Di’s face made her feel repressed.

Zi Di returned to the topic at hand.

She had brought Bu Quan out of the alchemy workshop and to the shop for an inspection not to discuss their relationship, but for a more important purpose.

Zi Di continued directly, “Now, you've seen the situation at the shop.”

“I hope you can focus on producing dragon rider potions from now on, at least to meet the market demand in Snow Bird Harbor.”

“This is our top priority at the moment!”

Bu Quan's eyes flickered slightly.

Looking at the female magician closely associated with Commander Long Fu, she instinctively felt resistant.

But she didn't refuse outright. After a moment's thought, she came up with a reason.

"In the past two days, two more silver level alchemists have joined the mercenary group. Combined with Zhong Yong from before, we could easily delegate these pharmaceutical tasks to them."

But Zi Di shook her head: “I wish we could do that.”

“Unfortunately, they have more important tasks to attend to and cannot spare the time right now.”

Bu Quan slightly pursed her lips.

This subtle gesture caught Zi Di's attention, immediately revealing Bu Quan's displeasure.

Zi Di remained composed.

Bu Quan's reaction was exactly what Zi Di had anticipated.

Bu Quan was a master of alchemy, always approaching it as a researcher rather than a competent producer or merchant.

If Zi Di had Bu Quan's talent and the backing of the Alchemy Guild, her assets would long since have surpassed four alchemy workshops—they would have expanded tenfold or more!

But how to put it?

If Zi Di were Bu Quan, she wouldn't be able to achieve such high alchemy accomplishments either.

“Magician Yao Ma, please take a look at this first.” Bu Quan took out a document.

Zi Di's expression changed slightly upon seeing the document.

The document clearly contained information about the Bloodline Purification formation.

Bu Quan explained, "This is the result I deduced recently. How about it? Isn't it a significant improvement compared to before?"

“That's right. It's a huge improvement!" Zi Di's pupils contracted slightly. She realized that the result Bu Quan had calculated had left her speechless.

Even though she hadn't provided Bu Quan with any additional formation diagrams, he had still made such a breakthrough.

“Such alchemy talent...” Zi Di sighed inwardly, then casually remarked, "Have you been researching the half of your ‘annual salary’ we paid you in advance? Why are you investing so much effort and time into this? In fact, this was just a test question for us; we didn't have any expectations for it."

Bu Quan laughed, “How could it take any effort or time! I just had a sudden flash of insight during my lunch break, resolving many contradictions from previous calculations and figuring out some of the structural concepts of this formation.”

“I have a feeling I’ve finally figured out the direction!”

Zi Di forced herself to suppress her concerns: “How long will it take you at most to calculate this complete alchemy formation?”

Bu Quan shook her head: "It's very difficult. Although we've made a breakthrough, this kind of success is hard to replicate."

"We only have this one diagram. If we had a few more, that would be great. Even if it's just one, it has to be the one in the opposite position."

"Otherwise, even if I succeed in calculating it, the result is likely to be vastly different from the original answer. We've merely created a new formation based on the original."

“That reassures me,” Zi Di thought to herself, but on the surface she comforted her, "It doesn't matter. This formation diagram is just a small part of our adventurous gains; the prospects are slim, and it's not worth wasting time and effort on this."

But Bu Quan shook her head: "This formation has a simple structure and ingenious design; it's definitely the work of a master. The original version must have had significant effects."

“Enough,” Zi Di interrupted her, “Magician Bu Quan, I understand why you gave me this information.”

“Your talent is truly remarkable!”

"You want to prove that the work where you can truly showcase your value is this kind of work. Not to have you act as a human alchemy workshop, endlessly refining potions, is that correct?"

Bu Quan nodded, openly admitting, “Exactly.”

Zi Di sighed deeply, “That is precisely my concern about you.”

When she saw the look of confusion on Bu Quan's face, Zi Di continued, “Magician Bu Quan, we are very welcoming of your joining us.”

“But in fact, we also have our concerns.”

“We have gathered information about you. Although you joined the Alchemy Guild, you have only studied under your mentor and have never truly participated in any organizational activities.”

“You are actually a lone wolf.”

“Your mentor has never involved you in her organization but has focused solely on cultivating your alchemy talents.”

“Am I right?”

Bu Quan nodded.

Zi Di continued, “Precisely because you have long acted as a lone wolf, your thinking has become rigid. You tend to view issues and matters from your own perspective, prioritizing your own interests.”

“However, once you join an organization, this mindset must change. You must learn to consider problems from the collective perspective and resolve issues accordingly.”

“Often, when two ways of thinking clash and cannot be balanced, we must prioritize the collective interest.”

“That is what an individual who truly joins an organization should do.”

Bu Quan furrowed her brows.

Zi Di spoke earnestly, “I’m not asking you to sacrifice your personal interests and emotions for the collective.”

“In fact, we need to establish this positive feedback cycle.”

“Each of us sacrifices a little of our personal interests for the organization, in exchange for the realization of the organization's grand plan and the attainment of significant benefits.”

“These benefits are something we couldn't achieve on our own.”

“When divided among everyone, it might be less than what we'd get on our own, but more often than not, it's more.”

“That's the difference between joining a collective and living as a lone wolf.”

Bu Quan furrowed her brows deeply, pondered for a moment, then said, “Since I agreed to Commander Long Fu's recruitment, I naturally have the resolve to contribute to the collective.”

“Otherwise, I wouldn't have offered my four alchemy workshops.”

"But how can you guarantee that after the organization achieves its plans and goals, our overall personal benefits will be greater? What if the plan fails? Many organizations have collapsed before."

Zi Di wanted to say that giving up her alchemy workshops wasn't contributing to the collective; it's essentially acting on one's own emotions.

Zi Di was actually quite unimpressed with Bu Quan's approach.

If it were her, she would have given gold coins or alchemy tools at most, rather than giving away the alchemy workshop outright. From a business perspective, this was not selling eggs but the hen.

As a leader, Bu Quan had never considered the well-being of those working in the alchemy workshop. She lacked the responsibility of a leader.

Her gift was generous, but at its core, it was merely a personal act.

She did it to make herself feel better.

In comparison, the dragonman youngster was far superior to her.

The dragonman youngster was a believer in justice. Often, he prioritized the organization's interests over his own or suppressed his personal emotions.

Bu Quan couldn't do that—or more accurately, she couldn't do it at this stage.

“Although she has joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, can she truly integrate into it?” On the night after the contract was signed, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di discussed this, expressing such concerns.

Zi Di brought her here to inspect the shops this time, partly to increase the production of dragon rider potions, and partly to guide Bu Quan, helping her change from within so she could more smoothly integrate into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

In response to Bu Quan's questions, Zi Di answered seriously, “The situations you mentioned are objectively real.”

“Organizations can fail, and even collapse.”

“Organizational plans and actions carry risks. But aren't such risks also present when individuals act alone?”

“In fact, an organization's ability to handle risks is far greater than that of an individual.”

“Organizational actions are also far more stable and reliable. Because many factors affect individuals, such as sudden serious illness or traffic accidents, but for an organization, even when many unexpected events occur, the organization can still function normally."

“With lower risks and greater stability and reliability, collective gains become more stable.”

"An organization often has more resources than an individual. Resources that a lone wolf finds difficult to obtain can be more easily acquired by an organization, which can then concentrate its efforts and invest heavily in cultivating a particular member. Once this member makes significant progress, they can break through certain limitations of the organization's capabilities, driving the collective to earn more benefits."

“This is the win-win situation between the collective and the individual!”

After a pause, Zi Di continued, “Let's take the current situation as an example.”

“From your personal perspective, engaging in research work allows you to maximize your own value.”

“However, for the entire mercenary corps, producing a sufficient quantity of dragon rider potions in the short term to capture the market will yield far greater profits.”

“As you mentioned earlier, there is no guarantee that the formation can be reproduced, and it still requires significant investment of manpower and resources.”

"But once the mercenary group's plan is realized, we will reap the profits of the entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom. These profits will greatly fund your various alchemy experiments in the future."

“I think that even if you have a good mentor and are favored by the Alchemy Guild, you couldn't possibly experiment with various materials and conduct trials at will, could you?”

Bu Quan fell into a long silence.

Though she harbored some subtle reservations toward Zi Di, she couldn't deny that Zi Di's words made perfect sense.

“You've convinced me, Magician Yao Ma.” Bu Quan said, her gaze fixed on Zi Di.

"I've always been a loner. Even after I joined the Alchemy Guild, I didn't feel a sense of belonging to it. It’s more like a protective umbrella or a resource platform.”

“Even between me and my mentor, our daily interactions are primarily transactional. Even if I want to acquire alchemy knowledge, I need to make corresponding contributions and exchange them with my mentor.”

“Thank you. You’ve taught me a lesson.”

“Joining the mercenary corps has indeed given me a different life experience.”

“After I return, I’ll put my studies and research on hold and focus on producing dragon rider potions.”

Zi Di smiled upon hearing this: “Bu Quan, trust me, you made the right choice.”

Meanwhile, the ghost girl was secretly thrilled inside: “First successful conditioning!”

Chapter 573: Store Dispute

Inside the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' shop, an argument was taking place.

The dwarf with the big beard stood at the counter, straining to look up at the snow elf shop assistant, his eyes wide with anger, "What, you're out of stock?!"

The shop assistant hurriedly apologized, "It's true. Sir, you're too late. I can't deceive you. Look at the shelves in the store—they're all empty. Where would there be any dragon rider potions?"

But the dwarf with the big beard was different from the other customers.

He raised his hand: “This is impossible!”

"Even if you don't have stock for others, you must provide it to me!"

“I made a reservation with your store manager a day ago.”

“The pickup slip is right here, and the time is now.”

"Now that I've come, you tell me there's no stock?! Even if this is Commander Long Fu's business, I demand an explanation!"

The snow elf clerk's face paled slightly, and he hurriedly said, "Sir, you are indeed a special case. I’ll go get the store manager right away!”

Meanwhile, the store clerk who had just made the private deal turned ashen faced. He suddenly realized that the potions stored in the warehouse were meant for the dwarf with the big beard.

But now, he had already sold them all to his uncle in private.

With this commotion, his scheme was about to be exposed.

This was terrible!

The dwarf with the big beard didn't wait for the store manager but saw two female magicians walking into the store together.

It was Bu Quan and Zi Di.

Seeing the two female magicians, the dwarf with the big beard paused, suppressed his anger, and greeted them proactively: "Magician Yao Ma, Magician Bu Quan, hello. I didn't expect to see you two in the store."

“This is great!”

"I had already ordered the goods but was told they were out of stock. Not only did I make a wasted trip, but does the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps have no credibility in business?"

“Magician Bu Quan, we're old friends. You must help me with this matter.”

The dwarf with the big beard was a well-known figure in Snow Bird Harbor, owning numerous fishing boats and a large business empire.

Back when the dragonman youngster first began his challenges, Bu Quan, the dwarf with the big beard, and the human elder had all watched the dragonman youngster from afar at the dock. At the time, there had been some discussion.

In Snow Bird Harbor, Bu Quan indeed had a closer relationship with the dwarf with the big beard, and the human elder.

The three of them frequently collaborated on various matters.

At that moment, the shopkeeper approached.

The shopkeeper was a snow elf. He looked at the dwarf with a big beard and smiled, "Your goods are in the warehouse behind the shop. The regular staff are unaware of this matter. I will have someone fetch them for you immediately."

The shopkeeper was unaware that the magic potions specifically reserved in the warehouse had already been sold.

The store clerk who had been selling them privately felt as though he had fallen into an icy abyss.

But the next moment, Zi Di said, "There's no need to fetch them; the warehouse is empty. His order has already been purchased by someone else."

"What?!" The store manager was startled and looked at Zi Di, wanting to ask but dared not.

Zi Di could tell from his expression that he had misunderstood.

The ghost girl chuckled softly and turned her gaze toward the clerk.

The clerk, seeing Zi Di's gaze, broke out in a cold sweat and stepped forward to admit to the matter.

The store manager glared at him and scolded, "I told you to take the inventory, not to do such things! I thought you were a clever fellow, but I never expected you to be this foolish..."

“Quickly, tell me who you sold the potions to?”

“Go get it back immediately!”

“Those are all this gentleman's goods!”

“I sold it to my uncle,” the clerk finally revealed the truth.

The store manager asked again, “Who is your uncle?”

The clerk then explained his uncle's identity.

The store manager stood there speechless.

It was because the store clerk's uncle and the dwarf with the big beard were of similar status. If he wanted to curry favor with the dwarf with the big beard, he would have to offend another powerful figure in high places.

The snow elf store manager hesitated for a second or two, then turned around and approached Zi Di, bowing and scraping: “Magician Yao Ma, how should we handle this matter?”

Zi Di gave him a faint glance but did not speak.

The dwarf with a long beard looked at Zi Di.

He wanted to purchase an adequate supply of dragon rider potion. However, forcibly demanding it back would offend someone of his own status.

This was something he did not want to see.

However, he also did not want to compromise, so he could only continue to formally demand an explanation from the shop.

Inside the shop, there were still many dissatisfied buyers, who were watching the scene with curiosity, casting their attention toward Zi Di.

Zi Di smiled slightly, then turned her head to ask Bu Quan beside her, “Magician Bu Quan, what do you think we should do about this matter?”

Bu Quan looked deeply at Zi Di and understood her meaning.

Bu Quan thought for a moment and said to the dwarf with the big beard, “You ordered 50 portions of the dragon rider potion. You’re a fisherman—what do you need so many for?”

They were business partners and friends, so Bu Quan didn’t sugarcoat her words.

The dwarf with the big beard actually liked this bluntness.

It meant that their relationship was very close.

The dwarf with the big beard said, “Magician Bu Quan, are you looking down on me again?”

“Although I use a boat for fishing, after using this potion, I found that having the fishermen control the butter sailfish mounts to coordinate net casting resulted in a harvest more than twice as large as before.”

“Many times, we could even catch rare fish species.”

“If the dragon rider potion is supplied long-term, I'll definitely be your biggest customer here!”

Butter sailfish mounts were a common type of fish mount; fishermen often kept one or two at home.

These sailfish were large in size, had a stocky build, were easy to raise, and had decent speed.

The only drawback was that they leaked oil.

Once these sailfish engaged in prolonged movement, they would begin to seep fish oil. If not cleaned promptly, a thin layer of butter would accumulate, making it difficult for riders to stay seated.

The waters near the Ice Sculpture Kingdom were abundant in this type of butter sailfish, but the military never used them.

The reason for their rejection by the military was not the stability issue, but rather that the leaked butter, carried by seawater currents, left extremely obvious traces, making it easy for enemies to track the military's movements.

While solutions existed, the effort required did not justify the benefits.

Additionally, the military had better alternatives, so this option was not chosen.

After hearing the dwarf's response, Bu Quan paused for a moment: “Since the potion has already been sold, I cannot sell it to you.”

“However, I can personally intervene and expedite production. At the latest, I can provide you with 50 doses of dragon rider potion by 2 PM today.”

The dwarf with the big beard was taken aback by this answer.

He couldn't help but look at Bu Quan with a look of astonishment.

Prior to this, he had collaborated with Bu Quan many times, purchasing numerous alchemy tools from her.

He had never seen Bu Quan act this way before!

Even if there were delays in delivery, Bu Quan would only coldly state that it was due to her alchemy experiments and ask if the items on the order were still needed.

The dwarf with the big beard would say yes.

Bu Quan would then set a later delivery date and mention that such situations might occur again in the future.

The dwarf with the big beard would rub his nose but still placed the order.

After all, the alchemy tools produced by Bu Quan's workshop were of guaranteed quality

Bu Quan had always focused on her own research into alchemy and had never voluntarily paused her experiments to rush orders.

But at this moment, she did just that.

The dwarf with the big beard couldn't help but ask, “So, Magician Bu Quan, you've really joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

He had heard the rumors, but he just laughed them off.

Bu Quan nodded, "That's right.”

She admitted it in front of everyone, immediately drawing a slight gasp from those around her.

Everyone was very surprised that Bu Quan would make such a choice.

Bu Quan looked at the dwarf with the big beard and simply explained, “I want to try a new life.”

“Youth is wonderful,” the dwarf with the big beard sighed as a friend, offering his blessings, then remarked, “No matter how your new life turns out, but today, I truly saw a different side of you.”
