Zi Di finally spoke up, “Regarding this incident, it was a mistake on the part of our staff.”

“Hereby, I promise you that from now on, whenever you purchase our dragon rider potion, we will sell it to you at a 10% discount!”

Zi Di's compensation offer brought an unexpected delight to the dwarf with the big beard.

He purchased 50 units each time, so over time, the purchase volume would become enormous.

A 10% discount might seem small, but when accumulated, the savings would become quite substantial.

The compensation offered by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was very sincere, and the dwarf with the big beard was so delighted that he immediately flattered Zi Di with a compliment.

After resolving this minor dispute, Zi Di led Bu Quan, the store manager, and several staff members to the room behind the hall.

Moments later.

The staff member who had conducted the private transaction emerged with a grateful expression.

Zi Di only gave him a mild reprimand, making the staff member feel like he had narrowly escaped punishment, while the store manager and other staff members pondered the situation.

Bu Quan watched Zi Di reprimand her subordinates throughout the process and, seeing this approach, gained some insight.

The market influence of the dragon rider potion was rapidly helping the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps establish commercial relationships. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had deliberately arranged this store clerk here, leveraging his connections to establish a firm foothold in the shops, which was proceeding smoothly.

“However, even if I give it my all, the quantity of potions I can refine won't be enough for the entire Snow Bird Harbor,” Bu Quan said to Zi Di, "We'll need the assistance of the other three alchemists.”

Zi Di looked at Bu Quan, her smile growing brighter.

In resolving this dispute, she had Bu Quan publicly acknowledge her joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, confirming the rumors she had deliberately spread earlier. This further expanded the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ influence and instantly established the shop's credibility in the business world.

After all, Bu Quan had already joined. In the past, the alchemy tools produced by Bu Quan were renowned for their quality.

With Bu Quan stepping forward, the dwarf with the big beard had no choice but to back down out of consideration for their relationship.

Furthermore, his compromise was compensated by Zi Di. It was a 10% discount, but with such a major customer purchasing regularly, Zi Di might earn less but would definitely not lose out.

She handled the erring staff member leniently, which also served as a wake-up call for the other staff members, ensuring that there would be many high-quality connections and major buyers in the future.

Good products alone could not build a solid network.

Zi Di understood this well: one had to mix personal relationships into sales.

What pleased her most was Bu Quan's performance. She took the initiative to handle the dispute and even proposed the need for manpower. All of this proved that Bu Quan had truly changed her mind set and habits.

Wasn't this precisely the result of Zi Di's previous conditioning?

In all these aspects, Zi Di was undoubtedly the biggest winner.

After thinking it over, Zi Di responded, "Additional manpower will come, but the exact time is uncertain. If everything goes smoothly, perhaps they will arrive by tomorrow morning."

The corresponding parts of the formation have been constructed on the formation diagram panel.

Only the final combination remained.

This was the most critical step.

If this step was successful, the Bloodline Purification formation would be complete!

Chapter 574: Formation Completed Successfully

Deep Sea Monster Fish, alchemy cabin.

Fishman Zhong Yong looked serious, occasionally glancing at the blueprints for the Bloodline Purification formation and occasionally checking the formation diagram panels.

There were 11 formation diagram panels in total, each engraved with a part of the Bloodline Purification formation.

Now only the final step remained: assembling all the panels together.

And for this step, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di chose Zhong Yong to carry it out.

The main reason was: Zhong Yong was the strongest among the three silver level alchemists.

Though he was a fishman, he had decent talent, and most importantly, he had been nurtured from a young age.

From another perspective, alchemists who could be traded as bargaining chips were naturally the lowest-quality batch in the seller's hands.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had only been established for a short time and lacked various types of talent. The addition of these individuals brought significant improvements to the mercenary group.

In the eyes of the dragonman youngster, they were all highly sought after.

Zhong Yong meticulously compared the blueprints with the formation diagram panels, not missing a single detail.

His fingers slowly glided along each engraved line of the formation diagram.

His eyes were bloodshot, fully focused, exerting every ounce of effort.

The entire alchemy cabin was deathly quiet, except for the occasional low murmurs from the fishman magician.

After comparing them three times, Zhong Yong exhaled a breath of stale air, finally confirming the panels were correct.

“This alchemy formation actually enhances the bloodline concentration of living beings...” Zhong Yong was filled with shock.

As an alchemist with a complete formation diagram, he naturally understood the formation's function.

However, despite recognizing it, Zhong Yong remained deeply skeptical.

"Purifying the bloodline concentration within the body? That's too far-fetched."

"How is that possible?"

“But from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ perspective, they're so desperate—could it be that this formation actually works?”

“Or are they just rushing to verify it?”

The fishman magician Zhong Yong licked his dry lips.

He was also filled with curiosity and suspicion about this alchemy formation.

After resting for a moment and adjusting his state, Zhong Yong began the formal assembly work.

First was a large square base plate.

The alchemy formation lines on the base plate were the simplest. But at the same time, they were also the heaviest.

Placing the base plate at the bottom, Zhong Yong immediately activated the switch.

Immediately, a small stream of magic power was drawn from the magic power reservoir of the Deep Sea Monster Fish, gently flowing into the base plate.

The engraved lines on the base plate gradually lit up, emitting a faint white glow.

Zhong Yong worked quickly, not daring to delay, and immediately installed three auxiliary plates along the edges of the base plate.

The content on these three auxiliary plates complemented each other, with the formation patterns and magic insignias being much more intricate than those on the base plate.

Zhong Yong held the alchemy pen in one hand and swiftly connected the joints between the auxiliary plates and the base plate.

Once the connection was successful, the white glow from the base plate's formation patterns spread like flowing water onto the auxiliary plates.

With each successful connection, Zhong Yong's mental energy was depleted by a fraction.

By the time he had successfully connected all the necessary points between the three auxiliary panels and the base panel, his spirit had dropped below 50%.

Zhong Yong set the alchemy pen aside and pulled out a magic potion from his robe.

He had been prepared for this moment.

Without hesitation, he tilted his head back and swallowed the potion in one gulp.

His spirit power was immediately restored.

Zhong Yong began installing the main board.

The three sub-boards were arranged in a triangular formation at the three corners of the base board, while the main board was positioned directly above the center of the base board.

This step was extremely challenging.

The process of connecting the main board to the base board differed from previous steps.

Zhong Yong first commanded the alchemy puppet to hold the main board steadily, suspending it directly above the center of the base board.

He then slowly lowered the height.

When the distance between the base board and the main board was reduced to 0.5 centimeters, Zhong Yong finally halted the alchemy puppet's movement.

Zhong Yong placed both hands over the main board, channeling his spiritual energy while directing magic power from the magic power pool into it.

Once the magic power entered the main board, immediate changes occurred.

The alchemy points previously engraved within the main board transformed from solid to liquid form, rapidly spreading outward.

Following the force of gravity, these liquid metals extended toward the base plate, gradually stretching into extremely fine needle-like structures.

Below the main board, there were at least 3,000 metal needles densely packed together

After the metal needles made contact with the base plate, they continued to penetrate

Zhong Yong gathered his spirit, ensuring that every needle had penetrated before commanding the puppet to lower its height once more.

In the next moment, the main board adhered to the central part of the base plate.

Its edges and the three auxiliary plates at the edges of the base plate fit together seamlessly, forming a flat surface.

Zhong Yong slightly increased the input of magic power.

But he remained restrained.

The formation plate was not fully assembled, and the alchemy formation was incomplete. Excessive magic power lingering in the incomplete formation would damage the alchemy engravings, formation nodes, magic runes, and so on.

Zhong Yong proceeded cautiously, advancing the work bit by bit.

After about ten minutes, the main board not only successfully connected to the base plate but also established proper connections with the three surrounding auxiliary plates.

The main board, three sub-boards, and base board became a single unit.

Zhong Yong's face was pale, his gaze wavering, and his mental energy was severely depleted.

He couldn't stop, so he downed three more bottles of potions and continued connecting the sub-boards.

Connecting the sub-boards was much easier and simpler than installing the main board.

The workload was also not too heavy, so it didn't drain Zhong Yong as much.

However, it still took him half an hour to install all the sub-boards.

His condition had deteriorated significantly, so his efficiency was far lower than at the beginning.

Only one formation diagram board remained—the encapsulation board.

Zhong Yong pushed on and spent over ten minutes successfully installing the encapsulation board as well.

The next step was the tense moment—the first official activation of the formation!

Zhong Yong rested for half an hour before proceeding with this step.

He mentally activated the alchemy formation, fully activating it while increasing the output of magic power.

The engraved lines, runes, and patterns on the formation diagram were activated by a surge of magic power, emitting a brilliance several times brighter than before.

The formation diagram was tightly connected, with all previous connection points seamlessly channeling magic power without any failures.

Buzz...

The formation activated at full power, emitting a sound akin to the buzzing of insects.

This state persisted for ten minutes before Zhong Yong gradually reduced the magic power input and finally closed the formation.

“Success! I've successfully assembled it!”

Zhong Yong was overjoyed and suddenly relaxed, plopping down onto the floor.

He was in poor condition, his limbs weak and limp, his head buzzing, and his spirit at an all-time low.

But he was happy.

This was his first major mission since joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zhong Yong was well aware that his performance in this job would have a significant impact on his future life.

He could sense the high-ranking members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ emphasis on this magic formation and their sense of urgency.

If Zhong Yong failed this time, it would inevitably lead to the high-ranking members losing faith in him. He would not be given important tasks anymore for a long time.

“But fortunately, I succeeded in the end.”

“But is this magic formation really effective?”

Zhong Yong wanted to conduct an experiment immediately.

But he quickly suppressed that strong urge.

He sent a message to the higher-ups informing them of the successful formation construction.

Zi Di returned shortly afterward.

Not only her, but the dragonman youngster also came aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

“Commander Long Fu, Magician Yao Ma.” Zhong Yong performed a magician's greeting. Having been raised by snow elf nobles since childhood, he was well-versed in etiquette.

“Magician Zhong Yong, you did well. You didn't disappoint us,” the dragonman youngster encouraged him.

Zi Di was about to dismiss Zhong Yong from the alchemy cabin: “You may leave now.”

Upon hearing this, Zhong Yong grew anxious, gazing up at the dragonman youngster and Zi Di with eyes brimming with anticipation and longing.

"Let me stay! I'm so curious! Can this formation truly purify bloodlines?"

“If it succeeds, this would be extraordinary—a world-changing event!”

Zi Di smiled reassuringly, "Don't get too excited, Magician Zhong Yong. This alchemy formation diagram was an accidental discovery. We only built it as an experiment, without any expectations.”

“But regardless of whether the experiment succeeds or fails, this is the most critical secret of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“The fewer people who know about it, the better.”

Zhong Yong persisted, “I’ve already seen the complete formation diagram, and I personally assembled the formation panels. Let me take a look, even just a glance!”

He was too curious about whether the formation would succeed and what the specific experimental conditions would be like.

But in the end, he was still driven out.

Under the dragonman youngster's orders, he was escorted by an alchemy puppet through the corridor and sent to his crew cabin.

“Now we have another person who knows about it,” Zi Di sighed.

The dragonman youngster shrugged: "There's no way around it. We need someone to assemble the entire formation diagram. Leaks of confidential information are unavoidable, at least for now."

Zi Di's gaze grew deep: “In that case, let Zhong Yong become the ship's magician aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.”

“As for the other two alchemists, they can be allowed limited movement outside.”

“They don't know much; they've only engraved a few formation diagram panels.”

“The confidentiality agreements we signed with them can largely prevent this information from leaking out.”

“Basically, we've eliminated the possibility of Bu Quan coming into contact with these formation diagrams.”

“We can't let Bu Quan know about this; her alchemy talent is truly astonishing!” Zi Di sighed.

The dragonman youngster watched the humming alchemy formation spinning on its own, growing impatient. He urged, “Alright, let’s proceed with the first experiment now.”

He approached the edge of the alchemy formation diagram and, following Zi Di’s instructions, extracted the Bloodcore's magic.

The blood-red energy flowed into the formation diagram panels.

With the correct energy, the alchemy formation suddenly erupted with blood-red brilliance, forming a three-dimensional formation centered on a seven-pointed star.

The formation wasn't large, occupying only one-quarter of the alchemy chamber's space.

Zi Di placed the pre-prepared Experiment Subject 1 into the formation.

As the Bloodcore's magic power continued to diminish, Experiment Subject 1 grew restless within the formation.

It took less than 30 seconds. The bloodline purification was complete.

Zi Di, filled with excitement and anticipation, immediately tested the bloodline of Experiment Subject 1.

"The bloodline concentration has indeed increased significantly. We've succeeded!" Zi Di tried to stay calm, but her voice trembled noticeably.

The dragonman youngster empathized but remained cautious: “Continue the experiment.”

One experiment was clearly insufficient.

After conducting it seven times, Zi Di and the dragonman youngster finally confirmed that the magic formation was functioning normally.

“Next, it's my turn.” Zi Di retrieved an ice coffin.

Sealed within the ice coffin was her own body.

“Begin.” The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, his heart tightening once more.

Moments later, Zi Di looked at the bloodline test results in front of her and burst into tears of joy.

Chapter 575: Purifying Lan Zao

The results of Zi Di's bloodline test had already been obtained.

Gunpowder Flower bloodline.

Now, after the effect of the Bloodline Purification formation, the bloodline within her body had been purified into a gold level Gunpowder Flower bloodline!

It was that easy.

No wonder it was an alchemy formation personally designed by the War Merchant.

Zi Di's eyes were blurred with tears as she wept with joy.

In her life, she only had an iron level bloodline. Since stepping into the transcendent world, she had been tasting the bitterness of insufficient talent.

This pain continued until the moment of her death.

No, to be more accurate, even now, she was still constrained by her own innate talent. However, her existence has changed.

Essentially, she was an undead being with unknown innate talent, making it difficult to detect using potions.

Her physical body, however, had been clearly identified as having a Gunpowder Flower bloodline, which was successfully purified just now.

"Once you are revived, you will at least become a gold level magician. Congratulations, Zi Di."

The dragonman youngster embraced Zi Di.

This was a rare moment.

Normally, Zi Di was a ghost girl who could only pass through objects.

But just now, to purify her bloodline, Zi Di first used the Soul Send spell to return to her own body.

Only at this moment did she have the right to embrace the dragonman youngster.

“Lord...” Zi Di nestled against the dragonman youngster's broad chest, softly weeping.

At this moment, her emotions were extremely complex.

There was resentment and sorrow over the unfairness of fate. If she had possessed such talent earlier, what would her situation be like now?

There was endless gratitude and attachment toward the dragonman youngster. The dragonman youngster could have easily concealed the existence of the Bloodcore, but he did not! It was precisely because of the dragonman youngster's honesty that Zi Di achieved her current status.

There was also anticipation for future happiness. Gold level!

Zi Di's future achievements would at least make her the ruler of a city. Once she matured, she could become someone above others. The limitations on her natural talent that had once bound her had been raised to a new height, giving her a vast expanse of room for growth.

The dragonman youngster gently stroked Zi Di's shoulder. He had guessed some of her inner thoughts and comforted her, “Actually, you are lucky.”

“Imagine if you had been born with a gold level Gunpowder Flower bloodline. What would have happened?”

“Would the great nobles of the empire have allowed you to grow up and take over the Wisteria Merchant Alliance?”

“Clearly, that would have been impossible.”

"It was precisely because of your previous weakness, with only an iron level bloodline, that those villains and murderers let their guard down and didn't act against you. Isn't that right?"

“Lord, you're correct.” Zi Di's gaze grew deep, “Once we successfully atone for our sins, establish ourselves, and I grow stronger, I will definitely make those murderers face the punishment they deserve.”

Her talent had surged, giving Zi Di an even stronger desire for revenge.

The dragonman youngster embraced the girl while gazing at the Bloodline Purification formation: “I can’t wait. If only all our companions could use this formation once, that would be wonderful.”

“Unfortunately, the Bloodcore is still missing some corresponding bloodlines. Some of our companions haven’t received accurate bloodline answers yet.”

Zi Di took over the conversation: "We also need to consider the timing. To be honest, I don't recommend revealing the Bloodline Purification formation to others."

“This alchemy formation is too important!”

“Look at Zhong Yong's attitude earlier.”

“Even in uncertainty, he was already so eager and curious. If he were to find out that the Bloodline Purification formation is highly effective and can enhance a life form's bloodline, what would he do?"

“Such a major secret should be known by as few people as possible!”

“Currently, only the three of us know about the Bloodcore's secrets, and that's already more than enough.”

“Cang Xu traveling outside poses a risk of exposing the secret.”

“And these alchemists like Zhong Yong have all come into contact with parts of the Bloodline Purification formation, with Zhong Yong even completing the final assembly. Every day these people are alive, there is a risk of the Bloodline Purification formation being exposed…”

The dragonman youngster interrupted Zi Di’s words: “Zi Di, these people have already joined us!”

The dragonman youngster spoke earnestly: "I know you're worried, and so am I. But we can't abandon our principles because of worry."

“We're not evil; we're just doing what we have to do to survive.”

“They all have bound contracts, the highest level we can currently make.”

“They don't know the full truth, and even Zhong Yong is only speculating and curious.”

“There's also the matter of the secrets surrounding the Bloodcore and the Bloodline Purification formation. There's always been a risk of leaks, hasn't there?”

Zi Di nodded in understanding: “Lord, are you referring to Jian Zuan?”

Jian Zuan was a student of the War Merchant, but he vanished without a trace on Mystifying Monster Island, leaving no body behind.

The dragonman youngster nodded slightly: “It's not just Jian Zuan. What if the War Merchant deliberately left something behind, or if the Empire has already uncovered part of the truth?”

“If we only focus on keeping the secrets, but the truth leaks out in other areas, then we're actually tying our own hands and slowing down our development, aren't we?”

"Rather than going to great lengths to keep secrets, I would rather grow quickly and become stronger. Even if the whole world knows we possess such secrets, they will fear our strength and dare not provoke us lightly."

“Moreover, I trust Cang Xu!”

"Though he is a necromancer, everything that happened on Mystifying Monster Island was not fabricated. I know his character and integrity."

Zi Di bowed her head: "Lord, I understand your meaning. However, we can make some reservations."

“For example, when others use this Bloodline Purification formation in the future, we don't need to disclose this secret.”

"We can simply say that we have developed a potion that improves one's potential when consumed. The side effect of the potion is that it causes them to fall into a coma for a period of time.”

“In fact, we can use the potion to knock them unconscious, then transport them here for bloodline purification.”

“This way, we can keep the secret while still helping them.”

“There are too many divine spells and magic in this world. Like you, I am not concerned about the rebellion of my companions. However, there are many ways to steal this information against their will. For example, memory crystals."

The dragonman youngster fell silent.

Zi Di continued to persuade him, “Of course, we are not going to keep it hidden forever.”

“When the time is right, we can tell them the truth about the Bloodcore.”

“But now, the time is not right.”

“In fact, we are keeping it hidden to protect the others.”

"For example, if others fall into the hands of the enemy and are controlled by their spells, they might reveal the ‘truth’. The enemy will covet the potion that enhances one's potential, and not to kill you for the Bloodcore. This will give us a lot of room to maneuver. Isn't that right?"

The dragonman youngster pondered for a moment, then sighed, “Zi Di, I must admit, you have a point.”

“A benevolent temporary lie could be advantageous for our survival.”

“Let's proceed with that plan.”

“When the truth is eventually revealed, I will apologize to the others.”

The second candidate for bloodline purification was none other than Lan Zao.

The dragonman youngster summoned Lan Zao to the captain's cabin and informed him that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could now produce a magic potion that could enhance his bloodline qualities.

After annihilating the Bayonet Gang, the dragonman youngster had a meeting with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been actively procuring supplies from outside sources.

Thus, the dragonman youngster not only absorbed a large amount of gunpowder flowers but also obtained a complete Fish Bladder bloodline in his Bloodcore.

“Lord, are you joking with me?” Lan Zao heard this and widened his eyes; his face filled with disbelief.

He admired and respected the dragonman youngster, but when it came to a potion that could improve bloodline qualities, he still found it hard to believe.

This was simply too shocking to his worldview, which had been formed over many years!

“Drink this potion and let it tell you for yourself.” The dragonman youngster smiled, took out a potion, and handed it to Lan Zao.

Lan Zao trusted the dragonman youngster completely and drank it without hesitation.

In less than a second, the bottle was empty.

The potion took effect quickly, and Lan Zao's eyes rolled back, and he fell into a deep sleep.

The dragonman youngster then teamed up with Zi Di to secretly bring him back to the Deep Sea Monster Fish ship for bloodline purification.

When Lan Zao slowly came to, two hours had already passed.

“How do you feel?” the dragonman youngster asked.

Lan Zao’s vision was still blurry, but upon hearing the dragonman youngster’s question, he hurriedly replied, “I feel… normal.”

He spoke the truth.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “You can try cultivating your battle qi.”

“Right here?” Lan Zao's vision cleared completely. He realized he was still in the captain's cabin and assumed he hadn't moved since taking the medicine.

“Right here. Just practice a little,” the dragonman youngster said.

Lan Zao cultivated the Water Grass battle qi technique. He had been practicing this all along and had never changed it.

He tried practicing, but stopped right away, his face full of surprise.

“It's... it's unbelievable!”

“My, my current cultivation efficiency is probably more than three times what it was before.”

At that moment, Lan Zao was so excited that he stammered and became tongue-tied.

The dragonman youngster patted his shoulder: “Your bloodline is likely the Fish Bladder bloodline. Practicing the Water Grass battle qi technique isn't suitable for you. Switch to a more compatible one, and you'll extract more battle qi.”

“The Fish Bladder bloodline is a silver level bloodline. You're no longer just a bronze level sailor.”

Lan Zao's expression froze. He had received so much information at once that he couldn't process it all.

The dragonman youngster waited patiently.

Lan Zao's expression gradually became livelier, his eyes reddening, almost shedding tears.

He looked down at his hands: “Is this true?”

“This is too incredible.”

“Can I really become silver level?”

He couldn't help but recall his past.

Once upon a time, after learning of the limits of his bloodline, he tossed and turned, unable to sleep.

How many times had he looked at iron level and silver level transcendents, his gaze shifting from envy and longing to sorrow and despair?

“This is how my life will be.”

“For the rest of my life, I will only be a bronze level battle qi cultivator.”

“Though I am a transcendent, I am only of the lower ranks, the lowest tier among them.”

"Why is this so! Damn it, why wasn't I born a genius?"

“Damn it, even if you gave me just a little more talent, even if you let me reach iron level, that would be enough!”

“Never mind, never mind, this is just my fate.”

“Even if I'm bronze level, it's still better than being an ordinary person, right?”

Over time, Lan Zao's mindset continued to change.

From unwillingness to envy and jealousy, from envy and jealousy to accepting reality, finding solace in those weaker than himself, adjusting his emotions and mindset.

Now.

Lan Zao looked at the dragonman youngster and suddenly burst into tears: “Lord!”

“You gave me a new life!”

He knelt down directly and bowed his head to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster sighed, helped him up, and instructed, “Don't make a scene; this is our secret.”

“Understood!” Lan Zao nodded firmly, then suddenly his expression changed, and he spat out a mouthful of blood.

Immediately after, his eyes rolled back, and he fainted.

“What’s going on?!” The dragonman youngster’s expression changed abruptly.

It was clear that Lan Zao’s sudden coughing up of blood and fainting were caused by the purification of his bloodline.

But why did this happen?

The dragonman youngster hurriedly contacted Zi Di.

Chapter 576: Passive Meditation

Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Meditation cabin.

A stunningly beautiful young woman with black hair and fair skin sat with her eyes closed, meditating.

A large amount of fire elements actively poured into the magic pool deep within her head, quickly transforming into her personal magic.

Moments later, the girl opened her eyes.

Her features were exquisite, and even with her eyes closed, she already surpassed the ordinary. Now, as she opened her eyes, her pair of purple eyes shone like crystal, instantly adding the most stunning touch to her face!

The young woman was Zi Di.

A look of intense joy flashed in her eyes, and she quickly assessed the situation in her mind.

“The gold level Gunpowder Flower bloodline is truly extraordinary!”

"The range within which I can absorb surrounding elements has expanded by at least five times compared to before my death. The absorption speed has increased by six times, approaching seven times."

“The efficiency of converting elements into personal magic power has also increased by at least 80%.”

In her previous life, Zi Di had only an iron level bloodline, and in terms of the range, speed, and conversion rate of element absorption, she was far inferior to her current state.

Comparing the two, Zi Di felt a mix of emotions.

“No wonder the bloodline is the foundation of all cultivation!”

“If I had such cultivation efficiency, I would already be silver level.”

If Zi Di were still alive, she would still be young. Even with a gold level bloodline, considering the time spent cultivating, reaching silver level would be quite possible. However, there was still a significant gap to reach the gold level.

“The assistance of bloodline is not limited to meditation.”

“The Gunpowder Flower bloodline has advantages in alchemy and is most skilled at refining gunpowder.”

“The War Merchant's database records many gunpowder formulas; I can choose to refine some and experience it thoroughly.”

As she was thinking, Zi Di's upper body swayed slightly, and she felt a wave of dizziness.

The dizziness caused her face to darken, and the joyful expression quickly faded into a shadow.

She touched her forehead.

Burning hot.

“My body still needs time to fully adapt to the gold level bloodline.”

“But the most critical issue is that I am already dead.”

“My soul and body are mutually repelling each other. Even if I can control my body, it can only last for a short time.”

“Beyond that timeframe, the repulsive force will simultaneously damage both the body and the soul.”

Zi Di's current situation was quite complex.

She had died and became an undead being, a ghost girl.

She appeared to be alive now, but this was an illusion created by her casting the Soul Send spell to re-enter her own body with her ghostly form.

The ghost body was filled with negative energy. Meanwhile, her physical body, due to proper preservation measures, still retained a significant amount of vitality.

Negative energy destroyed vitality.

At the same time, when she meditated, she absorbed external fire elements and converted them into her own fire-based magic power.

This magic power also conflicted with the negative energy, triggering backlash.

During meditation, Zi Di had to expend a great deal of energy to monitor both energies and minimize the scale of the conflict.

Even when she exerted all her strength, the conflict between the two was still unavoidable.

For ordinary people, meditation allowed them to feel their strength growing and their magic power pool filling up, which was a fulfilling and pleasant sensation.

However, when Zi Di meditated, she had to be cautious, maintain stability, and endure the backlash from both energies, with both her physical body and soul enduring different kinds of pain.

This situation had significant drawbacks, but it also had advantages.

Currently, Zi Di possessed two magic pools.

One resided within her physical body from before her death, which was a fire-element magic pool.

The other was the undead magic pool within her ghost body.

Zi Di was an iron level fire magician in life, and after her death, she transformed into a ghost, with her life essence remaining at the iron level. She then re-trained as a necromancer, starting from zero.

However, thanks to the soul crystals and other resources obtained by Cang Xu, her current necromancer level is transitioning from bronze level to iron level.

Zi Di's eyes flickered as she planned her development path given the current situation.

"Typically, resurrecting life requires the power of the gods, that is, through divine resurrection. But we are still seeking connections and atoning for our sins. Additionally, I have become an undead being rather than a soul, so no clergy would be willing to perform divine resurrection for me."

"There is no opportunity for resurrection, but I currently need strength to rely on. Since I have abundant necromancer heritage, I will continue my cultivation for now. With strength, I can protect myself and assist the Lord."

"However, I cannot neglect the physical aspect. I can use the Soul Send spell to control the body, employing the previous meditation method to help the body absorb magic power and gradually elevate the magician's level."

Zi Di's ghostly bloodline paled in comparison to the Gunpowder Flower bloodline. This was evident from the efficiency of meditation alone.

In Zi Di's heart, resurrection had always been the top priority.

Even if her innate talent were lower after resurrection, she would still choose this path.

Only then could she be with the dragonman youngster. Now, the real her couldn't even embrace her beloved.

“Wait.” A sudden insight flashed through Zi Di's mind, giving her a better idea.

“This method of assisting the body in meditation is too inefficient.”

“Not only is it inefficient, but over time and with repeated use, it will also cause damage to both the body and the soul.”

“I can let the body cultivate on its own.”

“I remember seeing a lot of related materials in the War Merchant's database.”

Zi Di thought of this and immediately dissolved the state of body and soul unity.

After settling the body, she found the tower spirit and searched for the information.

Soon, she found what she was looking for.

War Merchant conducted in-depth research in this area and achieved significant alchemy results.

This Alchemy Great Grandmaster had once dedicated himself to the development of artificial magic beasts, including a project on how to enable artificial magic beasts to practice cultivation autonomously.

However, since magic beasts had limited intelligence, the War Merchant devised numerous alternative approaches to address this issue.

“This approach seems quite suitable for me,” Zi Di thought to herself after reviewing it.

This alchemy approach involved creating a suitable external environment by constructing a series of alchemy components to actively infuse external elements into the body of transcendent.

Applied to Zi Di, this meant allowing her body to cultivate passively without the need for active meditation.

“The most critical alchemy device is the magic power needle.”

“By inserting these needles into various parts of the body, the magic power needles channel elements into the body.”

“Then, through the engraved alchemy formation, the meditation technique is solidified, awakening the magic pool to absorb internal elements and complete the transformation from elements to personal magic power.”

“Over time, this will steadily elevate the body's magician level!”

After reviewing the information several times, Zi Di marveled at the War Merchant's profound expertise.

She also consulted reference materials related to this topic.

These included Eastern martial artists' acupuncture techniques, blueprints for vampire coffins, and comprehensive guides on implementing meditation within alchemy formations, among others.

“I am unable to craft these alchemy devices myself.”

"But now we have three silver level alchemists joining us, along with an alchemy prodigy like Bu Quan. Crafting them should pose no problem!"

Just then, the communication device beeped.

Zi Di answered, and the voice of the dragonman youngster sounded anxious on the other end.

“Lan Zao vomited blood?” Zi Di was slightly startled, then reassured the dragonman youngster, “Don't worry, this should be normal.”

“The purpose of the bloodline purification formation is to purify the bloodline of a life form, making it unique and reaching maximum concentration.”

“100%”

“The bloodline within Lan Zao should now be 100% Fish Bladder bloodline.”

"However, before this, his body contained not only Fish Bladder bloodline but also other mixed bloodlines. The Fish Bladder bloodline has a higher quality and a certain concentration, so it was detected as his primary bloodline."

“Now that the Fish Bladder bloodline has become his sole bloodline and reached maximum concentration, it is actively transforming his body.”

"Over the next period, his body will exhibit some symptoms, but this is actually the bloodline undergoing transformation. His original body will be transformed from his previous mixed bloodlines. The current bloodline and body are incompatible, so there must be an adaptation process."

The dragonman youngster immediately asked, “Will there be any life-threatening risks during this adaptation process?”

“No, there won't be,” Zi Di laughed and replied with certainty.

“Lord, if there is a bloodline conflict, it could lead to the collapse of the life form.”

"But Lan Zao is no longer a mixed-blooded individual; he is now pure-blooded. His body contains only the Fish Bladder bloodline, so there will be no conflicts."

“Of course, during the adaptation process, he may feel discomfort or even experience symptoms of severe illness.”

“No treatment is needed; just eat well and drink well.”

“I see.” The dragonman youngster relaxed.

Zi Di added, “In fact, my body also has fever symptoms.”

“But my symptoms are much milder than Lan Zao's.”

“The adaptation process after bloodline purification varies from person to person.”

"In my life, I was iron level, surpassing Lan Zao's bronze level status, and my bloodline was much simpler than his. Furthermore, my physical vitality was far inferior to his, which is why the symptoms of my physical transformation were milder."

“Once the physical transformation is complete, both Lan Zao and I should exhibit some bloodline characteristics.”

“Therefore, we may need to perform the Deceptive Disguise spell once more.”

The dragonman youngster felt reassured by such a detailed explanation.

He patiently waited in the captain's cabin, hoping to witness Lan Zao's second awakening.

However, before that, the city government palace sent a messenger to convey the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's invitation to the dragonman youngster.

“Should I go there immediately?” the dragonman youngster asked in surprise.

“Yes,” the messenger replied respectfully, "There has been a major breakthrough in the interrogation of the upper echelons of the Bayonet Gang. The city Lord has already gathered evidence of their involvement in your previous heroic duel, Lord Long Fu.”

"Oh? So that's how it is." A hint of joy flashed across the dragonman youngster's face.

He glanced at the unconscious Lan Zao. Since the latter's condition was already confirmed, he might as well head to the city government palace first.

He quickly issued orders, instructing Zi Di, the big guy, and others to temporarily care for Lan Zao, while he descended from the flagship, climbed into a carriage, and set off toward the city government palace.

Chapter 577: A Gang Is Needed

At the city government palace.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord led the dragonman youngster to the underground dungeon.

He introduced to the dragonman youngster: “This dungeon was built by the seventh-generation city Lord. Almost every subsequent city Lord contributed funds to expand it, and at the very least, they maintained it.”

“It can be said that this dungeon is the safest place.”

“I'm concerned that if we place the leaders of the Bayonet Gang in ordinary prisons, they might be rescued.”

“But here, it's almost impossible!”

“This way.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held a torch and led the way.

The dungeon was dimly lit, but there were no signs of mold or water stains from neglect; instead, it was very dry.

The brick walls were thick and sturdy, with massive iron gates every so often that required the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to operate manually to raise or lower them.

Along the walls of the corridors, there were hollowed-out sections where two-meter-tall alchemy puppets stood.

“The entire dungeon is shrouded in a dark, disorienting magic effect.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke in a somber tone, "Without the alchemy torch in my hand, entering here would leave us in complete darkness, even the sense of touch against the walls would be distorted. We would only perceive an endless darkness around us, unable to find our way out."

“Alright, we've arrived.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stopped before an iron door.

He reached out and gently touched the surface of the iron door.

A flash of flame-like light suddenly appeared on the otherwise ordinary iron door. The light quickly spread across the entire surface of the iron door, tracing out the patterns of an alchemy formation before fading away.

The iron door opened, revealing the prisoner inside.

The prisoner was devouring a feast of meat and fish, eating with such voracity that it seemed as though he were swallowing it whole.

Upon seeing the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the dragonman youngster arrive, the prisoner hurriedly stood up, then knelt on both knees and bowed his head in respect: “City Lord!”

The dragonman youngster looked closely and realized that this prisoner was none other than the demon hybrid ringleader of the Bayonet Gang.

“What's going on here?” the dragonman youngster asked aloud, but he already had the answer in his heart.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said triumphantly, “After I subjected him to severe torture, he couldn't take it anymore and surrendered.”

“That Evil Eye was originally his.”

“He has confessed without reservation that it was he who secretly intervened and manipulated the Evil Eye.”

“While you and Xiong Ju were fighting, he attempted to use the Evil Eye to kill you!”

“However, his plan was quickly thwarted by Xiong Ju himself.”

“And the leader of the Bayonet Gang, Teng Dong Lang, arrived shortly after to erase any traces of his involvement.”

The dragonman youngster was speechless for a moment.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's methods were ruthless; he had directly subdued the demon hybrid.

However, the dragonman youngster reconsidered and understood the demon hybrid ringleader's motives.

“The Bayonet Gang was once powerful, but at its core, it was merely a local gang.”

“The loyalty of its members to the Bayonet Gang was largely based on self-interest.”

"Among the six ringleaders of the Bayonet Gang, there were few snow elves, and the group was racially diverse. Each represents their own interests and those of their respective racial groups."

“The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord dealt a decisive blow to the Bayonet Gang, swiftly annihilating them. These individuals now have no hope left, making surrender a viable option.”

Having grasped these insights, the dragonman youngster smiled and flattered the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: “City Lord, you truly are a master strategist; everything is under your control.”

“Now that we have this eyewitness testimony, we should make it public as soon as possible to let the people know that eliminating the Bayonet Gang was the right thing to do.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nodded: "It's not just this crime; the Bayonet Gang has committed countless crimes over the years. In addition to the confessions of the demon hybrid ringleader, we also made significant gains from the other ringleaders."

“We'll discuss these gains later. Let's deal with this guy first.”

“He interfered with your heroic duel. According to the customs of our Ice Sculpture Kingdom, he is now your common enemy, both yours and Xiong Ju's.”

"Therefore, I must deal with him. I would like to hear your thoughts on the matter. After all, our cooperation has been very pleasant, and this relationship will only grow stronger in the future!"

Upon hearing this, the demon ringleader hurriedly crawled on his knees to the feet of the dragonman youngster.

“Lord Long Fu, spare me! Spare this dog’s life!”

“I was wrong. I failed to recognize your greatness and dared to plot against you in secret!”

“But I failed. After Xiong Ju destroyed the Evil Eye, I suffered backlash and was severely injured.”

“I will never dare to oppose you again!”

“Please, Lord Long Fu, please spare me.”

“I am willing to pay any price to redeem myself.”

The demon ringleader pleaded loudly, tears streaming down his face.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord remained silent.

The dragonman youngster looked at the demon ringleader begging for mercy at his feet, then at the silent Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, and suddenly understood the city Lord’s intentions in inviting him here.

The dragonman youngster turned to ask the city Lord, “City Lord, what do you think?”

The city Lord smiled, “In fact, sparing his life would benefit us all.”

“Although the Bayonet Gang has been eradicated, the aftermath is still troublesome."

"The Bayonet Gang was a criminal organization that had been entrenched for years. Although its core has been eradicated by us, it still has countless connections with the outside world. In the hearts of many citizens, the Bayonet Gang's prestige surpasses that of the city government palace."

“For example, the dwarfs under the dwarf ringleader.”

"In Snow Bird Harbor, there lives a group of dwarves. Their leader joined the Bayonet Gang and became one of the six major ringleaders."

"The dwarf ringleader participates in the Bayonet Gang's operations, contributing his own strength, and also mobilizes the dwarves to forge weapons and equipment for the Bayonet Gang. However, most of the dwarves have not joined the Bayonet Gang."

"Snow Bird Harbor is one of the eight major harbors of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, very prosperous, and an important window for the kingdom's foreign relations. Therefore, there is a large influx of people, and the population is diverse. Dwarves, humans, goblins, and others are active on the streets and alleys of Snow Bird Harbor, in every corner. They make a living here, and many are undocumented."

“The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's Mansion finds it difficult to regulate these people.”

“The city government palace represents the kingdom, represents the snow elves, not these foreign groups.”

"Thus, gangs like the Bayonet Gang emerge naturally. There is fertile ground for gangs to thrive here, rooted in the survival dynamics and logic of the lower classes."

"Do you believe it? The Bayonet Gang may be destroyed, but soon, new gangs will emerge here."

"If we do not intervene, these gangs will clash with one another, form alliances, and eventually establish another stable power structure. It might be called the Knife Gang, or it might be several gangs facing off against each other."

"In this process, there will be a great deal of bloodshed and casualties. The public order of Snow Bird Harbor will be severely disrupted, and the taxes collected by the city government palace will be greatly affected."

“As for me, I don't want my final days in office to be marred by such a poor record.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's words shook the dragonman youngster to his core.

He had always believed that organizations like the Bayonet Gang should not exist. They were criminals, a cancer, bringing disaster to those around them for their own gain.

But the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord offered a different perspective—while the Bayonet Gang was a criminal organization, its very existence maintained order in many ways.

From the perspective of managing Snow Bird Harbor, it serves as a supplementary layer to the city government palace.

In a sense, the city government palace could not function without the gangs. Gangs are like weeds—cut them down, and they'll sprout again in time. They cannot be eradicated.

The dragonman youngster fell silent for a moment before lifting his eyes to look at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

He chose his words carefully and said, “I understand your meaning, City Lord.”

“To be honest, I am not a snow elf, nor am I a citizen of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. My understanding and feelings toward heroic duels are far less profound than those of your countrymen.”

“I am willing to let go of this hatred.” The dragonman youngster paused and added, “As long as the benefits are reasonable.”

“Hahaha.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord patted the dragonman youngster on the shoulder, "I didn't misjudge you, Commander Long Fu. You are an excellent partner!”

“With his defection, I can quickly stabilize the situation, win over the hearts of the people, and control the emerging gangs.”

“I thank you on behalf of the people of Snow Bird Harbor!”

"As for the rewards, I have already prepared them for you. I am sure you will be satisfied. Please come this way!"

"As for the rewards, I have already prepared them for you. I am sure you will be satisfied. Please come this way!"

In his study, he retrieved a document and handed it to the dragonman youngster.

“There are some interesting things here; I believe you will find them intriguing.”

"Without your slaying of Teng Dong Lang, my annihilation of the Bayonet Gang would not have been so straightforward. These ringleaders would have been far more stubborn and resistant."

“These are all spoils of war.”

“You may select three items from them.”

The dragonman youngster examined them carefully and soon his eyes lit up.

He discovered Bing Xiao's giant cultivation method.

This was the complete cultivation method.

He also saw gold level weapons and equipment like the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

He also saw a large number of alchemy blueprints and methods for crafting weapons and armor.

“So, Bing Xiao confessed...”

“With so many blueprints and methods for crafting weapons and armor, it means the dwarf ringleader has also surrendered.”

“The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's interrogation techniques are quite effective; I didn't expect Bing Xiao to give in so quickly.”

The dragonman youngster analyzed the situation and secretly looked at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord with new respect.

However, he did not take the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's suggestion.

After everything he had been through, the dragonman youngster was no longer the same person as before. He immediately stated that there were many things he was interested in, and if the conditions were right, he could engage in trade.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord did not reject it. As the two were discussing, Lan Zao slowly opened his eyes.

“What… happened to me?”

His memories quickly returned, and he also realized that he was no longer in the captain’s cabin but in the forecastle.

The big guy was by his side, tending to him.

“You're awake. Dad let me take care of you,” the big guy said.

Lan Zao tried to stand up but nearly failed.

He touched his forehead, which was burning hot.

At the same time, his throat ached terribly; even swallowing saliva felt like a razor blade slicing through it.

“Where is the commander?” Lan Zao asked the big guy.

The big guy didn’t know the details, only saying, “Dad has left, and he’s in a hurry.”

Lan Zao’s face darkened: “It seems there’s been an accident!”

“A potion that can enhance one’s potential—how could it be so easily concocted?”

“Back then, when the Life God conducted the bloodline experiment, over a million people lost their lives, and only a hundred or so survived, with their bloodlines enhanced.”

“Even the gods were so strict; how could I possibly be so lucky to take a perfect potion and effortlessly enhance my talent?”

“Now that my bloodlines are in conflict, I’m probably going to die!”

Chapter 578: Lan Zao and Zi Di Suspect Each Other

Love Triangle.

Forecastle.

Zi Di manipulated the scout-type alchemy puppet, flying down in front of Lan Zao.

She was still on the Deep Sea Monster Fish Ship, observing Lan Zao while speaking through the alchemy device in a light tone: "You're awake? Looks like you're doing okay."

Lan Zao looked at the scout-type alchemy puppet in front of him, then glanced at the dazed giant beside him, shook his head, and said solemnly, “Actually, I feel terrible.”

He mistakenly believed he was close to death.

He continued, "The potion is destroying me; I can feel it. This potion can't be used!"

Zi Di was taken aback for a moment before asking why.

Lan Zao sighed wryly and explained his suspicions, revealing the experiment involving the bloodline of the former Life God.

After listening, Zi Di felt both annoyed and amused, with a touch of contempt and pity for Lan Zao.

"Don't worry, the magic potion is fine, " Zi Di reassured him again, but she couldn't say too much, as this was a classified matter.

She had previously advised the dragonman youngster that the fewer people who knew about it, the better.

Lan Zao was taken aback, then raised his voice and pointed at himself: "I took the potion; how could I not know? This feeling is terrible. Not only have I coughed up blood, but now I'm experiencing intense dizziness. Even if I faint again in the next moment, I wouldn’t be surprised.”

“Bloodlines aren’t something that can be easily altered.”

“Even the Life God is like this; how much more so for us?”

“Bloodline conflicts can be fatal.”

“What kind of potion is this?”

Lan Zao trusted the dragonman youngster implicitly.

But when it came to Zi Di, his trust was greatly diminished.

Lan Zao’s words carried a hint of accusation, leaving Zi Di in a brief silence.

She would not reveal the truth about the blood core to Lan Zao, so she said, “Don’t worry, Lan Zao. The potion is fine; all these symptoms are just your body adapting to it.”

“It will be better in a few days.”

“As for the potion, you know, anything that enhances bloodlines is highly classified.”

“I can only tell you that it all comes from the War Merchant's database.”

Lan Zao paused for a moment, then gave a silent, bitter smile.

The first thing he thought of was the War Merchant's artificial magic beasts.

These beasts were both powerful and fragile; without a magic-suppressing environment, they would destroy themselves.

Lan Zao pressed further, “What does ‘a few days’ mean?”

Zi Di replied, “The adaptation process varies from person to person; I can't give you an exact answer.”

Lan Zao asked again, "Am I the first one to use this medicine? Was the efficacy confirmed before it was used? Have the newly joined alchemists confirmed it?"

Zi Di fell silent again: “I said these are classified, and those people aren't trustworthy yet.”

Lan Zao found it hard to believe: "These alchemists have joined us and are bound by contracts. Even if we don't give them the formula, we should at least provide samples for them to test the efficacy and consequences of the drug."

"The others can be ignored, but Bu Quan is a genius alchemist. Shouldn't such an asset be utilized properly?"

The ghost girl couldn't help but take a deep breath.

Even though she didn't rely on breathing to survive.

Zi Di said, "I know what you want to ask, Lan Zao. The first person to use the potion wasn't you, but me."

“You can verify this with the commander.”

“The potion is fine; I don't want to repeat myself.”

"Additionally, I also have a fever, and my body is exhibiting similar symptoms. I said, all of this is just..."

Zi Di hadn't finished speaking when Lan Zao interrupted her: “But you're already dead!”

The conversation came to an abrupt halt, and the forecastle fell into silence.

The big guy looked at Lan Zao, his expression confused.

Lan Zao realized he had said the wrong thing and hurried to clarify: "That's not what I meant. What I meant was, you understand... well, why would such symptoms arise?"

“I have no intention of revealing the specifics to you, Lan Zao.” Zi Di's expression betrayed a hint of irritation, "I understand your perspective; perhaps you believe you are in mortal danger.”

“But that is not the case.”

“If you don't believe me, you can ask the commander.”

“You shouldn't doubt his words, right?”

Lan Zao nodded: “Without a doubt, I believe every word the captain says!”

“But...” he added in his heart, “what if the commander has also been deceived by you?”

Zi Di...

Lan Zao's feelings toward Zi Di were complex.

On one hand, he recognized her talent and understood her contributions. On the other hand, he maintained a respectful attitude toward her due to her relationship with the dragonman youngster.

However, the crux of the matter was that Zi Di deliberately orchestrated a maritime disaster to gain access to the Mystifying Monster Island, resulting in most of the passengers either perishing in the disaster or dying on the island.

Lan Zao couldn't help but think, "Since Zi Di once dragged everyone on the ship down for her own gain, would she do the same now?"

"She's already a ghost; she's already dead. And undead naturally hates the living."

“Has her nature not been corrupted by negative energy?”

“Did she really not hide anything from the commander?”

"Could it be that this potion has no side effects on the dead, but she hid it from the commander? She intends for everyone to die after taking it and be forced to become undead?"

Lan Zao harbored doubts, but he did not voice them.

Similarly, doubts also arose in Zi Di's heart.

Lan Zao’s attitude toward her at this moment was not as respectful as usual.

Zi Di understood him.

Because he felt his life was threatened, anyone under such pressure would behave differently from usual. Being a bit agitated was normal.

However…

This couldn’t help but remind Zi Di of Lan Zao’s past.

On the Mystifying Monster Island, Lan Zhao had killed his own younger brother to survive!

This had always been an obstacle in Zi Di's heart

When the dragonman youngster rescued him, Zi Di had directly advised him, “What is there to save about such a person?”

But the dragonman youngster had saved Lan Zhao's life, earning his loyalty and subordination.

In subsequent experiences, Zi Di also recognized Lan Zao's value.

Though Lan Zao was only a bronze level, his loyalty to the dragonman youngster bordered on worship, and this intense emotion could influence others.

During the early days of the mercenary group, Lan Zao, as a low-ranking ringleader, also helped the survivors manage the mercenary group.

But the emotional barrier in Zi Di's heart had not faded.

When Lan Zao subtly questioned Zi Di about his current situation, the caution Zi Di had harbored toward Lan Zao surfaced in his mind.

The ghost girl couldn't help but think, “Back then, when Lan Zao faced the pressure of death, he killed his younger brother to secure his own survival.”

“And now, under the mistaken belief that the magic potion he consumed posed a life-threatening danger, how would he react?”

“In ordinary times, his loyalty to the commander was evident, but now, under the assumption that he was in mortal danger, would he still maintain that loyalty?”

“Especially since the potion he had taken was personally handed to him by the commander!”

At this point, Zi Di no longer felt the need to explain and instead said, “I admit it, Lan Zao.”

“This is a potion from the War Merchant, and there are experimental data in the database confirming its safety.”

“But you know, when it comes to alchemy, once it involves specific circumstances, accidents are bound to happen.”

“I cannot guarantee absolute safety.”

“From this perspective, your concerns are reasonable.”

“What I want to tell you is that I was the first to take this potion.”

“I have one more question for you to ponder. If this potion were to end your life, what would you do?”

After saying this, she controlled the alchemy bird and left the forecastle.

The conversation ended, and Lan Zao sat motionless in his spot for a long time.

Zi Di observed his expression and state, feeling relieved but not overly concerned.

The reason was simple.

Once the “symptoms” faded after a few days, Lan Zao would naturally be overjoyed at the improvement in his abilities.

She didn't offer any guarantees.

On one hand, she had no proof. The magic potion formula mentioned as an excuse was something she simply couldn't produce.

On the other hand, she also wanted to observe Lan Zao to verify his loyalty to the dragonman youngster.

“It would be better for the commander to handle this personally and comfort him.”

“The facts that follow will make him realize his own foolishness.”

“Once he learns the truth, his loyalty to the commander will become even more unwavering.”

Chapter 579: Jailbreak

Night fell, and the stars twinkled in the sky.

In the city government palace.

Several servants carried a huge platter on their shoulders and entered banquet hall No.7.

The banquet hall was not large, and only two important figures were present.

They were the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the dragonman youngster.

The massive platter was placed at the center of the long dining table. The half-spherical lid was opened, revealing a delicious dish.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord introduced the dragonman youngster: “This is the sea forest lobster, made from gold level or higher magic beasts.”

“These lobsters live in the underwater forest. Their meat is not only delicious but also carries the scent of the forest.”

“For other races like humans to catch these lobsters, they must venture into the underwater forest. If discovered by the sea elf tribes there, they would be attacked en masse, posing a high risk.”

“However, our country, due to our racial ties, has connections with several large sea elf clans in the iceberg sea, so these sea forest lobsters have become an imported trade item for our country.”

The main population of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom were snow elves, while the Sea Forest was the home of the sea elves, who had specially created their own living environment on the seabed.

Though their habitats differ, both belonged to the larger elf race, so establishing diplomatic relations between them was far easier than with others.

In this world, racial barriers were everywhere.

Servants had already separated the lobster meat.

Placed before the dragonman youngster is a piece of translucent, pink lobster meat emanating a herbal fragrance. The shrimp meat was massive, resembling a basin. However, the basin-sized piece of shrimp meat accounted for only a small portion of the main course.

The dragonman youngster opened his mouth wide and bit down forcefully.

The tender, elastic, and delicious shrimp meat nearly filled his mouth.

As he chewed vigorously, his cheeks puffed out slightly on both sides.

The happiness brought by the delicious taste spread through the dragonman youngster's heart like a stream of clear water.

He quickly finished what was in his mouth, suppressed the urge to take another bite, and, following noble etiquette, was about to speak to express his gratitude and praise to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

At that moment, the ground suddenly shook.

In the banquet hall, the expressions of both men changed.

Immediately afterward, the ground shook again, along with the city government palace.

“What's going on?” The dragonman youngster stood up straight.

“It's an enemy attack!” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord looked grim, "They've actually made their way into the dungeon. How did they appear?"

He had previously assured the dragonman youngster of the dungeon's security, only to be proven wrong that very night.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord apologized to the dragonman youngster and stated his intention to go underground to eliminate the enemy.

Even in an emergency, he maintained his noble demeanor. His actions were swift, yet every movement exuded elegance and composure.

“The enemy's surprise attack on the dungeon must be to rescue the high-ranking members of the Bayonet Gang.”

“This is our common enemy so please allow me to contribute!”

The dragonman youngster did not hesitate and immediately volunteered.

The reason was simple.

At present, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Bayonet Gang are sworn enemies.

If these ringleaders were allowed to escape, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would inevitably become one of their targets for revenge in the future.

Of course, the dragonman youngster had no intention of taking the lead.

After all, this was the city government palace, and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was a gold level powerhouse. There was no need for him to charge to the front.

If the enemies were too strong, the dragonman youngster would wisely choose to retreat and avoid danger.

In the dungeon.

“Wake up, wake up!” Bing Xiao slapped Spring Boxer's cheeks hard.

Spring Boxer struggled to open his eyes, and his blurry vision gradually cleared.

He couldn't understand why Bing Xiao was suddenly in front of him. He tried to speak in astonishment but failed.

He had been tortured severely, his body covered in wounds, and his vocal cords had been cut, leaving him unable to speak.

“Good lad!” Bing Xiao looked approvingly at the dying Spring Boxer. From his injuries, it was clear that Spring Boxer had been fighting back and had not given in.

"But just to be sure, I still need to ask you a few questions. Kid, answer me quickly!"

Bing Xiao first administered a potion to Spring Boxer, healing his vocal cords.

Then, he exchanged the gang's secret code with him.

He also revealed several secrets known only to a select few high-ranking members.

After confirming Spring Boxer's true identity, Bing Xiao immediately had him carried out of the cell.

Once outside the cell, Spring Boxer heard the sounds of battle growing even more intense.

Outside the cell door, a towering figure stood guard.

Spring Boxer recognized him immediately—it was Xiong Ju.

“Hurry up! The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord has arrived, accompanied by Long Fu!” Xiong Ju spoke urgently.

After being captured, he had also been subjected to brutal torture. Although he had recently consumed some magic potions, his combat strength remained poor.

“Who is coming to rescue us?” Spring Boxer asked, peering over someone's shoulder.

Xiong Ju sneered, "Who else could rescue people like us? Naturally, it’s our gang members.”

“Ask the old gang leader for details; I’m hearing this for the first time too.”

Bing Xiao explained, “This is a secret power within our Bayonet Gang that only the successive gang leaders know about.”

“This group of people has been secretly maintained using the personal funds of the successive gang leaders.”

“It’s for preparing for unexpected situations. Their loyalty is beyond question!"

“Now that the Bayonet Gang has been destroyed and we've all been captured, we don't need to send a message—they'll definitely come to save us!”

Bing Xiao had also endured brutal interrogations in recent days.

To lull the enemy into a false sense of security and buy time, he had no choice but to reveal many of the Bayonet Gang's secrets, including his own methods for cultivating gold level giant slaves, which he was forced to disclose yesterday.

He had been waiting for the right moment, and now it had finally arrived.

“Let's go!” Bing Xiao led the way, walking at the front.

Xiong Ju and Spring Boxer followed closely behind.

Along the way, they met up with Ban Lan Gen.

Ban Lan Gen was even more pitiful than Spring Boxer, with his limbs severed, leaving only a tree trunk with a face.

Shortly after, the human ringleader also joined the group.

“Wait, what about the others?” Spring Boxer hurriedly reminded them.

Xiong Ju snorted coldly, “It's just the few of us!”

“Those two have already surrendered. Traitors will surely face punishment.”

Boom!

A loud explosion echoed, and flames surged in.

The intense heat engulfed them.

The gang fighters at the front were overwhelmed by the flames, suffering heavy casualties in an instant.

A charred battle qi cultivator rushed to Bing Xiao and the others: “Old gang leader, hurry up and leave! We can only hold on for another two minutes!”

Bing Xiao nodded but did not rush toward the entrance. Instead, he headed deeper into the dungeon.

Soon, they reached the end of a dark passageway.

From the outside, it appeared that the passageway had been abandoned due to neglect.

But the next moment, Bing Xiao's actions dispelled this misconception from Spring Boxer, Xiong Ju, and the others.

Bing Xiao approached the abandoned wall tiles, continuously striking the tiles scattered throughout the area, infusing his battle qi into each strike.

The wall tiles showed no initial reaction, but after Bing Xiao finished his dazzling series of strikes, the tiles at the end of the passageway began to retract inward before spreading outward on both sides, forming a new secret passage.

“Old gang leader, how did you know...”

Before Xiong Ju could finish his question, Bing Xiao sneered, “Back when this underground prison was being built, members of our Bayonet Gang were involved.”

This response left Spring Boxer and the others wide-eyed.

“The Bayonet Gang has been destroyed, but as long as we're here, there's still hope for rebuilding the gang.”

“We still have powerful external allies we can turn to for help.”

“As long as we leave Snow Bird Harbor, anything is possible.” Bing Xiao said encouragingly.

Everyone entered the secret passage, but several battle qi cultivators remained behind.

“Aren’t you coming?” Spring Boxer asked in confusion.

The battle qi cultivators shook their heads in unison: “Our mission is to rescue you and ensure your safe departure. Hurry up and go!"

“You!” Spring Boxer was stunned.

Everyone knew that those who stayed behind would face the Snow Bird Harbor Lord's furious wrath.

They would sacrifice themselves.

In fact, sacrifice was already a good outcome.

If captured alive, under those brutal tortures, they would suffer a fate worse than death.

A few minutes later.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stood before the secret passage.

Those who had blocked him were almost all dead, and those who had survived were only spared by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's deliberate decision to leave some alive.

"There's actually a secret passage here!" The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke with shock, “Ah, how deeply had the official authorities been infiltrated by the Bayonet Gang back then?"

“What should we do now?” the dragonman youngster asked.

He had also intervened several times during the previous battle.

Among the Bayonet Gang's elite fighters sent for rescue, there were many skilled battle qi cultivators, with both the leader and deputy leader being silver level, and their equipment was top-notch.

They had temporarily repelled the dragonman youngster's assault.

But without any gold level expert, who could withstand the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's might?

In the end, both the leader and deputy leader fell in battle at the hands of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord stared at the secret passage, his expression somber: “This batch of elite fighters from the Bayonet Gang infiltrated through this secret passage.”

“Since they control this secret passage, it must be filled with traps. Forcing our way through will inevitably result in casualties.”

The dragonman youngster furrowed his brows: “Are we to abandon the pursuit?”

“Of course not!” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord declared firmly, “We won't enter the secret passage, but we can use the magician tower to investigate and deduce its exit.”

“Previously, the secret passage remained undiscovered, but now that it's exposed, it will only take a little time for us to uncover their true intentions.”

“Additionally, we can surround and block their retreat.”

"I don't believe this secret passage can directly traverse the entire city of Snow Bird Harbor. A secret passage that has remained hidden for so long cannot be too large in scale."

“In that case, we only need to control the key points, impose a curfew on the entire city, and we can once again catch them in a trap.”

“Their combat strength has been greatly weakened, and it will be difficult for them to recover in the short term.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke confidently, strategizing with ease.

He immediately issued an order, mobilizing the entire city’s forces to once again launch a manhunt and pursuit of the escaped members of the Bayonet Gang.

The dragonman youngster’s expression changed slightly: “It seems I need to return to the dock to take command.”

Since the Bayonet Gang had successfully escaped, they certainly wouldn’t linger in Snow Bird Harbor.

If the dragonman youngster were in their shoes, he would leave the harbor immediately.

However, Snow Bird Harbor was surrounded by inland areas on three sides, with only one side facing the sea. Would escaping via the inland route be more convenient than taking the sea route?

The answer was obvious!

The likelihood of the Bayonet Gang fugitives escaping through the harbor and leaving Snow Bird Harbor was very high.

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ship was anchored at Snow Bird Harbor.

If this group were to spot their enemies and decide to take revenge along the way, that would be disastrous.

The dragonman youngster, with a strong sense of leadership responsibility, immediately bid farewell to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and rushed to the harbor.

On his way back, he activated the alchemy device to inform Zi Di and the others of the current situation.

Chapter 580: Rocket Assault Boat

Snow Bird Harbor.

Inside the sewer.

Bing Xiao and the others stumbled and panted, finally coming to a halt.

He tapped the sewer wall once more, revealing a small shipyard.

There were no torches in the shipyard, but cultivated glowing moss provided a dim blue-green light.

Spring Boxer could barely make out the shape of a ship hidden within the dock.

The ship's design was peculiar.

It resembled an arrow magnified many times over.

The bow resembled a bullet, the hull a long cylindrical body adorned with various totems, and the stern resembled a grass skirt covered in yellow-green thatch.

Bing Xiao introduced it to the others: "This is a rocket assault boat. As you can see, it is a beastman warship."

“Beastman?” Spring Boxer expressed his surprise, “What kind of alchemy technology could they possibly have?”

Bing Xiao quickly glanced at Xiong Ju.

Xiong Ju felt nothing.

Though he was a beastman, he was born and raised in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and lacked the typical beastman mindset.

Bing Xiao finally replied to Spring Boxer, "You're right; beastman alchemy isn't as advanced as ours. But they do have their own unique techniques."

"Like this ship you see here, it uses the totem painting technique of beastman shamans. The kingdom's alchemy techniques can't detect it."

"We have sufficient funds to prepare better ships. But magic ships using the kingdom's or common alchemy techniques are likely to be detected."

“We have hidden four or five rocket assault boats in the sewers of Snow Bird Harbor, but they have never been exposed.”

The human ringleader nodded in realization: “I see.”

He then asked, “Are we going to escape Snow Bird Harbor using these ships?”

Bing Xiao nodded: “That's correct.”

“Snow Bird Harbor is surrounded by land on three sides and only faces the sea on one side.”

“Traveling by land is too slow, and there are numerous checkpoints, making it difficult to evade pursuers.”

“But taking the sea route is different.”

“We can escape the Ice Sculpture Kingdom or, after leaving Snow Bird Harbor, take a detour and head toward other ports.”

Xiong Ju worried, "But the magician towers in Snow Bird Harbor must have already been activated. And the patrol fleets..."

Bing Xiao smiled slightly, “It seems you don't understand rocket assault boats.”

“These magic ships are disposable consumables, and their most distinctive feature is their straight-line speed, which is extremely swift!”

“The rocket assault boats we've prepared are all silver level, but their maximum speed can reach 35 knots.”

"35 knots?!" Everyone was shocked.

Silver level magic power ships typically had a normal speed of less than 25 knots. The rocket assault boat's speed was 10 knots faster, which was quite impressive.

Gold level magic power ships typically had speeds of 28 or 29 knots, with most below 30 knots. The underwater speed of the Deep Sea Monster Fish was only 32 knots.

A silver level rocket assault boat could reach 35 knots, surpassing even the Deep Sea Monster Fish. One could only imagine how fast it was!

Although Xiong Ju, Spring Boxer, and others were all gang members, they had lived in harbor cities for years and had a decent understanding of speed concepts.

When they heard the unimaginable speed of 35 knots, they were both surprised and delighted.

However, Bing Xiao poured cold water on their excitement: “Don't get too excited.”

“The rocket assault boat needs time to accelerate to reach its maximum speed.”

"Even when it reaches maximum speed, it can only maintain it for a short time. By the time the rocket assault boat sinks, we'll be out of the magician tower's attack range, but we won't have completely left the patrol fleet's patrol area."

Spring Boxer asked, “What do we do then?”

Bing Xiao stared at him: “The magic ship coming to pick us up has already reached the designated location.”

He then scanned the others: “The most dangerous part of the journey is the next segment.”

“Space is limited in the rocket assault boat. Spring Boxer and I will go together, the two of you will go together, and the remaining rocket assault boats will also be activated to serve as decoys.”

“The assault boats will emerge from the sewer and be exposed at the dock.”

“As soon as we surface, the magician tower's attacks will rain down on us.”

“Our assault routes will not be consistent, which will help split up the enemy forces.”

“This boat is for the two of you. Spring Boxer, come with me.”

Bing Xiao guided Xiong Ju and the human ringleader onto the rocket assault boat.
