The boarding method for this ship was also unique, with a beast totem opening its mouth to reveal the interior space.

Once the totem closed its mouth, the exterior of the assault boat remained seamless and without any gaps.

Xiong Ju and the human ringleader entered the narrow, cramped cabin, leaving barely any room to move.

Especially Xiong Ju, with his imposing build, was squeezed inside, forced to bend his head and hunch his back, feeling extremely uncomfortable.

The two were stunned. The human ringleader hurriedly asked, "Wait, old gang leader, where's the steering wheel? Without it, how can we maneuver the ship?"

Bing Xiao scoffed, “Even if I gave you the steering wheel, could you operate it?”

“Do you have any experience maneuvering a sea vessel?”

The human ringleader fell silent.

Bing Xiao continued, “Considering this, that's why we opted for rocket assault boats.”

“Once these boats launch, they're like arrows fired from a bow—they won't change direction; they'll charge straight ahead.”

“So, there are no components for steering the direction.”

“I understand,” Xiong Ju nodded.

Bing Xiao said, “When the time is right, I will activate all the assault boats, and then we will charge out together.”

After making arrangements for Xiong Ju and the Human ringleader, Bing Xiao led Spring Boxer as they jogged to another shipyard.

The two followed the previous method, activating the totem, and entered the rocket assault boat.

The interior space of this ship was similar to the previous one.

However, neither Bing Xiao nor Spring Boxer had Xiong Ju's build, so the space felt much more spacious.

Bing Xiao pulled out a crystal ball from his chest.

This crystal ball had been given to him by the suicide squad who had rescued them earlier.

Bing Xiao infused it with his battle qi and mentally manipulated the crystal ball.

The crystal ball quickly displayed the scene of the magician tower, then flickered, showing the dock, and flickered again, revealing the gang members in the dimly lit streets.

Spring Boxer was startled: “These people...”

His eyes turned red.

He recognized many familiar faces.

These were all veteran gang members, like Bing Xiao, who had retired.

There were also many die-hard gang members.

These people were about to cover their retreat, and their fate was predictable.

Bing Xiao didn't turn around, but he knew what Spring Boxer was thinking: “Many of them were betrayed by traitors and have been hiding in the city these past few days.”

“They'll follow us and escape together.”

“This is their best chance to escape.”

“So that's how it is!” Spring Boxer's eyes lit up, “But how will they get out?”

Bing Xiao laughed: “Our Bayonet Gang has ships, but most of the ones on the surface have probably been seized.”

“There are still some ships in secret that can leave the harbor.”

“The number of ships might not be enough.”

"But it doesn't matter. I believe that for this golden opportunity, they will resort to any means necessary—stealing or looting—even if it's just a fishing boat. As long as they can break out a short distance and be met by the magic ships arranged for them, they'll have escaped to safety!"

“Next, let's stir up chaos throughout Snow Bird Harbor.”

As soon as Bing Xiao finished speaking, the crystal ball erupted like fireworks, with a series of explosions unleashing waves of fire and heat.

Countless citizens screamed in terror, panicking and fleeing in all directions.

The ground trembled faintly.

The entire Snow Bird Harbor was plunged into chaos.

Bing Xiao laughed triumphantly, “This should keep the city Lord busy for a while.”

Spring Boxer stared in disbelief at the horrific scene in the crystal ball, a hint of reluctance in his eyes.

Bing Xiao said, “Don't worry, the explosion sites were carefully designed.”

“The main purpose was to create chaos while minimizing civilian casualties.”

“Especially among the lower classes.”

“Most of the bombs were planted in areas close to the city government palace, where the residents are either wealthy or noble, and their homes already have strong defensive capabilities.”

“Due to the tight schedule, to ensure secrecy, our bombs couldn't be placed at critical locations.”

“But when these people panic, they will inevitably seek help from the city Lord.”

“The city Lord cannot ignore these people; they are his political base.”

"In my estimation, the city Lord will likely dispatch the city guard to stabilize the situation, with the defensive focus on those wealthy districts. This way, we’ll face far fewer obstacles.”

Bing Xiao explained at length, and the image in the crystal ball shifted to the dock.

At the dock, the ships of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were bustling with activity as they hurriedly set up defenses.

The dragonman youngster had already sent a message back, and he himself was on his way back swiftly.

Zong Ge led most of the mercenaries out to sea for training and had not yet returned. Tripleblade naturally stayed close by, accompanied by the silver level gunner Chi Lai.

Fortunately, the dwarf Men Shi had stayed behind.

In recent days, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had recruited many dwarves from outside.

As one of the eight harbors, Snow Bird Harbor was home to a diverse formation of races, with dwarves being particularly numerous.

This was evident from the leadership of the Bayonet Gang: the dwarf population is so large that the gang had appointed a dwarf ringleader to serve as a bridge between the gang and the dwarf community.

As a dwarf, Men Shi was seen as a symbol by the dragonman youngster, playing an irreplaceable role in recruiting dwarves and soothing newly joined dwarves.

At this moment, Zi Di was still aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

However, the alchemy puppet impersonating her had already flown to the mast, overlooking the surroundings from above.

Men Shi boarded the flagship's deck, looked up at her, and hurriedly called out, “Magician Yao Ma, please come down immediately.”

In an emergency, the magicians had to hide themselves to prevent being ambushed by enemies.

But Zi Di, relying on her alchemy puppet form, shook her head and said, “Let me cast a reconnaissance spell first.”

As soon as she finished speaking, all the sewer outlets along the dock's shoreline erupted simultaneously, spewing thick, emerald-green oil.

The oil quickly covered the water surface near the dock, and one by one, elemental forms emerged from the oil, attacking randomly in all directions.

Zi Di's pupils contracted slightly: “This color... it's the swamp oil elemental!”

“Do not use firearms, and do not use rockets.”

“This swamp oil not only ignites easily but may also cause explosions!”

Chapter 581: Swamp Oil Amalgamation

“Hehehe, swamp oil elemental, even though it's artificial, how can you resist something on this scale?” In the sewer, Bing Xiao looked at the scene in the crystal ball and let out a smug cold laugh.

Spring Boxer was utterly astonished. He knew about the swamp oil elemental, but the swamp oil that had been released just now shrouded the entire dock and even spread toward the magician tower.

“There's so much swamp oil!” Spring Boxer muttered under his breath.

Bing Xiao snorted coldly: "Our former gang leader from two generations ago coincidentally rescued a priest of the Voodoo Sect. From then on, our gang became connected to the Voodoo Sect."

“Every year, we obtain some swamp oil elementals from the Voodoo Sect.”

“Over time, we accumulated this quantity.”

“However, after this battle, our stockpile is depleted.”

Bing Xiao sighed deeply.

The Bayonet Gang had been entrenched in Snow Bird Harbor for so many years, naturally accumulating a solid foundation.

Bing Xiao and the others were able to escape the dungeon thanks to this foundation.

The rocket assault boats, and swamp oil elementals were no exception.

However, now the Bayonet Gang's overt forces had been annihilated, and these foundations were being depleted one by one.

As the former gang leader, Bing Xiao felt a mix of emotions.

There was pride and honor, but also the sorrow and anger of seeing his own forces destroyed and the glory of the past fade away.

Large amounts of swamp oil quickly covered the stone bricks on the ground.

As the thick oil churned, small mushrooms began to emerge, then swelled rapidly within seconds, forming half-human shapes, and rushed toward the magician tower.

The magician tower was shrouded in a faint golden glow, immediately forming a massive light sphere that enveloped the area hundreds of meters around the tower.

The swamp oil elementals were blocked by the light barrier and could only temporarily attack it.

The magician tower immediately scattered hundreds of icy cold beams.

The beams froze each swamp oil elemental, and the ice layer quickly spread across the oil surface, freezing large areas of swamp oil.

But the effect did not last long. The faint golden glow of the magician tower suddenly dimmed, and it took a few seconds for it to gradually return to normal.

“What's going on?” Spring Boxer stared intently at the crystal ball, and the changes in the magician tower were naturally visible to him.

Bing Xiao smiled smugly, “We chose the location of the bombs carefully.”

“It seems we've damaged some of the magician tower's magic power supply lines.”

"You must understand that magician towers are massive consumers of magic power, with enormous energy requirements for their operations. Therefore, they are typically constructed atop elemental veins."

“However, the magician tower in Snow Bird Harbor lacks this condition.”

“There are no elemental veins beneath Snow Bird Harbor.”

“In such cases, aside from the magician tower itself constructing a magic pool, the only option is to manually build energy pipelines to supplement the tower's power supply.”

“As for the pipeline locations, those are top-secret.”

“However, having been in Snow Bird Harbor for so long, the Bayonet Gang has inevitably gathered some clues.” Based on these clues, we calculated that this explosion would hit the mark!"

“We blew up at least one of the energy pipelines.”

Spring Boxer wore a look of delight.

He hadn't expected Bing Xiao to employ such a grand scheme, nor had he anticipated that the situation would develop so favorably for them.

“What do you think? Besides restraining the magician tower, where else should we have the swamp oil elemental focus its attacks?” Bing Xiao asked Spring Boxer.

Spring Boxer looked at the constantly flickering scenes in the crystal ball, his expression complex.

After a few seconds, he suddenly spoke up: “The patrol fleet, and... the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Bing Xiao feigned surprise and asked, “Xiao Tan, aren't you quite impressed by Long Fu?”

Spring Boxer nodded, then shook his head.

“Commander Long Fu killed the gang leader, but I don't blame him.”

“It was a heroic duel. The gang leader died in battle—it's the honor of a fighter!”

“But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is indeed our enemy. When we flee Snow Bird Harbor, they will likely dispatch ships to intercept us.”

“Let's make sure they have no ships to sail—that would be best!”

Bing Xiao fell silent for a moment, then curled his lips into a sinister smile: "I'm different from you. I have no good impressions of Long Fu at all! The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will first taste the vengeful blade of our Bayonet Gang."

As soon as Bing Xiao issued the order, a large amount of swamp oil elementals immediately surged toward the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zi Di, whose true form was hidden within the Deep Sea Monster Fish, immediately noticed the situation and swiftly relayed the urgent report to Men Shi and the others through the alchemy puppet.

The dwarf Men Shi wore a solemn expression.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was currently in a weakened state, with neither the commander nor the deputy commander present.

“All hands, hold the line!”

“Even if we die, we will die on the front lines!”

The dwarf Men Shi roared, displaying unwavering resolve.

The mercenaries stood on the ship's rail, firing with all their might.

A large number of alchemy arrows and bullets struck the swamp oil elementals, temporarily halting their advance.

However, their numbers were too great. Even if they were instantly eliminated, a new swamp oil elemental would reappear at the spot where they fell.

This gave the mercenaries the feeling that “no matter how many we eliminate, the enemies are endless.”

This illusion was undoubtedly demoralizing.

“Both teams have been taken out for training by the deputy commander.”

"Ordinary arrows and swords cannot harm these elementals. We can't start a fire either! Perhaps starting a fire and burning down the dock is exactly what the enemy wants to see."

"What really works are frost-type alchemy arrows and bullets. But our stockpile is too small to sustain prolonged combat!"

Men Shi assessed the situation, feeling immense pressure.

“Sail away, leave the port!” 'Zi Di' beside Men Shi spoke up.

“It's too late.” Men Shi shook his head, “The swamp oil covering the sea surface will also slow our speed.”

"Have the other two ships come closer to act as shields. We have to abandon these two ships." Men Shi thought for a moment and issued the order decisively.

Since Zong Ge had taken most of the people, there were only three ships left from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps at the dock.

The other two were ordinary sea vessels, while the Love Triangle was the only iron level magic ship.

The original berthing positions of the two sea vessels were right next to the Love Triangle. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn't have time to sail away from the port, but at this distance, maneuvering them closer was still feasible.

Men Shi commanded from the center, dispatching mercenaries to use the two merchant ships on either side as temporary walls for defense.

The mercenaries primarily used overwhelming firepower to eliminate the swamp oil elementals.

However, as Men Shi had anticipated, the swamp oil elementals kept coming in endless numbers, and the alchemy arrows and bullets of the frost-type quickly ran out.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ alchemy arrows and bullets were almost entirely sourced from the War Merchant’s arsenal.

However, the survivors had already endured numerous battles while escaping from the Mystifying Monster Island, including clashes with Rou Zang, the Snake Rat Island Sea battle, the Two Eyes Island battle, and the Weeping Pine Island Sea battle, among others. Their once-abundant ammunition supplies had been depleted to a significant extent.

By now, although some reserves remained, they were far from sufficient.

Especially when facing the swamp oil elemental bodies, most of the ammunition was ineffective.

The ice-based attack effect ammunition was quickly depleted.

The swamp oil elementals boarded the two ships on either side.

The mercenaries engaged in close combat.

Men Shi charged to the front lines, wielding his two-handed war hammer, becoming the backbone of the front lines.

“Zi Di” used scrolls and magic items to cast spells continuously, barely stabilizing the other side.

The swamp oil elementals' offensive was once again contained.

But soon, they underwent a transformation.

"What is that?!" the mercenaries exclaimed.

Many swamp oil elementals gathered in one place, fusing together to form a super entity.

The super elemental stood over three meters tall, like a small giant, charging recklessly.

The big guy tried to break through but was promptly stopped by Zi Di.

The swamp oil elemental amalgamation collided violently with the sea ship, shattering the hull on impact and nearly splitting it in two.

The wreckage of the sea ship tilted and crashed directly into the flagship, the Love Triangle.

The Love Triangle also shook violently.

The unconscious Lan Zao was no longer in the forecastle but had been moved to his own crew cabin.

The violent shaking nearly caused him to fall from the bed onto the deck.

In the nightmare Lan Zao was trapped in, the volcano beneath his feet erupted, propelling his entire body into the air.

As he watched himself about to fall into the lava, he couldn't help but let out a terrified scream.

“I'm here to save you!” At the moment of crisis, a voice called out Lan Zao's name.

At the same time, a rope was thrown out, landing in front of Lan Zao.

Lan Zao reached out and grabbed the lifesaving rope, holding on tightly.

The other end of the rope was quickly pulled by the rescuer.

“I'm saved!” Lan Zao landed in the desert and looked up at the person who had rescued him.

It was Bai Ya.

“Bai Ya, thank you for saving me.”

Bai Ya extended his hand toward Lan Zao, his face shrouded in shadow as he stood with his back to the sun.

A dangerous alarm suddenly rang in Lan Zao's heart.

He looked up and was horrified to see that Bai Ya's face had turned into Huang Zao.

His younger brother glared at Lan Zao, reaching out to grab his hair: "Brother, why? Why didn't you let me live? Why did you make me die!?"

Huang Zao demanded.

Lan Zao opened his mouth to answer, but Huang Zao forcefully pressed down his head, filling his mouth with yellow sand.

The sand entered his mouth, as if alive, rushing into his throat, into his lungs, filling his chest and abdomen.

Lan Zao struggled desperately.

The one on the bed also had a hideous expression.

Inside the rocket assault boat.

Bing Xiao watched as the swamp oil elemental amalgamation tore apart the ordinary sea vessel and boarded the Love Triangle, unable to suppress a hearty laugh.

“Now!”

He acted decisively, activating the rocket charge.

The rear of the rocket assault boat was adorned with layers of intricately woven broad-leaf grass skirts.

In that instant, the grass leaves ignited.

Soon, the rear of the rocket assault boat also caught fire, erupting with intense heat and thrust.

Boom!

The rocket assault boat burst out of the hidden shipyard, following the sewer, charging forward.

Wherever it went, flames erupted, and sewage evaporated instantly.

One knot, two knots, three knots...

The speed continued to surge.

The intense recoil caused Spring Boxer to lose his balance and fall backward.

The vibrations of the assault boat also subjected him to severe jolts.

Bam!

The rocket assault boat shot out of the sewer exit on the shore like a cannonball, soaring at least two hundred meters through the air before landing on the sea.

The sea surface was covered in oil.

It ignited.

In an instant, the dock was engulfed in flames!

Chapter 582: God Did Not Respond

“Oh no!” The dragonman youngster looked up and saw flames shooting into the sky in the direction of the dock.

His heart sank, and he decisively activated his Bloodcore.

Spurting smoke dragon ring mounds appeared on his hind legs and back.

The Surge battle skill was unleashed, greatly increasing the dragonman youngster’s speed. He transformed into a crimson afterimage and shot toward the dock.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Zi Di watched the various magic images and felt her heart sink.

“Damn it.”

“How could such a thing happen at a time like this…”

The issue of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ shortage of alchemy ammunition had been discussed by the higher-ups.

In the end, they unanimously decided to prioritize the production of dragon rider potions to capture the market and generate substantial funds.

This matter was of great importance.

The higher-ups of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had not anticipated that the ringleaders of the Bayonet Gang, who were imprisoned in the dungeon, would manage to escape.

What they never imagined was that their escape would cause such a commotion!

The ghost girl was shocked.

“No wonder the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had been playing along with the Bayonet Gang for so many years, preparing thoroughly before taking action in the final days of his term.”

At the same time, the ghost girl felt relieved.

“Thankfully, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord struck swiftly, completely annihilating the entire Bayonet Gang.”

“If they had engaged in direct combat and the conflict dragged on, the entire Snow Bird Harbor might have been destroyed in the fighting between the two sides.”

Men Shi, stationed at the front lines, felt the heat wave engulfing him, his face bathed in flames.

Men Shi, stationed at the front lines, felt the heat wave engulfing him, his face bathed in flames.

However, such amalgamations quickly regenerated their heads.

As long as the magic crystals within their bodies were not struck, they could not be completely destroyed.

And within these collectives, the magic crystals were densely packed together. Men Shi was powerless, unsure of their exact location.

It could be in the head, the chest, or even hidden in the hind legs.

In fact, even if detected, if Men Shi missed the magic crystal, its location would quickly shift.

Flames licked at the ship's hull.

Both ordinary sea vessels had basic fire-resistant measures, but under the swamp oil's aid, they soon couldn't withstand the flames and burst into combustion.

The entire body of the swamp oil amalgamation with the elongated head also burst into flames. In the blink of an eye, it became a flame giant.

The flames were so intense that even the Men Shi couldn't approach.

The dwarf could only shout orders for the archers to fire the torrent arrows.

The torrential arrows slightly suppressed the flames.

Men Shi was filled with anxiety.

He knew that the supply of torrential arrows was also limited and would soon follow the same fate as the frost-type ammunition.

At that moment, the swamp oil amalgamation abandoned its attack, turned around, and slowly walked back into the fiery inferno behind it.

Surrounded by raging flames, the swamp oil amalgamation opened its jaws and took a deep breath.

It sucked large amounts of flames into its belly.

The flames burned fiercely inside its belly, releasing massive amounts of heat that couldn't be vented, causing the swamp oil amalgamation's belly to swell larger and larger.

After completing this step, the swamp oil amalgamation turned again and charged toward the Love Triangle.

Men Shi's expression changed drastically.

The swamp oil amalgamation, controlled by the enemy, showed no fear of death. The swamp oil amalgamation before him had become a massive bomb with its fuse lit! If it were to explode on the Love Triangle, the consequences would be unimaginable.

“Commander Long Fu, the debt of gratitude I owe your clan for saving us will be repaid in this battle!” A resolute light flickered in Men Shi's eyes.

Holding his double-handed war hammer, he channeled his battle qi to the limit, ready to charge alone toward the swamp oil elemental cluster without regard for his life.

'Zi Di' blocked him.

"Have you lost your mind?"

The ghost girl manipulated the alchemy puppet, pressing it onto Men Shi's shoulder.

“Let me go!” Men Shi strained his shoulders, only to discover in astonishment that the delicate human magician before him possessed incredible strength! He couldn't break free no matter how hard he tried.

“I must act to stop the explosion!” Men Shi cried urgently.

“Zi Di” shook her head: “You’re only a silver level, and your best weapon is this war hammer. Going there is just suicide.”

“Besides, it’s just an iron level magic ship…”

“We can always abandon the ship.”

Men Shi frowned: But there are still many people on board, many dwarves, and they won't have time to evacuate!"

Just then, several cannon blasts echoed.

The Love Triangle had fired.

Due to the close proximity, the alchemy cannonballs all struck the swamp oil amalgamation.

The cannonballs exploded, and lush vines and thorns began to grow.

Countless thick vines, resembling pythons, tightly wrapped around the swamp oil elemental amalgamation, rendering it unable to move.

Not only that, but the countless sharp thorns on the vines pierced into the swamp oil amalgamation's body.

The swamp oil amalgamation, having swallowed flames, was burning fiercely inside its belly, causing the heat to make it swell continuously.

This led to its body expanding constantly.

On the other hand, the originally thick swamp oil had spread out due to the expansion, becoming much thinner, like the surface of a fully inflated balloon.

After the thorns on the vines pierced through, a large amount of heat and flames erupted, shooting out five or six meters away.

The vines, being so close, naturally suffered from the burning, turning from deep green to withered yellow.

The raging flames spread along the vine, burning one section after another.

But the Love Triangle continued to fire.

These thorn-wrapped shells were purchased by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps at the auction on Two Eyes Island, and there were plenty in stock.

Originally intended for hunting sea beasts, they were popular among pirates.

Now used here, they proved highly effective.

Inside the rocket assault boat.

Bing Xiao observed this scene through the crystal ball and realized that the swamp oil amalgamation could no longer approach, so he decisively manipulated it to explode.

Boom!!!

A deafening explosion erupted as the swamp oil amalgamation detonated the aggregated magic crystals within its body.

Chaotic elemental energy expanded outward, intensifying the flames.

The distance was too close, and the three ships of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were all within the explosion's range.

The ordinary ship that had been previously rammed and broken apart was shattered into countless pieces by the explosion's shockwave.

Fortunately, the Love Triangle was an iron level magic ship and managed to withstand the explosion with great difficulty.

The side of the ship facing the explosion was melted and twisted, with many areas burned through, exposing the internal space.

Large flames were burning on the Love Triangle, and the sails that had been furled were already reduced to ashes.

Even the ordinary sea vessel behind the Love Triangle was engulfed in flames, turning into a giant torch.

However, there were few casualties among the mercenaries on the deck.

This was mainly because, at a critical moment, 'Zi Di' had used a protective spell scroll.

However, in areas where Zi Di could not protect them, Lan Zao was already engulfed in flames.

His personal cabin was located on the side of the ship that had exploded.

The explosion had shattered the hull, and flames had spread into his cabin, igniting his bedding and other furnishings.

The air was filled with the agonized screams and mercenaries crying for help.

The intense flames produced thick smoke, causing the unconscious Lan Zao to gradually struggle to breathe.

In his nightmare, his head was being pressed firmly into the gravel by Huang Zao.

He opened his mouth and nose, gasping for air, feeling the scorching hot gravel constantly pouring into his nostrils, filling his mouth, and forcing its way into his airways.

Huang Zao's maniacal screams echoed in his mind.

“You're going to die too, you're going to die too, hahaha.”

“Join me in death!”

"Let me kill you, let me kill you! Now you're going to die too, ahahaha."

Lan Zao tried to fight back, but his limbs were utterly weak, and he had no strength.

In the midst of overwhelming panic and fear, he instinctively began to pray.

“Fishnet Goddess, I pray to You, please save me...”

The Fishnet Goddess did not respond.

"Wave God, please bestow Your great mercy upon me. If you save my life, I will devote myself to you alone for the rest of my life!"

The Wave God did not respond.

“Forest Mother, grant me another chance at life. Please have mercy on me, please lend me your hand...”

The Forest Mother also did not respond.

Lan Zao was not a pious devotee; he only had shallow faith.

Such people often held faith in multiple deities. For example, praying to the Road God when going outside, praying to the Mother Goddess when giving birth, and thanking the Wealth God when making an important transaction. Numerous deities take responsibility for various aspects of one's life.

But no matter who Lan Zao prayed to, he got no response.

He became furious.

Anger burned in his heart as even Huang Zao started screaming with him: "Why are you not responding to me?!"

Chapter 583: You Are My…Salvation!

In the chaotic nightmare that Lan Zao experienced, he and Huang Zao simultaneously confronted the gods.

"Why won't you save me?!”

"Why?"

“Oh gods, how mighty you are—you could save my life without so much as lifting a finger.”

“Why won't you save me!?”

“I had faith in you.”

In a daze, Huang Zao's face transformed into Lan Zao.

Lan Zao pressed his other self's head fiercely into the scorching gravel.

Rage seethed within him, grinding his teeth until his eyes blazed crimson.

He clutched the other self's throat, suddenly yanking him out to demand: "Why won't you save me?! Why won't you save me?!”

Lan Zao was pulled from the sand but couldn't gasp for breath.

Already near death, he weakly replied: “Because...”

“I want to live too.”

These self-spoken words only fueled Lan Zao's fury.

He gripped his dying self tightly, screaming with all his might: "Yes, I want to live! Yet you won't save me! Why won't you save me?!"

“If you don't save me, I'll kill you!”

Lan Zao clutched his other self's throat fiercely, then plunged him back into the gravel.

“Save me! Save me!” He roared for help, yet in doing so, he was killing himself.

Meanwhile, the other Lan Zao had his eyes, nose, and mouth buried in the sand. Soon, he could no longer hold on. He pounded Lan Zao's arm with his hands, signaling for mercy.

Lan Zao was overjoyed and hastily pulled himself out, "You're finally willing to save me?”

But the Lan Zao he pulled out had changed form.

He had become the Fishnet Goddess.

Her face was expressionless, like a statue.

Lan Zao roared in fury, “You won't save me!”

He forcefully pressed the goddess into the sand. Soon, she began pounding his arm.

Lan Zao rejoiced, pulling her out: “Are you finally lending a hand?”

The Fishnet Goddess transformed into the Wave God, expressionless.

Lan Zao roared in fury: “You won't save me!”

He slammed the Wave God into the sand too, only to feel someone pat his shoulder again.

“Are you going to save me now?”

He pulled the Wave God up, only to see Him transform into the elf deity—Forest Mother.

Forest Mother wore a frozen smile, unresponsive to Lan Zao's desperate pleas.

Lan Zao pressed the Forest Mother into the sand again. When he pulled her up, she had become Huang Zao.

Huang Zao smiled at him: “Brother, you've already killed me. Do you wish to kill me a second time?”

Lan Zao's body convulsed violently. In his panic, he let go abruptly, and Huang Zao crashed into the sand.

The pebbles transformed into quicksand, slowly beginning to swallow Huang Zao.

Lan Zao screamed, “No, don't!”

He reached out to grab Huang Zao, desperate to stop his descent.

Simultaneously, he shouted explanations: “I didn't mean to kill you!”

“I truly didn't mean it!”

Wailing, he frantically dug, his fingers stained red with blood. Yet Huang Zao sank completely into the quicksand, vanishing without a trace.

Lan Zao completely broke down, covering his face with his hands and wailing uncontrollably.

Back to reality.

At the dock.

“Did we survive?” Dwarf Men Shi, covered in soot and dust, recovered from the blast and straightened up.

The right side of the Love Triangle's hull was already more than half burned away.

The crater in the sea caused by the explosion was rapidly filling back in.

Surrounding seawater surged in, creating a massive wave.

The wave crashed against the battered hull, and one surge poured directly into Lan Zao's cabin.

Most of the cabin's flames were extinguished, but the unconscious Lan Zao was struck squarely by the wave.

Then, carried by the receding tide, he tumbled through the gaping hole in the hull and plunged into the sea.

This sequence played out in his nightmare.

A pair of hands suddenly shot out from the quicksand, grabbing hold of Lan Zao's arms.

There was a violent tug.

Lan Zao's entire body was yanked into the quicksand.

It was dark. Endless, pitch-black darkness.

The crushing pressure surged toward him from the darkness, pressing and grinding in from all sides.

Lan Zao sensed his impending death.

Struggling desperately, he seized those hands and yanked with all his might, dragging the murderer who had pulled him into the quicksand before him.

Lan Zao stared intently and suddenly let out a startled cry.

This time, it was neither Huang Zao nor any other deity—it was the dragonman youngster.

“Lan Zao, are you going to kill me?” the dragonman youngster asked, his gaze steady and calm.

A faint smile played upon his lips.

Lan Zao's eyes widened, fixed intently upon the dragonman youngster.

His blood-stained hands, radiating the savage aura of murderous weapons, slowly reached toward the dragonman youngster's chest.

In reality, Lan Zao sank beneath the water.

He couldn't breathe. Vast quantities of seawater sealed his nostrils and surged desperately inward.

The suffocating sensation made the veins on Lan Zao's face in the nightmare bulge violently, making him look like a demon.

The demon's claws reached for the dragonman youngster's chest.

Yet the dragonman youngster seemed oblivious, his expression serene and gentle as he said, “I'm sorry, Lan Zao. It was I who gave you the magic potion.”

“Aaah—!” Lan Zao opened his mouth, letting out a roar of fury beyond measure, "You killed me!”

The dragonman youngster wore a look of pity, "Yes, I killed you.”

“Now, will you kill me?”

“Aaah—!” Lan Zao roared once more, seemingly intent on taking the dragonman youngster down with him.

Yet his blood-stained hands slowed with each inch closer to the dragonman youngster's chest.

His fingers, once splayed wide, gradually curled inward, losing all strength.

Thud.

With just one step left to reach the dragonman youngster, Lan Zao finally collapsed. His knees buckled, and he knelt at the dragonman youngster's feet.

His face was deathly pale, like a terminally ill man. He looked up at the dragonman youngster, tears streaming down his cheeks, leaving two deep trails.

His hands, curled like chicken claws, froze mid-air.

He cried out to the dragonman youngster, “Commander! My Lord!”

“How could I ever lay a hand on you?”

The dragonman youngster continued to watch him silently, "But Lan Zao, it was I who gave you the potion. After you took it, death came. I am the one who killed you."

Lan Zao collapsed onto the ground, still gazing up at the dragonman youngster.

Tears streaming down his face, he cried, “How could I ever harm you!?”

“You must have been deceived! It was Zi Di! It must be that ghost...”

But the next moment, the dragonman youngster cut him off: "But what if I told you I did it on purpose? You must understand—the fewer who know about Mystifying Monster Island, the better. The fewer who share in Mystifying Monster Island's riches, the sweeter the spoils."

Lan Zao wept like a broken man; his hands clutched tightly to his chest as he kept gazing up at the dragonman youngster: “Even so... even if that's the case...”

“I'd still willingly die!”

The dragonman youngster fell silent, gazing down at Lan Zao as if awaiting his explanation.

Lan Zao dug deep into his heart and spoke: “My Lord.”

“You saved me on Mystifying Monster Island.”

“You saved my life in the first place, so returning it now is only right.”

“I committed an unforgivable mistake!”

“You took me in; you told me I could keep going forward!”

“Being able to walk alongside you for a while has been my greatest honor.”

“You...”

“You are my salvation!”

Lan Zao's words came in fits and starts, but by the end, they were shouted at the top of his lungs.

A faint, almost imperceptible smile touched the dragonman youngster's face.

His form suddenly shot upward—ten meters, fifty meters, a hundred meters!

He transformed into a giant.

Lan Zao, at his feet, seemed as tiny as an insect.

The dragonman youngster's voice echoed forth. In that instant, it resonated like the tolling of temple bells, vast and boundless, reverberating throughout this darkness.

The sound exploded within Lan Zao's heart— “Since this is so, why do you not bow?”

Lan Zao's expression froze.

The next instant, he knew what to do. The claws curled at his chest slowly clasped together.

He bowed his head: “My Lord, you are my God. Your path is my path...”

He was dying.

Saltwater flooded his lungs and chest.

His body slowly sank into the sea.

Zi Di, in her true form, observed this scene from aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, yet did not dispatch the alchemy puppet to retrieve him.

It was because the dragonman youngster had already arrived at the dock.

Through the alchemy device, she relayed Lan Zao's peril to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster dove headfirst into the seawater, now thick with oil sludge.

Explosive battle qi surged from the ring mound behind him, allowing him to seize Lan Zao.

Plop.

In the next instant, he leapt upward, propelling himself and Lan Zao out of the water.

With successive bounds, he reached the deck of the Love Triangle in mere breaths.

"Commander!"

"Lord Commander!"

The crew erupted in joyful cheers.

The dragonman youngster's arrival greatly boosted the morale of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

By now, the dragonman youngster's prestige far surpassed that of Zong Ge and the others.

The primary reason was his legendary feat of slaying Teng Dong Lang. A silver level slaying a gold level—such an extraordinary achievement commanded undeniable respect.

A significant portion of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' new recruits joined specifically for the dragonman youngster, eager to serve under such a formidable leader.

This was the world of the strong.

The alchemy puppet impersonating Zi Di retrieved healing scrolls and potions, then hurried to the dragonman youngster's side.

Lan Zao received treatment.

Opening his eyes, he found the dragonman youngster standing before him.

He lay on the deck while the dragonman youngster looked down upon him.

The sea, ablaze with raging fires, cast towering flames behind the dragonman youngster, making him appear as towering and majestic as in Lan Zao's nightmares.

“Lord...” Lan Zao cried out involuntarily, nearly breaking down in tears once more.

The dragonman youngster smiled at him, "I'm sorry...”

He didn't finish before abruptly turning away.

In the next instant, a thick white beam of light swept across, freezing the sea surrounding the Love Triangle solid.

Support from the magician tower!

The swamp oil elementals surrounding the tower had all been frozen into dense clusters of statues.

Once the magician tower recovered, it immediately rushed to aid the dock.

After all, if this dock were burned to the ground, the city government palace would suffer truly catastrophic losses.

The thick white beams swept across the area, extinguishing the flames wherever they touched and instantly freezing the sea water and swamp oil.

The crisis for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was resolved.

Chapter 584: Divine Wind Pirates

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hadn't yet arrived at the docks, but the urgent message reached him first.

He requested the dragonman youngster's assistance—many remnants of the Bayonet Gang were already fleeing the harbor aboard various vessels.

Leading the charge were several rocket assault boats.

But they had already gained considerable distance, making pursuit seem unlikely.

There was no time for hesitation. The dragonman youngster immediately pledged to pursue relentlessly, aiming to eliminate as many enemies as possible.

The battlefield waited for no one. Without further soothing Lan Zao, the dragonman youngster led the available mercenaries in leaping from the ship and charging into battle.

He hadn't considered that his unfinished words might have created a massive misunderstanding for Lan Zao.

All Lan Zao heard was the dragonman youngster apologizing to him.

Though Lan Zao had recovered most of his strength thanks to the spell scrolls and healing potions, one mustn't forget that his bloodline had just undergone purification. His body was still adapting.

During this adjustment period, Lan Zao not only suffered from high fever, ringing ears, and accelerated heartbeat, but also experienced mental fog and difficulty concentrating.

Compounded by his recent nightmare, Lan Zao's mind had jumped to the conclusion that the dragonman youngster's apology stemmed from giving him a magic potion that had brought him to the brink of death.

Watching the dragonman youngster's retreating figure join the battle, Lan Zao found his breathing growing labored.

This time, it wasn't the suffocation from seawater flooding his lungs, but the crushing weight of facing death itself.

But soon, his breathing steadied.

“My Lord, I... am now resolved!” he declared inwardly.

Then he rose, scanning his surroundings, "Give me a blade.”

'Zi Di', standing beside him, cautioned, “You should rest now.”

But Lan Zao's mind was foggy, and his senses unclear. He glared at the alchemy puppet before him: “I said, give me a blade!”

Zi Di was furious.

She had kindly urged him to rest, and it was her who had alerted the dragonman youngster, saving Lan Zao's life. Yet this was the ungrateful, harsh rebuke she received in return.

Lan Zao insisted: "I must follow the commander while I still can fight!"

Zi Di fell silent.

Beside them, Men Shi roared with laughter: “He's a fighter! Give him a blade!”

Men Shi had just been temporarily assigned by the dragonman youngster to organize the flagship.

As a non-survivor, the dwarf knew nothing of Lan Zao's true identity or the secret connections between Lan Zao, the dragonman youngster, Zi Di, and others.

What the dwarf saw was a bronze level boatswain, restored to near-full strength by potions and scrolls, displaying the courage and will to grab a weapon and rejoin the fight!

This undoubtedly delighted the dwarf, who naturally wouldn't refuse. Instead, he eagerly fulfilled Lan Zao's request to boost their own morale.

Under these circumstances, Zi Di could no longer forcefully intervene.

She could only resolve silently: Keep a close watch on Lan Zao and the others and do her utmost to protect them.

Lan Zao's actions indeed ignited the morale of the surrounding mercenaries. Many who hadn't immediately followed the dragonman youngster into battle were now inspired by Lan Zao, crowding around him.

Among them were the big guy, Bai Ya, and others.

Their joining only added to Zi Di's headache. She knew that on this battlefield, with the magician tower monitoring everything, many of her methods couldn't be freely deployed.

With no other choice, Zi Di could only glare fiercely at Lan Zao.

The dragonman youngster had deliberately rushed into battle precisely to keep Lan Zao and the others safe.

But Lan Zao was mired in profound misunderstanding, completely oblivious to the goodwill of the dragonman youngster and Zi Di.

He even harbored deep suspicion that Zi Di harbored ill intent.

The nightmares he'd endured had clarified his purpose, strengthening his resolve to follow the dragonman youngster.

Even unto death!

“While I still can fight, let me offer you more assistance.” With this resolve, Lan Zao's eyes blazed with a death-defying fighting spirit.

He roared, “Follow me!”

And so, sword raised, he leapt from the ship first, charging onto the ice to strike at the remnants of the Bayonet Gang attempting to escape Snow Bird Harbor.

The big guy wanted to help his dad, and Bai Ya was never one to shy away from battle.

The group charged out with fierce momentum.

Men Shi wanted to follow, but he still had the task the dragonman youngster had entrusted to him.

Watching Lan Zao charge forward, he couldn't help but admire him from the bottom of his heart: "He's truly remarkable! Just a mere bronze level battle qi cultivator, yet he feels like a silver level battle qi cultivator to me!"

At that moment, Men Shi suddenly sensed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps seemed different from other organizations!

He had joined the corps precisely because the dragonman youngster had saved his people. To repay a debt of gratitude, he had sacrificed himself.

But now, he felt a strong admiration for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This admiration had nothing to do with a desire to follow the strong.

Lan Zao's battle qi cultivator level was far below his own.

This admiration stemmed from respect for a brave soul!

Lan Zao's actions perfectly matched Men Shi's temperament. Men Shi, too, was one who feared neither life nor death and was willing to sacrifice.

Otherwise, he wouldn't have risked his life at the auction to save his clan. Nor would he have joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps out of gratitude. And certainly not moments ago, when he was about to sacrifice himself by charging into the swamp oil amalgamation just before its explosion.

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the ghost girl sighed, pressing her hand to her forehead.

These people were really good at causing trouble!

Why couldn't they just preserve their useful bodies?

“Lan Zao, you're destined to be at least a silver level battle qi cultivator someday.”

Zi Di's inner thoughts were inaudible to Lan Zao.

Likewise, Lan Zao's inner thoughts were something Zi Di simply couldn't accept.

Each person was an individual; achieving complete understanding was exceedingly difficult.

Even between the dragonman youngster and Lan Zao, a minor misunderstanding could shatter coordination and fragment their actions.

A rocket assault boat raced across the sea.

Unlike its initial form, it was now significantly shorter.

“The third section has detached,” Bing Xiao stated, staring at the crystal ball within the sole cabin.

The crystal ball's display flickered, revealing the status of each rocket assault boat.

Almost simultaneously, they shed the rearmost sections of their hulls.

Spring Boxer watched silently.

He now deeply understood why these vessels were named rocket assault boats.

This specialized craft was designed solely for assault—a consumable from the start. Ignition began at the stern, the flames rapidly consuming the hull as they raced forward. With each section engulfed in fire, Spring Boxer felt as though he heard the roars and howls of countless beasts.

This wasn't mere fuel combustion; it involved certain techniques from the beastman's totems.

It was precisely this self-sacrificing method that allowed the entire vessel's speed to skyrocket, reaching heights unattainable by a silver level hull.

In terms of pure speed alone, the rocket assault boat ranked among the top few in the silver level.

“At this distance, we're safe!” Spring Boxer declared.

They had caught the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord off guard. By the time the magician tower could respond, they had already escaped its striking range.

“Exactly,” Bing Xiao's voice rang with excitement, "Look, there's the ship sent to retrieve us.”

Spring Boxer followed the direction of the voice to the crystal ball.

Three slender magic ships came into view.

Their sails were disproportionately large compared to the hulls, far exceeding standard dimensions. They resembled triangular clouds draped over the decks.

Spotting the pirate crew's emblem on the sails, Spring Boxer exclaimed in astonishment, "That's... the Divine Wind Pirates?

Almost simultaneously, the dragonman youngster received the latest intelligence from the city Lord through the alchemy device.

“Magic ships have appeared offshore to rendezvous with the Bayonet Gang!”

“They all belong to the Divine Wind Pirates.”

“Divine Wind Pirates?” the dragonman youngster exclaimed in surprise, "Pirates are actually active in the waters near the Ice Sculpture Kingdom?”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord admitted frankly, “Indeed, there are several.”

"Pirates are like greedy jackals. Our nation's import-export trade is enormous, with countless merchant ships coming and going. There are over a dozen major shipping routes alone."

“As long as these profits exist, pirates will persist.”

"However, among them, several pirate crews operate year-round. Even when the Kingdom's navy mobilizes, it's difficult to eradicate them."

“The Divine Wind Pirates are one such group!”

"This pirate crew possesses astonishing speed, far exceeding the norm. Not only that, but their magic ships can also coordinate to boost their velocity."

"The Divine Wind has already appeared. This gold level magic ship is their flagship. Their leader is likely aboard as well."

The dragonman youngster furrowed his brow, "A gold level flagship? Then who exactly is their captain..."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord knew what he was getting at and answered directly, "Rest assured, this intelligence has been verified multiple times. The Divine Wind Pirates' captain is merely gold level in cultivation."

"He was originally the heir to a major trading house. The Divine Wind Pirates' ships are all custom-made magic ships from Gear Island, built at great expense by the merchant guild before its bankruptcy."

The dragonman youngster still found it hard to believe, "Your nation's alchemy technology is so advanced, yet even deploying the navy couldn't catch up to them?”

“Then how are we supposed to fight them now?”

Anger surged within the dragonman youngster.

The remnants of the Bayonet Gang had inflicted heavy casualties on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. He yearned to capture and eliminate all their leaders to eradicate the threat.

A cold laugh echoed through the communication device from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord: “Even if they flee to the Divine Wind Pirates' ship, I will make them pay!”

The moment the city Lord's words faded, the magician tower erupted with a staggering surge of energy.

Within mere breaths, vast magic power was exhausted, forming a colossal magic formation above the tower.

The pentagram formation slowly rotated in midair.

Suddenly, a blinding bolt of lightning erupted from its center, shooting skyward and vanishing from sight.

The next instant, the lightning bolt crashed down.

Having traversed over ten thousand meters, it struck one of the rocket assault boats with pinpoint accuracy.

Rocket assault boats weren't fragile; they possessed defensive capabilities.

Yet after the lightning strike, the rocket assault boat lost all momentum. The various totems painted on its hull were completely obliterated.

Most of the hull shattered, leaving only the bow and cabin intact.

Carried by inertia, the rocket assault boat skidded over ten meters before grinding to a halt on the sea surface.

“Charge!” The dragonman youngster hesitated not a moment, leading the charge toward the damaged rocket assault boat.

Chapter 585: Purple Star Lighting Bolt Magic Formation

The rocket assault boat cut through the waves, its hemispherical bow perpetually raised as it forged ahead.

The stern blazed with searing flames, generating immense thrust.

Waves crashed head-on, but the bow plowed through like a battering ram, smashing through the water with thunderous booms that sent towering spray flying.

With unyielding resolve, the rocket assault boat slammed into the Divine Wind.

A masked figure emerged onto the Divine Wind's deck.

Tall and gaunt, he wore a wide white feather cloak and a bronze beast-faced mask. In his left hand, he held a scythe with a shaft nearly two meters long.

Leaning on the scythe with his right hand, he slowly extended his left arm, pointing it toward the rocket assault boat.

In the next instant, a howling gale surged forth, pushing back the rocket assault boat.

Inside the cabin, Bing Xiao immediately coordinated, actively maneuvering to jettison the final section of the rocket that hadn't fully ignited.

Deprived of propulsion and buffeted by the headwind, the rocket assault boat's speed plummeted sharply.

By the time it reached the vicinity of the Divine Wind, all forward momentum had been lost.

The beast totems on the assault boat were now sparse and scattered, starkly contrasting with their earlier dense, layered clusters.

The totems shifted for a moment, their beast mouths gaping wide to reveal the inner cabin.

Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer emerged one after the other.

Two ropes were thrown from the Divine Wind's gunwale.

Spring Boxer was pale and dizzy, struggling to grasp the swaying rope.

He was in terrible shape.

Fortunately, Bing Xiao was nearby. Seeing this, he immediately offered assistance, pulling the rope down and wrapping it several times around Spring Boxer's limbs and waist.

Bing Xiao climbed the rope, pulling Spring Boxer up with him.

Both men stepped onto the deck.

The pirates on deck met them with cold, scrutinizing stares.

Bing Xiao hurriedly saluted, "Captain Feng Lian, we owe you our lives for this rescue. I never imagined you would personally intervene this time!"

Feng Lian, wielding his long-handled scythe, was none other than the captain of the Divine Wind and leader of the Divine Wind Pirates.

His hoarse voice came from beneath the bronze beast mask: “I don't trust the others enough to let them come over.”

“After all, this is one of the eight harbors of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

Feng Lian lifted his head slightly: “See that?”

Bing Xiao turned slightly, gazing toward the direction of the magician tower.

Above the magician tower, the pentagram magic formation that had materialized continued to rotate slowly, strikingly conspicuously.

Bing Xiao sighed softly, "I see it. It's the Purple Star Lighting Bolt Magic Formation.”

“Ah, I never imagined the magician tower of Snow Bird Harbor would one day actually deploy a Thunder Bolt Magic Formation.”

Spring Boxer furrowed his brow in puzzlement, "Doesn't the city Lord fear the Sea Bird Church will oppose him?”

The Lightning Bolt Magic Formation was a classic, long-range precision strike spell.

Though its setup was overly cumbersome, once activated, it was extremely difficult to disrupt. It also boasted advantages like a vast attack range and pinpoint accuracy.

Most harbors possessed such defensive measures.

Snow Bird Harbor, however, was unique.

For here lay the Sea Bird Church's largest—and nearly sole—parish.

Before ascending to godhood, the Sea Bird God had endured a fierce battle where he nearly perished. His assailant was a battle qi cultivator wielding the divinity of thunder.

After the Sea Bird ascended to godhood, this battle qi cultivator also achieved divinity, becoming a minor god wielding authority over lightning.

The relationship between these two deities has always been like water and fire. Throughout their respective growth phases, they experienced countless frictions, accumulating irreconcilable hatred and conflicts.

The snow elf city Lord, who preceded the current Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, had made numerous attempts to install the Purple Star Lighting Bolt Magic Formation in Snow Bird Harbor.

But each effort was met with opposition and obstruction from the Sea Bird Church, never succeeding.

Unexpectedly, the current Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had secretly constructed it.

This was clearly a taboo act.

“Hahahaha, the city Lord's desperate!” Spring Boxer roared with laughter, "Now that he's activated the Lighting Bolt Magic Formation, the Sea Bird Church will surely come after him!”

Though not a native of Snow Bird Harbor, Spring Boxer had lived here for many years and knew the Sea Bird Church's ways intimately.

Yet Feng Lian, captain of the Divine Wind Pirates, shook his head slowly, "I doubt it.”

Spring Boxer shot him a puzzled look.

Bing Xiao sighed deeply, "This current Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is no ordinary man.”

“He's held his temper this long, waiting until the end of his term to strike against our Bayonet Gang.”

“He's prepared so thoroughly that we've no chance to fight back.”

“You tell me, would someone like that make such a rookie mistake?”

At this point, Bing Xiao shook his head, his tone unwavering, "The fact that the magician tower could openly deploy the Lightning Bolt Formation proves the Sea Bird Church is no longer a concern."

"Looking back, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and the Sea Bird Church have collaborated repeatedly, advancing and retreating together. Their collusion ran deep and tight. They must have forged numerous secret pacts behind the scenes."

“We were far too negligent.”

“This time, the gang's annihilation hinged on the relationship between the Sea Bird Church and the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's city government palace.”

“Had we detected their clandestine alliance earlier, we would have remained vigilant and avoided such a sudden, catastrophic downfall.”

Bing Xiao reflected, his face etched with bitterness.

Feng Lian spoke up: “As long as you're alive, there's hope.”

“Preserve your useful body and make amends for your mistakes.”

Bing Xiao nodded, sinking into silence.

Spring Boxer couldn't help but feel puzzled.

From this exchange, he sensed that the relationship between Bing Xiao and Feng Lian wasn't merely a simple business transaction.

“Could it be... the old leader has another identity?”

Hidden behind his bronze beast mask, Spring Boxer couldn't see Feng Lian's expression. His gaze fell on Bing Xiao instead.

Bing Xiao caught the look, nodding and smiling at Spring Boxer, "There are many things the gang leader hasn't told you. The timing is too soon for you. Once we get through this current crisis, I'll explain everything to you."

Spring Boxer's curiosity only deepened.

From Bing Xiao's words, it seemed Teng Dong Lang also harbored similar secrets—far more than the simple identity Spring Boxer knew.

Boom!

Purple light flashed as another bolt of lightning struck the rocket assault boat.

This was already the second rocket assault boat to be hit.

The sheer scale of the attack drew the attention of Feng Lian, Bing Xiao, and the others.

"No! It's the boat carrying Xiong Ju and the others!" Spring Boxer's face paled as he cried out in alarm.

Bing Xiao swiftly retrieved the crystal orb and remotely activated it.

On the rocket assault boat, the totem opened its beastly maw, allowing Xiong Ju and the human ringleader to crawl out.

The human ringleader staggered, while Xiong Ju fared worse.

His upper body barely emerged before he collapsed onto the hull, vomiting violently.

“Damn it, I remember now—Xiong Ju can't handle boats. He gets seasick no matter what vessel he's on,” Spring Boxer said hoarsely, "There was even gossip in the gang about it. They say when he was a kid, if you rocked his cradle just a little, he'd foam at the mouth."

“We have to save them!”

Feng Lian watched Spring Boxer, silently sizing him up, but said nothing.

Bing Xiao's attention wasn't on Xiong Ju and the others. Instead, he was fixated on another rocket assault boat speeding across the sea.

But in the next instant, Purple Star Lighting Bolt fired again, striking its target with pinpoint accuracy.

Bing Xiao's expression paled slightly.

The rocket assault boat he'd been secretly monitoring went down as well.

The long, cylindrical rocket hull was blown clean in two. The rear section, engulfed in flames, careened wildly like a headless fly. The front section, deprived of power, slowed steadily until it finally drifted lifelessly on the sea surface.

Bing Xiao sighed bitterly, "I must return to assist!”

“Stay here,” he instructed Spring Boxer.

But Spring Boxer rejected Bing Xiao's offer without hesitation, "No, I'm going with you to save them.”

“Not just Xiong Ju and the others—there are many more gang members too.”

Chaos reigned at Snow Bird Harbor's dock.

Swamp oil elementals were still wreaking havoc everywhere.

Hordes of gang members boarded fishing boats, cruise ships, even small wooden boats, racing toward the open sea to escape the harbor.

Others were locked in combat on the dock.

They lacked the capability for large-scale military formations, but acts of destruction—looting, burning, and smashing—were second nature to them.

Even with a squad of city guards rushing to the scene, they struggled to contain the situation.

“Lord Feng Lian, could you let our other gang members board your ship too?” asked Spring Boxer.

Feng Lian fixed his gaze on Spring Boxer, then suddenly chuckled softly, "So you're the coffin bearer chosen by Teng Dong Lang.”

“Huh? What do you mean?” Spring Boxer was bewildered.

Feng Lian offered no explanation, instead turning to Bing Xiao, "You know I can't bring the ship any closer.”

"This distance is the limit. Even if the Purple Star Lightning Bolt strikes, we can evade it."

"But I suspect the magician tower has other tricks up its sleeve. I smell a trap."

“So, if you want to help, you'll have to get them yourselves.”

“My ship stays here, maintaining distance, and I'll only give you 15 minutes.”

“Understood!” Bing Xiao nodded, "Give us one landing craft. If we're not back in 15 minutes, you can leave. Don't come to rescue us."

“Give them a ship!” Feng Lian ordered immediately.

The Divine Wind Pirates' landing craft mirrored the flagship's style—long and slender, with sleek lines.

Spring Boxer leaped from the rope ladder onto the craft, only to stare in disbelief.

He found no rudder, let alone oars.

Bing Xiao already stood at the bow.

The next moment, Feng Lian spoke: “Let me give you a ride.”

He extended his left hand, palm facing the landing craft.

The landing craft instantly activated, radiating a silver level aura.

This vessel, designed for twenty passengers, was in fact a silver level magic ship!

The alchemy insignias on its hull ignited, unleashing a powerful gust of wind. The wind lifted the hull and propelled it forward.

Spring Boxer let out a startled cry and plopped down onto his backside.

The landing craft shot forward like an arrow released from its bowstring.

Bam!

The dragonman youngster's fist smashed through, crushing the skull of a bayonet gang member blocking his path.

His dragon scales were drenched in blood, all belonging to others.

Beside him lay the wreckage of the first rocket assault boat he had destroyed.

The dragonman youngster charged first, obliterating the entire vessel only to find it empty.

But now, his keen dragon eyes reflected the image of a landing craft.

Upon it stood Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer.

Gritting his teeth, the dragonman youngster murmured to himself.

"Fantastic."

Chapter 586: Thunder Numbing Light Bullet

The dragonman youngster had seen several battlefields and understood clearly that the current chaos at the dock was actually orchestrated to facilitate the breakout of the Bayonet Gang's high-ranking members.

Therefore, the primary targets were the several rocket assault boats that had appeared.

Chi Lai wasn't present; he had gone to train with Zong Ge. Thus, even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' sole gold level cannon had been repaired, they'd be powerless against these rocket assault boats.

The magician tower didn't disappoint them. True to its role as the defensive core, it destroyed one rocket assault boat with its first strike.

But when the dragonman youngster rushed over, he found it empty. He instantly realized it was merely a decoy!

The dragonman youngster harbored an innate loathing for gangs. Returning to the dock, he witnessed his faction's heavy losses and perilous ordeal. The flagship, the Love Triangle, had never been in such a dire state. Rage naturally surged within the dragonman youngster's heart.

Now, seeing the landing craft returning with Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer aboard, excitement surged through him, his breathing growing heavy.

Yet he remained composed.

The dragonman youngster knew his speed couldn't match the landing craft's. Pursuing it recklessly would only backfire. If the landing craft changed course, he'd be left trailing in its wake.

His sharp gaze swept the area, quickly discerning the landing craft's clear destination: another rocket assault boat.

That vessel had lost all power, drifting helplessly on the sea.

Xiong Ju lay sprawled on the deck, vomiting, while the human ringleader's face was deathly pale.

Many were rushing toward their location.

Most were gang members attempting to rescue their two ringleaders. Others were enemies of the Bayonet Gang, seeking to claim the bounty on the leaders' heads.

Their identities were mixed. Judging by their clothing and equipment, the dragonman youngster recognized many as fishermen, along with members of other mercenary groups. There were also a few merchants, magician apprentices, and others.

This was because moments earlier, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had publicly issued a wanted notice, offering a hefty bounty for the lives of Bayonet Gang members.

This had mobilized the citizens of Snow Bird Harbor!

In the past, ordinary folk dared not lay a finger on the Bayonet Gang.

But now, with the Bayonet Gang clearly facing annihilation, their leaders escaped prison and fled, their combat strength plummeted to rock bottom. They were nothing more than walking, walking bounties.

Money was the greatest temptation. For some low level transcendents, if they managed to take down a ringleader, the bounty offered by the city Lord would be enough to cover their expenses for a lifetime.

Even if they couldn't kill a ringleader, every last remnant of the Bayonet Gang was worth a bounty.

“Qing Pi, heh heh, back when you bullied me and stole my fruit stall, you never imagined this moment, did you?”

“Hmph, kill me if you must. Enough talk.”

Some abandoned resistance, kneeling to beg for mercy.

"Spare my life! Joining the Bayonet Gang was forced upon me! Mercy! Mercy! I have a two-year-old child to raise!"

Driven by the bounty, the men's eyes had long turned red. They showed no mercy.

With a crack, blood spattered as a head was severed from its body.

The killer raised his arms in triumph: These criminals deserved every bit of their deaths!"

Others scrambled for headcounts.

Two muskets struck a gang member simultaneously, sending him crashing to his death.

“I got him!”

“No, I did!”

"Calm down! Haven't you heard the city Lord's proclamation? The magician tower records every battle in real-time. Your contributions won't go uncounted."

Only then did the commotion subside.

The Bayonet Gang had been annihilated, their assets confiscated. Most of the remnants sought only to flee Snow Bird Harbor, not to fight.

The gang's true core had been decimated long ago, killed or captured by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord on the day the dragonman youngster and Teng Dong Lang engaged in their heroic duel.

The dragonman youngster looked down on the ordinary stragglers, fixing his gaze instead on Xiong Ju and his men.

He raised his foot and kicked forward with all his might, shattering the ship's hull before him.

Amidst flying wood, he leapt up and seized a long plank with pinpoint accuracy

As he landed on the sea surface, his feet planted firmly on the plank, using it as a surfboard.

The dragonman youngster crouched low, plunging his palms into the seawater. A ring mound formed in his palm as his Surge battle skill erupted. With a soft boom, a spray of water erupted.

The plank beneath his feet shot upward violently, launching him into the air. He didn't travel far—only a few meters—before splashing back down onto the sea surface, sending up a large spray of water.

Propelled by momentum, the plank surged forward another dozen meters before its speed began to wane.

The dragonman youngster pressed on, sustaining the Surge battle skill.

Thus, amid a series of booms, the plank carried him like an arrow released from its string. Drifting and drifting across the sea, it charged toward the location of Xiong Ju and the others.

The commotion created by the dragonman youngster was too conspicuous. Crucially, his fame had spread far and wide. The moment he appeared he was certain to draw countless eyes.

His movements were detected almost immediately by Xiong Ju, Bing Xiao, and the others.

“Damn it! Long Fu is coming for us!” The human ringleader's face drained of color as panic set in.

Xiong Ju snorted coldly, about to put on a brave front, when he suddenly retched and vomited again.

In the distance, the landing craft carrying Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer had already closed in on Xiong Ju and his group.

Bing Xiao, intent on stopping the dragonman youngster, drew a short-barreled flintlock from his coat.

The firearm radiated a silver aura.

Bing Xiao pulled the trigger. The bell-shaped muzzle instantly erupted with a bolt of lightning.

The bolt raced forward with astonishing speed, reaching the dragonman youngster in an instant.

The dragonman youngster was long prepared for this. He flipped his palm, unleashing his Surge battle skill. The ship's deck shifted sideways effortlessly, evading the bolt's strike.

Bing Xiao showed no surprise. Instead, a smug grin touched his lips.

The next instant, the lightning bolt that had flown behind the dragonman youngster detonated on its own, erupting into a vast cloud of shattering fragments.

Caught off guard, the dragonman youngster was engulfed within the explosion, immediately feeling an intense, numbing tingle.

A torrent of electric light not only clung to the surface of his dragon scales but also seeped into his body, disrupting the flow of his battle qi.

Silver level alchemy bullet—Thunder Numbing Light Bullet!

The dragonman youngster's brow furrowed slightly.

His defenses were exceptional—his dragon scales offered protection against both physical and magic attacks, and he wore the Gold Silk Chainmail. Though the chainmail was damaged, its defensive capabilities still far exceeded silver level standards.

Combined with the Grand Arena Medal, even after returning the borrowed equipment, the dragonman youngster could still dominate the current battlefield.

If Bing Xiao's musket and bullets were both gold level, they might inflict damage on the dragonman youngster. But being silver level, they posed no real threat to him.

However, posing no threat to his life didn't mean they couldn't cause trouble.

The Thunder Numbing Light Bullet could detonate, unleashing an area-of-effect attack. Facing such an assault, the dragonman youngster found it difficult to evade.

Countless fragments of lightning scattered across the field, significantly hindering the dragonman youngster and preventing him from charging freely.

Bing Xiao maneuvered the landing craft while firing long-range shots at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster's martial skills were exceptionally solid—thanks to the Grand Arena Medal and the contributions of Xiong Ju and others.

He countered the interference, maintaining perfect balance on the deck.

His evasion of bullets grew increasingly adept.

Thus, by the time Bing Xiao's landing craft reached Xiong Ju, the dragonman youngster was merely two hundred meters away.

“Long Fu is coming!” the human ringleader cried out in alarm.

“I can still...” Xiong Ju tried to stand, but his seasickness was so severe he couldn't even straighten his body. He swayed violently from side to side, utterly useless.

Spring Boxer gritted his teeth, about to step forward regardless of the danger of stopping the dragonman youngster, but Bing Xiao pressed down on his shoulder.

Bing Xiao smiled, "Watch me.”

Though still aboard the landing craft, he leaned sideways and pressed his hand against the hull of the rocket assault boat.

The crystal ball in his embrace suddenly burst into light.

In the next instant, every totem on the rocket assault boat rose, coalescing into tangible forms midair.

These totems, bizarre in form and shimmering with multicolored light, charged toward the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster's Machine Gun Fist battle skill was perfectly suited for this situation.

Without hesitation, he unleashed it, shattering the totem beasts one after another.

Yet he was inevitably held back temporarily.

These totem beasts were all silver level. The dragonman youngster hadn't yet ascended to gold level, lacking the ability to break through.

“Lord Commander!” In the distance, Lan Zao had seized a fishing boat. Seeing the dragonman youngster besieged by the totems, he rushed to assist.

This level of combat far exceeded his capabilities. After all, he was merely bronze level.

But Lan Zao had long since abandoned all thought of survival in this fight.

A minor misunderstanding had led Lan Zao to believe that after swallowing the magic potion, his bloodline had gone haywire, leaving him unable to survive.

His physical condition was dire now—a high fever burned his forehead, and waves of dizziness washed over his mind.

Even the hand gripping the knife hilt felt tingling numbness.

“How much longer can I follow you?”

“I'm about to die!”

“But before I die, I will give my all and contribute my strength to you!”

Just as he rushed to assist the dragonman youngster, the alchemy device at his waist activated.

Zi Di had been monitoring his movements.

Seeing his intended course of action, she immediately intervened: “Are you trying to get yourself killed?”

“That battle isn't for you to join.”

“Even if you want to die, don't complicate things for the commander.”

Lan Zao's resolve wavered at her words: "I was wrong! I only thought of myself. Zi Di is right."

“If I join the fight, the commander will sacrifice countless opportunities in battle to protect me.”

“I can't become a burden to the commander!”

Just then, Zi Di added, "Head over there. My scans indicate the rocket assault boat there seems to be carrying unusual cargo."

“Don't let anyone else get to it first.”

Following Zi Di's guidance, Lan Zao quickly located the target.

This was the third rocket assault boat destroyed by the magician tower.

It drifted on the sea surface, spinning alone.

Its hull was damaged, exposing the cabin and revealing a corner of a black wooden cabinet.

This wasn't a purely decoy vessel.

Several people had noticed the boat's condition and were approaching.

Lan Zao charged toward the rocket assault boat.

Along the way, gang members attacked Lan Zao, attempting to stop him.

Lan Zao, knife clenched between his teeth, leaped onto enemy vessels like a monkey, slaughtering all in his path.

Among the foes was a bronze level cultivator. The moment he clashed with Lan Zao, he was instantly cowed by the overwhelming presence.

Lan Zao was truly fearless, his aura fierce. Three swift blows ended the enemy's life.

The rest were mere mortals, and he slaughtered most of them as well.

Lan Zao didn't pursue those who jumped ship to escape. He continued steering the captured boat toward his target location.

Soon, he boarded the rocket assault boat.

Just as Lan Zao was about to inspect the black cabinet, another group attacked.

Their leader, an iron level battle qi cultivator, bellowed, “Freeze! This is our spoils!”

Meanwhile, the dragonman youngster had completely destroyed the totem beasts and charged onto the rocket assault boat.

Xiong Ju and Spring Boxer stood ready for battle.

The human ringleader tried to stall, shouting, “Commander Long Fu, let's talk this out peacefully.”

The dragonman youngster remained expressionless, his voice icy: “Have a nice chat with my fists.”

But Bing Xiao chuckled: “Long Fu, you've fallen for the trap.”

With that, a blinding flash erupted from the rocket assault boat. Bing Xiao and his companions vanished from the spot, leaving only the dragonman youngster behind.

Only then did the dragonman youngster lower his guard, only to see Bing Xiao and the others already aboard another rocket assault boat.

He suddenly realized that the rocket assault boats could actually teleport people between each other!

But by the time he grasped this, it was already too late.

“No! Lan Zao is there!” The dragonman youngster's expression turned into shock.

Chapter 587: Misunderstanding

Ten minutes earlier.

Lan Zao had just reached the rocket assault boat and was about to slash at the black chest with his blade when a group of people rushed over.

Their leader, an iron level battle qi cultivator, shouted threats at Lan Zao: “Freeze! This is our loot!”

Lan Zao was only bronze level, while the group numbered nearly ten. Not only were they outnumbered, but their leader was iron level.

The old Lan Zao would have been contemplating retreat by now.

But now, mistakenly believing his end was near, the iron level battle qi cultivator’s warning felt like an invitation.

An invitation to perform a final, dazzling display of life before death arrived.

Especially when Lan Zao noticed the iron level battle qi cultivator wielding a long-handled, square-headed war hammer. His mind made a calculation.

Thud.

The next moment, Lan Zao plunged headfirst into the water.

The iron level battle qi cultivator assumed his threat had worked—after all, the opponent was merely bronze level.

But soon after, a gleaming blade pierced the boat's bottom. With a swift pull, it left a gaping hole.

Seawater gurgled through the crack, flooding the vessel.

Before anyone could react, the blade reappeared elsewhere.

This time, it stabbed clean through some unlucky soul's sole.

The blade withdrew, and the victim let out a piercing scream, clutching his wounded foot as he toppled backward.

These men were crammed into a small boat. Its hull was low and thin, the space cramped—barely suitable for fishing at sea.

The iron level battle qi cultivator instantly realized Lan Zao was behind this. Enraged, he roared and slammed his square hammer down onto the deck.

Damn it!

His single blow shattered the plank, creating a hole far larger than anything Lan Zao had managed.

Where two small cracks had previously allowed water to gurgle in, this massive hole now let seawater pour in with a roaring rush.

The boat sank visibly with each passing moment.

Lan Zao had sensed danger long before and dodged the hammer blow.

Failing to strike his target, the iron level battle qi cultivator took a deep breath and leapt into the sea himself.

Clearly, he had no intention of letting Lan Zao off so easily.

Spotting the enemy entering the water and closing in, Lan Zao immediately dove deeper.

The iron level battle qi cultivator gave chase, but instead of closing the distance, he found himself falling further behind.

Watching Lan Zao move with unnatural agility through the water, he could only abandon the pursuit, filled with both anger and frustration.

The iron level battle qi cultivator surfaced and swam with all his might toward the rocket assault boat.

This development was precisely what Lan Zao had anticipated.

From the moment he first saw the iron level battle qi cultivator, he knew this human was no seafarer.

Advance when the enemy retreated, retreat when the enemy advanced.

Lan Zao swam back.

Halfway there, the iron level battle qi cultivator heard agonized screams echoing behind him.

The human fighter whipped his head around, his expression a mix of shock and fury.

He hadn't pursued Lan Zao relentlessly; he had let him go. He never expected Lan Zao to dare return!

The small boat was nearly submerged. The others aboard were also swimming, their destination the same rocket assault boat.

Their strength was weak, and their swimming skills couldn't match Lan Zao's. He disposed of them with ease.

One by one, they became corpses floating on the sea surface.

The iron level battle qi cultivator hesitated but chose not to return for support. Instead, he continued toward the rocket assault boat.

They were guards belonging to the same merchant guild—merely colleagues, not sworn brothers bound by life-and-death loyalty.
