Lan Zao quickly disposed of everyone else, leaving only the iron level battle qi cultivator standing.

“My physical condition is better than I expected.”

He had been running a high fever, his entire body burning hot, but the icy seawater cooled him down, bringing waves of comfort.

He was surprised himself: “I seem to be quite adept at naval combat?”

Before, he had merely been a boatswain on merchant ships. Though he had experienced naval battles before, none had felt as exhilarating as this one.

His bloodline had been purified, now being 100% Fish Bladder bloodline. All those “ailments” were merely symptoms of his body undergoing drastic transformation by the bloodline.

The changes brought by the bloodline were beginning to show.

Soon, Lan Zao caught up to the iron level battle qi cultivator again.

The latter still hadn't managed to board the rocket assault boat.

He had no choice but to fight Lan Zao in the water.

Waves churned as they traded blows, fighting to a stalemate.

The iron level battle qi cultivator was unskilled in aquatic combat. His bronze level long-handled square war hammer proved awkward and cumbersome in the water.

Lan Zao, however, moved like a fish in water. He avoided direct confrontations, constantly maneuvering around his opponent.

A few minutes later.

His opponent was ultimately one level above him. Even as Lan Zao fought fiercely, the iron level battle qi cultivator managed to swim onto the rocket assault boat.

“Come on! Watch me smash you with this hammer!” The iron level battle qi cultivator, dripping water from head to toe, stood on the boat, shouting defiantly without bothering to wipe his eyes.

Lan Zao kept only his head above water; bloodshot eyes fixed silently on the iron level battle qi cultivator.

The iron level battle qi cultivator felt a chill run down his spine under that stare.

Their earlier clash had made it clear: “This bronze level battle qi cultivator is no ordinary foe—he's ruthless!”

But the next moment, Lan Zao submerged his head and swam away.

The iron level battle qi cultivator remained on guard. After waiting over a minute without seeing Lan Zao resurface, he relaxed slightly and turned his attention to the black chest.

Lan Zao hadn't given up.

Paddling with arms and legs, he swam faster and faster until he reached a floating corpse.

He reached out, groping the body until he found an alchemy crossbow.

He continued searching, pulling several arrows from the breast pocket.

Lan Zao moved away from the corpse and continued clearing the battlefield.

Among the shipwreck debris and bodies in the water, he found numerous arrows, along with several muskets and bullets.

Most muskets were waterlogged and unusable, but one possessed waterproofing.

His precise search was entirely guided by Zi Di's direction.

Zi Di's true form remained stationed aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, conducting meticulous battlefield reconnaissance. Through alchemy devices, she relayed the precise locations of these ranged weapons to Lan Zao.

Though alchemy puppets existed within the Deep Sea Monster Fish, deploying them was risky—the magician tower monitored the entire dock battlefield.

Lan Zao swiftly returned to the vicinity of the rocket assault boat, deploying crossbows and muskets to fire at the iron level battle qi cultivator from a distance.

The iron level battle qi cultivator made several attempts to smash the black chest but only drew Lan Zao's attacks.

He had no shield, and the rocket assault boat offered no cover.

He tried to retreat into the cabin, but it was occupied by the black chest.

The iron level battle qi cultivator could barely fend off arrows and bullets with his long-handled war hammer.

The effect was poor.

Soon, he took numerous hits.

He had no choice but to activate defensive battle skills, barely holding on.

But quickly, his battle qi plummeted to dangerous levels.

Sensing trouble, the iron level battle qi cultivator had no option but to dive into the water to escape.

Lan Zao pursued relentlessly, firing shots in his wake.

Had the iron level battle qi cultivator seized the rocket assault boat to make a last stand, he could have held out longer.

Instead, in his panic, he chose to dive into the water and swim away—a move that was nothing short of suicide.

“Spare me! Spare my life! I swear I'll never oppose you again!”

“Look behind you! A group boarded the boat! They're after your cargo!”

The iron level battle qi cultivator begged for mercy in his final moments.

Lan Zao wore a cold expression as he riddled the iron level battle qi cultivator with bullets, turning him into a beehive without the slightest mercy.

After dealing with the man, he hurried back to the rocket assault boat.

While he'd been pursuing the iron level battle qi cultivator, another group boarded the vessel.

Their roles were clearly defined. Some stood guard while others worked inside, seemingly figuring out how to extract the black chest or simply pry it open.

“Friend, hold your fire! Whatever we retrieve, we'll share with you,” the leader of this group shouted as Lan Zao swam toward them, his expression shifting abruptly.

“Get lost. These belong to me,” Lan Zao retorted bluntly.

The leader's face darkened, a cold smile spreading across his lips, "Killing an iron level must have taken a toll on you. You're only bronze level. We have three bronze level cultivators here."

“Being polite to you doesn't mean you can push your luck.”

“Truth is, there's no need for us to fight to the death. These fleeing remnants of the Bayonet Gang are all bounty targets.”

“Of course, if you insist on fighting us to the bitter end, we won't back down. At worst...”

Whoosh.

Before the leader could finish, he was cut off by an arrow fired by Lan Zao.

“Hmph! Let's see if you can even get up here!” The leader was enraged, but he didn't rashly dive in to fight Lan Zao head-on.

He'd just witnessed Lan Zao hunting down the iron level battle qi cultivator and knew Lan Zao's aquatic combat skills were exceptional. The leader didn't have that kind of confidence.

He retreated to the inner circle.

Five or six ordinary men guarded the outer perimeter.

Along with the leader, three bronze level cultivators remained in the inner circle.

This put Lan Zao in a difficult position.

Charging forward wasn't hard, nor was killing one or two ordinary outer defenders. But afterward, he'd face the coordinated assault of three cultivators of the same level.

The slightest misstep meant certain death in battle.

Zi Di saw the danger Lan Zao faced.

This time was different from before.

Previously, when Lan Zao faced the iron level battle qi cultivator, though risky, he had maintained control throughout.

Now, attacking would be far too dangerous.

Zi Di urged Lan Zao to back off: “If it's impossible, then let it be.”

“We can't just give up. How will we know without trying?” Lan Zao retorted.

He submerged once more, scanning the surface around the rocket assault boat with intense focus, searching for defensive weaknesses.

Zi Di was startled, "Why has Lan Zao become so bold and aggressive?”

She could only urge him to be cautious and avoid recklessness before her attention was drawn elsewhere—to the dragonman youngster.

Over there, the dragonman youngster had closed in on another rocket assault boat.

On board were Xiong Ju, Bing Xiao, and four other high-ranking members of the Bayonet Gang.

Just as the dragonman youngster was about to leap aboard, Bing Xiao activated his backup plan.

The rocket assault boat erupted in a blinding flash of light.

When the light faded, so too did Bing Xiao and his companions.

Lan Zao was searching for the enemy's defensive weak points when he suddenly witnessed the rocket assault boat erupt in intense light.

The blinding light quickly dissipated, and Bing Xiao and his group were teleported over.

The original group were not members of the Bayonet Gang and had never anticipated such a turn of events.

Caught off guard, they had no choice but to engage Bing Xiao and his group.

The two factions engaged in chaotic combat aboard the rocket assault boat.

Lan Zao spotted Bing Xiao and his group arriving and prepared to retreat, only to witness both sides locked in a fierce, deadlocked battle.

Though Bing Xiao's group possessed higher levels, their condition was abysmal.

Not only were their physical states dreadful, but they were also completely unarmed, practically empty-handed.

Xiong Ju was the worst off, utterly incapable of combat.

“An opportunity!” Lan Zao spotted the opening and grew excited.

“Kill these men and clear the way for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“This is likely the greatest contribution I can make to my Lord.”

With this thought, Lan Zao charged forward.

Bing Xiao and his group were too preoccupied with their own struggles.

They had been forcibly teleported here, leaving them unable to counterattack as Lan Zao charged.

Lan Zao swung his blade at Bing Xiao, but his foot slipped, striking the bronze level leader instead.

Bing Xiao was startled.

Lan Zao took a deep breath and swung again.

But another bronze level intervened, stabbing him in the back leg with a dagger.

Gritting his teeth against the excruciating pain, Lan Zao continued his attack, his resolve to slay Bing Xiao unwavering.

But fate dealt him a cruel blow—a sudden dizzy spell overtook him. His vision darkened, and he nearly collapsed unconscious.

When his sight cleared, he saw his blade had already flown from his grasp. The bronze level battle qi cultivator now bore a gash across his chest, blood gushing forth as he glared furiously at Lan Zao.

“Die!” The bronze leader, enraged beyond measure, abandoned Bing Xiao and charged at Lan Zao.

This finally gave Bing Xiao an opening. He drew his flintlock and shot the bronze leader dead.

Bang. Bang. Bang.

Several more shots rang out, taking down several more men.

The rest scattered in panic, diving into the water to escape.

Bing Xiao didn't pursue them. Instead, he helped Lan Zao to his feet and called out to Xiong Ju and the others, “Quick! Get ready. We're teleporting again.”

Chapter 588: Coffin Bearer

The moment Lan Zao was helped up by Bing Xiao, his mind was a fog.

After seeing Bing Xiao shoot and kill several people, he’d assumed he’d meet the same fate.

Instead, Bing Xiao had mistaken him for one of his own.

Lan Zao never imagined such a turn of events.

Yet from Bing Xiao's perspective, everything made perfect sense.

Bing Xiao didn't know Lan Zao had slipped, nor that his mind was foggy. What Bing Xiao witnessed firsthand was Lan Zao risking his life to help him survive the perilous moment of teleportation.

“He must be one of the gang members!” Bing Xiao instinctively concluded.

As the former gang leader, Bing Xiao had retired long ago and wasn't familiar with the current members.

In truth, even in a regular army, commanders don't know every soldier under their command. Not to mention in the complex, motley crew that was the Bayonet Gang.

Bing Xiao assumed Lan Zao was simply another unfamiliar face in the gang.

His presence here, attempting to flee Snow Bird Harbor, and his recent assistance to Bing Xiao—his loyalty to the Bayonet Gang was undeniable!

This very act of Lan Zao's was all too familiar to Bing Xiao.

He had witnessed it many times during his tenure.

Gang members would throw themselves into danger, shielding their boss from blades—even when the boss was powerful enough not to need it.

Through such acts, members expressed their loyalty, earning either generous rewards or promotions.

The gang leader would always lavish praise on such behavior, as it bolstered the organization's cohesion. For any gang, the pursuit of organizational unity was relentless.

In truth, every leader of any group desired such cohesion to be as strong as possible.

Bing Xiao was no ordinary member—he was the former gang leader.

Thus, instinctively rushing to support Lan Zao was almost a primal reflex for this superior figure.

Lan Zao understood at once, his gaze locking onto Bing Xiao's chest.

He pondered: Could a single stab here end Bing Xiao's life?

He noticed Bing Xiao wore bronze level leather armor and carried a silver level flintlock pistol in his other hand.

Lan Zao weighed his options. A direct stab seemed risky, so he resolved to wait for a better opportunity.

The next moment, he heard Bing Xiao mention teleportation.

Lan Zao's heart sank. An impulse surged through him to strike immediately.

But by then, Xiong Ju, Spring Boxer, and the others had already closed in.

He lost his chance once more.

Soon, Bing Xiao piloted the rocket assault boat and initiated teleportation again.

Rocket assault boats could teleport between each other.

When the beastmen developed these vessels, anticipating possible destruction by ranged fire, they specifically designed this feature.

The intent was to allow suicide troops aboard the assault boats to teleport each other, minimizing casualties before charging the enemy.

But teleportation could only be used once.

After activation, the original rocket assault boat could neither transmit nor receive teleportation signals.

In combat, the rocket assault boat initiating teleportation was often facing annihilation or was already severely damaged. Multiple teleports would be futile.

Bing Xiao kept the teleportation secret hidden the entire time.

It wasn't until the dragonman youngster launched his fierce assault that he performed his first teleportation.

He rescued Xiong Ju and the human ringleader, instantly transporting them to the rocket assault boat carrying the black coffin.

Then, carrying the black coffin, Lan Zao, and the others, he returned them to the very rocket assault boat he had first boarded.

This rocket assault boat was moored alongside the Divine Wind.

They had returned safely!

Lan Zao was speechless.

The situation had changed too rapidly.

He had already reached the enemy's flagship.

Only when Bing Xiao and the others stepped onto the deck of the Divine Wind did the old former leader exhale a cloud of stale breath and finally relax.

He had just returned to reinforce them, which was actually extremely risky.

If the magician tower attacked them, they wouldn't have much ability to resist.

But just as Bing Xiao had anticipated, precisely because of their weakness, the magician tower didn't target them first. Instead, it directed its attacks at the rocket assault boats.

Bing Xiao gauged the enemy's intentions: seeing him and his men return; the enemy would be pleased.

After revealing the rocket assault boat's teleportation capability, his situation grew even more perilous

The magician tower realized Bing Xiao might attempt another escape, likely shifting their focus entirely onto him!

Thus, Bing Xiao exploited a gap in Purple Star Lighting Bolt's attack to initiate teleportation.

The magician tower had been fully focused on channeling the Purple Star Lighting Bolt Magic Formation. By the time they realized something was amiss, Bing Xiao had already teleported again, carrying the target item, and successfully returned to the vicinity of the Divine Wind.

This time, even if the magician tower wanted to attack, it was too late.

The Divine Wind was now outside the magic formation's range.

Bing Xiao once again thanked Captain Feng Lian of the Divine Wind.

Feng Lian smiled, "This is the result of your efforts. I merely held my position.”

He held considerable respect for Bing Xiao's daring venture.

“Set sail,” Feng Lian ordered.

But Spring Boxer called out, “Wait! One of our companions hasn't returned yet.”

Bing Xiao explained, “I've made other arrangements concerning Ban Lan Gen.”

The old gang leader gazed at the black chest: "The most crucial item has been retrieved. There's no need to linger any longer."

Lan Zao suddenly spoke up: "But what about the other gang members? Honorable leaders, are we simply abandoning them?"

He disguised himself and spoke boldly.

He had no intention of letting this group escape with him.

The human ringleader immediately shot Lan Zao a glare, the message unmistakable: You're just a lowly bronze level member—keep your mouth shut. You have no right to speak.

Lan Zao caught Xiong Ju's gaze and shifted his eyes to Spring Boxer.

He knew these ringleaders of the Bayonet Gang well.

First, as one of the survivors and a core member all along, he possessed vital intelligence.

Second, he'd witnessed nearly the entire confrontation—the heroic duel between the dragonman youngster and Spring Boxer—and understood Spring Boxer's temperament intimately.

Spring Boxer sensed anticipation in Lan Zao's gaze.

Lan Zao's interruption was bold, bordering on offensive, yet Spring Boxer understood the motivation behind it—coming from a “rank-and-file gang member.”

Spring Boxer replied, “Yes, if we leave, what about the others?”

To support Bing Xiao and the others' breakout, the Bayonet Gang's loyalists had mobilized nearly all their remaining forces. The vast majority of these gang members intended to flee Snow Bird Harbor under their leaders' guidance.

That was precisely what they were doing now.

But they lacked vessels—and even the few boats available were too small for long voyages.

Their only hope lay aboard ships like the Divine Wind.

Now, if Bing Xiao and the others withdrew prematurely, these men would face capture and slaughter in utter despair.

Spring Boxer pleaded with Feng Lian: "Lord Feng Lian, could you use your ship to carry our gang members? Please help us—how much it would cost?"

Feng Lian starred at Spring Boxer with keen interest.

At that moment, the Divine Wind's first mate lost patience: "Wait any longer? Impossible!"

“Every minute we linger here brings another minute of danger.”

"You brat, don't push your luck." Most of your gang members are ordinary people—utterly worthless to save."

But Feng Lian waved his hand, and the first mate immediately fell silent.

Feng Lian smiled, "Interesting. Never imagined Teng Dong Lang would choose a coffin-bearer with such a temperament.”

“I've agreed to your terms, young man.”

“No need for further payment.”

“My ship stays anchored here. Let your gang members come aboard anytime.”

“But this is my bottom line: the Divine Wind will not voluntarily enter the magic formation's attack range.”

Spring Boxer hurriedly thanked him, yet his mind filled with more questions.

Feng Lian and Bing Xiao treated him with unusual respect.

The reason lay in his identity—a coffin bearer.

More precisely, Teng Dong Lang's coffin bearer.

But Spring Boxer genuinely knew nothing about this identity.

Unable to contain himself, Spring Boxer voiced his confusion directly.

Feng Lian offered no explanation. Instead, Bing Xiao spoke: "A coffin bearer is exactly what it sounds like. You will carry Teng Dong Lang's coffin."

As he spoke, Bing Xiao's hand brushed the black coffin.

The coffin disintegrated, revealing a long block of ice within.

Sealed within that ice lay a person.

It was Teng Dong Lang's corpse!

“Ah, gang leader!” Spring Boxer never imagined the black coffin held Teng Dong Lang's corpse.

Teng Dong Lang had always looked out for him; he was his father's comrade-in-arms.

Spring Boxer held deep admiration and reverence for him.

Seeing Teng Dong Lang's body, tears welled in Spring Boxer's eyes. Overcome with emotion, he stepped forward and placed his hands on the transparent ice coffin.

“What exactly happened?” Feng Lian inquired.

Bing Xiao replied, "We got lucky. I recovered his body before it was too late. But I can't predict the final outcome—it's up to his luck now."

He then turned to Spring Boxer: “Teng Dong Lang designated you as his coffin bearer.”

“From here on, you must carry this ice coffin and bring him back to Tranquil Hill.”

“Tranquil Hill?” Spring Boxer was startled.

The name was all too familiar to him, tied to some legendary tale.

“Does Tranquil Hill truly exist in this world?” Spring Boxer questioned.

“Of course it does,” Feng Lian answered, “At least within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, there is a Tranquil Hill.”

Lan Zao was utterly confused.

Not being a native, he'd never heard the name Tranquil Hill before.

“Tranquil Hill?”

“They're heading to Tranquil Hill!”

“I must relay this vital intelligence to the commander.”

Even without being an intelligence operative, Lan Zao recognized the value of this information.

But how to transmit it back?

He carried a communication device in his embrace.

Yet he needed to find the right moment—he couldn't just broadcast to the dragonman youngster in front of all these people.

Until an opportunity arose, Lan Zao resolved to bide his time.

Bing Xiao had misunderstood him, and he, in turn, had misjudged Zi Di and the dragonman youngster—convinced that the magic potion he’d consumed meant certain death.

Thus, even now, surrounded by enemies, his gaze remained steady and utterly fearless!

It was the dragonman youngster who grew far more anxious.

Gazing toward the pirate ship, the Divine Wind, he declared, “This won’t do! I must find a way to rescue Lan Zao!”

Chapter 589: Mutual Understanding

Snow Bird Harbor Dock.

The magician tower hummed with activity as the colossal Purple Star Lighting Bolt Magic Formation dominated the harbor sky, intimidating the Divine Wind Pirates.

The slaughter and combat on the sea raged on.

Corpses bobbed in the waves, while fragments of ships sank or floated.

The rocket assault boats had all been destroyed.

Yet remnants of the Bayonet Gang remained active.

Most still struggled, while a few had escaped the lightning zone and boarded pirate ships.

The dragonman youngster transmitted urgently to Zi Di: "If we use the Deep Sea Monster Fish to sneak beneath the Divine Wind Pirates and launch a surprise attack, what are our odds of success?"

Zi Di was startled by this and hurriedly advised, “Sir, please don't be so anxious!”

“Although Lan Zao has been taken away, it's still safe for now.”

The dragonman youngster sighed and shook his head slightly, "This is merely Bing Xiao's misunderstanding. He can't conceal the truth for long."

“The battlefield was crowded with onlookers. Lan Zao fought his way through, killing numerous Bayonet Gang members.”

"Moreover, once the post-battle reorganization begins, they will inevitably conduct a thorough review. At that point, Lan Zao will be exposed as an outsider with no ties to any gang members. He will be discovered."

"Even if he pretends to be an ordinary citizen of Snow Bird Harbor who admires the Bayonet Gang, it's a huge flaw. He can't even clearly name the streets of Snow Bird Harbor."

“We must rescue him quickly, or he'll truly be in danger!”

Zi Di took a deep breath.

She had anticipated the dragonman youngster's determination to rescue him.

But his plan was truly foolish.

Right now, they were at Snow Bird Harbor's dock. If the Deep Sea Monster Fish was exposed, what would the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord think?

Right now, they were at Snow Bird Harbor's dock. If the Deepsea Monster Fish was exposed, what would the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord think?

Zi Di was unwilling to take such a risk to rescue Lan Zao.

Yet she also knew she couldn't outright stop the dragonman youngster.

Fortunately, her negotiation skills were sharp. She immediately shifted her approach and reasoned, “Lord, even if we expose the Deep Sea Monster Fish and use it to approach the Divine Wind, can we truly save him?”

“The enemy isn't a silver level magic ship—it's gold level, with formidable defenses!”

"Moreover, the Divine Wind Pirates' crew hasn't engaged in combat and remains fully intact. Most critically, their gold level leader, Feng Lian, is present!"

Zi Di paused briefly before continuing, “Truthfully, I have no concern for your personal safety, my Lord.”

“Your previous heroic duel proved your mettle beyond doubt.”

“You are capable of facing a gold level opponent.”

“But rescuing Lan Zao from the enemy ranks? That difficulty is on an entirely different level from fighting a gold level alone, isn't it?”

“What worries me more is Lan Zao's reaction...”

The dragonman youngster cut her off: “No, I trust Lan Zao!”

Zi Di immediately added: "I trust him too! That's precisely why I'm more concerned."

“My Lord, imagine this: if Lan Zao sees you charging recklessly into danger, how do you think he'll react?”

“He's your most steadfast follower. His heart must be filled with worry for you.”

"He'll believe he's the one dragging you down. If it weren't for saving him, you wouldn't be in this perilous situation."

“So, once you're in mortal peril, what do you think he'll do?”

The dragonman youngster's heart lurched as the most likely outcome flashed through his mind.

His voice heavy, he replied to Zi Di, “By then, even if Lan Zao hasn't revealed himself, he'll throw himself into battle. He'll seek death in combat, so I won't risk my life to rescue him again.”

Inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish, a faint smile curved Zi Di's lips. “Precisely so, my Lord!”

“Damn it.” The dragonman youngster clenched his fists, his heart filled with both urgency and helplessness.

He knew his own limitations.

Although he had slain Teng Dong Lang, the final blow remained unclear.

He understood that even if he created chaos and an opportunity to rescue others, he would inevitably face a siege and find himself in dire straits.

Lan Zao would most likely choose to “sacrifice” himself.

“What should I do?” The dragonman youngster felt utterly at a loss.

He stood rooted to the spot, scanning the battlefield with furrowed brows, his mind racing.

Suddenly, a flash of insight struck him. He spun around sharply; his gaze fixed on the magician tower at the dock.

He contacted Zi Di again, his voice brimming with excitement: "The magician tower hasn't attacked the Bayonet Gang members still lingering at sea. They pose little threat. The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord intends to use them to delay the Divine Wind Pirates!"

The dragonman youngster revealed the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's plan.

Since the Divine Wind Pirates hadn't immediately departed but remained at sea to retrieve more Bayonet Gang members, there was still a chance.

The dragonman youngster continued his analysis: "The city Lord had already reported his achievement in crushing the Bayonet Gang. This unexpected turn of events means that if the gang ringleaders escape, that achievement will be severely diminished."

“Since he hasn't resorted to slaughtering these ordinary gang members in a fit of rage, but is instead stalling for time, he must be devising a plan.”

Zi Di immediately signaled her agreement with the dragonman youngster's assessment.

The dragonman youngster took a deep breath, growing increasingly composed.

He was no longer the person he once was. His experiences in Snow Bird Harbor had forged him into a more capable leader.

He had learned to leverage power and momentum.

He had used the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's influence against the Bayonet Gang before—why couldn't he do the same now?

They were partners, and their collaboration had always been pleasant. More crucially, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord should be even more desperate than he was!

The dragonman youngster immediately used his communication device to contact the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. After a moment's deliberation, he spoke in a voice thick with unchecked fury: "City Lord! Are we just going to stand by and watch? Watch these damned pirates whisk away all these gang criminals?"

“They destroyed my flagship. My losses are catastrophic.”

“We can't let them escape!”

Yet the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's response caught the dragonman youngster off guard.

“I'm afraid we have no choice,” the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord said gravely.

“Why?” the dragonman youngster pressed immediately.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord admitted frankly, “Because of Feng Lian.”

“This human is no ordinary man. His combat prowess is formidable, far surpassing my own.”

"Pirates active near Ice Sculpture Island have all been battle-hardened. Our nation once dispatched a saint level expert to eliminate the Divine Wind Pirates but failed."

“What?” The dragonman youngster gasped, "Could Feng Lian possibly match a saint level expert?”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord chuckled, "Of course not.”

“A gold level who can rival a saint? The difficulty is astronomical—virtually impossible.”

“Back then, our saint finally cornered the Divine Wind Pirates and struck directly, executing a decapitation tactic against Feng Lian.”

“Feng Lian abandoned ship to escape, drawing our saint away from the naval battlefield.”

“In the end, he escaped gravely wounded, and most of the pirates aboard the Divine Wind and other ships also managed to flee.”

"After that battle, our nation elevated the threat level of the Divine Wind Pirates. We won't act unless absolutely necessary."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued, “As for Feng Lian himself, he is exceptionally cool-headed and cautious.”

“He rarely takes unnecessary risks.”

"He also knows when to be pragmatic, likely a lesson learned from the saint. Since that battle, the Divine Wind Pirates have become far more restrained under his leadership."

“They'll scarcely raid our merchant ships anymore, instead targeting other foreign vessels.”

“Let's be honest—I'd never risk a sea battle against him.”

“Because I'd likely be cut down by him!”

The dragonman youngster fell silent.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's candid words shook him to the core.

The dragonman youngster had witnessed the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's battle firsthand.

He weighed the comparison in his mind: based on what he knew, the Snow Bird Harbor Lord's combat prowess was at least on par with Teng Dong Lang's.

If one disregarded any hidden trump cards the Snow Bird Harbor Lord might possess, their fighting abilities were comparable.

Of course, it was highly probable that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's combat prowess surpassed Teng Dong Lang's.

The reason was simple.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had launched a lightning-swift operation to wipe out the Bayonet Gang, clearly targeting Teng Dong Lang.

Ultimately, since he had the confidence to lead his forces in surrounding the Snow Bird Arena, he must have been confident in suppressing Teng Dong Lang.

Yet this very gold level battle qi cultivator had directly warned the dragonman youngster that facing Feng Lian could cost him his life!

Feng Lian's strength was thus evident.

More and more gang members were being ferried aboard pirate ships like the Divine Wind.

Bayonet Gang members were overcome with gratitude toward the Divine Wind Pirates.

Many gang members instinctively gathered with familiar faces, some even sobbing uncontrollably.

Lan Zao grew increasingly anxious.

As an outsider, he had no gang members he knew personally.

The longer this dragged on, the more isolated he appeared, the greater he stood out.

His only relief was that the dragonman youngster remained stationary, not rashly attacking this location.

“He must be waiting for my reaction.”

“I have to do something to save myself!”

"Even if I die, it'd be a waste to go down like this. At least I'll put up a fight."

With that resolve, Lan Zao sprang into action.

Fueled by rage, he tried to persuade Spring Boxer to fire on Snow Bird Harbor's docks.

Spring Boxer shook his head in refusal: “Rescue our own people first. Firing now would provoke the patrol fleet into launching a massacre against our men—that would be disastrous!”

Bing Xiao soothed him: “Rest assured, one day we will exact our revenge.”

Seeing Lan Zao's face twisted with hatred and his eagerness for vengeance, Bing Xiao felt deeply gratified.

He'd witnessed parts of Lan Zao's earlier battles and recognized his fearless, fierce combat style.

More importantly, the indomitable spirit Lan Zao displayed deeply impressed Bing Xiao.

He surveyed the Bayonet Gang members before him. After being rescued, they'd all lost their nerve—some were even weeping openly. Even several ringleaders had gone quiet.

Only Lan Zao stood alone, vowing to fire back and counterattack.

The contrast was stark.

Bing Xiao turned to Lan Zao and asked with interest, “Kid, what's your name? Which leader do you serve under?”

Lan Zao dared not answer that question. Instead, he pressed, “Former gang leader, now that we've saved these people, can we fire the cannons?”

“We'll evacuate immediately,” the Divine Wind's first mate interjected upon hearing the exchange.

"Why?" Lan Zao's eyes widened.

He'd been hoping for a fight as it would be his chance to slip away.

The first mate sneered at Lan Zao, "Bronze level kid, don't get too cocky.”

“You lot owe your survival to our captain's authority.”

“If the magician tower decides to slaughter you, how many of you think you could swim to our ship?”

"The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hasn't given the order to attack, so we can't fire. This is the long-standing understanding between our group and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom!"

“Be grateful we saved your lot.”

“Do you really expect us to bombard one of the eight harbors, shatter this understanding, and provoke the Ice Sculpture Kingdom into directing its full fury against our pirate crew?”

Lan Zao was stunned.

Reality often felt ironic and absurd.

Who could have imagined the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and a pirate group reaching such an unspoken understanding?

At least Lan Zao hadn't.

The highest position he'd ever held was merely boatswain on a merchant ship.

His standing was too low.

Yet, upon reflection.

Behind this absurd reality lay a delicate coexistence born of mutual apprehension.

The Divine Wind Pirates' strength couldn't compare to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's, but the kingdom also avoided direct confrontation.

Surrounded by sea on all sides, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom possessed an excessively long coastline. Should the Divine Wind Pirates ever become desperate and launch a full-scale retaliation, it would cause immense trouble for the kingdom.

Yet after Feng Lian's successful escape from the kingdom's saint, the Divine Wind Pirates gained the standing necessary to forge this tacit understanding with the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

He proved himself much like how the dragonman youngster killed Teng Dong Lang.

This was the world of transcendents.

This was the world of the strong.

Chapter 590: Secret Operation

Snow Bird Harbor Dock.

The chaotic battle had calmed considerably.

The situation was now clear: both the Divine Wind Pirates and Snow Bird Harbor had exercised restraint, preventing the conflict from escalating further.

The dragonman youngster remained rooted to the spot for a moment, gazing at the Divine Wind and other pirate ships. His gaze was solemn, his expression impassive.

The tacit understanding between the Divine Wind Pirates and Snow Bird Harbor's Lord was something the dragonman youngster hadn't anticipated.

But after communicating with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, the dragonman youngster grasped this layer of understanding.

Only then did he realize his earlier misunderstanding—the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had genuinely chosen a conservative strategy, refraining from rash attacks.

Upon careful reflection, the dragonman youngster found the city Lord's approach entirely reasonable.

Recalling the Snow Bird Harbor Lord's modus operandi, he was adept at patience and restraint, using that time to prepare relentlessly. Once those preparations were complete, he would strike without hesitation. When he moved, it was with devastating force.

Conversely, the fact that the Snow Bird Harbor Lord exercised such restraint spoke volumes about the Divine Wind Pirates' formidable strength.

“Feng Lian... even a saint level powerhouse couldn't touch you?” the dragonman youngster sighed inwardly.

Gold level existences were rulers of a territory.

Saint level existences were pillars of the nation!

The entire Ice Sculpture Kingdom boasted only three saints.

Legend level powerhouses were even more formidable, possessing strength capable of toppling nations and wielding immense influence.

Take the War Merchant, for instance. He controlled the majority of the global arms market, stirring up conflicts everywhere, manipulating smaller nations at his whim.

“Compared to these powerhouses, I'm still far too weak.”

“If borrowing power fails, there's nothing I can do.”

“Therefore, the true foundation lies in one's own strength!”

The dragonman youngster craved power even more intensely.

But alas, a battle qi cultivator's cultivation could never be achieved overnight.

Though his innate talent was exceptional, it still demanded time and immense dedication to transform potential into tangible strength.

His current battle qi cultivation was merely silver level—not even a hint of gold luster had appeared yet.

“Zi Di, given the current situation, we need to adjust our plan.” Gathering his composure, the dragonman youngster contacted Zi Di.

She showed no surprise. Knowing him well, she understood he would never give up easily. Not even the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's refusal to fight could deter him.

Zi Di felt both helpless and reassured.

The dragonman youngster's unwavering commitment to never abandon any member had never changed.

He was the most reliable leader imaginable.

It was precisely this quality that earned him the survivors' unwavering trust.

No one but the dragonman youngster could fill the role of their leader.

Before the dragonman youngster could speak again, Zi Di took the initiative: "We must spare no effort to rescue Lan Zao. But we also need to reach the capital of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom as soon as possible."

“We've wasted time at Snow Bird Harbor, and we still haven't determined why we couldn't receive replies from Two Eyes Island.”

“Time is running out for us.”

“Only by contacting the Empire's higher-ups as soon as possible and atoning for our sins can we secure a stable environment for development.”

Zi Di emphasized the survivors' most crucial mission.

The dragonman youngster sighed, feeling torn.

The situation with Lan Zao was urgent. Without a quick rescue, he risked discovery and execution.

Contacting the Empire was even more critical—it determined the survival and future of all survivors.

After a moment of deep thought, the dragonman youngster declared, “It seems we must split our forces.”

“Zi Di, you know the Wisteria store best. Only you and Fei She should go. Besides, no one else is qualified for this task.”

"Therefore, you must proceed to the royal capital next. Take my alchemy puppet, disguised as me. After meeting up with Zong Ge, depart Snow Bird Harbor immediately."

"Regarding securing the potion market share, leave that to Bu Quan. Hand over our magic medicine bath tubs and such to her as well, increasing her production. As for the three newly joined silver level alchemists, keep Zhong Yong aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish. The other two should assist too."

“I, meanwhile, will pilot the Deep Sea Monster Fish, tracking beneath the sea in search of an opportunity to rescue Lan Zao.”

Zi Di's brows furrowed deeply.

Truthfully, she yearned to accompany the dragonman youngster on this mission.

She was profoundly concerned about his safety throughout this journey.

But there was no choice.

The dragonman youngster's arrangements had been meticulously planned, with personnel assigned logically.

Setting aside emotional considerations, this plan was nearly flawless when viewed rationally.

Zi Di had considered refusing and hesitated briefly, but ultimately chose to support the dragonman youngster, just as she had on Mystifying Monster Island.

“I understand. Please take every precaution!”

“If the situation becomes untenable, do not force the issue.”

“Even if Lan Zao were to sacrifice himself, he would never want to see you endangered because of him.”

"If you were to fall, I could not live on alone. The rest of us would crumble. No one could replace your leadership. Zong Ge falls far short of you—he lacks the authority to command our loyalty."

The dragonman youngster nodded slightly, sensing the profound care in Zi Di's words.

A wave of warmth washed over him. "I understand. You must stay safe too!"

However, just as the dragonman youngster sought an opportunity to dive into the Deep Sea Monster Fish, an envoy arrived bearing an invitation from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. Important matters demanded his presence.

The dragonman youngster glanced at the pirate ship in the distance, still receiving members of the Bayonet Gang. He thought: This is also my chance to bid farewell to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

So, he went.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord welcomed the dragonman youngster's arrival.

“Commander Long Fu, first I must apologize to you.”

“This time, the Bayonet Gang's escape caused losses to your group—it was my fault.”

“I can compensate for part of those losses.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's apology and offer of compensation softened much of the dragonman youngster's anger.

“Thank you, City Lord,” the dragonman youngster replied immediately.

He then sighed, "My flagship still requires your assistance to be repaired. Since I acquired it, it has never sustained such severe damage."

“Many of my men were also killed or wounded.”

“I must take revenge!”

“City Lord, are we truly to stand by and watch the pirate ships carry away these remnants of the Bayonet Gang?”

“We've sworn mortal enmity with them. If they return to seek vengeance, it'll be impossible to guard against.”

The dragonman youngster persisted in his efforts, attempting to persuade the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Feng Lian to engage in combat.

He was anxious for Lan Zao but couldn't voice it plainly.

To rescue a mere bronze level boatswain?

That justification wouldn't hold water.

In truth, Lan Zao's greatest vulnerability lay in the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's hands.

His magician tower recorded every detail of the battlefield.

The magic footage showed Lan Zao setting sail from the Love Triangle and embarking on a campaign of conquest. This was irrefutable proof of Lan Zao's identity.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord shook his head, patting the dragonman youngster on the shoulder, "I am the Lord of this city, its ruler.”

“Even if these ringleaders escaped, I've eradicated the Bayonet Gang—an achievement no previous Lord ever accomplished. My merit remains undeniable.”

"After my term ends, with some maneuvering, my position might even rise higher than it is now. Why would I risk it all?"

“There's no need to lock horns with Feng Lian's crew.”

“Commander Long Fu, your situation mirrors mine.” You've already made a name for yourself, and your talent is truly remarkable. Why not preserve your valuable life, actively grow, and build up your strength?"

“Don't let the blind fury of revenge cloud your judgment.”

The dragonman youngster's efforts had failed, only to be met with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's earnest and heartfelt advice.

The dragonman youngster's face darkened, yet the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's words held undeniable truth.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord continued, “I invited you here tonight to share some good news.”

“The ship you purchased has arrived.”

“It's a silver level magic ship—a military vessel from our nation that was ‘retired early’.”

“I believe it will make an excellent flagship!”

Upon hearing this, a thought flashed through the dragonman youngster's mind: Could this silver level magic ship be used to pursue the Divine Wind Pirates?

He immediately shook his head inwardly, dismissing this impractical notion.

Since his final effort had failed, the dragonman youngster exchanged a few polite words with the city Lord before formally taking his leave.

The city Lord showed no surprise.

He understood: the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had a mission to expand their market, and heading to the capital was the most crucial step in that endeavor.

After the dragonman youngster departed, an unfamiliar voice sounded through the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's communication device.

"If Long Fu seeks revenge, why not let him go? He can serve as cannon fodder for us."

Throughout their earlier conversation, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's communicator remained active.

He shook his head, "I deliberately let those ringleaders go precisely to plant spies.”

“I never imagined that right after planting them, we'd reel in a big fish like Feng Lian!”

"Hehe."

“This is the Divine Wind Pirates, after all.”

"Truthfully, I never intended to target them. But fate had it that you were the ones transporting that decommissioned warship."

“This matter must remain strictly confidential.”

“Before the official operation, Feng Lian must not catch wind of anything.”

“Long Fu... Their origins are unclear. They don't deserve our trust yet.”

On the Divine Wind.

“Kid.” Bing Xiao patted Lan Zao's shoulder, "You saved me back on that rocket assault boat.”

“So, what reward do you want?”

Bing Xiao gradually relaxed.

Most of the gang members had boarded the ship, and Snow Bird Harbor's threats had proven to be all bark and no bite.

Lan Zao glanced at Bing Xiao, thinking: How about your life?

But those words were absolutely forbidden to utter.

At least not now.

“I saw it with my own eyes—he killed Lao Dong!”

Chapter 591: Buying a Silver Level Warship

The accuser was merely a commoner, naturally too afraid to confront the bronze level Lan Zao.

Surrounding pirates and gang members scattered to avoid the chaos, allowing the iron level battle qi cultivator to roll toward the mast.

Bing Xiao growled, his face darkening.

Covered in blood, the iron level battle qi cultivator had been about to retaliate when Bing Xiao stopped him, leaving him seething with frustration.

Lan Zao glared at the iron level battle qi cultivator, continuing his provocation: “Your worthless trash! If I were at your cultivation level, you'd be dead already!”

His sole aim was to escalate the situation, spark chaos, and divert everyone's attention.

But Lan Zao's mind stirred.

Throw me overboard?

Perfect!

That night.

A small fleet entered Snow Bird Harbor's dock.

Among them was the decommissioned warship purchased by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Zi Di and the alchemy puppet disguised as the dragonman youngster boarded this vessel.

The warship was entirely white, measuring 112 meters in length with an average width of 31 meters. Its hull was nearly twice the size of the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Two masts stood: the central mainmast and the aft mast.

The lookout towers atop these masts were meticulously engineered alchemy assemblies—the mainmast housing a silver level spellcasting platform, the aft mast an iron level long-range firing platform.

The mainmast bore square sails, while the aft mast carried triangular sails.

74361

The warship boasted 74 gunports: 36 on each side of the hull, and one each at the bow and stern.

The cannons in these gunports were all common items—not a single bronze level one among them.

In fact, the same applies to the sails.

Originally, the warship's sails were all silver level, and its cannons were all alchemy cannons. However, these components had been dismantled and either repurposed for new warships or used as military supplies.

The primary subject of the transaction was this warship itself.

The Grand Seal Kinetic Reactor, the Ice Elemental Magic Pool, the Cold City Armor, and the Snow Honey Energy Storage Tank.

After thorough inspection, Zi Di and the 'dragonman youngster' formed their assessment.

“The Grand Seal Kinetic Reactor is an enhanced version of the Seal Kinetic Reactor. The latter is used in assault ships, emphasizing acceleration, but its performance in top speed, endurance, and energy consumption is mediocre.”

"The Ice Elemental Magic Pool has considerable capacity, specifically converting ice elements into magic power. The advantage of such specialized elemental magic pools lies in their higher conversion efficiency for a single element."

"The Snow Honey Energy Storage Tank is for attrition. ‘Snow honey’ is a peculiar resource. Humans can consume it, not only to satisfy hunger but also to replenish battle qi. Additionally, it can be used for ships—deploying them into magic pools can also convert them into magic power, though the conversion rate is lower than that of ice elements."

Zi Di now possessed extensive intelligence on the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Snow honey wasn't considered a specialty resource, but due to its high practical value, it became standard equipment for the kingdom's army and navy long ago.

"Among the four primary alchemy components, the most valuable is undoubtedly the Cold City Armor. This alchemy technology represents the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's third-generation military innovation, consistently demonstrating stable performance on the battlefield. Primarily providing physical defense, it is rarely sold separately on the market."

Beyond that, the platforms for spellcasting and archery on the two masts were also alchemy components, though secondary and easily removable.

In truth, they functioned as battlefield consumables.

A mercenary crew would need time to familiarize themselves with an unfamiliar warship before unleashing its full potential.

Installing gold level cannons would consume significant labor hours.

At least they now possessed a silver level magic ship. The Cold City Armor could safeguard the gold level cannons, preventing them from being easily destroyed by enemies as before.

Yet Zi Di felt little joy over acquiring the new ship.

Chapter 592: Ice Sculpture Navy vs Divine Wind Pirates

This ambush came far too suddenly.

Fifteen warships seemed to materialize out of thin air!

Even he had been caught off guard, let alone the pirates of the Divine Wind Pirates.

In truth, since departing Snow Bird Harbor, they had pushed their speed to near maximum.

At that very moment, a hail of cannonballs struck!

The Divine Wind Pirates suffered a devastating blow.

“These sails hold significant tactical value. If we could acquire them, perhaps we could equip our own ships.”

Whirlwinds swept in, intercepting the follow-up cannon fire.

Fifteen new warships encircled the three pirate vessels at the center.

Within the new warship, a gold level magician stood with her eyes closed, sustaining a spell.

Unlike most snow elves, she was not thin, in fact, she was quite plump.

Kong Pan Li Jian stood with her eyes slightly closed, holding a magic artifact in both hands.

This artifact resembled an empty white dinner plate.

This was her primary spellcasting artifact, enabling her to lock down a specific spatial area.

It was indeed Kong Pan's spellcasting that caused the battlefield space to warp, temporarily trapping the three ships of the Divine Wind Pirates.

Feng Lian didn't engage them in prolonged combat. He charged deep into the ship, then swiftly flew back out.

He had lunged at nothing.

It was a trap. An alchemy device inside the ship had mimicked Kong Pan's magic aura, causing Feng Lian to misjudge the situation.

It was a trap. An alchemy device inside the ship had mimicked Kong Pan's magic aura, causing Feng Lian to misjudge the situation.

Yue Qian stormed the Divine Wind, unleashing his own storm of carnage.

In a solitary cabin.

Chapter 593: Sand Between Fingers

The tornado absorbed nearly all the magic attacks, swelling to several times its original size and bursting into a myriad of colors.

Witnessing this spectacle, the dragonman youngster gained a profound understanding of the Divine Wind Pirates' formidable strength.

“To operate as pirates near Ice Sculpture Island, this crew possesses power beyond the ordinary.”

“The Ice Sculpture Kingdom is a maritime power renowned for its magic and alchemy. The Divine Wind Pirates excel at defensive magic and likely possess countermeasures against alchemy devices too.”

The dragonman youngster observed the battle, drawing lessons from the unfolding events.

When the ambushing navy first appeared, the dragonman youngster had assumed the Divine Wind Pirates, outnumbered and caught off guard, would be overwhelmed. Yet they executed two outstanding offensive and defensive maneuvers.

The battle continued to shift.

The tornado began to move slowly.

It shifted from above the Divine Wind, turning toward the naval vessels.

Originally surrounded by a barrel-shaped wind barrier formed by the tornado, the Divine Wind now risked its hull piercing through this barrier as it shifted.

The tornado had absorbed a massive amount of magic attacks, and the force of these assaults was far from dissipating. For the Divine Wind to pass through, it would inevitably endure another barrage of magic attacks.

Yet the next scene once again surpassed the dragonman youngster's expectations—and those of the entire naval force.

Beneath the tornado, magic power was being drawn upward by a powerful gravitational force.

Originally thick at both ends and narrow in the middle, the tornado now bulged at its center, tapering slightly at the top and bottom. Most crucially, while the middle and upper sections still swirled with vibrant, multicolored magic power, the lower portion had transformed into a pale blue, pure wind.

“What kind of alchemy technique is this, capable of channeling magic power to such an extent!” Kong Pan couldn't help but reveal a look of astonishment.

They relied on their new warship, while the Divine Wind Pirates brought out a trump card they had been hiding all along.

The Divine Wind pierced through the wind barrier. Throughout the process, it was merely suppressed by the raging winds, causing its draft to deepen slightly.

Pirates on deck had obeyed orders to lie prone or dive below deck in advance.

The next instant, healing light enveloped these stricken figures.

Soon, they too were back on their feet, full of life.

Seeing this, the dragonman youngster's pupils narrowed slightly: “Divine spell?”

The vast majority of the Divine Wind Pirates worshipped the Sea Wind God. The ship carried priests of the Sea Wind God.

Though their numbers were scarce, their presence made all the difference!

Clergy almost invariably possessed healing abilities.

On the battlefield, timely divine healing spells served not only their curative purpose but also wielded immense power to boost morale and steady nerves!

The Divine Wind broke free from the tornado's obstruction. Its movement gradually accelerated as it charged toward the naval forces.

During its shift, the tornado resumed its characteristic shape—thick at both ends and narrow in the middle—while its multicolored magic power once again spread across its lower section.

It resembled a dancer in a puffy skirt, first lifting the multicolored hem slightly before letting it fall again

But the magic assault was once more absorbed and swallowed by the tornado, only strengthening its destructive force.

His left eye was covered by a circular lens.

One of the primary functions of this gold level artifact was magic vision, allowing Yue Qian to see through the core of most spells.

Once Yue Qian identified the core, he could strike it directly, shattering the magic with his slender sword.

“Cease magic attacks on the tornado!” Upon receiving Yue Qian's critical intelligence, Kong Pan immediately issued the order, “Deploy the breaching shells!”

“But sir, our ship isn't equipped with such ammunition.”

Kong Pan was slightly taken aback.

Their primary mission this time was to test the operational effectiveness of the new warship.

Transporting the “decommissioned warship” to Snow Bird Harbor was merely a side job.

One of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's influential connections was the Li Jian clan.

Naturally, a test deployment wouldn't carry a large supply of ammunition. Before the Second Naval Fleet set sail, they never anticipated ambushing the Divine Wind Pirates here.

Thus, their military preparations were far from adequate.

Moreover, breaching shells like these were designed for storming fortified positions. Their utility in naval warfare is extremely limited—they're only stockpiled in large quantities when planning to bombard ports with warship artillery.

Yue Qian declared, "My glasses have locked onto that alchemy artifact. It's a pity Yun Zhong didn't join us. Otherwise, with my help, a single arrow could pierce this thing."

“Let me handle it.”

The slender sword in Yue Qian's hand glowed with a faint radiance.

Elven bloodlines were renowned for producing archers and magicians.

Snow elves shared this trait.

Yet when it comes to specific lineages, differences emerge.

The Li Jian clan, bearing the Inner World bloodline, has long lacked outstanding archers of their own. They boasted many close combatants and spatial magicians.

But for archers, they had to recruit talent from outside.

Yun Zhong was currently the most renowned archer in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Yet Yun Zhong, with his free-spirited nature, had declined the Li Jian clan's lavish recruitment offer.

Yue Qian was about to tear open a spatial rift and charge directly into the tornado when 'Sand Between Fingers' spoke up to halt him: “Hold on a moment. Let me make a prediction first.”

Sand Between Fingers was not a name, but a title.

This female magician resided aboard another warship, shielded from detection by its protective wards that concealed all magic activity.

Sand Between Fingers possessed blue eyes and crimson, wavy hair. Her elven ears were adorned with soft, orange-yellow fur.

Her skin wasn't the typical snow-white of pure snow elves, but rather a warm, wheat-like hue.

She wasn't a pure-blooded snow elf, but a hybrid of snow elf and foxman.

Sand Between Fingers plucked a small handful of sand from a blue pouch at her slender waist.

She raised her hand, continuously rubbing her fingers together as the sand slowly drifted down before her eyes.

This was the time sand, a rare magic ingredient.

Sand Between Fingers used it as the primary material for her spell.

The grains drifted down through the air, shimmering with a faint white glow.

The light twisted, forming ripples like swirling mist.

Through the mist, Sand Between Fingers glimpsed the unfolding battle.

Her expression shifted, "Yue Qian, don't strike it! I see it—this eyeball can self-detonate. When it explodes, it activates a spatial anchor, locking down the surrounding area."

“It's a trap. If you go in, you'll be gravely injured if not killed!”

Yue Qian's expression darkened, and he immediately abandoned his attack plan. “I'll just harass Feng Lian instead.”

“Be careful,” Kong Pan cautioned, “Feng Lian is very powerful, stronger than you. Don't get too entangled in battle.”

After issuing the warning, Kong Pan spurred Sand Between Fingers onward: "Well done, San Sha Man Tuo. You've just earned a merit. Can you contain this tornado?"

San Sha Man Tuo was Sand Between Fingers' real name. Clearly, she wasn't a member of the Li Jian clan but had been recruited.

If Yun Zhong agreed to join them, his role within the Second Navy would be similar to San Sha Man Tuo's.

San Sha Man Tuo nodded, "I can do it, but it will consume a great deal of time sand.”

Kong Pan immediately replied, "It doesn't matter. All materials expended will be reimbursed—never less than what was used. The Li Jian clan never shortchanges its own."

Having received the assurance she sought, San Sha Man Tuo sprang into action.

She released over a dozen alchemy hummingbirds, which swiftly flew toward the tornado.

They plunged directly into the vortex, each detonating to scatter vast quantities of time sand.

San Sha Man Tuo seized the moment to cast her spell. The time magic took effect swiftly.

The tornado's rotational speed and movement both slowed dramatically.

“Time magic?!”

“It must be that Sand Between Fingers casting it.”

The dragonman youngster stared in astonishment.

Time magic was exceptionally rare, even more so than spatial magic.

Yet now, the dragonman youngster witnessed it firsthand.

Beyond broadening his horizons, it filled him with awe: “The Ice Sculpture Kingdom truly lives up to its reputation as an elven kingdom—even time magicians exist here.”

On the surface, the tornado appeared to slow down, but in reality, the flow of time within it had become sluggish.

Feng Lian watched this scene unfold, his expression growing grave.

Truthfully, among the three commanders of the Second Fleet, it wasn't Kong Pan or Yue Qian he feared most—it was San Sha Man Tuo.

If he were struck by such a time spell during his fierce battle with Yue Qian, causing his movements to become “sluggish,” the consequences were truly unimaginable.

This signature time magic left Feng Lian utterly convinced that San Sha Man Tuo was also within the navy.

Earlier, although Sand Between Fingers had spoken, he hadn't acted—there was still a possibility it was a bluff.

The time magic completely extinguished Feng Lian's lingering hope.

“No wonder the Second Fleet had the confidence to ambush me. It's not just the new warships—the crucial factor is that all three gold level commanders are present!”

Feng Lian took a deep breath and shot skyward, soaring into the heavens.

He channeled his battle qi to its fullest, unleashing a terrifying aura.

“Watch out! He's going all out!” Kong Pan immediately warned.

Yue Qian sneered, eager for the fight, “Then let me see Feng Lian's true strength!”

He vanished through the air, reappearing behind Feng Lian in an instant.

Without even turning his head, Feng Lian casually swung his arm.

The long-handled scythe sliced through the sky, forming a three-meter-long azure wind scythe.

Yue Qian's slender sword collided with the wind scythe. The next moment, his expression changed drastically as he was sent flying backward.

But mid-air, he vanished once more.

The dragonman youngster's expression also shifted slightly.

Feng Lian had begun to unleash his full power, and Yue Qian was no match for him after a single strike. No wonder the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord held Feng Lian in such high regard!

The dragonman youngster sensed: “The decisive moment of this ambush has arrived.”

Chapter 594: Wind Blade - Heart of Freedom

The dragonman youngster had seen his share of battlefields.

Summarizing these experiences, he recognized that the decisive factor in determining the outcome of a war was the presence of peak powerhouses.

In this ambush naval battle, Feng Lian, Yue Qian, Kong Pan, and San Sha Man Tuo were all gold level—the pinnacle of strength on the battlefield.

Undoubtedly, only the outcome of their clash would determine the battle's result.

Feng Lian hovered high above, drew a deep breath, and disregarding his battle qi depletion, unleashed his full power!

Battle skill—Wind Blade: Continuous Slash.

Battle skill—Wind Blade: Gigantic Slash.

Battle skill—Wind Blade: Swift Slash.

He simultaneously activated three battle skills.

He swung his long-handled scythe, each strike unleashing a wind blade.

The blades streaked through the air, rapidly enlarging until they reached over 10 meters in length!

Wave after wave of azure wind blades crashed against the resilient, transparent spatial barrier.

With every strike, Kong Pan's face paled further.

Within mere seconds, Feng Lian had delivered over a dozen strikes, leaving Kong Pan's complexion as pale as if struck by a severe illness.

"Quick! Stop him!" Kong Pan called for backup.

Yue Qian had already acted first, teleporting behind Feng Lian.

Feng Lian, prepared, spun instantly, his scythe slashing wildly.

Clang! Clang! Clang!

Yue Qian unleashed his own battle skill, his slender sword erupting in dazzling flashes of light.

White sword beams collided with azure wind blades, slicing through the air with a series of shrill, metallic clangs.

Three seconds later, Yue Qian could no longer hold on.

Yet retreat was impossible. He had to buy Kong Pan time to catch her breath and tend her wounds.

Battle skill—Five Stage: Aerial Assault!

Yue Qian retreated, vanishing into another dimension.

Instantly, he materialized beside Feng Lian, extending his slender sword.

Battle qi surged through the blade, especially its tip, sharpening it to a blinding edge that glinted like sunlight.

Feng Lian raised his other hand, casting a spell.

A magic wind shield abruptly formed, blocking the first strike.

Yue Qian's strike missed, and he retreated into the space behind him, vanishing.

When he reappeared, he was already behind Feng Lian.

He thrust the sword forward once more.

Feng Lian's brow furrowed.

He recognized the Aerial Assault battle skill. It wasn't exclusive to the Li Jian clan, but a classic spatial-type attack technique, combining teleportation and assault.

Employing this battle skill allowed the battle qi cultivator to burst through the air, instantly materializing elsewhere. The first attack after such a displacement carried the special effect of spatial penetration, granting it immense destructive power.

Yue Qian's Five Stage: Aerial Assault comprised five successive positional shifts and attacks.

Feng Lian could not simply stand by.

Yet if he became tired from defense, he would fall into the enemy's trap, squandering precious opportunities!

Feng Lian shouted, unleashing a fourth battle skill on top of the three already active.

Wind Blade: Chaos!

His attack speed surged fivefold in an instant as he wildly swung his scythe.

His arms and scythe blurred into countless dark shadows in the air.

Whoosh! Whoosh! Whoosh!

Nearly a hundred wind blades erupted, unleashing a terrifying assault that engulfed the surrounding area—above, below, and all around.

Yue Qian dared not continue his attack.

He swiftly retreated from Feng Lian using Aerial Assault, landing on a naval warship.

"Damn it! We can't defend this place." Horror gripped him as he watched nearly a hundred gigantic wind blades slash violently against the surrounding spatial barrier.

Yue Qian knew Kong Pan's capabilities well. Facing such a terrifying assault, Kong Pan simply couldn't withstand it.

However, miraculously, Kong Pan held firm.

The spatial barrier remained intact!

Feng Lian's brow furrowed deeply, and Yue Qian felt puzzled.

“I cast a delay spell!” San Sha Man Tuo's voice rang in Yue Qian's ear, “This wave of attacks won't take effect until later.”

“I'm buffing you with this spell too. Hurry and stall Feng Lian! We can't let him keep attacking like this!”

Sand Between Fingers' tone was urgent.

The time magic settled upon Yue Qian, causing diamond-like sparks to occasionally flicker across his skin.

Bolstered by this newfound resilience, Yue Qian charged at Feng Lian once more.

Feng Lian, clashing with him, soon realized his attacks had no effect whatsoever on Yue Qian.

Yue Qian fought with reckless abandon, each strike being a desperate gamble with his life.

Though Feng Lian's strength clearly surpassed his, he struggled to counter such relentless tactics and could only focus on defense.

Seizing this opening, Kong Pan decisively ordered the fleet to launch a full-scale assault.

The naval fleet, which had maintained distance from the Divine Wind and other pirate ships, immediately responded to the command. Half the vessels surged forward like a swarm of sharks, charging toward the pirates' three ships.

The pirates fired cannons in resistance, but their efforts proved largely ineffective.

As the navy's new warships closed in for collision, the first mate activated two alchemy components in succession.

The first component coalesced a massive chain of azure air currents between the three pirate ships.

The chain fused the three pirate vessels into a single entity.

The second alchemy component unleashed a violent gale that swept outward, forming a constantly expanding wind barrier.

The naval warships were all pushed back by the barrier.

But the navy had anticipated this.

Both alchemy components had been exposed long ago, and the navy possessed this intelligence.

After closing the distance, they began deploying their troops.

Propelled by the warships' teleportation devices, the squad materialized directly onto the decks of the three pirate ships.

The Avalanche Squad thus revealed itself to the dragonman youngster's gaze.

This was the most renowned battle squad of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Its members were almost exclusively composed of snow elves.

Though snow elves were typically slender and tall, the Avalanche Squad members were each “ball-like in their plumpness.”

This was due to their peculiar alchemy armor, known as Snowball Armor.

Once equipped and activated, this full-body armor caused the snow elves' surfaces to absorb surrounding frost elements, enveloping them and transforming them into snowmen.

Consequently, Avalanche Squad members stood over 2 meters tall, with waists as thick as barrels, resembling miniature giants.

The pirates launched a coordinated assault on these blue-and-white snowmen, but the Snowball Armor proved exceptionally thick. Their attacks barely made a dent, at most chipping away flakes of snow.

The moment the assault paused or weakened, the snowmen's surfaces rapidly regenerated.

The Snowball Armor was also self-repairing equipment.

The Avalanche Squad charged across the deck. Some rolled through arrows and blades, crushing pirates beneath them. Others formed snowballs and hurled them indiscriminately. Wherever a snowball struck, it froze into human-sized blocks of ice, sealing sails, ropes, and cannon barrels.

Though the pirate ship had silver level members, no Avalanche Squad member fell despite their relentless, death-defying assaults.

Their coordination was flawless.

Whenever a member's Snowball Armor was worn down to its limit, nearby teammates immediately reinforced them, transferring ice elements from their own bodies.

The reinforced squad member's armor regeneration spiked severalfold. Within seconds, fully restored, they were back in action!

Such defense left the pirates despairing.

The Avalanche Squad rampaged through the ship, some launching assaults on the cabins to stir up even greater chaos.

“The moment has come!” Seeing this, the dragonman youngster sprang into action.

He slipped away from the Deep Sea Monster Fish, diving silently all the way into the Divine Wind.

Before his operation, he'd employed the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell to disguise himself as one of the pirates.

The utter chaos made his infiltration remarkably smooth

He chose not to disguise himself as a snowman for two reasons: frequent squad training had made members highly familiar with each other, and transforming into a snowman would inevitably draw the pirates' attacks.

The dragonman youngster probed the ship's hold, searching for Lan Zao, when his alchemy device sounded an alarm.

After his departure, the tower spirit took control of the ship.

The tower spirit informed the dragonman youngster that a large fleet of ships had appeared at the edge of the battlefield, charging straight toward them.

“It's our people!” the pirate first mate shouted ecstatically.

The pirates' morale soared.

The main force of the Divine Wind Pirates arrived in timely support.

They inserted themselves into the fray, activating their alchemy components.

Chains of wind linked the pirate ships together, coalescing into a surge of azure wind that surged toward the Divine Wind.

The blue wind was halted by the spatial barrier.

Feng Lian snorted coldly, gripping the scythe with both hands for the first time. He straightened his arms and raised it above his head.

Wind Blade: Great Slash!

Swish.

A faint sound echoed as a hundred-meter-long wind blade sliced through the air.

Kong Pan's heart raced with warning: “This attack must not be met head-on! Even with time magic to delay its impact, I'd still be doomed!”

She had no choice but to cancel the spell.

Without the spatial barrier, the azure wind currents surged toward the Divine Wind, rapidly coalescing into a massive, thick wind chain.

Two more wind chains connected the Divine Wind to the other two pirate ships.

The chains sprang into action, pulling all three vessels outward.

Even without their sails, the immense pulling force propelled the pirates to terrifying speeds.

The dragonman youngster, still searching for Lan Zao in the hold, was caught off guard. The sudden movement of the Divine Wind nearly knocked him off balance, threatening to send him tumbling.

“They can't escape!” Kong Pan barked out the order, “Avalanche Squad, activate the snow giant!”

The Avalanche Squad members immediately gathered in one spot, piling atop each other to form a pyramid-shaped human tower.

Ice and snow elements swiftly spread across their armor, coalescing into a unified force.

Through the combined effect of their battle skills, they merged into a snow giant over ten meters tall.

The colossal snowman delivered a brutal shove, snapping the Divine Wind's main mast.

Another stomp crushed a gaping hole through the deck.

Just as the snow giant prepared to unleash further fury, Feng Lian flashed into position above its head.

Yue Qian appeared in time to intercept Feng Lian.

Without so much as a glance, Feng Lian remained impassive as he unleashed his trump card battle skill.

Wind Blade: Heart of Freedom!

Closing his eyes, he exhaled a breath toward his scythe.

A shadowy human form materialized from his body, dragging a phantom scythe. It sliced lightly straight ahead.

A small, emerald-green wind qi swirled out—no more than 30 centimeters long. It stood in stark contrast to the earlier terrifying, raging giant wind blade.

Yue Qian's eyes widened in shock as he sensed the scent of death within that breeze.

He scrambled to retreat through the air, returning to the warship in the next instant.

“How...?” He hadn't evaded the attack. Horrified, he watched as a strand of emerald wind qi burrowed into his chest.

The emerald wind qi split into strands, seeping into the snowball giant's body.

The snowball giant disintegrated, leaving countless casualties among the squad members.

One strand of wind qi drifted below deck, reaching the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster smashed through the hull and plunged into the sea.

The wind, deceptively swift, pierced his back.

The dragonman youngster's body convulsed violently, then went limp. Eyes wide with fury, he sank to the ocean floor.

Boom!

The Divine Wind's speed erupted fully. Dragged by the massive wind chain, the entire ship leapt half a meter above the sea surface.

The next instant, the Divine Wind plunged back into the ocean, churning up a massive wave.

Towing the other two battered ships, it successfully broke through the encirclement and sped away.

Chapter 595: A Traitor’s Communication Device

Deploying the kingdom’s newest warships, they executed a flawless ambush. Three gold level commanders besieged Feng Lian alone. Yet the losers of this naval battle were none other than the Ice Sculpture Kingdom Navy’s Second Fleet!

Watching the Divine Wind Pirates sail away triumphantly, the naval officers fell silent.

Kong Pan and the other two commanders gathered together, each wearing an extremely grim expression. This outcome differed drastically from their initial expectations!

Suddenly, Kong Pan coughed up a mouthful of blood.

The time was nearing. Feng Lian’s attack would soon manifest its effects on Kong Pan’s body.

“Feng Lian... this man hid his true strength deeply. His actual combat power far exceeds what intelligence reported,” Kong Pan gritted out, her voice icy.

“My situation is even worse. I can barely hold on!” Yue Qian's face was the palest. His voice trembled as he explained to the other two how he had been struck by Wind Blade: Heart of Freedom.

Kong Pan's expression shifted slightly. She immediately examined Yue Qian's condition alongside Sand Between Fingers.

Both of them then revealed a hint of horror.

"Wind Blade: Heart of Freedom... Feng Lian used this battle skill during his battle against our nation's saint. To think its power has grown to such an extent by now!"

Sand Between Fingers gasped, "The time magic I cast on you is rapidly fading! I've never encountered such a situation."

Knowing his end was near, Yue Qian cast a wavering glance toward Kong Pan.

Kong Pan was his elder and the de facto leader of the Li Jian clan. Yue Qian, meanwhile, was the foremost figure among the Li Jian clan's rising generation.

Kong Pan would never stand by and let Yue Qian die!

Yue Qian was the pillar of the clan's future—he had already proven himself. His death would deal a devastating blow to the Li Jian clan. Not only would morale plummet, but they would also lose crucial future support.

The Li Jian clan boasted three gold level powerhouses among its core members.

Kong Pan and the current clan leader were both gold level, though advanced in age.

Should they pass away, the clan would rely on Yue Qian to hold it together.

As for outsiders like Sand Between Fingers, they were merely recruited additions, a nice bonus, but not a crucial reliance.

Without hesitation, Kong Pan set aside her own injuries and threw herself into aiding Yue Qian.

“Don't panic! Stay calm!”

“I'll cast a spell to strengthen your defenses.”

"San Sha Man Tuo, can you still cast a delay spell for Yue Qian? We need time now!"

“Of course!” Sand Between Fingers answered without hesitation, "I can buff him three more times, but after that, my primary spell component—time sand—will be depleted. Without this crucial material, I cannot cast spells."

“Excellent.” Kong Pan nodded, "The clan will replenish your consumed time sand afterward. Regardless of Yue Qian's survival, your contribution will be duly recorded."

Yue Qian was speechless, his gaze toward Kong Pan tinged with a hint of resentment.

The situation was urgent; Kong Pan had no time to tend to Yue Qian's emotions. He was already an adult.

Kong Pan ordered the fleet to sail at full speed toward Snow Bird Harbor, while directing the accompanying clergy to gather around Yue Qian.

Time slipped away rapidly, each second an agonizing torment for Yue Qian.

Soon, San Sha Man Tuo cast spells repeatedly until the time sand reserves fell below the threshold for another incantation.

Yue Qian entered the meditation chamber.

As the delay spell neared exhaustion, he had already unleashed every defensive battle skill at his command.

Moreover, several gold level defensive artifacts stood guard over him.

Kong Pan strained to bolster his defenses.

Ten of the most powerful clergymen—some sustaining defensive spells, others casting healing magic—all channeled their power into Yue Qian.

“Come on!” Yue Qian roared, rallying himself, “I refuse to believe I can't hold out like this!”

The next instant, the delay spell vanished completely.

The full might of Wind Blade: Heart of Freedom erupted.

Despite being bathed in radiant light, Yue Qian's eyes rolled back as he collapsed lifelessly to the ground.

Dead.

Plop.

Kong Pan coughed up another mouthful of blood.

All ten clergymen collapsed, each suffering varying degrees of backlash.

“Lord Kong Pan!” Sand Between Fingers, who had pushed her limits casting spells, rushed to support the captain.

Kong Pan gritted her teeth, her face twisted with dread, "This battle skill doesn't just use battle qi. Ordinary gold level cultivators would die instantly. Feng Lian... truly a terrifying individual..."

Regaining her footing with effort, Kong Pan asked Sand Between Fingers, “How far are we from Snow Bird Harbor now?”

“Only five nautical miles,” San Sha Man Tuo replied immediately.

“Excellent,” Kong Pan chuckled, “Though we failed, Yue Qian can still live!”
