The Second Naval Fleet practically charged into Snow Bird Harbor's dock.

The Snow Bird Harbor Lord had received the communication long ago and prepared everything in advance.

Before the warship docked, he and the Sea Bird Bishop hurriedly leapt aboard and entered the meditation chamber.

"I entrust this to you, Bishop! If you revive Yue Qian, you shall be the most honored guest of the Li Jian clan!" Kong Pan's expression was solemn as she pledged her promise.

The Sea Bird Bishop bowed respectfully, his tone humble: “All is by the grace and power of our Lord!”

Though also a gold level, his standing within the kingdom clearly paled beside that of Kong Pan, captain of the Second Naval Fleet.

The bishop's reply was masterfully crafted, avoiding any bold guarantees of success.

Whether failure or triumph, all was the will of the Sea Bird God.

Fortunately, the Sea Bird Bishop's resurrection spell succeeded.

Yue Qian slowly opened his eyes. His gaze remained vacant for a moment before clearing.

“I died?”

“Yes. The Sea Bird Bishop revived you.”

Yue Qian hastily rose to express his gratitude. His face was deathly pale; his relief mingled with profound dread.

Kong Pan exhaled a heavy breath. As her guard dropped, darkness instantly clouded her vision, nearly causing her to collapse on the spot.

The others rushed to aid her, casting healing spells that alleviated some of her condition, though she remained listless.

Though unharmed physically, her spirit was utterly drained. The previous naval battle had exhausted both her mental fortitude and magic reserves.

“I never imagined Feng Lian's strength could be so formidable!” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord spoke with a heavy tone, observing the three gold level naval commanders in such a battered state.

Kong Pan glanced at him and cautioned, "Feng Lian's intellect is also extraordinary. Given his cautious nature, he will surely realize there's a mole among the pirates. You must be prepared."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's face darkened once more. He nodded slightly and said nothing further.

Over at the Divine Wind Pirates.

The pirates cheered and rejoiced at their escape.

Feng Lian gathered his first mate and other subordinates for a discussion.

Bing Xiao was among them.

Feng Lian scanned the group, "The navy's new warships excel at stealth, but their ability to ambush us in advance means they knew our movements.”

“Such precision is highly unusual.”

The first mate's eyes widened, "Captain, are you saying we have a mole among us?”

Feng Lian nodded, looking at Bing Xiao, "Highly likely.”

Bing Xiao's heart sank abruptly.

Sure enough, the next moment, the pirate captains all shot him malicious glances.

The pirates naturally trusted their own men, but the Bayonet Gang members had only just boarded the ship—there was no foundation for trust.

The first mate spoke with a sinister edge: “Lord Bing Xiao, what do you suggest we do?”

“A thorough investigation—a complete investigation!” Bing Xiao roared the words without hesitation, his face twisted with fury, displaying an uncompromising stance against the traitor.

Feng Lian forced a thin smile: "Not just the Bayonet Gang—we must search ourselves too. Secure everyone first, then strip-search them one by one. I need to find the tracking device!"

In the recent battle, Feng Lian had single-handedly taken on three foes, displaying formidable prowess.

Within his own pirate crew, he held supreme authority.

Thus, his command was obeyed without question.

Pirates scoured every nook and cranny of the three ships, conducting thorough body searches on everyone.

Bayonet Gang members were herded onto the deck. Even Lan Zao, who had been lying unconscious in the hold, was dragged out.

“Commander, we found an alchemy communication device!!! We seized it from the Bayonet Gang," the pirate reported excitedly.

Bing Xiao's expression instantly turned grim.

Alchemy communication devices commanded a hefty price tag—ordinary gang members wouldn't invest heavily in acquiring one.

Even if they once owned one, for this voyage to flee the port, they'd likely sell it cheaply to acquire more useful weapons or vessels.

Such a device almost certainly marked the traitor.

Bing Xiao demanded immediately, “Who is it?”

The pirate shook his head, “I don't know his name, but Lord Bing Xiao should recognize him.”

Feng Lian sneered coldly, pushing open the captain's cabin door, "Come. Let's go see for ourselves.”

Bing Xiao hurried to follow.

Chapter 596: Acting As Long Fu?

The pirate fleet plowed through the waves.

On the Divine Wind, fresh sails were already unfurled.

On deck, members of the Bayonet Gang huddled together, surrounded by pirates radiating murderous intent.

Feng Lian, Bing Xiao, and others approached the Bayonet Gang members.

Two pirates escorted a captured gang member.

“Captain, the device was found on him.”

“Kneel!”

A pirate kicked the prisoner's knee from behind, forcing him to the ground.

Bing Xiao's brow furrowed instantly upon recognizing the man, "Mu Si, you betrayed us?”

The gang member named Mu Si looked at Bing Xiao with a complex expression. His lips moved, but no words came out.

Bing Xiao turned his gaze to Feng Lian.

Feng Lian was examining the alchemy device in his hand.

This communication device had been retrieved from Mu Si's stomach.

After analysis, it was confirmed not only to provide real-time location tracking but also long-range communication capabilities.

The location it tracked was Mu Si's precise personal position.

The moment Mu Si boarded the Divine Wind; the ship's positioning data was exposed.

Feng Lian shifted his gaze from the alchemy device to Mu Si but quickly turned it back to Bing Xiao.

“Bing Xiao, what's your take?” Feng Lian's tone was flat.

Bing Xiao weighed his words carefully before replying gravely, “Mu Si is an iron level veteran member of the gang. He's a compulsive gambler with no spare funds. It's impossible he'd purchase such an alchemy device without cause.”

“This alchemy device is compact and can be concealed within the body. It's clearly a specialized tool for espionage.”

“The evidence is sufficient.”

“He is the traitor!”

Bing Xiao paused before continuing, “Lord Feng Lian, the entire Bayonet Gang is eternally grateful for your personal assistance in our rescue.”

“Now, with a traitor within our ranks who nearly compromised your mission, I feel profoundly ashamed.”

“We will compensate you—specifically, double the gold promised for this mission. Is that acceptable?”

“As for this damned traitor, we leave his fate entirely to your discretion!”

"As for us... simply drop us off on any inhabited island nearby. We can build our own boat and continue our journey."

Feng Lian was somewhat surprised: “Seems you hold deep loyalty to your gang.”

Bing Xiao's face was grim as he shook his head: "We failed. Precisely because of that, we must hold fast to our principles! If we abandon everything now, we become utter failures. Even the dead Teng Dong Lang would look down on us."

“Hmm...” Feng Lian was about to speak when an abrupt change occurred.

Mu Si suddenly began bleeding from the corner of his mouth.

Soon, purple poisonous blood flowed from his eyes, ears, and nostrils.

Within two breaths, his entire complexion turned purplish-black, and all signs of life vanished.

“What the hell did you do?!” The first mate roared, his eyes blazing as he glared at the two pirates escorting the prisoner.

The pirates shouted back in panic, “Impossible!”

“We clearly suppressed him with our battle qi! He couldn't resist at all.”

“There's no device on him either—you personally inspected him with the ship's alchemy components, First Mate!”

Feng Lian channeled his battle qi.

Golden battle qi swiftly extended, seeping into the purplish-black corpse.

When Feng Lian withdrew it, his battle qi had turned purple.

He severed the connection immediately, letting the contaminated purple qi dissipate.

“That's potent poison—even golden battle qi gets corrupted.”

“This isn't your fault.”

"This traitor ingested poison before the mission. Now that the time has come, the toxin killed him!"

Feng Lian uncovered the truth.

The two escorting pirates instantly relaxed as if pardoned, flattering Feng Lian in relief.

The first mate glared, "He took poison before the operation? Was he a willing sacrificial fighter, or a forced unlucky bastard?"

Feng Lian remained silent, scanning the room instead.

The rest of the gang members were as quiet as mice, their faces showing fear and confusion.

Their expressions showed no flaw.

Feng Lian fell deep in thought: “We've found one mole, but are there others?”

Whether Mu Si was a willing sacrificial fighter or a forced victim, he had died from the poison.

The ruthless decisiveness displayed by whoever planned this operation was chilling.

Feng Lian leaned toward the belief that another traitor existed!

“Mu Si ingested poison before the operation, yet there was no trace of an antidote on his person.”

"The antidote must be held by another mole. With the antidote, this mole could effectively control Mu Si."

"If Mu Si died suddenly from the poison without receiving the antidote, it would inevitably create a suspicious case and draw investigation. Therefore, if I were the one orchestrating the mole's infiltration, I would never make such a foolish arrangement."

"Of course, if the mastermind merely intended to deploy a disposable mole—a sacrificial pawn to serve the earlier naval ambush—then a poison-induced death would be entirely reasonable."

Feng Lian turned his gaze to Bing Xiao.

Bing Xiao looked deeply troubled.

The two exchanged a glance, each understanding the other's deepest concern.

Bing Xiao had grasped this truth even before Feng Lian's inquiry.

It was highly probable that other traitors lurked within the gang!

Yet Bing Xiao used carefully measured words to present Feng Lian with his choice: to lead the Bayonet Gang members away.

The Bayonet Gang was already in shambles.

If Feng Lian insisted on investigating, it would become the final blow to crush the gang.

An internal investigation was far more terrifying than an external enemy attack. It would spark mutual suspicion and distrust among the members, causing the group to completely disintegrate.

As the former leader of the gang, Bing Xiao understood very well what the real crisis facing the Bayonet Gang was.

Traitors?

They might exist, or they might not.

But for now, in the greater scheme of the Bayonet Gang, it was a matter that could be set aside.

What brought Bing Xiao relief was that after a moment's thought, Feng Lian gave him a slight nod, "Then so be it.”

“We'll drop you off at Flower Skirt Island.”

“Be careful on your journey!”

Bing Xiao bowed deeply, expressing his gratitude solemnly: “Thank you, Lord!”

When Zong Ge led the fleet back to Snow Bird Harbor, the docks were still bustling with personnel cleaning up the battlefield.

Broken planks were cleared away, bodies recovered and burned in a mass pyre. Patrol ships maintained vigilant watch, circling the harbor with tenfold intensity.

“How could this happen...” Zong Ge grasped the full extent of the situation upon seeing Zi Di.

The half beastman snorted coldly, "He actually went after them alone!”

The target of the accusation was, of course, the dragonman youngster.

Seeing her beloved blamed, Zi Di felt displeased.

She shot Zong Ge a glance and remarked, “In hindsight, the commander's decision to pursue them was wise.”

“Who could have predicted the navy would ambush the Divine Wind Pirates?”

“From the intelligence we gathered, the navy suffered defeat. But the commander likely rescued Lan Zao amidst the chaotic naval battle.”

The navy's arrival at Snow Bird Harbor had caused such a commotion that Zi Di was initially alarmed and uncertain.

After gathering more intelligence, however, Zi Di began to feel hopeful.

Zong Ge shook his head, his expression grim.

"You had intelligence before the operation began. The Divine Wind's speed surpasses even the Deep Sea Monster Fish. What good would tracking it do?"

“The navy ambushing the Divine Wind Pirates was pure coincidence!”

“More crucially, he acted alone... That's not how a leader should behave.”

Zong Ge voiced his deep dissatisfaction with the dragonman youngster.

He drew a sharp breath, his face clouded with worry, “The navy suffered defeat—this was the Second Fleet of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

“Three gold level cultivators besieged Feng Lian, yet they were soundly crushed. One was even killed outright, only surviving by the grace of resurrection magic.”

“Judging by this battle, Feng Lian must possess peak gold level combat prowess, potentially even rivaling the saint level—like the Empire's gold level peak experts, Long Jia, Gang Duo, and Ge Si Rui.”

"Feng Lian once fled for his life from the saint's pursuit. This intelligence led the kingdom's navy to misjudge the situation. This was one of the primary factors in the navy's defeat."

“I'm deeply concerned for him now.”

“If he acts amidst the chaos, operating under Feng Lian's watchful eye—even with the Deceptive Disguise spell—the risks are immense.”

“Did he mention when he'd return?”

Zi Di shook her head, "No. Such agreements hold no meaning. The commander can only act according to the situation."

“No, it absolutely holds meaning!” Zong Ge declared firmly, “Setting a time limit means that when it expires, he should at least realize he ought to return!”

“Abandoning Lan Zao is the wisest choice.”

“Though he escaped the Mystifying Monster Island with us as a comrade, he remains only bronze level.”

“Sacrificing our leader and the Deep Sea Monster Fish for him would be the worst, most foolish mistake imaginable.”

“You know what worries me even more now?”

“I fear he failed to get what he wanted in the chaos yet refuses to give up. He might still be tailing the Divine Wind Pirates!”

Zi Di's heart skipped a beat, her anxiety intensifying.

Based on her understanding of the dragonman youngster, success might be manageable, but failure would likely mean he’d pursue them relentlessly, just as Zong Ge had warned!

Zi Di regretted, “I should have set a time limit with him.”

"Saying that now won’t change anything. We can only wait." Zong Ge sighed deeply, utterly exhausted.

Just then, a subordinate reported that the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had dispatched a messenger, inviting the dragonman youngster to an audience.

“This is troublesome,” Zong Ge frowned deeply, "The alchemy puppet disguised as the commander might suffice for minor engagements. But it can't replace him in negotiations with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord."

“Why would the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord suddenly invite him at this time?”

“Could it be related to that naval force?”

“Perhaps trouble from Bu Quan has resurfaced?”

Zi Di stood up and said, “No need to panic. We have a plan.”

“Before he departed, he discussed the plan with me.”

"Our strategy may require splitting our forces. To prevent unforeseen complications during this operation, before his departure, he entrusted me with the Deceptive Disguise Formation and some pearl bubbles."

“Now, it's your turn.”

Zong Ge paused, taken aback: “You want me to impersonate him?”

Chapter 597: The Dragonman Youngster Wakes Up

A moment later.

Snow Bird Harbor's city government palace.

Zong Ge and Zi Di were ushered into the study of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

“Commander Long Fu, Magician Yao Ma, I apologize for summoning you both so urgently,” the city lord gestured for them to take seats.

His brow furrowed with worry, yet he maintained a smile, never forsaking his noble bearing.

He discreetly appraised Zi Di, mentally assessing: “The silver level Magician Yao Ma, sent as backup by Long Fu's backers. Her presence here indicates her standing within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

The truth, however, was quite another matter.

To conceal the fact that the dragonman youngster had already departed Snow Bird Harbor, Zong Ge had to impersonate him.

But Zi Di remained uneasy, hence her accompanying presence this time.

She did not speak rashly.

At the outset of negotiations, she could not be the first to respond. It was customary for Long Fu to respond to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, showing due respect.

Otherwise, if the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord offered an apology and Long Fu remained silent, leaving his subordinate Yao Ma to answer, it would be downright arrogant!

Zong Ge nodded, “Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, thank you for your invitation. Might I ask the reason for this meeting?”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was slightly taken aback.

He keenly sensed that Long Fu's tone was different from before.

In past exchanges, the dragonman youngster had always addressed him as "city lord". But now, he used the full title.

“It seems the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has suffered significant losses this time, and Long Fu harbors a strong desire for retribution.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could not have imagined this was purely the Zong Ge militaristic toughness.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's smile broadened slightly: “Since Commander Long Fu is so forthright, I'll skip the pleasantries and get straight to the point.”

“First, please view this magic projection.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord tapped the desk surface.

A three-dimensional magic projection immediately rose from the center of the desk.

The footage played continuously, depicting the earlier scene where the Bayonet Gang leader broke through the blockade, triggering the chaotic battle at the docks.

Zi Di had witnessed it firsthand, but Zong Ge had not.

The magic projection instantly captured the half-beastman's attention.

His gaze was immediately drawn to the rocket assault boat, his heart racing with excitement. He silently assessed: “This silver level magic ship is exceptionally unique. Its speed is astonishing. Though a consumable asset, if deployed strategically, it could prove devastatingly effective on the battlefield.”

"Oh? It can even teleport between vessels!"

“This is the ideal transport craft for assault squads.”

"Is that the Divine Wind? A gold level magic ship, faster than the Deep Sea Monster Fish..."

The magic projection abruptly paused as the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord extended a finger, pointing at a specific figure within the display.

“You both recognize this individual, I presume?” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's gaze grew intense.

Zong Ge and Zi Di felt their hearts sink.

The figure the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord pointed at was none other than Lan Zao!

Lan Zao's discovery was hardly surprising.

The mage tower at Snow Bird Harbor possessed formidable magic recording capabilities.

The tower's surveillance covered nearly the entirety of Snow Bird Harbor, especially the dock area. Back when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps suffered from the Bayonet Gang's tactics, the patrol squad conducted a search. The patrol members had brazenly threatened the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' water pigmen mercenaries with their portable recording devices.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord deliberately released the Bayonet Gang leaders, allowing them to break through the blockade and spark a minor skirmish at the docks. The mage tower, of course, had prepared in advance, meticulously documenting the entire sequence of events.

Post-conflict, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would naturally receive a comprehensive debriefing. His intelligence operatives swiftly detected anomalies concerning Lan Zao.

Lan Zao had been in close proximity to Bing Xiao and others aboard the rocket assault boat, ultimately boarding the Divine Wind alongside them.

Bing Xiao was already a key surveillance target, drawing attention to Lan Zao by association.

Amidst the battlefield chaos, a series of coincidences led Bing Xiao to harbor a misunderstanding about Lan Zao. But the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's subordinates had the advantage of an outsider's perspective. They could replay magic footage, pause it for close-up analysis, and delve deeper into the details.

Thus, they swiftly uncovered the truth—Lan Zao had intended to harm Bing Xiao, but due to slipping footsteps and poor condition, he inadvertently ended up aiding Bing Xiao instead.

Under normal circumstances, such a fool would be mocked and despised. Yet now, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord saw exploitable value in Lan Zao.

That was why he had urgently summoned Long Fu.

“Long Fu” remained silent.

Zi Di finally seized the chance to respond: “City Lord, your insight is truly piercing, discerning the finest details.”

“You uncovered the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' scandal.”

"Indeed, this man was a mole planted by the Bayonet Gang. During the earlier chaos, he exposed himself while rescuing Bing Xiao."

“We only learned of this ourselves moments ago.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord laughed heartily: “Magician Yao Ma, spare us the nonsense. It only wastes our time.”

“I invited you both here for a private discussion precisely because I've already uncovered the truth.”

"This bronze level fighter joined your mercenary group long ago on Snake Rat Island. If the Bayonet Gang wanted to plant a mole, they wouldn't have sent him to Snake Rat Island in advance."

"So, he stumbled into Bing Xiao's trust by accident. Now that he is within the Divine Wind Pirates' ranks, can you contact him?"

Zi Di shook her head with a bitter smile: “City Lord, why must you expose this little lie?”

“This outcome leaves us feeling absurd and helpless too.”

"Please forgive my earlier deception. For the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, losing a brave junior crew to enemy lines puts us in a difficult position."

"Rescue is impossible. Even the navy can't defeat the Divine Wind Pirates—let alone us. So we've had to treat this unlucky fellow as ‘killed in action’.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord nodded slightly, “Truth be told, I also have a spy embedded within the Divine Wind Pirates.”

“It was precisely the specific location provided by that spy that allowed the navy to successfully ambush the Divine Wind Pirates.”

“I never imagined Feng Lian possessed such combat prowess. Had it not been for this unexpected turn, the leaders of the Bayonet Gang and Feng Lian himself would already be our prisoners.”

“Barring any surprises, the spy I sent should have succumbed to poison by now, dying a heroic death.”

"This battle has shattered the tacit understanding between our nation and the Divine Wind Pirates. This pirate crew will undoubtedly launch fierce retaliation against our coastal areas or merchant ships in the near future."

“Therefore, friends, can you discreetly contact your mercenary member?”

Zong Ge and Zi Di exchanged a swift glance.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's intent was clear: after sacrificing his own spy, he sought another set of eyes.

Lan Zao from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was an ideal candidate for development.

“No, we don't,” Zi Di shook her head again. "While our higher-ups possess alchemy communication devices, this mercenary... he's merely bronze level, at best a small group leader. He lacks the standing for such equipment."

“Truly none? He was the boatswain on your flagship.” The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had clearly conducted thorough inquiries.

“Truly not,” Zi Di shook her head again. "A bronze level boatswain could be eliminated in a single mishap. For our corps, an iron level boatswain would be far more suitable."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed.

Zong Ge interjected, “If we're discussing covert communication methods, would the mercenary group's unique code markings count?”

"I suppose if he managed to conceal his identity and survive within the Divine Wind Pirates, our corps could send someone to make contact in the future and recruit him as a spy."

“Of course, that possibility is quite low.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord pondered, "Even if the odds are slim, it's worth a try. Regarding this collaboration, let us first formalize the agreement."

The city lord produced a magic contract.

He had been prepared for this moment.

The contract's terms centered on developing Lan Zao into a spy serving both parties.

Zi Di scrutinized it carefully, confirming its safety.

Subsequently, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and Zong Ge signed the magic contract on the spot.

“I will edit this magic recording, destroy the original copy, and submit the edited version to higher authorities for reporting purposes.”

“As for those privy to this matter, I will ensure they sign subsequent confidentiality agreements.”

“Let us hope he survives—though the odds are slim.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord sighed.

Zong Ge immediately rose, “Since the matter is settled, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, we must take our leave.”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord looked slightly taken aback, “Is there an urgent matter, Commander Long Fu?”

Zong Ge strained to soften his tone, though it remained stiff. "It is not urgent, but there is much to discuss. As you know, we plan to depart for the royal capital shortly."

"Then join us for tonight's banquet. I'm hosting distinguished guests from the Li Jian Clan." The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smiled.

Zi Di's eyes brightened slightly: “May I ask, is it Lord Kong Pan?”

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord laughed heartily: "Precisely. Given our consistently pleasant collaborations, I'll offer you a friendly tip. Cultivating relations with the Li Jian Clan will greatly benefit both your trip to the capital and your arms trade!"

Darkness.

A profound, unfathomable darkness.

Within this void, the youngster's consciousness gradually stirred.

“Where?”

“What is this place?”

“Where am I?”

Boundless darkness enveloped him, silent and still.

“Then... who am I?”

“Who am I?”

“What... happened to me?”

No response.

Helpless, the young man groped his way through the darkness.

He had no idea how long he wandered, nor how far he'd gone.

“What's that sound?”

The young man thought he heard voices, people calling his name.

But he couldn't make it out clearly. He moved forward, following the sound.

A faint glow began to appear in the darkness.

The light was orange.

Very faint.

Almost imperceptible, flickering in and out.

The youngster strained toward the orange glow.

The next moment, he awoke.

Before him was the familiar ceiling, gleaming with the distinctive metallic sheen of the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

“I...” The dragonman youngster found himself lying in bed. The confusion in his eyes quickly faded, “I broke free from the wreckage, yet still got hit by a terrifyingly powerful battle skill.”

“So... the tower spirit saved me?”

"Lan Zao! Damn it..."

“I must find a way to rescue him. I hope I wasn't unconscious for long—the Divine Wind Pirates can't have gone far.”

The dragonman youngster cursed under his breath, leaping to his feet. He flung open the hatch and sprinted toward the bridge.

No sooner had he stepped into the corridor than the tower spirit's deliberately hushed voice echoed: "Captain, minimize all movement. Maintain absolute silence. We are in an unknown perilous situation. There is a risk of complete annihilation!"

Chapter 598: Saint Level Colossal Beast

Upon receiving the tower spirit's warning, the dragonman youngster halted abruptly, coming to a complete stop.

Only then did he notice the dim lighting along the passageway and the eerie silence permeating the entire Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The dragonman youngster inquired, "Tower spirit, what exactly is happening?”

The tower spirit replied, "Current ship coordinates unknown. The artifact Mermaid Fairy Tale has shown abnormal activity. Estimated teleportation probability: 99%. Probability of being trapped in a super-large illusion: 0.8%. Other possibilities: 0.2%. Captain, I recommend you consult the navigation log."

The Deep Sea Monster Fish's navigation logs existed in multiple formats, recording nearly every moment.

The dragonman youngster tiptoed to the bridge.

Standing before the alchemy control panel, he swiftly accessed the logs.

He discovered he had been unconscious for only a short time—just a few hours.

“What happened while I was unconscious?”

The magic projection began playing.

He witnessed Feng Lian's fierce battle against three gold level opponents.

This scene had naturally been recorded by the Deep Sea Monster Fish, but the dragonman youngster, trapped inside the Divine Wind at the time, remained unaware.

“Feng Lian's combat prowess is astonishing!”

“So that was Feng Lian's attack...”

The young man soon witnessed himself breaching the Divine Wind's hull and plunging into the sea.

Yet in the next instant, a faint blue wind pursued him, seeping into his body.

The dragonman youngster felt his body convulse violently, then all life left him.

His form slowly sank into the depths, but soon, the Deep Sea Monster Fish dispatched deep-sea salvagers to retrieve the motionless youngster aboard their vessel.

Throughout this process, a crimson glow began to flicker across the dragonman youngster's body.

“Had the Bloodcore activated?” the dragonman youngster's heart pounded violently.

He knew this glow all too well.

"So that means I was nearly killed. The Bloodcore activated, repairing the organs destroyed within me.

“This mysterious alchemy artifact can actually activate on its own?”

“Did it sense its host's life-threatening crisis and trigger itself?”

The dragonman youngster had sensed it when that wisp of breeze swept by.

But since it happened aboard the Divine Wind, the magic projection from the Deep Sea Monster Fish didn't capture that moment.

Still, the dragonman youngster remembered that breeze vividly!

For when he infiltrated the Divine Wind, he wore alchemy goggles.

This alchemy artifact was gifted to the dragonman youngster by Bu Quan. It could detect threats and mark their severity with different colors.

This treasure proved remarkably practical, offering significant assistance during the dragonman youngster's battle against Teng Dong Lang.

Naturally, the dragonman youngster wouldn't abandon such a practical alchemy tool for this infiltration.

Of course, like the Grand Arena Medal and the Golden Silk Chainmail, it had been enchanted with Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell.

A wisp of breeze, seemingly insignificant, yet in the dragonman youngster's vision, it glowed a deep, almost black purple!

The threat levels marked on the goggles ranged from white (lowest) to green, yellow, red, and purple (highest).

Purple signified the highest level, indicating combat power far surpassing the user's own. Retreat was the paramount recommendation.

That wisp of breeze wasn't merely purple—it was a deep, almost black purple.

Thus, the moment the dragonman youngster detected it, he fled without hesitation, breaking through the ship's hull.

Yet he still couldn't evade the strike.

“Without the Bloodcore, I'd be dead!” the dragonman youngster shuddered in the aftershock.

After being carried back aboard, the yellow crab alchemy puppet rushed over. Extending its crab-like legs, it examined the dragonman youngster thoroughly and delivered his diagnosis.

After receiving minimal treatment, the dragonman youngster was sent to the captain's cabin to rest.

Just as the alchemist automaton exited the cabin, a sudden burst of blue-white light erupted, filling the entire Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The logbook's entries halted at this point.

Recalling the tower spirit's earlier response, the dragonman youngster immediately retrieved the magic projection from the other cabin moments prior.

The artifact within the cabin, the Mermaid Fairy Tale, was indeed the source of the blue-white light's eruption.

Before the light erupted, the artifact had violently trembled, accompanied by a sharp, piercing sound. The noise resembled fingernails scraping across glass.

“The tower spirit was right—it's this artifact again!” the dragonman youngster sighed bitterly.

The Mermaid Fairy Tale had shown signs of disturbance before.

That was during the great battle on Two Eyes Island.

After its disturbance, the artifact had inadvertently exposed the fishman's crucial disguise, unexpectedly granting the survivors tangible benefits.

Now, it had stirred once again.

According to the tower spirit's analysis, the entire ship has been teleported to an unknown location.

The dragonman youngster continued reviewing the next segment of the navigation log.

The magic projection showed the Deep Sea Monster Fish scouting the surroundings.

Thus, the dragonman youngster beheld a 'mountain'.

A 'snow mountain'.

He couldn't help but gasp. This wasn't a snow mountain at all—it was clearly a gigantic, snow-white beast as vast as a mountain.

The life force emanating from the colossal beast sent a chill straight to his heart.

Saint level!

“What is this saint level colossal beast?”

“It's surrounded by seawater—it's still in the ocean.”

“But the light is intense... not from the beast itself...”

“This... oh heavens!”

The magic footage continued, revealing the snow-mountain beast's habitat.

His mouth fell open, his face frozen in shock.

Snow Bird Harbor City Palace.

The banquet hall glowed with radiance even more intense than the mysterious location the dragonman youngster found himself in.

Delicacies wafted their fragrances, and melodious tunes filled the air.

Guests gathered in small groups, their conversations punctuated by occasional glances toward a single corner.

There, nearly all the most important figures in Snow Bird Harbor at that moment were assembled.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Sea Bird Bishop, Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the two naval commanders, Kong Pan and Yue Qian.

Introduced by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Zong Ge bowed in greeting.

Kong Pan smiled, sizing Zong Ge up, “Commander Long Fu, slaying a gold level foe as a silver level fighter - your fame has spread across the nation.”

Zong Ge immediately responded: “A significant portion of that achievement belongs to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. It was precisely because he had voluntarily lent me his equipment, enhancing my combat strength, that I managed to kill Teng Dong Lang at the last moment."

This answer made the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smile even more broadly.

Zi Di stood nearby, holding a wine cup, a smile on her face.

Zong Ge's response, of course, wasn't his own idea. Zi Di had anticipated the conversation's direction and briefed Zong Ge beforehand.

Having recited the script, Zong Ge had managed to navigate the situation for now.

“I'm feeling a bit tired. You young folks should chat among yourselves.” After exchanging a few more words, Kong Pan frowned slightly, signaling her departure.

She was injured and undergoing treatment.

In contrast, Yue Qian, who had been fatally wounded, was now perfectly healthy thanks to a full resurrection.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hastily offered a specially prepared room for Kong Pan's use.

But Kong Pan shook her head, saying self-deprecatingly, "I feel more at ease staying on the ship. I'm getting old - perhaps it's just a bad habit of the elderly."

"Lord Kong Pan, forgive my boldness, but might I speak with you privately? Would a few minutes of your time be possible?" Seeing this, Zi Di quickly interjected.

Kong Pan's departure disrupted her plans, forcing her to act.

According to intelligence gathered, Sand Between Fingers was an outsider recruited by the Li Jian Clan. Though Yue Qian was their future pillar, his career had been almost entirely military, with little experience in governance.

For the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to gain the Li Jian Clan's support, they needed to approach through Kong Pan.

Kong Pan paused, glancing at the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord hastily returned a smile.

Only then did Kong Pan reluctantly nod, “Follow me. Before I leave the city government palace and board my carriage, I expect you to make yourself clear.”

In her eyes, Zi Di was merely a silver level magician and a member of a minor mercenary group. Even with arms dealer connections, she held no standing worthy of Kong Pan's time.

Zi Di hastily thanked her, shot a glance at Zong Ge before departing, then hurriedly accompanied Kong Pan out of the banquet hall.

She began by flattering Kong Pan, but noticed her gaze remained distant and expressionless.

She then mentioned that their side had prepared some supplies to satisfy the Kingdom Navy's Second Fleet.

This piqued Kong Pan's interest slightly.

She knew the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was backed by arms dealers.

But after inquiring, she was disappointed to discover the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was only gifting a batch of dragon rider potions.

Zi Di remained unhurried. As Kong Pan boarded the carriage, she handed over a storage pouch filled with a full 50,000 gold coins.

Kong Pan accepted the pouch and finally smiled, though she did not invite Zi Di aboard: “The day is already late, but we shall rest for three days at Snow Bird Harbor.”

Receiving this response, Zi Di understood perfectly. She watched until the carriage vanished at the end of the street before turning and reentering the banquet hall.

She discovered the atmosphere in the banquet hall had drastically changed.

No one danced on the dance floor anymore.

Zong Ge and Yue Qian stood facing each other in the center of the dance floor, weapons drawn, combat poised to erupt at any moment.

"Wh-what happened? How long have I been gone?!" Zi Di gasped in shock.

Chapter 599: Zong Ge vs Yue Qian

Zi Di hurriedly scanned the banquet hall for the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

She soon spotted her target and hurried over to confront him: "City Lord, what on earth is going on? Why are they suddenly fighting?"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord glanced at Zi Di before shifting his gaze back to the dance floor, “Commander Long Fu rashly probed military intelligence, provoking Commander Yue Qian.”

"Commander Yue Qian then challenged Commander Long Fu to a duel as a wager. One of the stakes is the specific military intelligence regarding their clash with the Divine Wind Pirates."

Zi Di was momentarily speechless.

Before attending this banquet, she had indeed discussed certain matters with Zong Ge.

One objective was to extract detailed battle intelligence from the navy to confirm the situation of the dragonman youngster.

Though absent, Zi Di could guess Zong Ge’s abrupt tone had secretly angered Yue Qian.

Zong Ge never minced words. His unyielding nature refused compromise, so when Yue Qian challenged him, he accepted without hesitation.

Zi Di's heart filled with dread: "The Commander is only silver level. Commander Yue Qian is gold level - one of the commanders of your nation's Second Fleet. His combat prowess far surpasses Teng Dong Lang's. This duel has already been decided before it even began. What do you think, City Lord?"

Zi Di sought this excuse to halt the duel.

The city lord didn't look at her as he replied, "Commander Yue Qian pledged before the fight that he would suppress his battle qi to the silver level to ensure fairness! And I was invited to serve as the referee, guaranteeing impartiality."

Zi Di gritted her teeth. "Commander Yue Qian voluntarily suppressing his cultivation? That shows he's confident he can handle the commander even at that level. Oh dear, what are we to do?"

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord offered no support, merely replying coolly, “We shall see.”

He now harbored resentment toward Zong Ge as well.

Why stir up old wounds when things are peaceful?

Hadn't the navy suffered defeat? Wasn't Yue Qian resurrected?

When giving in was wise, he remained stubborn. Did slaying Teng Dong Lang make Long Fu think he could handle all gold level cultivators?

One had to understand—not all gold level experts were the same. Sometimes, the gap between them was as vast as heaven and earth.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord never intended to preside over this battle.

His original purpose was merely to host a banquet to strengthen ties with the navy; the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was merely an afterthought.

He had facilitated introductions for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to the upper echelons of the Li Jian Clan—this was his way of aiding Long Fu.

Yet at the banquet, Long Fu spoke stiffly, showed no tact, and clashed with Yue Qian. Ignoring the city lord's disapproval, he publicly challenged him to a duel.

What manner of behavior was this from Long Fu?

It was nothing short of repaying kindness with ingratitude.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was highly perceptive. He could tell: though Yue Qian maintained a facade of cheerful conversation after being insulted by Zong Ge, he was actually intent on teaching Long Fu a lesson.

Thus, Snow Bird Harbor City Lord could not intervene and instead went with the flow, presiding over the duel.

“Come then. Let me see how skilled you are with your dual spears after slaying a gold level foe beyond your level!” Yue Qian taunted Zong Ge.

Zong Ge held a short spear in each hand.

The spears were silver level, drawn from the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's treasury.

The dragonman youngster's pair of gold level short spears, naturally, had departed Snow Bird Harbor aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish alongside their true owner.

It was worth noting that Yue Qian wielded not a slender sword, but a long staff.

The staff, too, was merely silver level.

Zong Ge's eyes locked onto Yue Qian. Suddenly lunging forward, he whirled his twin spears in a sweeping thrust, one to the left, one to the right.

Yue Qian leapt lightly backward, creating distance between himself and Zong Ge. Simultaneously, he shifted his stance, driving one end of the staff straight toward Zong Ge's chest.

Yue Qian's weapon was far longer than Zong Ge's. Before Zong Ge could land his thrust, he would be struck by the staff.

Had the dragonman youngster been here, he likely would have chosen to duck and break through.

The Surge battle skill could alter direction in an instant, making it highly effective in actual combat.

But Zong Ge could not use it.

He could only maintain his charging posture. As the staff approached, he abruptly withdrew his arms, using his left hand's short spear to brace against the staff.

The short spear and staff body scraped violently, sparks flying.

Zong Ge nearly shouldered the staff as he continued charging straight at Yue Qian.

Yue Qian raised an eyebrow, visibly surprised.

He had seen the magic footage of the dragonman youngster's duel with Teng Dong Lang.

The incident occurred before his current campaign.

A silver level slaying a gold level—it was the biggest news in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom for quite some time. The related magic footage sold like hotcakes.

Yue Qian had already gotten his hands on it.

Thus, he wasn't entirely unfamiliar with the dragonman youngster; on the contrary, he was quite familiar with certain behaviors.

Yue Qian's thrust with his long staff at Zong Ge was actually a feint, designed to lure his opponent into a low-stance charge. This way, Yue Qian would be prepared, executing a set combo to unleash a barrage of strikes on the dragonman youngster, ensuring he was overwhelmed and unable to retaliate.

But the “dragonman youngster” before him wasn't the real one—it was Zong Ge in disguise.

Zong Ge's fighting style bore the hallmarks of military training—straightforward, relentless, and focused on the most efficient means of elimination.

Yue Qian's plan backfired. Seeing Zong Ge close in, he hesitated, ultimately choosing not to use his signature battle skill to evade through spatial displacement.

The reason was simple.

Yue Qian was at the gold level. Even while suppressing his strength, his power and status far surpassed Long Fu's.

To resort to his signature battle skill to evade so early in the fight would be utterly humiliating.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

The long staff and short spears clashed repeatedly, their impacts echoing through the air.

The crowd gasped in astonishment.

They were attending a banquet, yet they were witnessing a clash between Long Fu and Yue Qian, this delighted everyone immensely.

But after the fight commenced, it was “Long Fu” who gained the upper hand, driving Yue Qian back step by step. This surprised the audience even more.

Yue Qian felt utterly frustrated.

He was being beaten into a defensive stance, unable to strike back!

Several attempts to counter were thwarted by Zong Ge's twin spears—whether thrusts or sweeps, all parried.

Zi Di's expression froze in disbelief.

She hadn't anticipated Zong Ge's advantage being so overwhelming!

Beside her, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord—a dual cultivator of magic and battle skills—possessed deeper insight than Zi Di.

"Yue Qian is suppressing his combat power, revealing only silver level strength. His long staff attacks are inherently slower than short spears. Once drawn into close quarters, even I would be forced into a disadvantage."

“But his greater distress stems from refusing to suffer even a scratch. He's so preoccupied with preserving his pride that he's actually losing face.”

Zong Ge's combat style was military-like, where trading minor injuries for major ones, or even trading injuries for death, was commonplace.

If Yue Qian had fought him head-on, they would have been evenly matched, and Zong Ge wouldn't have gained much advantage.

But Yue Qian's mindset was: As a proud gold level fighter, the Second Naval Commander, and the future banner of the Li Jian Clan, how could I possibly get injured in a duel with a silver level opponent?

It was precisely this mindset that left him exhausted from constant defense.

Zong Ge pressed Yue Qian back, striding forward with long strides while the latter could only fight while retreating.

They soon charged out of the dance floor.

In his retreat, Yue Qian slammed directly into a dining table.

He let out a fierce growl, his battle qi erupting as he severed the long staff in his hands.

Holding half a staff in each hand, his attack frequency surged, parrying Zong Ge's twin short spears.

He finally stabilized his footing!

A dinner plate tumbled from the table, poised to crash to the floor. As if seeing behind him, Yue Qian kicked backward, sending the plate flying back onto the table.

This deft maneuver drew gasps and murmurs of astonishment from the crowd.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, however, rolled his eyes inwardly.

Yue Qian was still too concerned with appearances!

Earlier, when he was being forced back, he could have easily used a battle skill to break this unfavorable situation.

But from start to finish, Yue Qian hadn't employed a single battle skill.

The reason was simple: “Long Fu” had not used his battle skills yet. If he were to be the first to use one, what reputation would he have left?

Thus, Yue Qian ultimately resorted to using his battle qi to sever his own long staff.

By doing so, he increased his attack frequency and overcame his disadvantage.

When the opportunity arose, he did not counterattack Zong Ge. Instead, he caught a tray that was about to crash to the ground. It seemed to say: Look, even in combat, I haven't damaged the host's property. Everything remains under my control.

This small gesture did indeed shift the audience's perception of Yue Qian.

Subsequently, Yue Qian gradually gained the upper hand.

With neither fighter employing battle skills and both possessing ample stamina, superior fundamental combat skills naturally determined the advantage.

Though Zong Ge was a weapon master, his specialty lay not in twin spears.

Yue Qian likewise wasn't adept with dual staffs, yet his techniques proved more refined than Zong Ge's.

Zong Ge came from a distinguished lineage, but having endured discrimination since childhood, he hadn't been taught his family's techniques. Instead, he'd been trained by the military.

Military techniques naturally couldn't match the depth of family traditions. Their hallmark was simplicity, practicality, and seamless coordination.

Though Yue Qian was a naval commander, he had been trained by the Li Jian Clan since childhood, naturally mastering more refined martial arts than Zong Ge.

Yue Qian gradually turned the tide.

Zong Ge steadily retreated.

The two fought their way back from the outer perimeter to the dance floor.

Spectators began cheering for Yue Qian.

Zong Ge's fighting style was crude and brutal, while Yue Qian's moves were intricate and elegant, making him far more appealing to the crowd.

Zi Di was frantic with worry. Amidst the roaring atmosphere, she deliberately shouted at Zong Ge, “Commander! Use your Spear Throw to fight back!”

Zong Ge's fighting spirit surged. He had been about to unleash his battle skills when Zi Di's reminder reached him.

He suddenly leapt back, creating distance, and threw both spears to the ground.

Yue Qian's pupils narrowed slightly. He thought to himself, “He wants to fight with bare hands?”

He hesitated inwardly, somewhat reluctant.

He had suggested using weapons earlier for a simple reason: engaging in close quarters combat with a dragonman was hardly dignified.

One careless moment, a punch to the face, a bruise forming, where would his dignity be then?

But if “Long Fu” insisted on bare-knuckle combat, he'd have no choice but to abandon his weapon and see it through.

As a proud gold level, evading battle was unthinkable.

The next moment, however, he heard “Long Fu” declare, “No more fighting!”

Those around them were astonished, deeply disappointed.

“I've only just warmed up. Why?” Yue Qian inquired.

Zong Ge shook his head: “Too many restrictions. It's no fun fighting like this.”

Zi Di had deliberately reminded Zong Ge to use a spear-based battle skill earlier—a reverse hint to remind him of the occasion and their original purpose!

“It was indeed unsatisfying,” Yue Qian relaxed, “But it is interesting. Long Fu, you're not quite what I imagined.”

After fighting for a while, the anger Yue Qian felt from being offended dissipated.

Zi Di hurried between them: "Since we initiated the ceasefire, we lost this wager. The subsequent stakes will be delivered personally by me to the naval warship."

She had intended to visit Kong Pan anyway.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord smiled, impressed by Zi Di's quick reaction.

But Zong Ge shook his head, his expression stern, “When I said the fight wasn't satisfying, I didn't mean I admit defeat, Magician Yao Ma.”

“How about a private duel between us? What do you say?”

Zi Di spun around instantly, glaring at Zong Ge with disbelief.

Zong Ge's fighting spirit surged, his gaze fixed solely on Yue Qian.

Yue Qian paused, then burst into laughter.

At that moment, he found himself developing goodwill towards Zong Ge.

Chapter 600: The Dragonman Youngster’s New Bloodline

Unknown ocean depths.

Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Seated within the cockpit, the dragonman youngster scanned the surrounding environment through this magic ship.

First in his sights was the colossal sea beast, towering like a snow-capped mountain.

The creature’s sheer size was awe-inspiring, but its life force surged at the peak of the saint level.

“This is absolutely beyond my current capabilities.”

The dragonman youngster's expression was grave.

Though he had slain Teng Dong Lang, it was primarily due to the reversal granted by the Lava Hell spell scroll. The final decisive moment remained unclear.

Earlier, while attempting to rescue Lan Zao, he had risked diving into the Divine Wind. There, he had suffered a direct strike from Feng Lian.

He wasn't even Feng Lian's primary target!

Under those circumstances, had the Bloodcore not performed its unforeseeable emergency healing, he would have perished.

Thus, the dragonman youngster understood his place all too well.

“My current combat strength might allow me to face off against someone like Teng Dong Lang, a gold level opponent, but I'm absolutely no match for Feng Lian.”

“In fact, after returning much of my gear to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, my combat power has dropped significantly.”

“Let alone a saint level opponent!”

The dragonman youngster had no intention of confronting this saint level colossal sea beast. His first thought was to evacuate this place.

Thus, he began surveying his surroundings.

The next moment, his eyes widened in astonishment, and he let out an incredulous gasp.

He saw countless pearls growing within the cave walls.

These pearls varied greatly in size, some were as large as carriages, while others were no bigger than fists.

The pearls were countless, densely packed!

Typically, pearls varied in quality—some dull and misshapen, others lustrous and perfectly formed.

But the dragonman youngster observed that every pearl on these walls, regardless of size, was perfectly spherical. All were a milky white hue, and instantly captivating to behold.

This was an undersea cavern.

Its walls were covered in milky-white pearls. At the cavern's center lay a snow-white colossal beast, curled up so its full form was hidden.

The cavern was flooded with seawater, its multicolored light rippling softly with the waves.

These vibrant hues emanated from the pearls themselves.

Each pearl glowed with its own halo—some in soft pastels, others in azure or emerald.

The light converged and intertwined, creating a dazzling display reminiscent of the northern lights, breathtakingly beautiful.

Such a wondrous sight left the dragonman youngster utterly transfixed.

What made his heart race, however, was his discovery that every single pearl—regardless of size—was a transcendent material!

Bronze level, iron level, saint level, gold level, saint level, legend level...

Even more astonishingly, the youngster detected one pearl radiating a god level aura!

A god level pearl!

“Where on earth have I been transported to?” the dragonman youngster wondered, his mind filled with confusion.

He wasn't a magician and lacked Cang Xu's vast knowledge.

He could only ask the tower spirit.

The tower spirit did not know.

“Could it be that even the War Merchant's database holds no records?”

“No, perhaps it's due to damage.”

The tower spirit's database was incomplete.

But there was no other way; this was already the result of the survivors' all-out rescue efforts.

Without specific intelligence, the dragonman youngster, though intensely covetous, cautiously restrained himself and did not lay a hand on these pearls.

The phrase “worth a fortune” was insufficient to measure the value of this mysterious cavern.

The god level pearl alone was an item extinct on the market.

The combined value of the legend level pearls alone could easily purchase Snow Bird Harbor.

“The mysterious colossal beast seems to be slumbering...”

"Now it appears this teleportation might have brought me a surprise? A windfall from heaven?"

Realizing this, the dragonman youngster gradually set aside his worries and began searching for an exit from the cavern.

First, confirm an escape route. Then, without disturbing the colossal beast, harvest as many of the most valuable pearls as possible.

That was the dragonman youngster's plan.

However, the result left him dumbfounded.

"How could there be no exit?!"

After thoroughly scouting the area, he discovered this peculiar cave was completely sealed. Not only was there no exit, not even the slightest crack!

This greatly exceeded the dragonman youngster's expectations.

With the underwater colossal beast dwelling here, if the cave were completely isolated, how could it hunt and survive?

“If it's cut off from the outside world, then whether this is truly the ocean floor is uncertain.”

The dragonman youngster had previously concluded it was the sea floor because the cavern was filled with seawater.

The water's composition had long been analyzed by the tower spirit, confirming it as seawater.

But now, who knew what lay beyond the cavern?

Even a desert was a possibility!

“No passageway? Are we supposed to bombard our way through and create one?” The dragonman youngster frowned deeply, his face clouded with worry.

Currently, the Deep Sea Monster Fish was cloaked in Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell, disguised as an undersea reef, drawing no reaction from the colossal sea beast below.

But if it resorted to bombardment, even with both spells concealing it, the explosions and shattered cavern walls would provoke a fierce response from the deep sea colossal beast.

“Can the Deep Sea Monster Fish even breach these walls?” The dragonman youngster voiced his doubts.

The vessel's cannons were merely gold level, while this sealed cavern produced a god level pearl, the disparity was staggering.

This made the youngster deeply uncertain about the effectiveness of bombardment.

He dared not attempt it recklessly.

The threat posed by a saint level mysterious colossal beast was too great.

He might very well perish if he tried.

“What should we do?” The dragonman youngster asked the tower spirit.

The tower spirit was equally stumped.

It possessed formidable computational abilities, but these relied on intelligence.

The more detailed the intelligence, the more effectively it could derive suitable solutions.

Yet now, both the dragonman youngster and the tower spirit knew nothing about the snow-white colossal beast or this mysterious cavern.

“It seems we must enhance our reconnaissance methods and probe deeper,” the dragonman youngster concluded after calming down and analyzing the situation.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish possessed three reconnaissance methods: sonar, light detection, and life detection scanning.

Its sonar device could even emit infrasound to repel most sea beasts.

Combining these three methods ultimately formed the magic projections displayed before the pilot.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish's reconnaissance capabilities were actually quite robust.

But they weren't comprehensive.

Previously, on Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island, the survivors had discovered its flaw through practical experience - the Deep Sea Monster Fish's reconnaissance was too coarse.

Therefore, survivors crafted numerous bird-type alchemy puppets for detailed reconnaissance. This somewhat compensated for its weakness.

Now, the dragonman youngster realized the Deep Sea Monster Fish's reconnaissance lacked depth.

Faced with a saint level colossal beast and a mysterious sealed cavern, it was utterly helpless, unable to penetrate the target's defenses.

“Is there any way to enhance our reconnaissance capabilities?” The dragonman youngster inquired.

The tower spirit performed calculations for over ten seconds, generating a multitude of solutions.

The youngster felt dizzy just looking at them. Not being a magician, he couldn't make sense of any of the proposals and could only continue questioning the tower spirit.

The tower spirit's response was to awaken Zhong Yong and have him use the alchemy cabin to craft several specialized reconnaissance puppets.

The second solution was to awaken Zhong Yong and upgrade the Deep Sea Monster Fish's alchemy reconnaissance devices. Currently, all three reconnaissance devices were only silver level, not yet reaching gold level.

The third plan was...

After hearing the first ten options, the dragonman youngster realized every single one involved Zhong Yong.

He was utterly useless at alchemy; only with Zhong Yong's help could any alchemy output be achieved.

The fishman magician Zhong Yong was currently asleep.

Ever since he had cobbled together the Bloodline Purification Formation, he had become the Deep Sea Monster Fish's resident magician. His movements were severely restricted, confined to a few designated cabins, and he was constantly accompanied and monitored by alchemy puppets.

When the dragonman youngster set sail from Snow Bird Harbor aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish, he had drugged Zhong Yong unconscious beforehand to prevent him from detecting the vibrations of the “Secret Alchemy Workshop” and realizing it was mobile.

This arrangement now served the dragonman youngster well.

After a moment's consideration, he did not immediately rouse Zhong Yong, choosing instead to prepare in advance.

He went to the storage hold and pulled out a freezer.

Sealed within the freezer lay a corpse.

It was not Zi Di, but a fishman.

The fishman's corpse emanated a saint level aura.

It was one of the unexpected spoils the survivors had obtained during the battle of Two Eyes Island.

After acquiring it, the dragonman youngster chose not to consume it with his Bloodcore.

The primary reason was resource efficiency.

If he absorbed it with his Bloodcore, the value of this saint corpse would only manifest within him, granting him a modest boost in combat power. Such a choice offered far too low a return on investment.

The youngster already possessed the legendary bloodline of the Flame Dragon King, along with immense potential for growth. He lacked the energy and time to develop another life form.

More importantly, in the survivors' current plans, the practical applications for a fishman form were limited.

By preserving the saint corpse, however, numerous avenues for resource utilization opened up. For instance, when short on funds, it could be sold off. But if absorbed by the Bloodcore, he couldn't sell it anymore!

The survivors haven't uncovered the specific secrets yet, and there remains a possibility: that survivors could utilize this corpse to gain immense benefits from the fishmen.

In short, preserving the fishman's saint corpse for future use is a highly prudent course of action.

But now, the dragonman youngster had no intention of keeping this saint corpse.

"If the Deep Sea Monster Fish is destroyed during the bombardment of the cavern walls or our escape, I can assume fishman form and survive in the water."

Looking through the Deep Sea Monster Fish's storehouses, what could possibly compare to the bloodline of this fishman saint corpse?

On the late-night sea, thick fog accompanied the ghost ship as it moved steadily.

After days of sailing, Jian Gui, Cang Xu, and their crew finally reached their destination.

“White Hair Trench lies ahead.”

“Qi Ling's clan should be within the trench, participating in the hunt,” stated Jian Gui.

Their mission was to rescue the fishman ghost, Qi Ling, who was also a necromancer.
